*PAGE 1*


 Ahmad meyasa kake irin haka, miye laifina a ciki, duk soyayyar da kanuna min a baya ban ta6a tunanin haka daga garekaba yanzu saboda kawai kyautar da ubangiji yabamu zaka butulce min, na rok'eka kataimaka kaja 'ya'yanka jikinka kamar yadda sauran iyaye sukeyi, kar ka mance wasu ma suna nema ammah basu samuba, kaduba kaga yadda kabanzatar da rayuwarsu kamar ba kaine mahaifinsuba, ka ajiye duk wani buri naka a gefe domin ubangiji shi yake azurtawa sannan idan yak'addara zakayi kud'i toh 'ya'ya basu isa suhanaba.

Cikin tsawa yace ke Maryam! Ya isheki haka indai abinda yakawoki d'akina kenan tashi kifita dan bazaki ta6a canza min ra'ayina ba, yaran banza ai tun farko na ce miki ni ban shirya fara tara 'ya'yaba ko ban gargad'eki da hakaba?

'Dago idanuwanta tayi da suka kumbura saboda tsananin kukan da take tace toh ya zanyi bayan haka ubangiji yatsara min rayuwata sannan 'ya'yana da ubansu ba na shege bane yaran.

Zancen banza da wofi ai kinga matsalarki tun lokacin da kika samu ciki na farko saida nace kizubar kizubar ammah kika k'i saida kika haifeshi saboda kin rainani ak'arshe saida kika ajiye min 'ya'ya d'aid'aya har ukku a gida, toh bari kiji ina k'ara tunatar da ke wad'annan 'ya'yan banda alak'a da su ban haifaba naira d'aya tawa bazata ta6a kuka ba wajen siyan koda ace maggi ne bare ma asa ran zan siya masu abinda zasuci, keda kikeson 'ya'yan sai kije gaki gasu nidai ban shirya fara haihuwa ba  sai na cika burina naga na samu aiki mekyau nayi kud'i sannan in auri wadda zata zataso ra'ayina inyaso daga baya idan nafara son 'ya'yan sai ta haifa min, haba ina amfanin ace daga aure anfara tara maka 'ya'ya kai kana fama da kanka sannan a k'ara aza maka wani nauyin ke ni duk ma lokacin da kikazo min da maganar yaran chan ji nake kamar inrufeki da duka, dan haka kitashi kibani waje ko insa6a maki yanzun nan.

Jinjina kai tayi hawaye suna cigaba da zuba daga idanunta tace naji zan fita ammah inaso kasani ubangiji shi zai raya min su insha Allahu zan basu farin cikin da duk wani me uba zai samu kaikuma ba zance komai ba saidai ince kaje Allah yashirya ka,,,mik'ewa tayi fuuuu zata fita har ta kai k'ofa yace idan kin fita kice ma 'ya'yanki kar insaki inji maganarsu dan bacci zanyi inko sukayi gigin tashina toh sai na sa6a masu agidannan.

'Dan guntun murmushi tayi cike da takaici sannan taficce.

Tana fita tsayawa tayi tagoge hawayenta sannan tawuce d'akinta tana shiga tasamu yaranta biyu zaune sunyi jugum, murmushi tayi taje kusa dasu tazauna tare da janyo yaron da bazai gaza shekaru shidda ba tace Ya dai Sadeek wa yata6a min ku?
Kallon fuskarta yake kamar zaiyi kuka yace Umma kema Abbah fad'a yayi miki irin wanda yakeyi mana?
Shafa kansa tayi tace a'a Sadeek ba fad'a yayi min ba.

Toh kuma Ummah fuskarki kamar kinyi kuka kuma munjiyo yana maki fad'a,,,cewar d'ayar yarinyar da bazata gaza shekaru goma ba.

Murmushi tayi tace ke Safiya baku dai ji sosai ba ammah ba fad'a yayi min ba kawai dai k'waro ne yafad'a min a ido kuma na cire kin gani?.

Toh Ummah meyasa Abbah baya sonmu? Ko bashine babanmu ba?

Da mamaki take kallon d'an nata ganin shi yayi maganar chan tace Sadeek ka cika magana dayawa toh wanene babanku inba shi ba? kawai dai dan kuna rashin ji ne saisa yakeyi maku fad'a ammah da kun daina shima zai daina.

Toh Ummah kice mashi nidai bazan k'ara rashin ji ba yadaina dukana da min fad'a, kema Yaya safiya kin daina ko?

Safiya da tayi shuru tana sauraren maganar mahaifiyartasu da bata yadda da hakan ba dan tasan wayau ne kawai takeyi masu juyawa tayi tana hawaye.

Shikenan Sadeek zan fad'a mashi yanzu dai kayi shuru Abbanku bacci zaiyi kar kutasheshi.

Cikin sauri yakalli k'ofa cike da tsoro yace Ummah nayi shuru na daina magana kice ma Abba kar yadokeni irin wanda yayi min rannan.

K'wallah ce tacika mata ido tunowa da tayi dukan da yata6a yi ma d'an nata lokacin da yana wasa  shikuma yana bacci kan ya farka shine yakamashi da duka, cikin ranta tace lallai ba k'aramin duka yayi mashiba da har sadeek yakasa mancewa, kwantar da yaron tayi saman cinyarta tana shafa kansa nan da nan shima yayi bacci.

Kallon Safiya tayi da take ta hawaye tace ya dai safiya ko yunwa kikeji?

Kamar jira take nan tafara sheshek'ar kuka tace ummah.
Safiya menene kuma abun kuka daga tambaya? Yanzu dai kiyi shuru kinga Abbanku bacci yake kar kitasheshi.

Goge hawayen fuskarta tayi tana me kallon umman tasu.

Sallamar da akayi ne yasa gabad'aya suka waiga tare da amsawa, wata matashiyar budurwace da bazata gaza shekaru goma sha takwas ba tashigo chocolate colour ce tana da kyau sosai da kaganta kaga zubin buzaye doguwa ce bata da jiki sosai da kaganta kaga kamarsu da matar da take zaune, cire hijab d'inta tayi tace ummah sannunku da hutawa.

Batare da ta kalletaba tace yauwa Meerah andawo islamiyyar?
Eh ummah na dawo kinga nima yau har so Malam Munir yayi yakoreni saboda kud'in makaranta saida nabashi hak'uri sannan yabarni yace gobe indai ban kawoba sai ya koreni.

Allah sarki ai insha Allahu zaku biya nima ba jin dad'in rashin biyan kud'in nakeba kinga su Safiya tun jiya aka korosu Allah dai yarufa mana asiri.

Ameen Ummah, juyawa tayi takalli Safiya da tayi shuru tace Safiya ya dai yau bama za'ayi min magana ba?
Murmushi tayi tace sannu da dawowa Aunty Meera.
Hmm yauwa,
Ai Abbanku yake bacci saisa kikaga ta nutsu haka.
 juyawa tayi takalli Ummansu tana shirin yin magana suka had'a ido gabanta saida yafad'i tace Ummah me yasameki? ya naga idanunki sunyi ja haka?
Murmushin k'arfin hali tayi tace bakomai.
Juyawa tayi ga Safiya tace Safiya me yasamu ummah?.
Aunty Meera abbah ne.
Runtse idanunta tayi tana jin wani irin ba dad'i a ranta dan tunda ta ambaci abbah tasan toh tabbas fad'a yayi ma ummansu, muryar ummah taji tana cewa gidanku safiya me abban naku yayi min bance maki k'waro bane yafad"a min a ido ba?
Shuru safiya tayi tana turo baki.
Bud'e idanuwanta tayi da suka cika da k'wallah tace Ummah meyasa Abbah yake miki haka? Na tabbata ba k'aramin 6ata maki rai yayi ba dan kina da hak'uri bakomai bane yake sawa kicanza haka, kokad'an bana son ganin 6acin ranki a duniya banason inga komai ya ta6a min ke.

Haba Meera meyasa kike biye ma zancen safiya nifa ba abinda Abbanku yayi min baccinsa ma yake yanzu kinga tashi kicire kayan nan kid'auko wannan hamsin d'in kisiyo mana garin kwaki dan nasan yunwa kukeji.

Cike da ladabi tace toh ummah sannan tamik'e tanufi wajen kayansu duk dahaka tana jin k'unci acikin ranta.

Tana cire kayan tad'auko wata kod'ad'ar atamfa tasaka sannan tad'auko hijab d'inta tasa tace ummah na tafi.
Adawo lafiya.
Allah yasa ummah, Aunty Meerah inzo muje?
Eh Safiya zo muje.
Tashi tayi tad'auko hijab d'inta cikin jin dad'i tabi yayartata.

Suna fita Maryam zugum tayi tana tunanin halayyar mijinta da kwatakwata takasa gane kansa shidai kullum magana d'aya sai yayi kud'i.

Koda suka dawo ana gab da kiran sallar magrib alwallah sukayi gabad'ayansu sukayi sallah bayan sun gama Meerah bata tashi daga saman sallayarba saida tayi karatun alk'ur'ani sannan taje takwad'a masu garin suka zauna suka ci.

Bayan sallar isha'i shirin bacci sukayi duk suka kwanta ammah Meerah bata runtsa ba tana ta tunanin rayuwarsu haskota ummah tayi da touchlight tace Meerah me kike har wannan lokacin bakiyi bacci ba?

Murmushi tayi tace bakomai Ummah kawai dai baccinne baizoba.

Hmm Meera kenan na fad'a maki kidinga cire damuwa a ranki kucigaba da taya Abbanku da addu'a itace maganin komai.

Toh ummah insha Allahu zamu cigaba da yin addu'ar.
Yauwa kokefa yanzu kisamu kiyi bacci.
Juyawa tayi d'ayan side d'in tare da runtse idanunta.

Ummah ita kanta tunanin takeyi jin motsin d'iyartata ne da yayi yawa yasa tahaskata taga idanunta biyu, aranar kasa rintsawa tayi tana tunanin abinda zata samo ma yaran nata suci da safe dan ko naira d'aya bata da ita, har wajen k'arfe d'ayan dare idanunta biyu nan tatashi tayi nafila sannan taje takwanta.


Washe gari koda suka tashi suna gama sallar asuba zaune tayi saman dardumarta tana tasbihi inda Meerah take karatun alk'ur'ani.

Tana gamawa addu'a tayi tashafa sannan tajuyo takalli ummansu da tayi shuru kamar me tunani cike da damuwa tace ummah.
Juyowa tayi takalleta tana murmushi tace minene Meerah?
Ummah meyasa kikeson zama cikin damuwa.
A'a Meerah ni tasbihi nake kinga tashi kije kikoma bacci tunda kingama tilawar.
nan saman dardumar takwanta tana me kallon mahaifiyartata
Ganin haka yasa Ummah tacigaba da tasbihinta d'inta daga k'arshe takwanta itama.


Chan wajen k'arfe tara Sadeek ne yatashesu yana cewa yunwa yakeji, Ummah hankalinta saida yatashi ganin abinda take tsoro shi yafaru.

Ganin yadda ummansu tashiga damuwa yasa Meerah tamik'e batare da tayi magana ba zuwa tayi tawanke bakinta sannan tad'auko hijab d'inta tafita, kitchen taje tad'auko d'an bokin plastic tafita.


Wajen wata me koko taje da take chan gaban layinsu tana zuwa tatarar da ita tana suyar k'osai ga bokin koko nan a gabanta, duk'awa Meerah tayi cikin girmamawa tagaishe da matar tace babah ina kwana?

Kallonta tayi fuskarta a sake tace lafiya lau Meerah koko zaki siya?

Duk'ar da kanta tayi tace Eh Babah dan Allah kitaimaka kibani bashi insha Allahu zuwa anjima zan kawo maki.

Shuru me kokon tayi sai chan tace kin tabbata 'yar nan zaki kawo min zuwa anjima? Dan gaskiya bana son bada bashi.
Insha Allahu zan kawo maki babah.
toh shikenan kawo wajen na nawa za'a zuba maki?
Na talatin.
Zuba mata tsohuwar tayi tabata nan takar6a tare da yin godiya sannan tatafi.

Tana isa gida Abbansu yana fitowa daga cikin d'akinshi cikin shirin fita cikin sauri Meerah tadurk'asa k'asa cike da ladabi tace Abbah ina kwana?

Wani irin kallo yawurga mata sannan yawuce yafita.

Tana tsugunne k'asan har yafita inda sabo ta saba da hakan dan magana bata had'ata da shi, mik'ewa tayi har zata wuce taga wata takardar nama da yafiddo daga d'akinshi, wajen takardar taje tad'auko tabud'e taga nama ne yaci sai kitse da k'ashi kawai yabari wucewa tayi tanufi d'akinsu tana shiga tasamu Ummanta tana ta lallashin Sadeek akan yayi hak'uri, safiya ma zuru tayi tana zare ido saboda yunwar da takeji.

Bokitin hannunta ta ajiye cikin sauri sadeek yazo wajenta nan tace safiya d'auko maku cup kusha koko, cikin sauri Safiya tamik'e tace toh Aunty.
Ummah ce tace Meerah ina kika samu kud'in da kika siyo koko?

Duk'ar da kanta tayi tace kiyi hak'uri ummah bashi nakar6o wajen babah.
Haba Meerah kinsan dai bana son cin bashi meyasa kika ciyo bayan bamuda kud'in da zamu biya?
Kiyi hak'uri bazan sakeba insha Allahu zamu biyata Allah zai bamu.
Jin tace Allah zai bada yasa tace toh Allah yashige mana gaba, wannan ledar fa ta mecece?
Daman su khalil nakawo mawa kitse ne ciki da k'ashi wanda Abbah yafito da su.
Shuru Ummah tayi batace komai ba dan daman indasabo toh sun saba da hakan.
Kokon suka zuba duk suka sha bayan sun gama Ummah tamik'e taje tayi wanka tana fitowa Meerah tashiga.
Bayan sun shirya ummah tace Meerah yanzu ya kikega za'ayi?
Shuru Meerah tayi dan ta gane abinda mahaifiyartata take nufi chan sai tace Ummah ko wankau zamu kar6o gidan Hajiya luba da tace asamo mata mewanki, kinga sai muyi mata a nan gida a maimakon muje chan muyi idan mun gama sai inje inshanya mata a chan gidanta tabiyamu kud'inmu.

Kina ganin babu matsala Meerah?
Eh Ummah insha Allahu babu.
toh shikenan kije ki amso.
Cikin jin dad'i tace toh ummah nan tamik'e taje tad'auko hijab d'inta tafita, tafiya take a nutse kanta sadde a k'asa har ta isa gidan koda da d'an nisa ammah a k'asa taje, gidane babba ba laifi da kaganshi kasan masu gidan suna da rufin asiri sosai, kasancewar a da tana d'an zuwa ko shara ko wanke wanke yasa megadin be hanata shigaba.

Tana shiga tagaisa da gudar matar da take tsakar gida sannan tashiga d'akin Hajiya Luba bakinta d'auke da sallama.
Hajiya luba da take zaune saman cushion amsa mata tayi tana d'an yatsine fuska, daga k'asa tazauna tagaisheta cike da girmamawa nan ta amsa.
Kanta sadde k'asa tace Hajiya daman Ummah ce tace ince kibada wankin naki zanikai maki.
Ita wadda zatayi wankin bazatazo nan tayi min ba salon inbada kayana ayi min k'azanta? Nifa ba kowa nakeson ba ma wankiba dan ma mewankina baya nan, salan aje ayi min yak'i fita.
Kiyi hak'uri hajiya zaya fita insha Allahu dan matar ta iya wanki sosai sannan nan gidan za"a zo ashanya maki idan angama.
Toh shikenan indai basu fitaba bazan biya ko naira ba.
Cikin jin dad'i Meerah tace toh hajiya mun amince.
Tashi muje kid'auka.
Tashi tayi suka shiga bedroom d'in hajiyar tabud'e wardrobe d'in nan tasa Meerah tad'auko marassa wanki tahad'a waje guda kaya sunkai kala goma sha biyar, sabulun wanki ukku tabata Meerah tace sunyi yawa,
Wane yawa sukayi indai ba so kuke suk'i fitaba.
Shuru tayi batare da tayi magana ba.
naira d'ari biyu tabata tace d'ari asai ruwan wanki kar asaki ayi mata da na rijiya sannan d'ari kuma tahau abun hawa saboda wankin suna da yawa.
Godiya Meerah tayi mata nan Hajiya Luba tak'ara jaddada mata tace ma mewanki kar fa kayanta suk'i fita.
Da toh tabita sannan taficce da kayan.
A k'ofan gate d'in gidan tatsaya tasamu me adaidaita tahau yakaita har k'ofar gidansu tabiya naira arba'in.

Tana shiga nan tafayyace ma ummanta duk yadda sukayi da hajiya luba itama ta ji dad'i sosai nan tasa Safiya tasamo masu meruwa tasiya jarka ukku, ita da Meerah suka zauna suka fara jibgar wannan wankin koda suka kusan gamawa Ummah tayi tayi da meerah tabarshi ita ta ida ammah tak'i saida suka gama tare.

Suna cikin d'auraye Abbansu Meerah yadawo gidan hannunsa rik'e da leda bakin k'ofa yatsaya yana kallonsu fuskarsa babu alamun walwala nan Sadeek da Safiya da suke wasa duk suka nutsu.
Meerah ce da mahaifiyarta sukayi mashi sannu da zuwa banza yayi masu yanufi d'akinshi yana shiga yak'walah ma ummansu kira, amsawa tayi tatsame hannunta tad'auraye sannan tawuce tanufi d'akinshi.

Tana shiga cike da ladabi tazauna tace gani megida,
Ledar da yashigo da ita yamik'a mata yace ga wannan shinkafa ce gwangwani biyu ga kuma kayan miya nan da nama kije kiyi min shinkafa da miya idan kin gama kiraba abinci kashi ukku kici kashi d'aya kiban kashi biyu.

Kallonsa kawai take ganin yadda yake maganar cikin rashin damuwa, girgiza kai tayi tace megida yaran fa?
Harararta yayi yace ina ruwana wannan ai basu dameniba nakine sai kisan yadda zakiyi da su danni banda kud'in da zan ciyar maki da su ai tun farko daman na fad'a maki, nidai kije kiyi min inkingama kikawo min ki ajiye sannan kikula kar kibari suyi min k'azanta danni wad'annan k'azaman yaran naki banaso suna gittawa inkina girki, yanzu bari infita zuwa anjima zan dawo ana neman mu ne akan screening d'in da za ayi mana nad'aukar ma'aikata.

In dasabo ta saba da halayyarsa dan kusan kullum daman indai zaice tayi mashi girki toh haka yakeyi mata 
cikin sanyin jiki tace toh shikenan megida Allah yataimaka yabada sa'a.
Ameen,
Toh saidai baka bada kud'in icce ba.
Kai kedai kinga matsalarki bakida aiki da nace kiyi min girki sai kince kud'i mtsww "yaja tsaki".
Allah yahuci zuciyarka toh ai ba iccen ne.
Naira d'ari yaciro yamik'a mata yace gashinan in kin siyo sai ki ajiye min canjina.
toh insha Allahu.
Na dai fad'a maki kikiyaye kar k'azaman yaranchan suje min wajen abinci.
Shuru tayi nan tad'auki kayan tafita takardunsa yad'auko shima yaficce fuskarnan a d'aure k'yam dan indai zaiga 'ya'yannan duk dariyar da yake zaibi yad'aure fuska.

Yana fita Meerah kallon mahaifiyartasu tayi cike da damuwa dan duk ta jiyo maganganun da Abban nasu yayi, ajiye ledojin da yabata tayi tazauna tatisasu a gaba tana kallo.
Duk'awa tayi tana me cigaba da d'aurayar wankin da sukayi har tagama still dai ummanta na zaune.
Matsowa tayi kusa da ita tare da tsugunnawa k'asa tace ummah.
'Dagowa tayi takalleta tana murmushi tace Meerah har kin gama d'aurayar ko?
Eh ummah dan Allah kidaina sa damuwa aranki akan abinda Abbah yakeyi kar fa kimance ubangiji shi yake taimakonmu.

toh kawo ingyara maki kayan miyan.

adawo lafiya meerah da ido tabi d'iyartata cike da tausayi dan tasan yunwa takeji kawai dai tana daurewa ne.
tana fita tasamu me adaidaita tasaka bokitan kayan yakaita har k'ofar gidan, ma'aikatan gidanne suka taimaka mata suka shigar mata da kayan nan tashanya sannan taje tasamu Hajiya luba zaune tana cin naman kaji shi kuma abincin gashinan a gefe an ajiye alamun anci an k'oshi, duk'awa tayi cikin ladabi tace hajiya anwanko gasu chan na shanya miki.

*PAGE 2*

Tana komawa tatarar da Ummanta tana girkin inda su Safiya da Sadeek suke zaune sunyi zuru zuru, cire hijab d'inta tayi tana cewa Sadeek ba dai yunwaba?
Sheshekar kuka yafara yace Aunty Meerah yunwa nikeji.
Toh in yunwa kukeji ai hak'uri zakuyi bakuga na kusa gamawaba da na gama zan zuba maku kuci,,,cewar ummansu.
Ledar da take hannunta tamik'a ma Safiya tace jeki kijuye maku wannan a plate sai kuci.
Cikin jin dad'i safiya ta amsa tace menene a ciki Aunty meerah?
Kije kijuye sai kigani.
Ummah rufe tukunyar miyar tayi tajuyo takalleta tace Meerah menene kika samo masu?
Murmushi tayi tace abinci ne?
Abinci kuma? Siyowa kikayi?
Zama tayi sannan takwashe duk yadda akayi tafad'a ma ummansu.
Jinjina kai ummah tayi tace toh shikenan Allah yasa mudace.
Ameen ummah kud'in fa yaza'ayi da su?
Kicire maku d'ari ukku kud'in wata sauran kuma sai ki ajiye munga abinda za'ayi.

fara'ar da take fuskar Meerah ce taragu tace kai ummah wallahi banso kikajeba matar da kwatakwata bata zuwa gidan nan sannan bata ganin mutuncinki bare na 'ya'yanki.
Meerah sai naje ai 'yar uwatace koda ace tana yankan naman jikina bazan ta6a barintaba dan banaso zumuncin mu yawatse.
zumunci kuma? tun yaushe ummah su fa basu taka inda kike saidai ke kike zuwa.
toh ya zanyi wannan kuma matsalarsu ce nidai Allah yana gani inayi domin shi, kar kusaki kudinga duba da yanayin mutum meerah ku dai duk abinda zakuyi kudinga yi saboda Allah.
Tausayin ummanta duk yakamata dan tasan ita tana son 'yan uwan nata biyu sosai, "tace toh ummah insha Allahu muma zamuyi koyi da ke"
Yauwa Allah yayi maku albarka, yanzu zo kije kisiyo mana rogo da k'uli muk'wad'a muci ko ma kiba safiya kawai tasiyo.
Ummah bari kawai inje kinsan safiya da ta fita sai ta tsaya wasa.
Hakane kuma toh yi sauri kinga magrib ta gabato.

Su Meerah sosai suke samun sauk'in Abbansu akan aikin da yafara zuwa dan sai yayi kusan sati baisa su a idoba saboda idan yafita tun safe sai dare yake dawowa, abun har mamaki yakeba Ummansu ganin yadda yadage da aiki ba dare da rana idan yadawo tayi mashi magana akan wai basuda hutu sai yace toh wanda yakeson yayi Arzik'i taya ba zai maida hankali ba akan arzik'in, ita dai saidai tace Allah yataimaka.

Haka tacigaba da fad'awa chan tamurgina chan duk wani wanki da surfe na cikin anguwa tana kar6a tayi ma mutane wani lokacin ma kamar abincin suna ko bikki tana kar6a tayi duk dan abiyata susamu na saka ma bakunan su.

Ranar da akayi ma Mahaifin su Meerah albashi ledoji yayo na nama da na lemukan kwali d'akinshi yakai ya ajiye sannan yakwala ma ummansu kira tana zuwa yabata 'yar k'aramar nokia sabuwa gal yace ga alk'awalin da yayi mata, sosai tayi murna tare da yin godiya, kashedi yayi mata akan indai ta ajiye yaranta suka 6ata toh babu ruwanshi shidai yayi iya abinda zaiyi mata.
Godiya tayi mashi sosai, cikin ledojin da yashigo da su yamik'a mata wata yace ga wannan rogo ne nasiyo maki kema kici.
Kar6a tayi cikin jin dad'i tayi mashi godiya sannan tafita.

Su Meerah sosai suka nuna farin ciki akan wayar da aka siyo ma ummansu nan suka jona mata charge sannan tace suzo suci rogon da yasiyo mata, safiya da sadeek ne gaba wajen tafiya cin rogon inda Meerah tayi zaune tana kallonsu.

Kud'ine suka fara zauna ma Ahmad lokaci guda daman abunka da babban ma'aikacin banki, yana kwasar albashinsa na ukku yasiya mota nan sukayi ta masa Allah yasa alkhairi hatta su Meerah saida ummansu ta tirsasa masu sukayi mashi saidai bai kulasu ba.

*PAGE 5*

Wajen k'arfe tara da rabi abbansu yadawo gidan Maryam tana so tayi bacci ammah tak'i dan duk a tunaninta zai kirata yahad'asu da amarya saidai me har wajen k'arfe sha d'ayan dare shuru dan haka tahak'ura takwanta dansu Meerah tuni sunyi bacci, cikin ranta addu'a tashiga yi akan Allah yaba mijin nasu ikon yin adalci sannan yabata ikon juriya da hak'uri akan koma menene.


Da asuba koda tatashi sallah basu tashiba taso tatashesu ammah tabarsu da tayi tunanin maybe sun tashi kafin tatashi dan haka tatada meerah da safiya sukayi sallah.


Abbansu meerah ko angwancewarsa yayi da bazawarar matarsa sunci gasassun kaji har suka rage, da asuba har sukaso sumakara a sallah dan sai da gari yafara haske sannan yafarka yatasheta.

Ummah tana saman sallaya tana azkhar taji fitowarsu sukayi wanka da alwallah suka koma.
Da gari ya ida wayewa tana so tayi masu karin kumalo ammah rashin kayan yi yahana dan kwatakwata babu abinda zata dafa masu suci daga k'arshe tashare dan tasan zai iya yo masu takeaway kamar yadda yasaba yin abunshi dasafe.

Wajen k'arfe takwas tatada meerah taje tayi brush tasa hijab tasiyo masu koko da k'osai lokacin da tadawo su sadeek sun farka har sunyi brush ita suke jira takawo suyi kalaci.

Har sukayi breakfast su abbansu basu fitoba dan haka meerah da ummanta suka gyara gidan tas inda Safiya tayi wanke-wanke bayan sun gama sukayi wanka.

Wajen k'arfe sha d'aya Ahmad da amaryarsa suka fito sunci gayu sunyi gwanin kyau tana rik'e da jakkarsa ta office inda shi kuma yasha shaddarsa sai k'abar k'abar yake yana gyara hula magana suke ahankali suna dariya, su ummah suna jiyosu nan tamik'e tafito dan sugaisa tana fitowa nan fara'ar fuskar abbah taragu, fuskarta d'auke da murmushi tace megida sannunku da fito, "sannunki kema", juyawa tayi ga amaryar da take ta cin magani ta zuba kitson attachment a baya fuskarnan duk tasha bilicin tace Amarya barka da safiya dafatan kun tashi lafiya.
"Lafiya"
"Masha Allah" juyawa tayi ga mijin tace megida kuyi hak'uri na barku da yunwa bansan me kukeso a had'a makuba na breakfast saisa banyi karanbaniba.
Ba damuwa ai mun ma yi breakfast da ace ina buk'atar taimakonki ai da zan nemeki tun wuri.
Har cikin ranta bata ji dad'in maganarshi ba ammah tashare tace toh shikenan, fitowar Meerah da Safiya ne yasa Amaryar tajuya takallesu a wulak'ance tsugunnawa sukayi cikin ladabi suka gaisheta, yamitsa fuska tayi sannan tad'aga masu kai alamun ta amsa sannan tamaida kallonta ga megidan tace Dear wad'annan sune 'ya'yan shegun da matarka ta haifa?
Meerah da Safiya lokaci guda suka mik'e tsaye, Ummah ko cikin sauri takalli megidan tace shegu kuma?
Cikin rashin damuwa yace eh man ko ba haka bane?
Ammah ai kasan shegu sune wad'anda aka haifa ba ta hanyar aureba marassa uba.
Toh menene banbancinsu da wad'annan tunda ba uban garesuba.
A fusace tace kaga megida ya isa haka watau ci min fuska zakayi gaban matarka miye na alak'anta min 'ya'ya da na shegu bayan da ubansu kai wallahi wannan ya fi k'arfin cin fuska me kake nufi da maganarka?
maryam kema kinsan 'ya'yannan ba nawa bane sai kije kisamo masu ubansu danni kwatakwata ban da alak'a da su.
Wasu irin zazzafan hawaye ne suka gangaro mata tace Ahmad kana cikin hayyacinka kuwa? Daman k'iyayyar da kakeyi ma 'ya'yana ta kai haka?
Eh man ta ma wuce haka dan wallahi ko kad'an ban ta6a sonsu ba ni da zaki taimaka kifitar min da su a cikin gida da zan fi jin dad'i.
Meerah da take tsaye hawaye sun wanke mata fuska saboda jin zafin maganganun da mahaifinsu yake jifarsu da su juyawa tayi dagudu tashige d'akinsu tafad'a saman katifarsu wani irin kuka take me shiga ran me saurare tana jin maganganun suna mata yawo a kunne wai sune shegu.

Amarya ce tace kin dai ji abinda yace dan haka ina jan hankalinku ke da 'ya'yanki kar kusaki kushiga gonata kutsaya iya matsayinku.

Jinjina kai ummah tayi cike da takaici tace hakan da kayi daidai ne Ahmad a yau na ida tabbatarwa da babu son 'ya'yana ko k'wayar zarra a cikin zuciyarka, juyawa tayi takalli Hannatu tace kekuma indai kina ganin abun burgewane hakan toh kicigaba da dariya akwai ranar kuka dan in gemon d'an uwanka ya kama da wuta toh kashafa ma naka ruwa.
"Ke bana son shashanci kar kik'ara saka min mata a cikin abunki in ni kinmin kin zauna lafiya toh ita kika ta6ata zan iya d'aukar tsatsauran mataki akanki ko kin d'auka tsoronki nake saisa nake kyaleki".

"Ai dear kabarni da ita nima daidai da kowa ce indai tace zatayi min toh bazata ta6a samun zaman lafiya ba, muje inrakaka katafi office kar kayi late".
Jinjina kai yayi yace maryam kikiyaye kar inji kar ingani kinma matata wani rashin kunya dan idan kikayi komai zai iya faruwa.
Juyawa ummah tayi tana share hawayenta tawuce takoma d'akinta batare da ta ce komai ba.
Tana jin hannatu tana ta rera wak'ar "ahayye wata ta fi wata ahayye ko ni na fi"

Tana shiga zaune tasamu safiya da Sadeek suna ta kuka inda Meerah take kwance tana rera nata kukan.
Ummah zama tayi kusa da su Sadeek tace autana wa yata6a min kai?
Jikinta tajanyoshi tace kadaina kuka ka ji d'an ummah.
"Ummah ba abbah bane yake maki fad'a"
yi shuru ka ji ba fad'a yayi min ba.
Kallon safiya tayi tace safiya kukan ya isa haka tashi kije kid'auko naira ashirin kuje kusiyo biscuit kuci.
Goge hawayen tayi tace toh ummah nan tamik'e taje tad'auko kud'in, Tashi Sadeek yayi yace ummah nima zan bita.
Toh kuje tare kar kudad'e da kun siyo kudawo.
Toh ummah.

Suna fita juyowa tayi takalli Meerah tace kema Meerah kukan kikeyi kina ji ina lallashinsu.
'Dagowa tayi daga k'wancen da take tana me cigaba da kuka tace ummah dole inyi kuka ko na samu sauk'in abinda nakeji a raina kokad'an banji zafin maganganun abbah ba agaremu sama da tozarci da cin mutuncin da yayi maki a gaban matarshi.
Toh menene a ciki Meerah? Daman ita rayuwar haka take inma ba hakaba wane dare ne jemage bai ganiba babu wani sabon wulak'anci acikin wanda yakeyi min duk da haka kucigaba da tayashi addu'a.
Toh ummah insha Allahu.


Wajen k'arfe sha d'aya Meerah tafito tajanyo murhu zata had'a wuta tana cikin had'a itace hannatu tafito hannunta rik'e da waya tana taku d'aid'aya ja tayi tatsaya tace ke menene haka zakiyi?
Shuru Meerah tayi kamar bada ita take magana ba cikin tsawa tace ke bakiji ina miki magana zaki min banza.
Batare da ta juyoba tace kina gani fa wuta nakeson had'awa inyi girki.
Eh lallai dole kice min haka tabbas dear ya yi gaskiya bakuda tarbiya watau ni kike ma magana a tsaitsaye haka, toh bari kiji ba ke ba hatta mahaifiyarki kar insake ganin wani ya kunna min murhu a cikin gida indai yi zakuyi saidai kuje chan zaure kukunna dan haka kitattara kikai zaure sai kiyi.

Juyowa Meerah tayi takalleta tace akan me zamuje zaure bayan ke kikazo kika samemu a gidanmu.

Ke kar kikuskura kice zakiyi min rashin kunya a cikin gidana dan daga ku har mahaifiyarku d'osane kuke a gidan a kowane lokaci zaku iya barinshi.

Meerah babu abinda tatsana sama da atozarta mata mahaifiya a harzuk'e tace k'aryane wallahi,,,ummah ce tafito tadaka mata tsawa tace me kike haka Meerah? 

Juyowa Meerah tayi takalleta k'wallah duk ta cika mata ido tace ummah kina ji fa itace...... "itace me? Kinsan bana son rashin kunya ko?

Kibarta tazageni tukun sai hankalinta yakwanta ai banso kika fitoba da kin barni da ita inga iya gudun ruwanta da wallahi yau bazaku k'ara kwana d'aya cikin gidannan ba.

Hanya ummah tanuna ma Meerah alamun tabar wajen, cikin sauri tawuce tashige d'aki.

Tsaki Hannatu taja tace aikin banza harara cikin ido watau ni za'a ma rashin kunya a cikin gidan miji.

Hannatu kinga fa yarinya ce bai dace kidinga biyetaba kuma fa duka jiya kikazo gidan ammah har kin fara tada mana hankali.
Eh d'in antada ina ce gidan mijina ne dole inyi tak'ama da hakan tunda duk d'osane kuke ke da d'iyanki dan haka kigargad'i yarinyar nan tayi hankali da ni inba hakaba zan fito mata da ainahin halina.

Koma menene dai kiyi hak'uri dan ni 'ya'yana suna da tarbiya bazasu iya fad'a da nasama da su ba.

Eh sannu masu tarbiya, nidai na fad'a kar wanda yak'ara kunna murhu acikin gidannan indai anason zaman lafiya,,,juyawa tayi tawuce d'akinta tana ta fad'a.

Girgiza kai ummah tayi tace Allah yakyauta nan tad'auki murhun tamaida zaure tahad'a wutar saida tad'aura girkin sannan takoma d'aki tasamu Meerah kwance kanta yana kallon Ceiling kallonta Ummah tayi tace Yanzu saboda Allah Meerah abinda kikayi kin kyauta?
Tashi tayi zaune ahankali tace kiyi hak'uri ummah wallahi itace tazo tasameni ban mata komai ba tahauni da fad'a.
"Shine kekuma kika biye mata ko?"
Ummah ban biye mataba, kawai gani nayi daga zuwanta ta fara nema tasanya mana doka bayan ita tazo tasamemu.
Toh tsaya banason wannan shashancin kar inkuma gani, ita matsayin uwa take a wajenki dan haka kidaina biye mata duk abinda zatayi maki.
ammah ummah kina gani fa itace take ta faman ci mana mutunci.
Nidai na fad'a maki kar kikuma.
Toh ummah kiyi hak'uri.

Wucewa tayi tad'auko 'yar taliyar kad'insu tafita da ita, Meerah komawa tayi takwanta cike da takaicin abinda akayi masu.

Har ummah tagama girkin tana nan kwance bata motsaba saida aka kira sallar azuhur sannan tatashi taje tayi alwallah tai sallah.


Wajen k'arfe biyu Ahmad yadawo da ledoji yashige d'akin amarya nan yabaje masu takeaway da gasassun kajin da yasiyo masu suka zauna suka ci suka k'oshi, bayan sun gama ne Hannatu tace megida daman haka wad'annan 'ya'yan suke basuda kunya?
Kallonta yayi yace wane 'ya'ya kuma?
Yoh wane fa bayan wad'annan na nan gidan.
"Wani abu sukayi maki?"
Hmm kaidai bari har da zagi yau cin mutunci kala kala mahaifiyarsu tasa sukayi min kan kawai nace sutaimaka sukoma girki a zaure saboda murhu ne kar yakashe mana tiles d'in gida ga kuma hayak'i zai dinga damunmu.

Tun kan takai aya tuni ya mik'e a fusace yaficce, Ummah tana zaune tana nazarin wani littafin addu'o'i sai ji tayi yana k'wala mata kira, amsawa tayi tamik'e cikin sauri tafita a bakin k'ofa tasameshi tsaye yana hucci, a nutse take kallonsa tace megida lafiya?
Cikin fad'a yace ubanwa yasa kika tura yaranki sukaima amaryata rashin kunya?
Da mamaki take kallonsa tace natura yarana kuma? Akan me zansa suje suyi mata rashin kunya, kawai dai itace tazo tasamu Meerah tahauta da fad'a.
Hannatu da take tsaye k'ofar d'akinta tace au k'arya zanyi miki? Bakisa su ba kike nufi?
Kallonta Ummah tayi tace toh daman in ba k'aryarba me kikayi?
A fusace Ahmad yace keee kar kikuskura kice zaki d'aga ma matata murya a cikin gidannan dan kokad'an ba matsayinku d'ayaba akanta zan iya korarki da 'ya'yanki.
Cike da mamaki tace Ahmad kana cikin hayyacinka kuwa kasan abinda kake cewa? Ni zaka kora?
"Sosai ma dan bakida wani matsayi a wajena kawai dai ina zaune da ke ne saboda darajar iyayenki"
"Karabu da ni dan Allah kaga idan ba zan ci gaba da rayuwa ba, kadaina ganin darajar iyayena tunda yanzu basu a raye."
Na dai fad'a maki kidai kiyaye inba hakaba komai zai iya faruwa,,,yana fad'in haka yajuya suka koma d'aki shi da hannatu saida tafakaici idanunshi tayi ma Maryam gwalo sannan tashige.

Tsaye Maryam tayi har suka shige sannan itama tajuya tashiga d'akinta kallon Meerah tayi da take zaune tana hawaye tace nasan dai kinji abinda yafaru kinga daman abinda nake guje mana dan nasan halin abban nan naku.
Goge hawayen tayi tace ummah dan Allah kiyi hak'uri nasan ni najamaki.
"A'a Meerah bake kika ja min ba daman chan da shirin wulak'ancin shi nidai fatana kidaina biye mata duk abinda zatace"
Insha Allahu ummah na daina dan banaso inga saboda mu abbah yana maki haka.
Kar kidamu Meerah komai lokacine akwai lokacin da ko ance yayi bazai yi ba, addu'a ita ce maganin komai"
Hakane ummah Allah yanuna mana lokacin.
Ameen, yanzu tashi kije kikira su Safiya gidansu kuluwa tun d'azu daga zuwa kitso shikenan sun tsaya wasa ita da Sadeek ko abinci basu ci ba.
Toh ummah, nan tamik'e tad'auko hijab d'inta tasaka tafita.

Tafiya take a nutse har tabar layinsu kanta na sunkuye a k'asa sai ganin mutum tayi tsaye a gabanta, ja tayi tatsaya tare da d'agowa takalleshi tana shirin yin magana sai ganin mutumin rannan tayi, d'aure fuska tayi tace malam lafiya zaka tare min hanya?
Murmushi yayi tare da shafa lallausan siririn sajen da yake a fuskarsa sannan yace baby keda zaki tayani murna na ganki shine zaki d'aure fuska? Kinsan wahalar nemanki da nasha tun ranar da nafara ganinki?
Harara tawurga mashi tace kaga malam kaban hanya inwuce.
Na ji zan baki ammah sai kin bari mun d'an gaisa kin min k'watancen gidanku.
Bazanyi ba dan Allah kakyaleni dan banda lokacinka.
Haba Baby meyasa zakiyi min haka nifa sonki nake, da zaki amince da munje na kaiki inda zakije.
"Bana buk'atar hakan nan taratsa ta gefenshi zata wuce kawai sai ji tayi ya rik'o mata hannu.
juyowa tayi cikin sauri a fusace tafizge hannunta tace wannan wane irin iskancine zaka ta6ani.
Afuwan baby wallahi nima bansan na rik'oki ba dan Allah kitaimaka ma zuciyata.
Nuna shi tayi da yatsa tace sannan yazama na farko kuma na k'arshe da hannunka zai ta6a jikina sannan ni ba kalarka bace kaje kasamu mace irinka kuyi soyayya ina ga hakan zai fi dacewa,,,tana fad'in haka tajuya tacigaba da tafiyarta.
Komawa yayi yajingina jikin motarsa yana kallonta fuskarsa d'auke da murmushi har ta6ulle sannan yabud'e motar yashiga.

Meerah saboda bak'in cikin rik'e hannunta da yayi a ranar bata wuni cikin walwala ba ko ummah ta tambayeta me yake faruwa taganta haka saidai tace bakomai.


Washe gari tun dasafe sukayi wanka sukayi shirin makaranta cikin sauri Meerah tashare gida bayan sunyi kalaci sukayi ma ummah sallama har sun fito zasu tafi makaranta sai ga hannatu ta fito sanye da rigar bacci, Safiya da Sadeek ne suka gaisheta saida tayamitsa fuska sannan tace ashe uwarku ta koya maku gaisuwa, jin abinda tace yasa Meerah ita tak'i gaisheta daman ummah ce tace sudinga gashaita, Wani irin kallo Hannatu tayi mata tace ke uwarki bata koya maki gaisuwa ba?
Cikin sauri Meerah tad'ago takalleta cike da takaici dan arayuwarta babu abinda tatsana sama da awulak'anta mata uwa har ta bud'e baki zatayi magana sai kuma tatuno kashedin da ummanta tayi mata gudun kar ta6ata mata rai yasa tayi shuru nan tawuce zata tafi.
Hannatu tace ke badake nake magana ba watau kin raina ni ko?
Abbah ne yafito daga d'akin yace wace 'yar bura'uba ce kike ma magana takyaleki?
Jin muryarsa yasa Meerah saurin tsayawa tace banji ki ba ne.
Eh ai dayake kinga megidan da ni kin wuce zaki ficce kibar gidan, kaganta dear daga ce mata Meerah babu gaisuwa shine take wurgo min harara taja tsaki tawuce zata fita.
Kallon Meerah yayi yace dan iskanci tana maki magana shine zakiyi mata tsaki, ita tsarar wasanki ce?
Duk'ar da kanta tayi cikin ladabi tace kayi hak'uri Abbah ni ba tsaki nayi mataba wallahi.
Au k'arya zatayi maki ko?
Girgiza kai tayi.
toh bari kiji wannan da kike gani ta fi uwarki mutunci da daraja a wajena.
'Dago kai tayi takalli Hannatu, jin jina kai Hannatu tayi tana murmushin jin dad'i,,,cigaba yayi da cewa indai har kunason cigaba da zama a gidannan dole kuyi mata biyayya, ko bakiji me naceba!
Muryarta tana rawa kamar zatayi kuka tace toh abbah kayi hak'uri insha Allahu zan kiyaye.
Kibata hak'uri.
Wani irin k'ululun bak'in cikine yatsaya mata ammah tadanne dakyar tabud'e baki tace kiyi hak'uri.
Kaga fa megida a tsaye take ban hak'uri.
Cikin tsawa yace bakida tarbiya da zaki tsaya a kanta kibata hak'uri bazaki rissina ba?
Duk'awa tayi k'asa tace Aunty kiyi hak'uri.
Ta6e baki tayi tace tashi kitafi, tun kan tarufe baki Meerah tamik'e tana jinta tana ce ma abbansu ammah yarannan basuda tarbiya yace toh ya aka iya tunda uwarsu bata basu ba.

Meerah kamar tafashe da kuka saboda takaici ammah ba damar hakan saidai tadaure ahaka taje makaranta har aka tashi batada wata walwala.
Ita kanta Ummah tana jiyosu lokacin kawai sharewa tayi tana mamakin makircin hannatu tace Allah yakawo mana sauk'in wannan lamarin.

Da marece bayan sun dawo daga Islamiyya Meerah tana zaune d'akinsu tana bitar karatunta nan taji hannatu tana k'wala mata kira har ta share kamar bata ji ba saida ummah tace bakiji ana kiranki, sannan tamik'e har zata fita ummah tace dan Allah Meerah kar kisaki kibiye mata dan banason tashin hankali.
Murmushi tayi tace toh ummah sannan tawuce tafita.
tsaye tasameta a k'ofar d'akinta tana zuwa taja tatsaya tace gani, naira d'ari biyar tawullo mata tace d'auka kije kisiyo min katin waya MTN yanzu kikawo min.
kamar kar tad'auka ammah tunowa da gargad'in da ummah tayi mata yasa tad'auka batare da tayi maganaba taficce.
Tana siyowa tadawo a bakin k'ofar d'akinta tatsaya tace gashi.
daga chan ciki hannatu tace nan zan zo in amsa bazaki kawo min ciki ba?
'Dage labulen tayi tashiga tasameta zaune saman kujera tana ta dannar waya batare da ta juyo ta kalletaba tamik'a mata hannu alamun tabata katin.
Mik'a mata Meerah tayi sannan tajuya tafita takoma d'akinsu, Ummah tace kin dawo ko?
Kamar zatayi kuka tace wai kati ta aikeni nasiyo mata duk ga yara ammah sai ni zata aika.
toh ya kika iya kidinga hak'uri kina binta a yadda takeso kinga in kina haka babu yadda zatayi tace kinyi mata wani abu har tasamu damar had'aki da mahaifinki yak'ara tsanarki.
Goge hawayen da yaziraro mata tayi tace wallahi ummah bazan ta6a damuwa ba dan tahad'a ni da shi tunda daman babu sonmu ko kad'an a cikin zuciyarshi kawai dai ina mata biyayya ne saboda ke dan banason abinda zai 6ata maki rai.
"A'a Meerah kidaina cewa haka mahaifinku yana sonku yanzu tashi kije kiyi alwallah yanzu ankira sallah.
Mik'ewa tayi tacire hijab d'inta sannan taje tad'auro alwallah.

*PAGE 6*

Maryam! Maryam!!,
 na'am gani nan tashi tayi tafita tasameshi tsaye a k'otar d'akinta kallonshi tayi tace lafiya megida? 
Lafiyar kenan, dama ince maki kice ma wannan d'iyar taki tazo tataya Hannatu girki.

6ata fuska ummah tayi tace ban fahimci abinda kake nufiba?
Toh kar Allah yasa kifahimta tunda dai kinji abinda nace ai shikenan.
Megida hakan bazai ta6a yuwuwa ba akan me zata tayata bayan kai kake aurenta sannan ka mance da ka ce bakaine mahaifinta ba? 
Kallon hadarin kaji yayi mata yace dakata ni dai umurni nabaki in tatayata ai taimakonki za'ayi kema kid'an ci,
 kurik'e abincinku bani buk'ata ammah d'iyata bazata koma 'yar aikin wataba taji ma da karatunta dan sati me zuwa zata fara jarabawa.
Ke! koma dai menene indai anason zaman lafiya a gidannan dole tafito tatayata inko ba hakaba toh wallahi saidai tabar gidannan dan ba yarda za'ayi ace suna zaune a gidana suna cin moriyata.
Moriya kuma? Me kata6a tsinana masu a gidan ka ta6a siyan ko kwalli kace gashi sushafa?
Ke!!! Banison zancen banza nidai umurni nabaki inba hakaba wallahi zan d'au tsatstsauran mataki akanki, ko anfad'a maki nima dan inaso ne zata taya matata.
"Hmm Allah dai yakyauta"  juyawa tayi tawuce d'akinta tasamu Meerah da take zaune tana karatu tace Meerah kinji abinda abbanku yace ko?
Murmushi tayi tace bakomai ummah bari inje intayata ai na gama karatun.
Toh shikenan Allah yayi maki albarka.
Ameen ummana.

Tana fita tasamu Hannatu a kitchen ta ajiye ledar kayan miya tana zuwa batare da tayi magana ba taja tatsaya, harara hannatu tawurga mata tace ke baki iya magana?
Shuru Meerah tayi, ledar kayan miyan tanuna mata tace kid'auka kigyara min.
Bata musaba tad'auki ledar tafito tsakar gida tazauna tagyara tana gamawa tawanke tace gashi.
Kallon kayan miyan tayi sannan tace ki min blending d'insu.
Haushi duk ya cika Meerah ahaka tadaure tayi a cikin blender d'in da taga ta jona jikin socket tana gamawa tace gashinan.
Saida tayamitsa fuska sannan tace toh ga nama nan kid'auka kiwanke kitanana min.
Naman da tagani dayawa tad'auka tana ta mamakin wannan jibgin naman da abbansu yasiyo cikin ranta tace Allah dai yasa har da mu za'ayi girkin, tana gama wankewa takunna risho tad'aura batare da ta juyo ta kalletaba tace maggi fa?
Gashi nan sai kid'iba kizuba.
Guda ukku da ajino tad'auko tasaka tayanka albasa.
Hannatu da take tsaye k'ofan kitchen tace toh gashinan sai kid'aura shinkafar fara da miya zanyi sannan ina gargad'inki da kayana kar kikuskura kid'aukar min komai dan idan naduba naga babu wani abu nawa toh sai kin gane kurenki.ta6e baki Meerah tayi tace toh ai mu a gidannan bamuda 6arawo dan haka duk abinda kikaga babu toh daman chan bakida shi kokuma cikin bak'in da kikeyi ne wani yasace.

Meerah ce tace kar insake jin haka safiya in munbar nan ina zamuje sannan anfad'a maki aure abun wasa ne? Taya ummah zata rabu da shi alhali da aurensa a saman kanta.
turo baki safiya tayi taje takwanta saman katifa.
Meerah kallon ummah tayi tace kiyi hak'uri ummah kinsan safiya ta fiya hauka, yanzu bari inje gidan Babah talatu ta bakin titi d'azu tace min inzo inkar6i k'uriga idan tagama dafawa.
kai Meeerah kedai kina son k'uriga toh shikenan sai kin dawo ammah inbata bakiba kar kisaki kirok'eta.
Allah ummah ba zan rok'etaba kinsan ba halina bane.
nasani meerah nima na fad'a ne kawai.
yaauwa ummana bari inyi sauri indawo.
toh shikenan sai kin dawo.
Hijab ta zumbula taficce daga gidan.
tana fita ummah takwanta saman katifar cike da damuwa dan dama tana 6oye damuwarta ne kawai saboda gudun kar Meerah tagane hankalinta yatashi.
koda Meerah tadawo k'uriga ce dayawa matar tabata dan tana shiri da Meerah sosai saida sukayi sallar magrib sannan suka zauna suka fara ci...

Saida tayo alwallah sannan tatashi Meerah da taketa juyi akan katifar tasu da kagani kasan ba dan tana jin dad'in baccin ba dakyar tasamu tamik'e jikinta duk yana mata ciwo saboda dukan da abbansu yayi mata.

{On Ward Together}



*PAGE 8*

Fitowa ummah tayi da wankinsu zatayi nan ta ajiye tad'auko robobi zata zuba ruwa sai ganin hannatu tayi ta zo ta d'auke robobin, tsaye ummah tayi tana kallonta tazuba ruwa tad'auka takai gefen d'akinta ta ajiye, tace haba hannatu bakiga wanki zanyiba zaki d'auke robobin?
Harara hannatu tawurga mata tace tunda ni ance miki ba wankin zanyi ba?
"Ammah ai kina gani ni nafara d'aukowa zanyi".
Toh sai me dan kin fara d'aukowa ina cewa robobin ba uban mutum bane yasiyo na mijina ne.

Ran ummah ba k'aramin 6aci yayi ba nunata da tsaya tayi tace kul kar kikuskura kizagar min iyaye dan zaki iya yin komai inshanye ammah banda cin zarafin iyayena.

Ta6a hannu tayi tace ahayye ansamu matsayi har ana d'aga min murya lallai maryam watau ni da gidan mijina shine kikeson min iko da shi ko?
Toh ai yadda yake mijinki nima mijine agareni dan haka babu abinda zaki nuna min.
Dariya Hannatu tayi tace sannunki malama watau har wani matsayi kike ganinki da shi a nan ko?
Banza ummah tayi mata taje tad'auko bokiti tafara zuba ruwa.
Hannatu cigaba tayi da cewa idan baki saniba toh bari infad'a maki ke da hoto bakuda banbanci a cikin gidan nan saboda ni kad'ai ce matar Ahmad kuma ni yakeso dan haka ke da shegun yaranki kuja tsummar rayuwarku gefe inba hakaba zan iya sawa akoreku daga gidan nan.
Ummah zama tayi tafara wankinta kamar ba da ita take ba haka tayi ta tsokanarta fad'a ita dai tashareta har tagama wankinta tayi shanya tashige d'akinta koda su Meerah suka dawo daga kitso haka suka sameta tana ta yada habaici.
Safiya ce ahankali tace him uhm Allah yakyauta.
Meerah ko tsaki taja suka shige d'aki suka samu ummansu zaune nan suma suka zauna Safiya tace ummah wai waccen matar da wa take fad'a?
Murmushi ummansu tayi tace wa ma yasanar mata.
Allah ummah da ke take ji nake kamar inje inmaida mata.
Kinga Meerah banason haka kar kikuskura kifara dan na lura baki tsoron rigima da matar nan ni fa har mamaki kike ban akan matar nan kina nema kikoya ma kanki halin da baki da shi.
Kwantar da murya tayi tace kiyi hak'uri ummah na daina kawai dai ina jin haushi inga tana maki haka.
Toh menene a ciki Meerah? Ai daga iya magana tatsaya ba wai dukana tayiba toh mi zaki tashi hankalinki akai, kinga ni d'auko min redio ta inji labarai.
Tashi Meerah tayi taje tad'auko mata redio takunna tacika k'ara duk dan sudaina jiyota sosai.

Haka tayi ta fad'a har tagaji dan kanta tayi shuru tashige d'aki batare da tayi wankinba.
 koda ummah tafito taga batayi wankinba cikin ranta tace daman dan inbiye mata ayi rigima saisa ba wani wankin da zatayi.




Washe gari Meerah kallon ummansu tayi da take kwance tana shirin yin bacci wajen sallar azuhur tace ummah ko inje inga idan wankin hajiya luba ya taru inkar6o inyi mata?
Shuru ummah tayi sai chan tace a'a Meerah kinga mahaifinku ya hana ayi mashi wanki acikin gida hak'uri kawai zamuyi da wankin nan.
ummah akan me zamu hak'ura bayan ta nan ne muke samun abincinmu.
Toh ya zamuyi tunda baya so.
Shuru tayi sai chan tace shikenan ummah bari toh indinga yi masu a gidansu ba sai na kawo nan ba.
A'a ni da naso muhak'ura dan bazaki iyaba ke kad'ai aikin zai miki yawa.
Murmushi tayi tace kar kidamu ummana zan iya kinga ba wani yawa ne da su ba kuma kinga kud'in islamiyya gobe ne akeso mukai sannan ga su Safiya da sadeek zasu koma hutun term ran Monday kuma za'a siya masu exercise book.
Shuru ummah tayi tana tunanin maganar d'iyartata sai chan tace shikenan meerah kije kiyi ammah dan Allah da kin gaji ki ajiye inyaso gobe sai kikoma ki idar.
Toh ummah insha Allahu.
Yauwa Allah yaimaku Albarka.
Ameen ummanmu.

Tashi tayi tad'auko hijab tasaka tace ummah sai na dawo.
Toh meerah adawo lafiya Allah yakiyaye.
Aunty meerah inzo inrakaki?
A'a Sadeek kaga akwai islamiyya yau kazauna kuje islamiyya ka ji?
'Daga kai yayi alamun toh tace yauwa d'an k'anena zanyo maka tsaraba ka ji?
Cikin jin dad'i yace toh Aunty Meerah sai kin dawo.

Tana fita takawa tayi k'asa tana tafiya a nutse sai ji tayi anyi mata sallama tsaki taja ahankali sannan ta amsa batare da ta tsaya ba tacigaba da tafiyarta haba Meerah wai meyasa kike min irin haka duk lokacin da zamu had'u bakibani dama mugaisa.
Bata kulashiba tacigaba da tafiyarta ganin haka yasa Ammar yarik'o mata hannu yace please kitsaya ko yayane mugaisa.
'Dago kai tayi tawurga mashi harara tace dallah malam kasakar min hannu banyi warning d'inka ba akan ta6ani?
Murmushi yayi batare da ya saketaba yace toh ai kece baby kike wahalar da ni kin k'i amince min mugaisa duk sadda zan ganki saidai kiyi ta d'auke min kai.
Fuskarta a d'aure tace kaga malam kasake min hannu dan ni ba 'yar iska bace.
Sakinta yayi yace ammah gaskiya dole kibari muyi magana dan indai kikace wucewa zakiyi toh gaban mutane zan rik'eki.
Harara tawurga mashi tajuya zata tafi cikin sauri yasha gabanta yace wai ke menene haka kikeyi ko dan kinga na damu da ke saisa kike wulak'anta ni?
Toh daman wa yace kadamu da ni mutum sai nacin tsiya da zaka taimaka karabu da ni da yafi maka.
Hakan ba zai ta6a yuwuwa ba baby kema kin sani sannan dole kidinga kulani.
Tsaki taja taratsa zata tafi sai ji tayi ya rik'ota.
Fizge hannunta tayi a fusace tace wai kai wane irin d'an iskan mutum ne.
Shafa sumar kansa yayi yace koma me zakice saidai kice ammah bazan bari kitafiba batare da kin bari munyi magana ba koda ace zamu kai dare a nan.
Ganin dagaske yake yasa meerah taja tatsaya tare da kallon gefe d'aya fuskarnan a d'aure.
Murmushin cin nasara yayi yace yauwa baby ko kefa yanzu dai inaso kitaimaka kifara sanar da ni gidanku.
Batare da ta kalleshiba tace bazaka samu hakan ba.
Uhm haka dai kikace ammah ni da kaina zan gano dan nasan cikin wannan anguwar kike nima ina zuwanta sosai dan akwai gidan sister d'ina a nan anguwar.
ta6e baki tayi batare da tayi magana ba.
Gaskiya baby kina da kyau sosai ammah ba wannan ba ko zaki taimaka min da phone number d'inki?
Banda ita dan Allah malam kabarni inwuce ka dameni da surutu.
Murmushi yayi yace afuwan baby yanzu muje inrakaki.
Bana so nan taratsa ta gefenshi ta wuce.
Murmushi yayi yabita da kallo cikin jin dad'i yace nasan nan gaba zata amince min tunda har ta fara saurarata wayarsa ce da tafara ringing yatunatar da shi inda zaije dan haka cikin sauri yawuce yashige motarshi yajata dagudu yatafi.


Meerah tana isa gidan hajiya luba bayan sun gaisa tayi mata maganar wanki tace a nan gidan zatayi mata
 amincewa tayi dan itama ta fi so ayi mata a nan dan haka taje tatattaro mata tarakata chan bayan gidansu tace ga ruwanan muna da shi available a tanki ga kuma robobi nan kiyi min wanki yafita sosai.
Toh hajiya insha Allahu.
Batare da tayi magana ba tawuce tabar wajen nan meerah tad'auko robobin tatara ruwa tafara wankin kusan awanta ukku tsugunne tana wankin duk gajiyar da tayi ammah hakan bai sa ta ajiye ba haka tadaure ta ida wankin tana gamawa taje tafad'a ma hajiyar tazo taduba kayan taga sun fita ganin yadda meerah tayi zuru zuru yasa hajiya luba taje tahad'o abincin da yaranta suka rage takawo mata tace gashinan taci kar6a tayi cikin jin dad'i tayi mata godiya, hajiya luba na barin wajen tajuye abincin cikin leda tashiga da plate d'in ciki ta ajiye sannan taje tace mata zata tafi, naira d'ari shidda tabata tace kud'in wankin da tayi.
Godiya tayi mata sannan tatafi gida.
Koda a gajiye take ammah hakan bai sa tahau abun hawaba a k'afa tataka har ta isa gida ummah koda taganta saida tatausaya mata dan tasan a galabaice take tana zama ummah takawo mata ruwa tace sannu meerah.
Murmushi tayi tace yauwa ummah daman baki d'ebo ba kin barshi na d'ebo.
A'a meerah keda kikasha aiki kar6a kisha.
Kar6a tayi tasha sannan tad'auko abincin da aka bata tace ummah gashi hajiya luba tabani.
Kallonta take cike da tausayi tace haba meerah meyasa kika zauna da yunwa kika k'ullo abincin kika kawo?
Ummah nafiso inga munci tare dan bana iya ci ku bakuci ba.
toh menene dan kinci mu bamu ciba meerah? Kinsan dai muma bazamu zauna da yunwa ba zamu samu abinda zamu ci.
Ummah kiyi hak'uri ba zan iyaba kuma kinga yana da yawa ba laifi.
Toh shikenan meerah yanzu kici sai kirage ma su safiya.
A'a ummaj bari dai inzuba mana muci ni da ke sai murage masu nan tatashi taje tad'auko plate tazuba masu rabi tazuba ma su safiya sauran a plate tarufe masu.
tare da ummah suke cin abincin kowa ahankali yake ci dan d'an uwanshi yaci dayawa daga k'arshe ummah tajanye hannunta tace meerah maida hankali kicinye abincin nan ni na k'oshi.
Har ta bud'e baki zatayi magana ummah tace bana son jin komai daga gareki yi sauri kicinye abincin nan, tsareta ummah tayi saida tacinye tas sannan tatashi taje tawanke hannunta kud'in tad'auko taba ummah nan ummah tanuna farin cikinta tayi mata addu'a tana yin sallar la'asar kwanciya tayi dan tahuta batasan lokacin da bacci yakwashetaba koda ummah bataso tayi baccin la'asar ba ammah bata tashetaba saboda ita kanta tasan agajiye take.

Sai wajen magrib sannan tatasheta lokacin har su safiya sun dawo daga islamiyya.



Ummah tana duk'e tana had'a murhu zata d'aura masu girki sai ga Ahmad da Hannatu sun fito zasu tafi unguwa sunyi ankon shadda sai k'amshi yake tashi ganinta yasa hannatu tace dear kaga yanzu mufara zuwa shopping d'in kafin muwuce gidanmu.
Washe baki yayi yace ai duk yadda kikeso haka za'ayi.
Kai dear gaskiya kai d'in na musamman ne saisa nake k'ara sonka ko me nakeso kanayi min.
Ai dole inyi miki tunda nima kina faranta min dan ni abu guda natsana shine haihuwa a yanzu nafiso sai mun gama more rayuwarmu sai mufara tara 'ya'ya ko da ba wasu dayawa nakeso ba.
Dariya Hannatu tayi tace toh ai ra'ayinmu d'aya da kai dear haba haihuwa ai tana saurin tsofar da mutum yanzu ni duk wanda yaganni ai yarinya zai ce min.
washe baki yayi tare da kallon maryam da tayi kamar bata jin abinda suke cewa yace yarinya ma ta gaske.

Ummah tana jinsu suna ta hirararsu har suka fita cikin ranta tace wai yarinya matar da da anganta ansan ta manyanta ammah itace za'a kalla ace ma yarinya murmushi tayi tare da girgiza kai tace Allah dai yakyauta.

Da dare suna zaune tsakar gida sun baza tabarma saboda zafi sai gasu sun dawo da kaya nik'i-nik'i a hannunsu Ahmad baiji dad'iba da yagansu tsakar gida ko inda suke basu kallaba ummah ce tayi k'arfin halin cewa sannunku da dawowa da mamakinta sai taji sunyi shuru, da gayya hannatu tabi ta gefen tabarmar zata wuce d'an taka k'afar ummah tayi nan tayi kamar ta yi tuntu6e tace kai maryam k'afa kika sa min dan in fad'i.
Abbah ne yayi saurin juyowa yace subhanallah baki dai fad'i ba ko?
'Dan cije le6e tayi tace ban fad'i ba dear saidai tuntu6e da nad'anyi, kallon ummah yayi da tasaki baki tana kallon ikon Allah yace maryam miye haka zaki sa mata k'afa dan tafad'i?
Ikon Allah ka jika da wata magana ni yaushe nasa mata k'afa? Ita dai tabiyo ta wajen k'afata tatakani.
Cikin fad'a hannatu tace kuji min mata au haka zakice bayan kin gama tad'eni da k'afarki?
Rik'ota Ahmad yayi yace yi hak'uri kikyaleta nasan duk bak'in cikine yayi mata yawa taji da abinda yadameta.
Wani irin kallo ummah tayi mashi tace wallahi k'arya ne kace ina bak'in ciki dan ni a kowane lokaci zuciyata fes take duk abinda zakuyi baya ta6a 6ata min rai dan baku gabana.
Mu ko muke gabanki tunda kika d'auko tabarma kika zauna dan kiga da me zamu shigo daman chan kin saba sa mana ido a harkokinmu,,,cewar hannatu.
Murmushi tayi tace hannatu kenan kema kanki kin sani baku gabana duk abinda zakuyi baku isa ind'aga kai inkallekuba dan ni banza naba ma ajiyarku musamman ma ke ina tausaya ma rayuwarki dan gabad'ayanta abun tausayi ce.
Rayuwarki dai ce abun tausayi ke da wad'annan shaggun yaran naki masu idanuwana kamar na mayu.
Ummah har ta bud'e baki zatayi magana sai kuma tafasa saboda bataso tana biyeta dan haka tayi banza da ita.
Ahmad da yake tsaye yana murmushi yace hannatu muje ciki.
Dear da kabarni inji abinda zatace yanzu in casata a cikin gidan nan.
Meerah da take zaune kanta  sunkuye a k'asa sai a lokacin tad'ago takalli Hannatu tawurga mata harara.
Kallonta itama hannatun tayi tace toh ko ke zaki wakilci uwartaku kice wani abu?
Idan su basuce wani abu ba toh ni na ce kece mekama da mayu,,,safiya tafad'a a fusace.
Kukan kura hannatu tayi tayo kan safiya zata doketa cikin sauri meerah tamik'e tare da fizgo safiya ta6oyeta a bayanta tana cewa wallahi kar kisaki kita6ata.
Idan na ta6ata akwai abinda zakiyi?
Nidai na fad'a maki.
A fusace Abbah yayo wajen yad'auke meerah da mari yace ke banason iskanci ke zaki hana adoketa?
Mik'ewa ummah tayi tare da zuwa tasha gabanshi tace kaga Ahmad kar kasaki kak'ara dukar min 'ya'ya matarka ita tafara jansu su kuma suka rama da batace masu mayu ba ai da suma bazasu ce mataba.
Cikin fad'a yace watau maryam ke kike sa su suna iskanci son ransu a cikin gidana ko?
Ni yarana basa iskanci dan suna da tarbiyarsu saidai in anyi masu rashin kunyar ne suke ramawa.
Nunata yayi da yatsa yace kikiyayeni maryam a cikin gidan nan.
Hannatu da take tsaye ta cika fam tana jira taga Ahmad ya rufe maryam da duka ganin bai doketaba yasa tace kaga megida kamatsa kaga inji da wannan matar dan nice daidai da ita.
Kibarta kawai hannatu ni nasan yadda zanyi da ita d'auko kayan mushiga d'aki.
Tsaki taja sannan tad'auki ledojin tawuce fuuuu tanufi d'akinta cike da takaici nan shima yatake mata baya.
Safiya ce taja tsaki tace banza kawai.
A fusace ummah tajuyo kanta tace wallahi safiya kikiyayeni inba so kike insa6a makiba ita tsaranki ce da zakice zaki mata rashin kunya?
turo baki tayi tace kiyi hak'uri na daina.
Meerah janye hannunta tayi da take dafe da k'uncinta kallonta safiya tayi tace sannu Aunty Meerah kiyi hak'uri ni najamaki 
Murmushi tayi tace a'a safiya ba ke bace saidai kidaina biyeta kinga nima na daina.
toh Aunty meerah.
Wucewa meerah tayi tad'auki sadeek da yake ta faman bacci tanufi d'aki da shi safiya kuma tanad'o masu tabarmar suka shige d'aki.


Tun daga ranar Hannatu da tagansu sai harara sudai sai sushareta wani sabon abu da tatsiro saka k'ananun kaya tatsuke tana yawo acikin gidan babu ruwanta,  in ko Ahmad yadawo taje tarungumeshi ko gaban su meerah ne bata damuwa saidai suyi saurin janye idanunsu nan abun yafara damun ummansu dan aganinta kar a6ata mata tarbiyar yara.

Wata rana da tagq abun kullum gaba yake ci taje tasamu Ahmad zaune a d'akinsa yana duba wasu files duk atunaninsa hannatu ce yayi saurin d'agowa ganin Maryam yasa yad'aure fuska yace lafiya zaki shigo min d'aki?
Haba Ahmad koma menene ai sai kabari kaji abinda yakawoni.
Kiyi sauri ki maganarki dan ina da abun yi yanzu.
Girgiza kai tayi sannan tace ba wai wata magana bace meyawa takawo ni daman ince maka kayi ma matarka magana dan Allah tadaina fitowa da irin shigar da take saboda yara dan agaskiya nidai 'ya'yana nabasu tarbiya mekyau.
Kamar anjefota tashigo d'akin a harzuk'e tace wace magana naji wannan matar tanayi Allah dai yasa ba gulmata takeba.
Murmushi Ahmad yayi yace dawa ma zatayi gulmarki ai bama zata faraba.
Juyowa Maryam tayi takalleta ranta a 6ace tace ai bakikai matsayin da zanyi gulmarkiba kawai dai ina mashi maganane akan yasa kigyara shigarnan da kikeyi tunda bake kad'ai bace a cikin gidan sai kibari daga ke sai shi sai kiyi irin shigar da kikeso.
Fashewa da dariya hannatu tayi tace babbar magana watau ke kin samu waje har kina nema kimin gyara a cikin gidan mijina, ke asuwa da zakice inyi yadda kikeso?
Ummah Kallon Ahmad tayi tace ka dai ji abinda nafad'a maka dan gaskiya yarana na basu tarbiya me kyau.
Kam bala'i wanene toh marar tarbiyar ba dai ni kike nufi ba?,,,cewar hannatu.
'Daga mata hannu Maryam tayi tace dakata malama ni ba dake nake magana ba da mijina nake dan haka kidaina saka min baki.
Tun kan tarufe baki Ahmad yace wai ke maryam meyasa kin cika neman rigima ina ruwanki da yanayin shigarta me tatsare maki ai nan cikin gidanta take ba waje tafita hakaba bare kice batayi daidai ba kuma da kike maganar yaraya ai ba wani yace ki ajiye mana su ba inkinji baki iyawa sai kifitar da su daga gidan.
Dakyau megida ka kyauta min ai wallahi ba wanda ya isa yasa inyi yadda yakeso dan ni rayuwata nake yadda natsara abuna sannan inba gulma ba ubanwa yace yaran naki sukalleni.
Jinjina kai maryam tayi tare da yin murmushi wanda baikai zuci ba tace watau ka goyi bayanta har kana ik'irarin yarana subar gidan.
Ta6e baki yayi tare da d'aga kafad'a yace matsalarki ce.
Hawaye ne suka gangaro daga idanunta tace nagode sosai Ahmad nan tajuya tabar d'akin tana jin hannatu tana ta balbala masifa.

Komawa tayi d'akinta tazauna nan tahad'e kai da gwiwa tafara kuka dan ma sauk'inta ma su meerah basunan ta aikesu, saida tasha kukanta me isarta sannan tatashi taje tawanke fuskarta a ranar haka tawuni ba walwala su meerah koda suka dawo damunta sukayi da tambaya me akayi mata tace bakomai suka tambayeta ko batada lafiya nan ma tace lafiyarta lau.
Nan suma duk sukabi suka canza jikunsu yayi sanyi ganin basu saba ganin mahaifiyarsu a irin wannan yanayinba daga k'arshe da taga yadda duk sukayi yasa tasaki ranta nan suma duk suka sake suna jin dad'in ganin mahaifiyartasu babu abinda yake damunta.



Tun daga ranar hannatu sai ma abinda yacigaba dan shigarta take tamatse tayi ta yawonta tsakar gida tana wak'e-wak'enta saidai maryam tak'unshe yaranta a d'aki tace sudaina fita inba makaranta zasuje ba itako wani irin dad'i takeji dan daman babu abinda tatsana sama da tafito tagansu a tsakar gidanta tafiso kullum suna d'aki saisa ma tace sukoma d'akin kusa da soro.

*PAGE 9*


*BAYAN WATA 'DAYA*

Fitowar hannatu daga d'aki kenan zata shiga kitchen dan tad'aura girki nan taga shanyar da su meerah sukayi na kayansu suka shanya saman igiya tsaki taja nan tanufi wajen kayan tana cewa wannan ai rainin wayaune adinga yi ma mutum shanya a gaban d'aki dan tsabar raini, kayan tafara janyowa tana yadawa a k'asa duk saida tazubar da su sannan tafara k'walah ma maryam kira dak'arfi.

Ummah da take tsaye tana gyara masu kayan da suke cikin wardrobe jin muryar hannatu tana kiranta yasa da farko har ta share daga k'arshe da taji kiran ba me k'arewa bane nan tafito tsaye tatarar da ita gaban kayan da tazubar k'asa ta rik'e k'ugu kallonta ummah tayi cike da mamaki tace haba hannatu menene haka kikayi kayanmu fa ne damuka wanke kika watsar.
Yatsine fuska tayi tace ina sane da haka saisa ai nakiraki kigani dan banga dalilin da zaisa adinga min shanya a gaban d'akiba.

Kallon igiyar Maryam tayi taga tsakiyar gida take 
Tace ammah ai igiyar ba kusa da d'akinki takeba akwai tazara sosai da d'akinki kedai kawai kice neman rigima kike.

Eh rigimar nake nema daman kinsan ai halina ne nidai na fad'a maki kar asaki ak'ara min shanya a kusa da d'aki inson shanyar kuke toh kuk'ulla igiya kusa da d'akinku dan indai kuka k'ara toh wallahi sai kun rasa kayanku dan ketasu zanyi.

Tsaki maryam taja taje tafara tattara kayan da hannatu tazubar
 'yar dariya Hannatu tayi tace sai ni hannatu dole inyi yadda nakeso a gidan mijina babu ubanda ya isa yahana.

Tana kwashe kayan tawuce takai d'aki tad'aura saman katifa tafara linkewa sai jiyo hannatu tayi tana cewa duk ubanda ya isa yayi magana man yaga aiki da cikawa ina ma amfanin zama da mayyya 'yar gidan mayu anzo anyi zaune a gida ance angaji da su ammah dan maita shine akayi zaune.

Wani irin haushine yakama maryam batasan lokacin da ta ajiye kayan da suke hannuntaba tafito tana zuwa tasamu hannatu inda take tsaye a fusace tace ya isheki hannatu kar kikuskura kikira iyayena da mayu.

Harararar sama da k'asa tayi mata tace toh  ai na d'auka da kika tafo haka dukana zakiyi sai kuma naga kinja kin tsaya.

Nidai na fad'a maki kar kikuskura kikirasu da mayu.

In nace fa me zaki iya yi?
Komai ma zan iya dan kidaina ganin ina kyale ki ba wai tsoronki nake jiba kawai dai ina kyaleki saboda darajar aure da kuma rashin lokacinki dan ke bakikai matsayin da zan tsaya cacar baki da ke ba.

Ayyiiiriiriiiii ahayyye nanayee yau su maryam baki ya bud'e watau har ni kike bud'e baki kina fad'a ma magana haka? Ai ban d'auka kina magana haka ba sannan kisani ba tsoronki nakeba na fad'a na sake fad'a ke mayya ce.

'Daga hannu maryam tayi tasharara mata mari har saida hannatu takusa kifawa bibiyu tafara gani cikin sauri tadafe kumatunta tana cewa ni kika mara maryam!

Tana bud'e baki zatayi magana sai ji tayi itama and'auketa da wani irin gigitaccen mari har saida tayi baya kamar zata fad'i tadawo wuta tad'auke mata nakusan minti d'aya saboda rad'ad'in marin da mamaki take kallon Ahmad da yake tsaye yana huci muryarta tana rawa tace Ahmad ni kamara? Wani marin yasake d'aukeya da shi yace ke har kin isa kid'aga hannu kimari matata, ke wa yamareki da har zaki mareta?

hawaye ne masu zafi suka gangaro daga idanunta tace mari biyu kayi min saboda wannan matar, yanzu ni duk wulak'ancin da cin mutuncin da tayi min baka ganiba kana ji takirani da mayya takira iyayena mayu.

Hannatu fashewa tayi da kukan munafunci tace ni yaushe nace maki mayya maryam ni zaki ja ma sharri?

Cikin fushi yace ke kinje mq kince mata mayyar wa ma yasani ko daman mayyarce.

Fashewa tayi da kuka tace Ahmad ni zaka ce ma mayya? daidai lokacin su meerah suka shigo gidan gabad'aya ja sukayi suka tsaya bakin k'ofa suka kasa k'arasawa, chan safiya tayi k'arfin halin tafiya ita da sadeek suna cewa ummah me akayi maki kike kuka?

Cikin kuka maryam tace nagode sosai Ahmad akan hannatu kayi min mari har biyu kakirani da mayya.

Na mareki maryam kuma ko yanzu hannunki yak'ara gigin ta6a fuskar hannatu wallahi ba ke ba hatta yaranki sai sun gwammace da baki haifesu ba dan ni ba irin sakarkarun mazan nan bane da zan zuba maki ido kiyi yadda kika ga dama.

Jinjina kai tayi tace ka kyauta Ahmad saidai inaso kasani wannan shine na k'arshe da hannunka zai k'ara ta6a fuskata dan wallahi hak'urina ya k'are dan aure ba hauka bane.

Ko ramawa zakiyi? Nace ko zaki rama ne eyyee!

Hannun su Safiya da sadeek taja suka wuce d'aki.

Meerah ko tsaye tayi takasa motsa k'afafunta saidai hawaye da suke gangarowa daga idanunta tana jin wani irin k'unci acikin ranta.
Ahmad da yake ta huci juyowa yayi yakalleta yace kekuma ubanme kike a nan zakibar wajen ko sai na zo na 6a66allaki.

Cikin sauri Meerah tawuce d'aki tare da fashewa da kuka tana shiga tasamu ummansu zaune bakin katifa hannuwanta duka biyun ta rufe fuskarta da su daga gefenta sadeek da safiya ne suna ta kuka cikin sauri ta isa inda take tare da fad'awa jikinta tatafashe da kuka.

Kallonta Maryam tayi cikin dasashiyar murya tace Meerah kukan ya isa haka.

K'ara shigewa tayi jikinta tace dole inyi kuka ummah akan me Abbah zai dinga ci maki mutunci meyasa zai d'aga hannu yamareki? Duk k'ok'arin da kike akansa baya gani? Meyasa yakeso yaga hak'urinki ya k'are?

Wasu sabbin hawaye ne suka gangaro daga idanun maryam tace koma menene kanshi yayi mawa  daidai da shi ne ban damuwa akan duk wulak'ancin da matarsa take min ammah yau nakasa jurewa ta ja hak'urina yak'are takirani da mayya d'iyar mayu shi kuma yagoya mata baya shin haka laifine dan na mareta?

Meerah rungume ummanta tayi tare da sake fashewa da kuka tace ummah kokad'an Abbah baya kyauta maki meyasa yakasa gane kece kike hak'urin zama da shi? Meyasa yakasa gane babu macen da zata soshi sama da yadda kike sonsa?

Maryam goge hawayen fuskarta tayi tace meerah nad'auki hakan a matsayin k'addarata kudaina kuka kucigaba da tayani da addu'a, janyo safiya tayi da itama taketa kuka tace safiya kukan ya isa haka kiyi shuru kinji?

Safiya komawa tayi chan k'arshen katifar takwanta tana me cigaba da kukanta.
Ummah janyo sadeek tayi takwantar saman cinyarta tace kadaina kuka ka ji ko d'ana zan baka biyar kasiyo biscuit jin haka yasa yafara rage kukan da yake.

Meerah d'ago kai tayi cikin kukan takalli ummansu ganin yadda sahun mari yakwanta a fuskarta yasa tasake fashewa da kuka tace yanzu ummah akan wacchan matar Abbah yayi maki wannan marin haka?

Toh me zakiyi? Nidai in dan nice shikenan abar maganar komai ya wuce tunda ni akayi ma laifin kuma na hak'ura.

nan hannatu ta ida k'ulewa tace badake nake magana ba? Me yakawoki d'akina ko shaye shayen da kika saba ne kikaje kikayi shine baki gane d'akin da kika shigoba.
still dai Meerah shuru tayi tana kallonta.

Tana shiga taga Hannatu tsaye tana kuka tana duba jikinta da duk sahun bulala yakwanta cikin sauri ta isa wajen da Ahmad yake dukan Meerah tana zuwa tace haba Ahmad me tayi maka kake dukanta dan Allah kadaina.
nunata yayi da bulala yace kina matsowa nan kema sai na zuba maki.
 jin haka yasa ummah taja tatsaya tana kallonshi zuba ma meerah bulala yake iya k'arfinshi ihu take tana rok'onshi yayi hak'uri nan ummah tafara hawaye cike da tausayin d'iyarta cikin sauri tanufi wajen yana d'aga bulalar tayi saurin rik'ewa wani irin wawan mari yad'auketa da shi yace sakar min bulala duk zafin marin da taji ammah baisa tasakiba tace wallahi ba zan sakaba Ahmad saidai kayi min abinda zakayi idan kai bakasan zafin haihuwa ba toh ni na sani.

Murmushi ummah tayi cike da takaici tace Tabbas a yanzu na ida tabbatarwa da cewa kai butulu ne a yau na ida da na sani a rayuwata akan za6arka da nayi kakasance abokin rayuwata saidai abinda nakasa ganewa shine kwata-kwata baka dace da rayuwataba baka dace da kakasance uba ga yarana ba saidai a kowane lokaci ina k'ara gargad'inka da ka dinga tunawa akwai *RANAR NADAMA* akwai ranar da zakayi kuka da idanunka, akwai ranar da zata kasance zakayi da na sani a rayuwarka, girgiza kai tayi tace koma ban fad'aba wannan ranar tana nan zuwa itace ranar da zata kasance ta bak'in ciki agareka, kallon Hannatu tayi da take ta murmushi tace kekuma kiguji zuwa rafi dan akwai ranar da tulu zaya fashe kibar murna dan gemon d'an uwanki ya kama da wuta kiyi k'ok'ari kishafa ma naki ruwa, kallon Meerah tayi da tamik'e dak'yar sannan tawuce tafita cikin sauri Meerah tabi bayanta tana tafiya jikinta duk ya yi sanyi ahaka tashiga d'aki ummah zama tayi saman katifa tabuga uban tagumi cikin zuciyarta tana furta innalilkahi wa'inna ilaihiraji'un, Safiya da Sadeek ne sukazo inda take suna tambayarta me yafaru, Meerah ko jingine kanta tayi jikin bango tana me cigaba da zubar da hawaye wani irin nadama taji a ranta tana ayyana da ta sani bata aikata hakaba ta dalilinta ta kashe ma ummanta aure wani irin haushin kanta yakamata wasu zafafan hawaye suka shiga kwarara.

Ummah mik'ewa tayi cikin sanyin jiki tad'auko hijab d'inta tasaka tace ma su safiya sutaso suje duk mik'ewa sukayi suna tambayar ummah ina zasuje tace unguwa sannan takalli Meerah tace kizo kisa hijab d'inki mutafi, kamar jira meerah take nan tagangare k'asa tazauna tare da fashewa da kuka tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri kiyafe min wallahi na kasa hak'urine duk yadda naso insaita zuciyata tahak'ura na kasa zan iya jure kowane irin wulak'anci ammah banda awulak'anta min ke tabbas nasan na aikata babban kuskure a rayuwata da nayi sanadiyar mutuwar aurenki wanda nasan bazaki ta6a yafe min wannan laifin da nayi ba.

Cikin k'arfin hali ummah tace dakata Meerah banason irin haka ke kanki kinsan daman chan haka ubangiji yatsara dan haka mutuwar aurena daman rubutacce ne ubangiji ya riga ya tsara ma kowa yadda rayuwarsa zata kasance dan haka tashi kid'auko hijab d'inki mutafi.

Mik'ewa tayi dak'yar tana kuka taje tad'auko hijab tasaka nan ummah tad'auko d'an makullin d'akin nasu suka fito tarufe d'akin safiya ma kanta kuka take dan taji abinda su ummantasu suke tattaunawa ta gane cewa rabuwa zasuyi da gidan uban nasu.

Haka suka fita babu kowa tsakar gidan dan Ahmad yana cikin d'aki yana lallashin matar tashi, me adaidaita suka samu tafad'a mashi anguwar da zaya kaisu haka suka tafi basu d'au komai ba sai 'yan kud'in da garesu.

Suna isa da sallamarsu suka shiga lokacin hajiya binta tana zaune tsakar gida ita da yaranta ganinsu yasa tazuba masu ido tana kallonsu cike da mamaki duk sunyi zuru zuru ummah ce tazauna saman tabarmar nan hajiya binta tace maryam lafiya naganku haka?

Au kekuma sai kika biye mashi kika fito da su duk ya zakiyi da su yanzu yara har ukku.
Yaya binta haka zan rik'e kayana nidai taimako nakeso kuyi min ko ke ko yaya muhammad wani yataimaka min da wajen da zan zauna ni da yarana.

Chab kinjiki da wata magana gaskiya nidai bazan iya zama da ke da yaran nan ba dan nauyi zaku d'aura min dayawa da ma dai ace kekad'ai ce da na ce kizauna indai kin amince zaki zauna da ni toh gaskiya kimaidasu gidan mahaifinsu dan shi yafi cancanta yarik'e kayanshi kema zan zauna da ke ne dan ba yadda na iya  inko bazaki maida suba toh saidai kije wajen Yaya Muhammad dan ya fi ni rufin asiri k'ila shi zai iya rik'onku.

Tunda tafara maganar cike da mamaki Maryam take kallonta har takai aya sannan tace Yaya binta yanzu ke dakanki kike cewa inmaida yarana wajen ubansu alhali kinsan halinshi? Yanzu kina nufin bazaki iya zama da mu ba?

Toh menene a ciki dan kin maidasu nidai na fad'a maki bazaki zauna min gida da su ba dan nauyi zaku d'aura min nima ina fama da kaina da nawa yaran kina gani mahaifinsu baya nan ya yi tafiya zan zauna da ke ne kawai kafin yadawo inji abinda zai ce dan in bai aminceba kema kanki gaskiya saidai kiyi hak'uri.

Murmushi ummah tayi cike da takaici tare da jinjina kai tace tabbas bazan iya rabuwa da yarana ba zanje inreni abuna Allah yana sane da halin da muke ciki ko da baki taimaka min ba agareshi nake neman taimako.

Ta6e baki hajiya binta tayi tace oho dai nidai na fad'a maki indai zaki zauna da ni saidai kiyi yadda nace.

Murmushi ummah tayi tace ma su meerah sutashi sutafi nan duk suka mik'e suka fita cikin sanyin jiki suna kuka, hajiya binta da mamaki tabi 'yar uwartata da kallo cikin ranta tana cewa watau bazata iya maida mashi yaranshi ba girgiza kai tayi tace Allah yakyauta.

Suna fita me adaidaita suka samu ummah tafad'a mashi inda zai kaisu tunda suka shiga nan ma duk shuru sukayi itadai cikin ranta addu'a take akan Allah yasa yayan nata ya amince yazauna da ita da yaranta.

Girgiza kai tayi tace ummah bazan iya yin bacciba a wannan lokacin domin ina jin takaicin abinda na aikata ina da na sani akan abinda nayi wanda yayi sanadiyar mutuwar aurenki, na san duk wani hali da kika shiga nice sila taya zan iya yafe ma kaina, fashewa tayi da kuka tace dan Allah ummah kiyafe min ko na ji sauk'in abinda nakeji a cikin zuciyata.
Ummah rik'ota tayi tace meerah kukan ya isa haka nidai bakiyi min komai ba inma kinyi na yafe sannan mutuwar aure daman chan Ahmad yana da niyar sakina ke kanki kinsan ba zaman dad'i nake da mahaifinku ba zaman hak'uri kawai nake.
Cikin sheshekar kuka tace nasan haka ummah tabbas tun tasowata nasan abbah yana tsangwamarki saidai abinda yakasa ganewa ba kowace mace bace zata iya yin irin hak'urin da kike da shi sannan ita haihuwa ubangiji yake bayarwa daman chan a rubuce yake sai an haifemu saidai shi abbah ya kasa gane hakan har yana alak'antamu da shegu.
Murmushi ummah tayi wanda yafi kuka ciwo tace ra'ayin mahaifinku ne haka meerah ammah shima tabbas nasan yana sane da hakan kawai dai son zuciya ne irin nashi koma me yayi nidai bana fata kurik'e shi a ranku domin ubane agareku matsayinsa bazai ta6a canzuwaba a wajenku, ni dan ya rabu da ni bakomai bane saboda ni matace ana canza mata saida d'a baya canzuwa kuma ni na d'auki hakan a matsayin k'addara tah ku ma ina fata zaku d'au hakan a matsayin *K'ADDARA CE* sannan zaku cigaba da yi ma mahaifinku addu'a in darabon shiryuwa zai shiryu yanemeku dan ku jininshi ne.
Share hawayen fuskarta tayi tace insha Allahu ummah zamu tayashi da addu'a saidai bana fata ink'ara komawa inda yake domin kece komai namu zamu iya rayuwa batare da mahaifi da dangi ba saidai bazamu iya rayuwaba batare da ke ba saisa akowane lokaci nake addu'a Allah ubangiji yabar mana ke yabamu abinda zamu saka maki da shi kiyi alfahari da haihuwar mu.
Ummah murmushi tayi tace Ameen meerah Allah yayi maku albarka.
Gyara zamanta meerah tayi a gefen ummah sannan tace ummah dan Allah inaso kiyi min taimako guda.
Kallonta ummah tayi tace wane irin taimako kikeso meerah kifad'a koma menene in baifi k'arfina ba zanyi miki.
Girgiza kai tayi tace baifi k'arfinki ba ummah saidai bansan yadda zaki d'au maganataba.
Koma menene kifad'a dan zan dubi maganarki a duba na basira.
Shuru meerah tayi sai chan tace ummah inaso kitaimaka dan Allah kifad'a min labarin rayuwarki tun daga farko har zuwa aurenki da abb....yanayin fuskar ummah da taga lokaci guda ya canza yasa tayi shuru takasa k'arasa maganar nan jikinta yayi sanyi cikin sanyin murya tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri idan na 6ata maki rai.
Girgiza kai ummah tayi tace ko d'aya baki 6ata min ba meerah ammah meyasa kikeso kisan tarihin rayuwata?
Kawai inaso inji ne  dan nasan zan k'aru da wani abu a ciki sannan inaso insan shin daman auren soyayya kukayi da abbah ko kuma ba na soyayya bane? Sannan insan yadda dangantakarku take da su hajiya binta.
Murmushi ummah tayi wanda iyakarshi le6e sannan tace toh shikenan Meerah a yanzu zan d'an tak'aita maki tarihin rayuwata saboda dare ne ko kuma mubari sai gobe.
Cikin sauri meerah tace dan Allah ummah yanzu kinga duka fa yanzu k'arfe goma tayi.
Kallon agogo ummah tayi sannan tace toh shikenan tunda kin matsa yanzu sai kigyara zamanki kiji labarina a tak'aice.
Murmushi Meerah tayi tare da zama gefen  tace ashirye nake ummana.
Ganin yadda tak'agu yasa ummah tayi murmushi...
