{On Ward Together}



*PAGE 11*


Kamar yadda nata6a fad'a maki a chan baya  Malam Abubakar  da Malama Aisha wadda take takwararki sune iyayenmu 'yan asalin garin katsina ne, sunyi aure a nan cikin garin katsina suna zaman lafiya tsakaninsu mu ukku ne 'ya'yansu Yaya Muhammad shine babba sai Hajiya Binta ba wani tazara bane sosai chan a tsakaninsu dan baifi shekara ukku yabata ba ni dai ce muke da tazara da hajiya binta dan saida takai shekara bakwai sannan aka haifeni, Muntaso a gaban iyayenmu cike da kulawa da tarbiya me kyau, mahaifinmu ba wani me kud'i bane ammah yana da rufin asirin shi bakin gwalgwado sannan babu abinda muke nema murasa gaba da baya mu 'yan gata ne musamman ma ni da tun daga kaina Allah bai k'ara basu haihuwa ba, ni nake kama da ummanmu saisa nafi su Yaya Muhammad da Yaya binta kyau saboda ummanmu fulani ce ta usul ba sosai take jin hausa ba inda mahaifinmu yakasance buzu shima yana da kyaun shi bakin gwalgwado dan duk shi suka biyo muntaso kanmu a had'e ni da 'yan uwana musamman ma ni da yaya binta da muka kasance mata sai nakoma kamar k'awa a gareta koda akwai tazara a tsakaninmu ammah komai namu iri d'aya ba a banbantamu munyi makarantar arabi da boko bakin gwalgwado dan mahaifinmu Allah ya yi shi me son karatu sosai yana iya k'ok'arin shi wajen ganin ya kula da karatunmu koda ba wani k'arfi gareshi ba sosai,
 mahaifiyata tana sona sosai kasancewar duk wata halayya tatace nad'auko dan ina da hak'uri kamarta ba komai bane akeyi min in d'aga kai inkalla sannan banda jan fad'a dan tun ina k'arama bana jan mutane fad'a, ko tsakanina da 'yan uwana baka ta6a jin kanmu hatta yaya binta da nake bi mawa bamu fad'an nan irin na mabiyi da mabiyi ina girmamata sosai suna gabana sosai a makarantar boko.

Lokacin da nagama primary school d'ina ba k'aramin dad'i najiba dan aganina nima zan shiga makarantarsu yaya binta ta 'yan mata ta gwamnati da take cikin jahar katsina, ba yabon kai ba gabad'ayanmu muna maida hankali sosai a karatunmu makarantar jeka kadawo muke inda a 6angare d'aya muke taimaka ma mahaifiyarmu a sauran hidindimu na gida.

Lokacin ina JSS 3 a lokacin Yaya binta tayi SSCE tana gama makaranta aka sa mata bikki da wani saurayinta da suke tare me suna Auwal bayan wata biyar muka sha bikkinta aka kaita a nan cikin garin katsina mijinta yana da rufin asiri sosai ko ma ince maki yana da kud'unshi ba laifi kasancewar bikkin farko ne iyayane sun taka rawa sosai wajen ganin sunyi mata kayan d'aki na gani nafad'a dan mahaifinmu yana da zuciyar yi duk abinda za'aji kunya baya sonshi.
Da za'ayi bikkin Yaya binta na sha kuka sosai dan aganina shikenan na rabu da 'yar uwata da takasance tamkar k'awa agareni saida akayi kusan sati biyu da bikkinta sannan nadawo daidai dan akowane lokaci ummanmu cikin lallashina take dan bak'aramin so take min ba bata son duk abinda zai 6ata min rai.

Tun bayan Auren Yaya binta nan nak'ara samun kulawa sosai wajen iyayena kome suka samo nawa ne bansaniba ko dan lokacin yaya Muhammad yana chan yana training d'in karatun d'an sanda, shekararsa ukku yana karatu yagama, lokacin ina SS2 yasamu aiki bakin gwalgwado yana samun kud'i sosai yana taimaka mana ahankali yasai fili yafara gini da shawarar da Abbanmu yabashi ina SS3 sukayi aure shi da matarshi ramatu 'yar asalin wani k'auyene da ke cikin garin mashi wani aiki ne da aka turasu a garin a chan yaganta har ya aurota itama bafullatana ce.

Ni kad'ai narage a gaban iyayenmu dan haka duk wani shirinsu akaina ne musamman ma ummanmu da taga tun ina SS1 samari suka fara min caaa saidai bana kulasu korarsu nake ummah akullum fad'a take min akan indaina wulak'antasu saidai in amsa mata da toh ammah hakan baya hana inwulak'anta duk wanda yace yana sona.

Wani gida da yake chan gaban gidanmu  wani yaron gidan me suna Yusuf yalik'e min iyayensa suna da kud'i sosai yana nuna min so sosai saidai ni bana sonsa duk inda zan ganshi d'aure fuska nake ammah hakan bai hana yayi min magana saidai inshareshi in zaimin gani goma a rana sai ya min magana ni kuma na yi kamar banji ba.

Ranar wata  alhamis lokacin ina SS3 a ranar muka had'u da Ahmad lokacin ba islamiyya na je gidan kitso a hanyata tadawowa ina tafiya a nutse yazo yayi min sallama na amsa mashi nan yace inba damuwa yanason magana da ni ko inda yake ban kallaba nara6a ta gefe zan wuce ganin haka yasa yace dan Allah intaimaka inmashi kwatancen gidanmu zaizo yasameni muyi magana dan shi kanshi ya san baidace yatareni a hanyaba.
Har cikin raina na ji dad'in hakan ni kaina saida nayi mamakin yadda na amince nayi mashi kwatancen gidanmu da dare yazo yasameni yagabatar min da kanshi da kuma soyayyarshi agareni, da fari da na nuna bana sonshi nan yayi ta rok'ona yana cewa in taimaka mashi sai nace yaje sai nayi shawara tun daga ranar duk ranar da zai zo gidanmu bana fitowa saidai ince ma yaron da ya aiko yaje yace bani nan sai yatsiri da aiko min da wasik'a ta soyayya wata inkaranra wata kuma ink'one batare da na karantaba ummah in tana min fad'a saidai inyi shuru har tagaji tadaina.

Ranar wata asabar na dawo daga islamiyya yatareni a hanya tunda naga shine nahad'e fuska ammah duk da haka bai hana yi min magana ba yace Haba maryam miye laifina da in na aika akira min ke zaki k'i fitowa wallah ni sonki nake tsakani ga Allah in kika ban dama aurenki zanyi.
Jin yadda yake maganar cikin damuwa yasa nad'ago nakalleshi muna had'a ido nan naji ya ban tausayi batare da nayi maganaba naratsa ta gefenshi zan wuce cikin sauri yasha gabana kawai sai gani nayi ya tsugunna k'asa ya ce dan Allah kitaimaka min wallahi sonki nake sosai maryam kece rayuwata kekad'ai ce macen da nakeso.
Murmushi nayi nace nagode sosai saidai ni a yanzu soyayya bata gaba na dan haka kayi hak'uri.
Mik'ewa yayi ganin ina shirin barin wajen yak'ara shan gabana yace haba Maryam da zaki taimaka kiban dama ni zan koya maki yadda zaki soni.

Kallonshi nayi a wulak'ance sannan nawuce nabarshi nan tsaye koda na isa duk sai jikina yayi sanyi naji banji dad'in abinda nayi mashi ba abun duk sai yayi ta damuna a rai nan nasa a raina insha Allahu idan yak'ara dawowa zan amince mashi.

Tun daga ranar bamu sake had'uwa da Ahmad ba sai bayan sati biyu ya aiko akaimin sallama a cikin gida lokacin ina cikin d'aki jin ance ana kirana yasa nayi saurin cewa ganinan zuwa ummah da take zaune tsakar gida saida tayi mamakin hakan dan bata ta6a jin na ce ganinan zuwa ba, fitowa nayi sanye da hijabi nace umnah bari inje inga me kirana.
Murmushi tayi tace toh adawo lafiya.
Ina fita naganshi jingine jikin bangon soron gidanmu sanye cikin k'ananun kaya dan agaskiya mahaifinku tun yana saurayi d'an gayu ne sosai ga kyau, yana ganina cikin sauri ya iso inda nake tsaye na had'e fuska, fuskarshi d'auke da murmushi yace maryama sarauniyar mata barka da fitowa.
saida na yatsina fuska sannan nace barka, ahankali nagaisheshi ya amsa nan yak'ara fad'a min shi fa sona yake dagaske intaimaka in amshi tayin soyayyarshi kallonshi nayi naga ko kad'an baida makusa nace shikenan yaban lokaci.
Damuwa ce tabayyana a fuskarshi yace haba maryam wanchan karon kince inbaki lokaci na baki babu wani bayani yanzu ma shine zakice inbaki lokaci ni fa dagaske sonki nake.

Ta6e baki nayi nace na ji sai kabari inyi shawara da iyayeba in sun amince toh nima na amince.
Cikin sanyin jiki yace toh shikenan kamar yaushe zan dawo?
Zuwa gobe sai kazo kaji shawarar da nayanke.
Ammah dan Allah kar inzo kik'i fitowa.
Batare da nayi magana ba najuya nashige gida.
Da dare na samu ummah da maganar Ahmad nan tanuna farin cikinta sosai dan dama burinta kenan taga na fara kula samari, nan tagoyi bayana akan inkula shi ammah yafara samun mahaifina suyi magana nace mata toh.

Washe gari koda yadawo nashaida mashi saidai yasamu mahaifina yanemi izini in ya amince toh nima na amince da shi ya ji dad'i sosai dan a ranar yasamu Abbana sukayi magana nan Abbah yatambayeshi gidansu da iyayenshi yace iyayenshi sun rasu saidai yana zaune a gidan wani yayanshi nan yaima Abbah kwatancen gidan.
abbah daganshi yaje yayi bincike akan Ahmad duk inda zaije yayi tambaya baiji wani aibu atare da shi ba dan haka bayan kwana biyu da yadawo abbah yabashi izini yafara nema na in da rabo zamuyi aure.

Daman nima kamar jira nake nan na amince ma Ahmad dan gaskiya ko kad'an baida makusa soyayya muka fara me tsafta kusan kullum muna tare da juna Ahmad ya yi karatunshi bakin gwalgwado dan yana da kwalinshi na diploma ya dad'e gida ba aiki daga k'arshe yasamu yabud'e shago a chan wata anguwa me suna K'ofar durbi d'an k'aramin shagone yabud'e yana 'yan saide saide ba laifi yana samu, Ahmad saida yasan yadda yayi mukayi wata irin shak'uwa kusan kullum muna tare da shi abinda zaifi baki mamaki shine ko da makarantar boko zanje da safe toh shi yake biyowa ta gidanmu yazo mutaka k'asa yarakani haka in zanje islamiyya shi yake zuwa yarakani duk abinda nakeso yana yi min baya son 6acin rai na ko kad'an, su ummanmu har mamaki suke akan irin shak'uwar da mukayi ahaka nagama secondary school d'ina, Ahmad ya yi rawar gani wajen yi min su stickers da memo wanda naraba lokacin da nayi graduation 
.

Yusuf tunda yaga na gama makaranta nan yak'ara zuwa yasameni akan maganar soyayya dan duk atunaninshi saboda ina makaranta ne yasa nak'i kulashi a wanchan lojacin ammah a karo na biyu nanuna mashi ni fa ban son shi ganin dagaske nake yasa yaje yasamu Yaya Muhammad akan maganar yana sona nan Yaya Muhammad yazo yasamu abbanmu da maganar Yusuf, abbah yana jin ance Yusuf ne yake sona kuma ya san yusuf dan suna gaisawa sosai dan haka yakirani a lokacin yayi min maganar yusuf fafur nace bana sonshi nan abbah yace tunda ban sonshi toh ak'yaleni abarni in auri wanda nakeso dan shi baya yi ma yaranshi auren dole yaya muhammad ya ji haushi sosai da nace ban son yusuf dan shi ya fison in auri yusuf akan Ahmad babu yadda ya iya kan dole yahak'ura tun daga lokacin ban k'ara jin d'uriyar yusuf ba dan ko zai wuce in wuce bai min magana saida nayi wata shekara d'aya da gama makaranta lokacin samari sai k'ara turaromin suke nan Abbah yace ba dashiba dole in fito da miji inyi aure nan nace babu wanda nakeso inba Ahmad ba.

Abbah sawa yayi nayi ma Ahmad magana yaturo danginshi akayi maganar aurenmu inda aka tsaida auren wata takwas masu zuwa gabad'ayanmu mun nuna farin cikinmu dan Ahmad kasa 6oye murnarshi yayi gaban kowa yake nunawa.

Iyayena sosai suka fara shirya min kayan aure abun har mamaki yake ban komai suka samo sai ace nawa ne hatta Yaya binta tana k'ok'ari wajen tara min abubuwa shi kanshi Ahmad tun daga lokacin yafara shirin aure gidansu na gado da aka bar masu da gonar babansu yasiyar da kasonshi yasamu gida d'an madaidaici yayanshi yacika mashi yasiya yagyara.

Lokacin da bikkinmu ya matso lefe yahad'a min bakin gwalgwado ya yi k'ok'ari sosai dan ya zuba min kaya sosai kowa ya yi mamakin hakan dan Abbah har kiranshi yayi yai mashi fad'a yace yarage kayan shi auren d'iya yabashi ba wai kaya yakesoba.
Hak'uri Ahmed yabashi yace abarmin tunda ya riga da ya zubo ammah zai kiyaye gaba, ni kaina saida nayi mashi magana yace kar indamu cancantace tasa haka kuma kud'in da yake bi bashi ne yasamu aka biyashi saisa.

Ranar juma'a aka d'aura auranmu ni da Ahmad gabad'ayanmu mun nuna farinci ko ma dai ince ya nuna saboda ni tashin hankali nashiga dan neman farin cikina nayi narasa ganin zan rabu da iyayena.

Da marece abbah da kanshi yad'aukeni yakaini gidan mijina Ahmed dan shi haka tsarinshi yake haka yayi ma Yaya Binta na sha kuka kamar raina zai fita kafin insaba ind'au dangana dan shi kanshi Ahmed kullum cikin lallashina yake har nasafko.

Zaman lafiya muke sosai ni da mijina mu dukanmu muna k'ok'arin faranta ma junanmu banda wata matsala da shi dan kullum cikin tattalina yake saidai abu d'aya a duk lokacin da muka zauna muna hira sai ya ce maryam zamanmu mu biyu ya fi min dad'i a kullum ina addu'a Allah yasa kar kisamu ciki har sai nan da wasu shekaru nidai duk jin abun nake wasa saidai inyi murmushi ammah fa ni ina da son yara sosai dan da ina gida yaya binta indai zatayi yayen yaranta ni nake d'aukosu,  ammah fa duk son yarana a gidan Ahmad yaran mak'ota basuda ikon sushigo sai ya min fad'a ya ce indai karkato mashi yara bayaso kan dole nahak'ura nadaina jawosu a jiki gudun 6acin ranshi dan shigowar yara ne kawai yakesa yana canza min fuska abun har damuna yake saidai da nalura shima ko 'ya'yan yayanshi ba sosai yake jansu a jiki ba.

watanmu biyar da aure nan nafara laulayin cikinki daga ni har Ahmad bamusan ciki bane da ni sai da mukaje asibiti nan aka tabbatar mana da ciki gareni na kusan wata ukku tun daga chan Ahmad yacanza fuska k'iri k'iri yafito yace min baya son cikin nan saidai acireshi nikuma nace gaskiya yabarmin abuna ina so ke agaban mutane mukayi ta jayayya a k'arshe a asibitin yabaroni yayi tafiyarshi ni kuma nahau abun hawa nadawo gida fad'a Ahmad yarufe ni da shi yace indai ina son zama da shi dole inje incire cikin nan nidai nashareshi tun daga lokacin zaman lafiya yayi k'aranci a tsakaninmu dan idan ma yafita baya ma dawowa gida sai cikin dare duk wasu kayanshi da suke d'akina kwashewa yayi yamaida d'akinshi hatta abinci innayi baya ci ko amai nayi a gaban idanunshi tob bazaisha ruwan gidan ba,
 haka muka cigaba da zama ban samun wata kulawa daga gareshi har cikina yakai wata bakwai duk nabi narame ga abu ga me ciki ga kuma damuwa da nad'aura ma kaina akan halin Ahmad iyayena da 'yan uwana suna ta tambayata ko akwai abinda yake damuna saidai ince masu a'a dan haka suka d'auka duk akan laulayin da nake ne.
 daga k'arshe da naga abun yana yawa gashi na kusa haihuwa yasa naje gidanmu nafad'a ma ummana halin da nake ciki akan zamanmu da ahmad sosai hankalinta yatashi nan itama tasamu Abbana tayi mashi magana nan yahau fad'a yana cewa akan me zaice baya son haihuwa shi ba a haihuwar aka haifeshi dakanshi yatasani gaba yamaidani gidana yace inyi hak'uri inzauna d'akin mijina zasuyi magana da yayan Ahmad toh nace mashi cikin raina ina addu'a Allah yasa Ahmad yagyara halinshi.

Koda Abbana yaje yasamu yaya sule da maganar nan shima yayi ta fad'a yaje yasamu Ahmad a shagonshi yayi mashi tas daman shi mutum ne me fad'a sosai hak'uri Ahmad yayi ta bashi har yad'an safko.

Ranar da yadawo gida na sha fad'a dan kad'an yarage yadokeni har cemin yake duk wanda zan fad'a mawa inje infad'a babu wanda ya isa yasashi yakar6i cikin nan dan shi ba cikinshi bane saidai inje innemo mashi uba a ranar saida nagwammace daman ban fad'a ba dan har kuka nayi jin yana neman yashegantakar min da cikin da yakasance mallakinshi.

Tun daga ranar yatsiro da wani sabon abu idan yafita tun safe sai nayi bacci yake dawowa tun ina damuwa da kawaici da ya isheni har nazo narage damuwa saidai rama nayi ba kad'an ba ga yaya binta ba wani zuwa take gidana sosai ba bare tasan damuwata nice daman me k'ok'arin lek'ata dan na fita zumunci gashi a lokacin gabad'aya Ahmad yahanani fita wai saidai inyi ta zama gida, haka nayi ta zama har cikina yacika wata takwas cikin ikon Allah ummana tazo gidan dan tadubani ganin yadda nakoma yasa hankalinta yatashi sosai har shago tabi Ahmad tace zata tafi da ni gida inhaihu a chan tunda ta farko ce ba dan ya soba hakanan ya amince tazo dakanta tahad'a min kayana muka tafi gida ni kaina ba k'aramin dad'i najiba da tabaroni da gidan Ahmad.

Kulawa sosai nake samu daga wajen iyayena ahaka har cikina yacika wata tara da 'yan kwanaki nahaifoki duk wasu kamanni na sak kika kwaso duk wanda yazo barka sai ya tofa albarkacin bakinshi ammah abinda zai baki mamaki tunda nahaihu Ahmad bai lek'o gidan ba sai ana saura kwana biyu suna yazo shima saida yayanshi yayi magana ko dayazo ko kallon babyn baiyiba yana dai zaune yana dannar wayarshi nice dai rik'e da babyna daga k'arshe yatashi yatafi tunda nayi haihuwar nan ko tsinke Ahmad bai badaba yace asa ma jaririya komai iyayena sukayi min hatta ran suna abbanmu yasiyo abinda za'a dafa yatambayi Ahmad sunan da yakeso asa ma yarinyar yace atambayeni insa mata sunan da nakeso koda Abbah yazo yafad'a min banyi mamakiba dan haka nace asa mata Aisha ammah muna kiranki da Meerah saboda sunan ummah ne, abbah ganin har azuhur Ahmad bai aiko da ragon yanka ba dan haka dakanshi yaje yasiyo rago aka yanka maki, Yaya binta da Yaya Muhammad fad'a sukayi ta yi akan halin Ahmad har suna cewa bazan koma gidanshi ba, anyi taron suna anwatse ni da baby mun samu kyautuka sosai daga jama'a abincin suna ma saidai nasa a kula na aika almajirin gidanmu a shagon Ahmad yakai mashu saidai abinda ban saniba ko ya ci ko bai ci ba.

Hankalina ya kwanta sosai a zaman da nayi gidanmu dan har k'iba nayi saboda kulawar da nake samu komai ina daga kwance saidai ayi min ahaka har nagama wankana tun ana sa ran Ahmad zai zo yad'aukeni ammah bai zoba wasa-wasa har nak'ara wata d'aya gidanmu ganin lamarin Ahmad ba na wasa bane yasa Abbah yamaida ni d'akina dakanshi shima saida yayi ma su yaya muhammad tas dan cewa sukayi babu inda zan koma inzauna gida inyi renon d'iyata idan nayaye sai inmaida mashi abunshi tunda shi baida mutunci nidai jinsu kawai nake ammah har ga Allah ina son mijina kullum addu'a d'aya nake mashi Allah yashirya min shi.

koda nadawo gida ko kad'an bai nuna farin ciki da hakan ba shareni yayi yacigaba da gudanar da hidimunshi inda ni kuma nake kula da d'iyata sosai ko da sau d'aya bai ta6a d'aukar min ke ba duk kukan da zakiyi in ina wanka ko sallah har sai na gama sannan zan zo in d'aukeki har kikayi wayau Ahmad bai ta6a siya miki ko da abinda ba'aso ba saidai iyayena suke min komai mahaifina kullum cikin yi min nasiha yake yana bani hak'uri akan halin mijina koda daman nuna masu nake bana cikin wani damuwa.

saidai daga baya na samu Ahmad yana kula ni komai yadawo yana min na ji dad'in hakan sosai ko da ke baiwani damu da ke ba ammah sake min da yake ina jin dad'i ina rage kewa muna zama muyi hira muyi komai muka koma kamar yadda muke da ko da bazai kai zaman mu na farko dad'iba.

Haka zamanmu yacigaba da tafiya ina kula da mijina sosai ina mashi duk abinda zai faranta mashi rai saidai ko kad'an bana bari kira6i inda yake dan hantararki yake sosai abun har mamaki yakeban in naga yana wani abun sai kace ba jininsaba ahaka har kikayi wayau kema d'in kanki baki zuwa inda yake dan kinsan duka kike sha.

Kina da shekaru shidda a duniya na san bazaki iya tuna lokacin ba Allah yayi ma mahaifinmu rasuwa hankalinmu ba k'aramin tashi yayi ba gabad'ayanmu mun shiga damuwa sosai musamman ma ni da gabad'aya shine yake taimaka ma d'iyata munyi kuka sosai kafin daga baya muka hak'ura muka dangana, kula da ke yadawo hannun ummana itama tana bakin k'ok'arinta dan tunda akasaki makaranta su suke kula da karatunki bayan rasuwar Abbana takoma ita kad'ai take d'aukar nauyinki da 'yan kud'in da take samu wajen su yaya muhammad da yaya binta koda nima ina bakin k'ok'ari da 'yan abinda nake samu wajen Ahmad, kina da bakwai tara da wata biyu nasake samun wani cikin nan Ahmad yakoma min kamar yadda yayi min lokacin cikinki yayi-yayi inzubar da cikin ammah na k'i ke hatta duka na ci wajen Ahmad duk akan cikin da nasamu har k'auracre min yake dan sai yakwana biyu ban sa shi a idona ba ni kaina da naga abun ya yi yawa saida naji kamar inzubar da cikin saidai kuma idan nayi tunani da cikin da ubanshi sai infasa haka nacigaba da jure wulak'ancin Ahmad tare da taimakon ummana da take kwantar min da hankali ahaka har na haifo Safiya wacce taci sunan kakarmu nan ma kamar sunanki Ahmad baimin komai ba a haihuwar saidai ummana tayi min ragon suna ma saida tamatsa ma Yaya Muhammad sannan yasiyo yana ta wannan tsegumi dan dai saboda ummah kawai yayi dan shi daman ya tsani Ahmad sosai hatta yaya binta ma ta tsaneshi.

Ke intak'aice miki itama safiya haka tataso saidai abun baci abinda bazan ta6a mancewa da shi ba shine rasuwar ummana wanda ba ciwo ba komai daren da zata rasu saida tazo gidana tayi min nasiha sosai akan zaman aure takawo wasu 'yan kune da sark'ar azurfa tabani tace naku ne ke da safiya nan nayi mata godiya sosai ta dad'e gidana har gab da magrib sannan tatafi wannan shine ganin da nayi mata na k'arshe washe gari ba'a tashi da itaba akace ta rasu ba k'aramin tashin hankali nashigaba dan har asibiti saida nakwanta tsawon kwana biyu inda saidai wata mak'wabciyarmu maman nuri tarik'e min ku wajenta har aka sallamoni na shiga damuwa sosai tun daga lokacin komai yaca6e min ni nake hidimar komai nawa dan abincin da Ahmad yake kawo min daidai wanda zan dafa ma mutum biyu ne saidai in ina da kud'i ink'aro in babu kuma ni inhak'ura inbaku kuci nawa Yayan Ahmad shima yana d'an taimaka min bayan rasuwar ummana dan yana aiko min da turmin atamfa da 'yar dubu d'aya akai akai, ummah bata dad'e da rasuwa ba suka tashi daga garin nan shi da iyalansa saboda transfer d'in da yasamu ta aiki yakoma chan kaduna.

Koda aka raba mana gadonmu kud'ina dasu nasiyo mana kayan abinci na ajiye sauran kuma naba ma mak'wabciyata maman nuri sun dad'e da barin anguwar ita ta ajiye min inda namaida ke makarantar gwamnati dan bazan iya cigaba da biya maki kud'in makaranta ba saboda hidima ta min yawa.

Itama Safiya haka tataso 'cikin rashin gata kamarki nasan ma zaki iya tuna hakan dan da wayonki lokacin,,,,

Tunda nahaifi safiya Ahmad bai dawo min a yarda yake da ba saidai banda matsalar abinci koda daman plate d'aya ne shi nake ci inbaku duk da haka saidai ina shan tsegumi da fad'a akanku ahaka har nasamu cikin Sadeek nan ma ba k'aramin rikici mukayi da Ahmad ba har cewa yata6a yi intattara kayana inbar mashi gida ammah nak'i cin mutunci kala-kala yadinga min duk dan infita ammah nahak'ura daga ciki har yazo yadaina bani abincin da zan ci hatta sabulun wanka nadaina gani saidai insiya ma kaina duk lokacin da naji kamar inbar gidan da na tuno da nasihar iyayena sai inyi hak'uri inzauna ahaka nahaifi sadeek da yaci sunan mahaifina ragon suna saidai kujerun d'akina nasiyar nayanka mashi nasiyo sauran kayan amfaninshi  da nawa nasiya maku kayan sawa ku ma, nasan kin san haka.

Duk wannan abun da ake su yaya muhammad suna nan dan babu wanda yalek'o min suna dan yaya binta turmin atamfa da dubu biyu ta aiko min tace ace bazata samu damar zuwa ba, yaya muhammad ko ban dai saniba ko yana garin ko baya nan saidai matarshi daman na san bazata zo ba saboda ita dama ba mutunci tacika ba dan batason dangin mijinta.

Daga k'arshe sai gani nayi Ahmad ya koma karatu shima ba shi yafad'a min ba ganin yadda yake fita da littafai da takardu yasa nagane ko kuma in yana waya injiyo shi dan ban ma isa inje intunkareshi da maganar ba saboda gabad'aya ya daina shiga sabgata saidai in buk'atar kanshi ta tashi.
Haka nayita gwagwarmaya intashi nan infad'a chan saida nasiyar da gabad'aya kayan d'akina wajen yi maku hidima hatta azurfar da ummah tabaku saida nasiyar k'arshe ma saida nahad'a da wankau, na sha inje insamu Ahmad induk'a k'asa ina kuka ina rok'onshi akan yayi hak'uri yataimaka yajaku a jikinsa a matsayinku na jininsa ammah ya k'i hatta wasu daga cikin mak'ota saida suka fahimci baya k'aunarku suna kwatanta mashi ammah ya k'i yagyara halinshi ku kanku sheda ne kunsan da hakan har zuwa yau babu abinda yaragu daga cikin halayyarsa har gashi mun rabu rabuwa ta har abada ni da shi saidai ku 'ya'yansa kune akowane lokaci nake addu'a akan Allah yajuyo maku da hankalinsa yanemeku...
Toh Meerah kinji tarihin rayuwata a tak'aice dan innace zan warware maki komai sai mukwana nan bamu gamaba sannan labarin da nabaki akan mahaifinku ba wai ina nufin kitsaneshiba saidai ina nufin kicigaba da mashi addu'a yagane gaskiya in da rabo.


Meerah da tunda ummah tafara labarin kuka take d'ago kai tayi takalli ummantasu da itama idanunta suka cika da k'wallah tanaso tayi mata magana ammah ta kasa nan tahad'e kai da gwiwa tacigaba da rera kukanta ahankali.

Ummah ce tayi saurin goge hawayen da suka gangaro mata a lokacin sannan tace Meerah kidaina kuka haka ba abun kuka bane nidai addu'a kawai nakeso kicigaba da yi min Allah yadafa min acikin lamurra na.

Rage sautin kukanta tayi tace insha Allahu ummah zamu cigaba da addu'a kamar yadda muka saba sannan a kowane hali ubangiji yana tare da mu ya san halin da muke ciki shi zai cigaba da taimaka mana insha Allahu sai mun mantar da ke rayuwar k'uncin da kikayi a baya sai munsakaki farin cikin da zaki dauwama a cikinshi.

Cikin jin dad'i tace Allah ya ida nufi Meerah nagode sosai akowane lokaci ina alfahari da ku 'ya'yana Allah ya albarkaci rayuwar ku, yanzu kinga dare ya yi sosai kikwanta kiyi bacci nima baccin zanyi yanzu.

Cikin sanyin jiki tace toh ummah nan taje takwanta, Umnah ma kashe fitilar d'akin tayi takwanta nan hawaye suka cigaba da wanke mata fuska akan tuna rayuwarta da tayi ta baya komai ji take ya dawo mata sabo saidai ba damar yin kuka saboda gudun tatada ma Meerah hankali, ita kanta Meerah hawayen kawai take cike da tausayin rayuwar ummansu da irin rayuwar da suka shiga a yanzu wadda basuda kowa sai Allah shi kad'ai zai dafa ma lamurransu..

Washe gari koda suka farka bayan sunyi sallar asuba gari ya fara haske Meerah tana shirin komawa bacci Ummah tace yanzu Meerah ya kike ganin ya dace muyi da sauran kud'in da suka rage min a hannu dan duka basufi dubu shidda ba.

Shuru Meerah tayi kamar me tunani sai chan tace Ummah sai inga kamar zaifi musiyo kayan abinci da abun zuba ruwa tunda su kad'ai ne bamuda su inyaso in kud'in sun rage sai musamu wata sana'a mufara yi.

Eh kin kawo shawara ammah wace irin sana'a yakamata muyi?

Toh ummah ko awara zamu fara soyawa.
girgiza kai tayi tace anya Meerah hakan zata yuwu, wa zai dinga kai min tallar awarar kinsan dai bazan amince ku kudinga tallaba.

Ummah ai ba talla zamuyi ba almajiri zamu samu yadinga kaiwa yana siyarwa kinga haka zaifi ko?

Toh Meerah zan dai duba muga jeki kikwanta in kin tashi dasafe munyi magana.

Toh tace nan tatashi taje takwanta kasancewar akwai bacci a idanunta nan da nan bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.

Ummah zaune tayi jugum tana ta tunanin lamarin nasu daga k'arshe itama takwanta nan a saman sallaya bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.



Wajen k'arfe takwas hayaniyar mutanen gidan ne da kukan yara suka tashesu safiya ce tatashi tana tsaki tace mutum yana bacci za'a dameshi da kuka yara saikace 'ya'yan karnuka.

Ke fa bakida mutunci wallahi kikiyayeni,,,,cewar ummah.

Tashi Safiya tayi tad'auko brush d'inta zata fita ummah tace salan inkin fita injiki kinyi rashin mutunci kiga yadda zanyi da ke a gidannan.

Ummah wallahi ba abinda zance masu.
Na dai fad'a maki.
Wucewa safiya tayi tana matsa makilin a brush d'inta ahankali tana cewa dan de ummah da yarannan sai sun gane kurenku.
Tana fita Suwaiba tasamu a tsakar gidan tana hura murhu batare da ta kalletaba tace ina kwana?

Suwaiba da tajuyo tana kallonta amsawa tayi tacigaba da abinda takeyi wajen maguji safiya taje tana brush tana bin gidan da kallo Sadeek ne yafito rik'e da nashi brush d'in tsayawa yayi yana kallon yaran Suwaiba da suke kuka safiya ce tace wai bazakazo kayi brush d'inba,  juyowa yayi yazo yatsugunna a wajenta shima yafara nashi brush d'in.

Meerah da take kwance ta juya baya tana jin duk abinda sukeyi ammah tashare dan itama baccin bai ishetaba  tana kwance su safiya suka shigo d"akin tana ji sadeek yana ce ma ummah yunwa yakeji.

Tashi Meerah tayi zaune tana cewa toh manya sarakan jin yunwa aifa kai da safiya bakuda aiki sai ci gaskiya ummah kina k'ok'ari
Murmushi ummah tayi tace toh ya na iya.
Kai Aunty Meerah yanzu mu mukeda ci?
"Uhm kema ai kin sani" cewar Meerah tare da tashi tad'auko brush d'inta tafito fuskarta d'auke da murmushi tagaishe da Suwaiba.

Amsa mata Suwaiba tayi tana me kallonta cikin ranta tace kai yarannan ba dai kyau ba, Meerah bata damu da kallon da takeyi mataba tawuce taje tafara brush d'inta tana cikin yi ummah tafito fuska a sake tagaishe da Suwaiba koda ta girmi suwaiba ammah ahaka ta amshe tare da tambayarta yara tace Alhamdulillah suna nan a tsaye mijin suwaiba yafito daga d'aki zai fita dan ma ummah Allah ya taimaketa da hijab a jikinta gaisheshi tayi ya amsa cikin sakin fuska yace sannunku da zuwa jiya da nadawo kunyi bacci bamu gaisaba.
Kan ummah a k'asa tace Allah sarki ai ba komai.
Toh Masha Allah, dafatan za'a daure ayi hak'urin zama da mu dan kinsan yanayin rayuwar yanzu sai da hak'uri musamman ma da gidan haya yahad'a mu.
Murmushi Ummah tayi tace insha Allahu.
Allah yak'ara rufa mana asiri .
"Ameen ya rabbi" Cewar ummah tare da juyawa tanufi d'akinta.
Yana zuwa wucewa Meerah ko gaisheshi batayiba yaficce dan ita takaicinta bata fito da hijab ba cikin sauri ta ida brush d'in tatashi tashige d'akinsu.

Ummah tasamu zaune rik'e da kud'i a hannunta ajiye brush d'in tayi tatafo gefen ummah tazauna tace ummah me za'a siyo?
Kud'in tamik'a mata tace abinda zamuyi karin kumalo kinga Sadeek ya dameni da yunwa yakeji ko zakije kitambayo  mutanen gidan kiji inda ake saida ko da koko da k'osai ne.
Kar6an kud'in tayi tace toh ummah nan tafita tana fita takalli Suwaiba da take d'aura ruwa tace dan Allah ko zaki taimaka kifad'a min inda ake siyar da koko?
'Dagowa suwaiba tayi tace eh babu nisa bari insa Musa yarakaki.
Toh Meerah tace nan suwaiba tashiga k'walah ma d'anta kira.
Wani yaro ne yafito daga cikin d'aki wanda bazaifi shekaru bakwai ba yace mamanmu gani.
Yauwa kuje karakata wajen siyar da koko da k'osai.
Toh yace yana kallon Meerah yana washe baki.
Saida tashiga d'aki tad'auko jug sannan tafito suka tafi, 'yar tafiya sukayi ba wata me yawaba a cikin wani lungu tahango me saida k'osan tana toyawa da wasu mutane ukku suna jira cema Musa tayi har koko tana siyarwa?
Eh akwai cikin gidanta wata tana yi.
Toh muje inbada kud'in k'osan sai mushiga ciki insiya.
Suna zuwa musa ne yace larai abada k'osai.
Kallon yaron tayi tace Musa rakiya kayi?
Eh larai bak'uwarmu ce ta gidanmu.
Toh bak'uwa kukayi ashe.
Meerah ce tace ina kwana?
Amsa mata tayi tare da cewa tubarkallah sannunki bak'uwa.
Yauwa tace fuska a sake tamik'a mata naira hamsin tace na hamsin za'a bada kar6a tayi tace toh bari insallami wad'annan sai inbaki.
Toh ba matsala.
Musa ne yace Mushiga kisiya kokon.
Har zasu wuce larai tace aikam koko ya k'are gaskiya saidai kuje chan gidan hassatu karakata.
Toh yace nan suka wuce gida na k'arshen lungun sunyi sa'a lokacin yana gab da k'arewa nan tamik'a naira talatin aka cika mata jug sannan suka fito suka komawa me k'osai saida tasallami wad'anchan sannan tazuba ma Meerah, naira goma Meerah tamik'a mata tace abata wani na goman aka zuba a farar leda suka tafo gida.
Suna shiga k'osan naira goma tabashi tace jeka kaci kaima.

Safiya da take kwance tashi tayi tace wallahi Aunty Meerah yunwa ba dad'i.
Hmm toh ai gashinan sai kuzo kusha, naira goma tamik'a ma ummah tace ga canjin 
Ummah ce tace kai Meerah maimakon asiyo na duka.
Murmushi tayi tace wannan ma ya ishemu ummah kinga wanda yarakani ma saida nasiya mashi na naira goma.
Haka yakamata daman kin kyauta yanzu kuyi kalacin kizo kitafi kasuwar.
Toh tace nan safiya duk tazuba masu tamik'a masu, k'osan naira goma goma suka d'auka suka mik'a ma ummansu na ishirin tace bazata ci hakaba ya yi mata yawa saidai tad'iba sauran tabasu Meerah tace nata ya isheta saidai su safiya da sadeek suka k'ara.

Suna gama kalacin Meerah tace ma Safiya tatashi suje sud'ebo ruwa, bokitinsu na wanka da suka zo da shi ciki suka juye sauran ruwan jarka suka d'auki jarka ummah ce tace a'a Meerah kudai samo almajiri yaje yad'ebo abiyashi dan gaskiya bazan dinga bari kuna zuwa d'ebo ruwaba a matsayinku na 'yan mata.
Murmushi Meerah tayi tace Allah ummah zamu iya kinga fa ba nisa.
Nidai ban yardaba safiya taje tasamo almajirin kekuma kije kiyi wanka da wanchan ruwan kizo kitafi kasuwar.
Toh tace nan tacire kayanta tad'aura zani tad'auko 'yar k'aramar hijab tasaka tana cewa yanzu nan ba daman mutum yafita sai ya sa hijab har wanka.
Toh ya muka iya ai daman haka zaman haya yake Meerah.
lallai kam Allah dai yarufa mana asiri.

Tana fita daga d'akinsu saida suka gaisa da Mariya da tafito itama daga d'akinta sannan tad'ibi ruwa a buta taje tad'auki 'yar k'aramar tsintsiyar da tagani a k'ofan bayi tawanke kewayen koda ba wani datti yayiba dan siminti ne ammah saida tawanke shi tas sannan tafito tad'auki ruwan tashige wanka.

Ummah fitowa tayi itama suka gaisa da Mariya sannan takoma d'aki.

Meerah tana fitowa daga wanka basilin kawai tashafa ma jikinta tamurza powder tasaka kaya hijab har k'asa tazumbula tazo tace ma ummah ta gama.

Toh ummah sai na dawo.

Toh sadeek.

Tana fita me adaidaita sahu tasamu yakaita kasuwa tabiyashi, saida tafara siya masu bokin babba med'aukar ruwa jarka biyu sannan tasiya masu jarkar d'aga nan wajen me kayan tabar mashi ajiya tawuce tasiyo masu tiyar shinkafa, fulawa tiya sai gero shima tiya tahad'o da wake rabin tiya na ci sai na suya tiya d'aya daga nan taje tasiyo masu mai gwangwani biyar tasiyo kayan miya na d'ari biyu tana gamawa taje tad'auko bokitanta tasamu me adaidaita yamaidota gida ummah ce tayi mata sannu da dawowa saida tazauna sannan tace kinga ummah na mance da tsarabar Sadeek wai ma suna ina?

K'ara wayar tayi alamun shigowar Message, tana dubawa taga number d'in da suka gama wayane nan tabud'e mesagge d'in _"kishirya gobe kina da babban bak'o dan zan zo har gida inganki ko na samu sauk'in rad'ad'in sonki da yake addabar zuciyata, ina maki fatan alkhairi abun k'aunata"_

Tana gama karantawa tsaki taja tare da wurgi da wayar tana cewa naga ta inda zaka ga gidan namu ba sai in kasan gidanba zakazo, mik'ewa tayi taje takuskure bakinta tazo tatada sallar isha'i da taji ana kira.

*BAYAN WATA BIYU*

Lokacin bikkin Sa'adatu baifi saura kwana ukku ba ummah kusan kullum idan taje gidan bata dawowa sai dare duk yadda taso sudinga tafiya da su Meerah k'iyawa sukayi suka dinga fakewa da islamiyya kan dole tahak'ura tabarsu tana zuwa ita kad'ai tana taimakawa da wasu ayyukan gidan, duk partyn da akayi su Meerah basuje ko d'ayaba ummah saida tak'ok'arta tayi masu anko d'in yinin bikki ita da yaranta.

Ranar asabar da yakama ranar yinin bikkin ne su Meerah saida sukaga ran ummah yana nema ya6aci sannan suka amince sukaje bikkin d'aki d'aya suka samu suka zauna dan ganin yadda yaran hajiya binta suke masu gani-gani ko kulasu basuyi saidai suyita nan nan da abokansu, hatta abinci saidai ummah takawo masu a d'akin daga k'arshe Meerah jan safiya tayi sukaje gidansu wata k'awarta suka zauna har sai gab da magrib sannan suka dawo gidan, suna yin sallah suka matsa ma ummansu akan sutafi gida, ita kanta ummah hak'ura tayi ta amince suka tafin saboda yadda taga komai yaya binta saidai tace Hajiya ramatu matar yaya muhammad, ita abinci ne kawai aka sakar mata tadinga raba ma mutane, bata ma bin ta kanta itama hajiya binta hidima kawai take da abokanta, sallama ummah tayi masu suka koma gida.

Washe gari walima su Meerah islamiyya suka tafi dan cewa sukayi bazasuje ba ayita masu gani gani bare ma ita sa'adatu da ta auri me kud'i sai d'age ma mutane kai take, sharesu ummah tayi saidai taje ita kad'ai.


Bayan kwana ukku da yin bikkin hajiya binta takira ummah a waya tace taturo Meerah ta amsar mata sak'o dan yaranta sunce bazasu iya gane gidanba.
Meerah haushi taji tace wallahi ummah batason dai suzo ne ammah ai gidan ba zaiyi wahalar ganewaba, su kullum saidai aje inda suke basu zuwa inda muke.
Ummah ganin yadda taketa surutai yasa tace Meerah ko bazakijeba ni inshirya intafi?
Jin haka yasa tace kiyi hak'uri zan je.
Haka tashirya ummah tabata kud'in abun hawa taje gidan bata wani zauna ba takar6i sak'on tatafo gida, Me adaidaita na safketa motar Ammar na tsayawa a k'ofan gidan ganin tana shirin shigewa yasa yayi saurin k'arasowa wajen.
Batare da ta kalleshiba tatsaya fuskarta d'auke da murmushi tace ashe kaine?
Shima murmushin yayi yace ai kinsan ninen kawai dai kin share saboda so kike kishige gida.
Kallonshi tayi tace a'a daman tsayawa zanyi.
Shima kallonta yake yace ban yardaba ai nasan halinki guduna kike na rasa dalili kusan watanmu nawa muna tare ammah kink'i kisake kokuma ita kanta ummah rowarta akeyi min ank'i barina ingaisa da ita.
Shuru tayi tana murmushi, ganin batada niyar magana yasa yace shine kikaje anguwa baki kirani narakaki ba?
Hanyar shiga gidan tanufa tana cewa a'a ai ba wani nesa najeba bin bayanta yayi suna shiga zauren tatsaya tace inda naje babu nisa ummah ce ta aikeni.
"Duk da haka da kin kirani munje tare".
Toh kabari sai wani lokacin zan kiraka muje.
"Hmm ban yarda ba nasan kin fad'a ne kawai 'yanmata"
Ko?
'Daga gira yayi yace kema kinsan haka, yanzu bari intafi inyaso gobe sai inzo muyi hira tunda ba'a islamiyya ko?
Hmm toh Allah yakaimu.
Ameen ammah dai kin tabbata zaki fito ko?
Eh man me zai hana.
Matsowa yayi kusa da ita yana me kallonta yace ban yarda ba sai kin min alk'awali dan nasan ina iya zuwa kik'i fitowa.
Ganin ba zai barta tashiga ba yasa tace dagaske zan fito kakirani a waya idan ka zo sai infito.
Kin tabbata?
'Daga mashi kai tayi alamun Eh.
Murmushi yayi yace toh na yarda da maganarki bari intafi sai goben, agaishen da su ummah.
Toh zasuji,,,yana tafiya tajuya tashige gida tana cewa mutum baida aiki sai damu.

Tana shiga d'akinsu taba ummah leda viva d'in da akabata takawo mata.
Ummah na bud'ewa shinkafa ce tiya d'aya sai turmin atamfa da su cincin
Cikin jin dad'i ummah tace aikam mungode sosai.
Safiya ce tata6e baki tace ummah atamfar roba ce suka baki nasan batafi 1,500 ba kud'inta yanzu dan rashin mutunci duk hidimar da kikayi masu ga bikkin abinda suka baki kenan.
Takalma ummah tad'auko tajefeta dasu tace tashi kiban waje ko ranki ya6aci yanzun nan.
Cikin sauri Safiya tatashi tafita daga d'akin kamar zatayi kuka.
Cikin fad'a ummah tace kibari kikaini k'arshe kiga yadda zanyi da ke cikin gidan nan.
Meerah ko dariya take k'asa-k'asa.
Turmin atamfar ummah tamik'a mata tace taje takai ad'inka masu gown ita da safiya.
K'in kar6a Meerah tayi tace a'a ummah ke yakamata kid'inka atamfarki.
Kikar6a man ku ne 'yan mata ku yafi dacewa kusa ba ni ba dan ni yanzu duk abinda zan samu ku kad'ai nake tunani.
Cike da tausayin ummansu tace munsan da haka ummah ai kina mana k'ok'ari Allah yak'ara rufa mana asiri, Ammah kid'inka atamfar nan kisa dan ni ko na d'inka bazata burgeniba indai ba'a jikinki nagantaba.
Murmushi ummah tayi tace nasan da haka Meerah yanzu dai kikar6a kud'inka kaina ciwo yake banason hayaniya.
Kar6a Meerah tayi tace toh ummah mungode Allah yak'ara bud'i.
Ameen Meerah.
Ashe har yanzu ciwon kan yana nan ummah.
Kwanciya ummah tayi sannan tace eh Meerah tun safe nake fama da shi har yanzu.
Cike da damuwa kamar zatayi kuka tace Allah sarki ummah Allah dai yasauk'e yabaki lafiya.
Ameen ya rabbi.

Tana shiga gidan yaran suwaiba ne kawai a tsakar gida lokacin suwaiba na cikin bayi, cikin sauri taje tabud'e d'akinsu tanufi ma'ajiyar kud'insu dukkansu dubu takwas suka rage takwaso tafito takoma  asibitin.

Toh sukace nan tawuce tafita.

"Muna Federal Medical Centre"
Kunji tsiyar daman chan da gayya kuka kaita chan bayan kunsan babu abinda suke sai cin kud'i ga ta gwamnati, ke kuje kud'aukota kumaidota gida gobe dakaina zanzo inkaita asibiti idan abun ba zai yiwuba sai muhad'a da na Islamic ammah wannan kud'in sunyi yawa duk salon cin kud'ine dan ni yanzu inba dubu biyu ba banda ko naira dan duk hidimar bikki ta cinye min kud'i ga hidimar yara kai abubuwan dai saidai addu'a, ganin yadda Meerah tatsareta da idanu yasa tace kuje kud'aukota kumaidota gida asamu paracetamol abata kafin gobe idan daddyn yara ya dawo zan shigo gidan.
Wasu sabbin hawaye ne suka gangaro daga idanunta tace Aunty binta wannan fa matsalar ta shafi zuciyarta indai ba'ayi mata aikiba komai zai iya faruwa da ita.
A fusace tace kefa kin cika gardama in aikin za'ayi ina zamu samu wad'annan mak'udan kud'in na ce maki kuje kumaidota gida ni zanzo inkaita wata asibitin da zasu d'aurata kan magani kawai batare da anyi aikiba insha Allahu zata samu sauk'i.

Mik'ewa Meerah tayi batare da tayi maganaba tajuya tana tafiya dakyar tana ji Aunty binta tana cewa kijira ind'auko maki kud'in abun hawa ammah bata tsaya bin ta kantaba taficce tabar gidan tana kuka tasamu adaidaita tahau tanufi gidan yaya muhammad tana isa tayi sa'a motarshi tana nan dan haka tashiga gidan tana ta sallama ammah ba wanda yakulata dan haka itama bata tsaya yi masu maganaba tanufi hanyar d'akinshi da tagani a bud'e, daga bakin k'ofa tatsaya tayi sallama.

Cike da farin ciki tagoge hawayen fuskarta tafita cikin sauri.

Zagaye wajen tashiga yi cike da damuwa tace mafita d'aya ce kawai gareni, hawaye ne suka gangaro mata tare da waigawa takalli ummah da take kwasar baccinta tace saidai ina jin tsoron haka ummana nafi kowa buk'atar ganin kin tashi daga wannan ciwon saidai bansan yadda komai zai kasanceba.
Ta dad'e a tsaye tana sak'e sak'e a ranta har su safiya suka dawo su dukansu suna murmushi itama murmushin tayi tace Har kun dawo?
Bud'e ledar suka shiga yi suna cewa Aunty meerah dankaline da kwai muka siyo.
Ganin yadda suke murna yasa tace toh kuzauna kuci kuyi sauri dan sai kunci sannan zan fita.
Takeaway d'aya safiya tad'auko tamik'a mata tace kema ga naki Aunty meerah.
Kallonta tayi batare da ta kar6aba tace au wai har da ni kika siyo mawa?
Eh Aunty meerah duk jiya fa bakici komai ba gaskiya indai bazaki ciba nima bazan ciba, shima Sadeek ture nashi takeaway d'in yayi yace niba bazan ciba, ganin duk sun k'i ci yasa takar6i takeaway d'in tace toh shikenan zan ci ku ma kuci, saida tabud'e tafara ci sannan suma suka fara ci turawa kawai takeyi ba dan dad'iba sai dan kawai su safiya suci.

Saida taci ba laifi sannan ta ajiye takoma gefen ummah tazauna tana kallonta cike da tausayin mahaifiyartasu chan sai tamik'e tace Safiya bari inje indawo kukula da kanku.
Safiya ce tace toh Aunty Meerah kud'in zaki samo?
Murmushi Meerah tayi tace eh safiya sai na dawo.
Fitowa tayi tasamu waje tazauna damuwa ce k'arara tabayyana a fuskarta lokaci guda tafara fitar da hawaye tunaninta d'aya ina zata samu kud'in da za'ayi ma ummah aiki saidai hanya d'aya ce tasan tanan zata iya samun kud'in, fashewa tayi da kuka tace taya hakan zata kasance meyasa Ammar zai 6ullo min ta wannan hanyar, dafe kanta tayi tacigaba da kukanta chan tad'auko wayar talalubo number d'in Ammar tana shiga bugu biyu yad'auka tare da cewa baby ya dai? Kin amince ne?
Meerah cikin sheshek'ar kuka tace Ammar ina jin tsoro bazan iyaba.
Ammar da yake kwance tashi yayi zaune yace haaba Meerah menene abun tsoro da fa kinzo ba wani dad'ewa zakiyiba zan baki kud'in kema fa zakiso mahaifiyarki tasamu sauk'i.
Tana me cigaba da kuka tace shikenan Ammar ina zan sameka?
Cike da farin ciki yace yanzu zan turo maki address d'in hotel d'in da zaki sameni.
Kashe wayar tayi batare da tayi magana ba tabuga tagumi cikin ranta tana cewa tabbas zan iya yin komai domin samun lafiyar ummana bazanso murasata, ba'ayi minti biyu ba sai ga message d'in ya shigo gabanta wani irin fad'uwa yayi dakyar tasamu tamik'e me napep tasamu tafad'a mashi inda zai kaita.
Tunda tashiga napep d'in inba fad'uwar gaba ba ba'abinta takeyi sai hawaye da take ta fitarwa daga idanunta bata san lokacin da suka isaba saida me napep d'in yace hajiya mun kawo sannan talura, kud'inshi tad'auko tabiyashi sannan tafito tsaye tayi gaban otel d'in tana binshi da kallo takasa takawa tashiga saida Ammar yakirata a waya yace baby wai har yanzu baki isoba.
Muryarta tana rawa tace na iso Ammar bansan inda zanyi ba.
Ohk kibari infito intafo da ke kokuma kishigo ciki kitambayi room 4 sai agwada maki.
Kashe wayar tayi sannan tataka ahankali tashiga tatambaya aka gwada mata saida tahau bene sannan taga number d'in d'akin, tsaye tayi a k'ofar d'akin takasa yin Knocking jikinta gabad'aya rawa yakeyi, Ammar daga chan ciki jin shurun ya yi yawa yasa yataso dan yaje yatafo da ita yana bud'e k'ofar yaganta tsaye ta jingine kanta da bango sai hawaye takeyi tsaye yayi yana kallonta yace baby daman kin iso shine baki shigoba? 
Kamar jira takeyi yayi magana nan tafashe da kuka.
rik'o hannunta yayi yace please kishigo ciki kar mutane suga kina kuka sud'auka wani abu akayi maki.
Bata musaba tabi bayanshi suka shiga saman gado yazaunar da ita sannan yakoma yarufe k'ofar, Meerah tana ganin yadawo nan tahad'e kai da gwiwa tashiga rusa kuka, daga gefenta yazauna yazuba mata ido ahankali yace Meerah meyasa kike kuka?
Cikin kuka tace bazan iyaba Ammar kabud'e min kawai intafi.
Murmushi yayi yace toh umman takifa kin hak'ura da lafiyarta? Ko kin mance Doctor ya ce kar awuce gobe ba'ayi mata aikin ba?
Mik'ewa tayi tsaye tana mecigaba da kuka tace nasan ummah bazataso in6ata rayuwataba Ammar.
Janyota yayi sai jinta tayi ta fad'o samanshi yarungumeta yace Meerah ba wani dad'ewa zanyi ba just 5 minutes ma ya isa kinga kawai sai inbaki kud'in yanzu kitafi da su inyaso ma yau sai ayi komai agama.
Shuru tayi tana me cigaba da kuka gabad'aya jikinta rawa yake tana jin lokacin da Ammar yarabata da hijab d'inta, tureshi tashigayi ammah takasa nan yakwantar da ita a saman gadon ganin yana shirin rabata da rigarta yasa tace dan Allah Ammar kakyaleni wallahi bazan iyaba na fasa, ko kallonta baiyiba bare yasaurari abinda take cewa yana rabata da rigar wata irin k'ara tasaki tace Ammar na rok'ek'a dan Allah kabarni.
Shareta yayi yashiga wasanni da jikinta inba kukaba ba abinda takeyi.
Hankalinta bai ida tashiba saida Ammar yazo zai shigeta da dukkan k'arfinta tashiga tureshi ganin zata 6ata mashi lokaci yasa yasakar mata nauyinshi.
wata irin k'ara tayi lokaci guda wasu irin hawaye masu d'umi suka gangaro mata saboda wani irin zafi da taji ya ziyarceta gabad'aya tarasa a wace duniya take, jikinta mutuwa yayi takasa koda motsa hannunta nishi sama-sama tafara ammah ko kad'an Ammar bai tausaya mataba baima san tana yiba kokad'an baisa tausayi ba saida nasamu nutsuwa sannan yabarta kwance tayi shame-shame idanuwanta a rufe tana me cigaba da zubar da hawaye.
Ammar shi kanshi kwanciyar yayi fuskarshi d'auke da murmushi yazuba mata idanu yana kallonta hannu yakai yana shafa fuskarta yace baby kidaina kukan hakanan.
janye fuskarta tayi gefe guda tare da tashi zaune dakyar tana jin zazza6i yana neman yarufeta samu tayi tajanyo kayanta tasaka, Ammar yace bazakiyi wankaba sannan kitafi?.
Shuru tayi mashi tajanyo hijab d'inta tasaka sannan tasamu tamik'e dakyar, ido yazuba mata yace kijira inshirya sai inmaidake.
Girgiza kai tayi tace bana buk'ata bani kud'in.
Bai musaba yad'auko kud'in yamik'a mata yace ga wannan dubu d'ari da hamsin ne.
Kar6a tayi tare da juyawa zata tafi wani irin zafi da tajine yasa tatsaya tacije le6e.
Ammar ne yace baby bazaki iya tafiya ba kibari inkaiki.
Batare da tajuyoba tace Ammar kazo kabud'e min k'ofa bana buk'atar kakaini idan har nace zan k'ara kallonka koda na second guda ne tsanarka zata yawaitu a cikin zuciyata.
Mik'ewa Ammar yayi yaje yabud'e mata k'ofar tana fara takawa nan tayi baya zata fad'i cikin sauri yarik'ota yace Meerah bazaki iyaba.
Tureshi tayi tadafa bango tana tafiya dakyar ahaka tafita.
tsaye yayi yana kallonta ba dan ya soba tatafi dan shi so yayi tatsaya ko ya samu d'an yak'ara, ahankali yace meerah kin cika taurin kai ammah ba yadda zanyi dole inbiki a yadda kikeso tunda najiki irin yadda nakeso, rufe d'akin yayi yaje yashiga wanka yana jin wani irin nishad'i a ranshi.

Tana bin bango ahaka tafita daga otel d'in tana ganin me napep ya ajiye wasu tatsaidashi tafad'a mashi inda zai kaita tana shiga tahad'e kai da gwiwa tashiga rusa kuka a ranta tana da tasani akan amince ma Ammar da tayi yarabata da budurcinta kuka take sosai kamar wadda aka aiko ma da sak'on mutuwa me napep d'inne da yaji kukan ya tsananta yasa yace Malama lafiya kike kuka ko rasuwa akayi maki?
Shuru Meerah tayi tacigaba da kukanta, k'ara maimaita mata tambayar yayi still dai bata tankashiba dan haka yaja bakinshi yayi shuru har yakaita asibitin yace mun iso safka tayi tabashi kud'inshi duk yadda taso tatsaida kukanta kasawa tayi dan haka tasamu waje tazauna taciga da kukanta saida tasamu daga baya dakyar kukan yatsaya ruwa tasamu tawanke fuskarta sannan tamik'e dak'yar tana tafiya tana k'yangashi tare da cizon le6e ahaka ta isa office d'in Doctor tana zuwa bud'ewa tayi tashiga babu ko sallama lokacin yana waya kujera kawai yanuna mata ammah bata zauna ba tsaye tayi wajen table d'in har yagama wayar.
Kud'in gabad'aya ta ajiye mashi tare da bud'e baki dak'yar tace ga kud'in nan na kawo doctor.
'Daukar kud'in yayi yafara k'irgawa itadai sai sharar k'wallah takeyi, 10 thousand yaciro yace ga cikon kud'inki ai dubu d'ari da arba'in ne nan kuma kin bada da hamsin.
Batare da tayi maganaba takar6a.
Yace Yanzu inkun amince sai ashiga da ita theatre room da k'arfe hud'u tunda ba'abinda taci tun jiya.
Muryarta tana rawa tace indai hakan zai yuwu muma zamufison hakan.

Ohk ba matsala nan yad'auko constant foam yanuna mata yadda zata cike sannan yace toh kuje kubita da addu'a nan da awa ukku za'azo ashiga da ita.
Toh tace sannan tajuya dakyar tafita takoma ward tun kan ta ida isa su safiya suka hangota dagudu sukayo wajenta cikin sauri tace sadeek kar kafad'o min.
Suna isowa duk tsayawa sukayi safiya tace Aunty Meerah ansamo kud'in?
'Daga mata kai tayi tace eh safiya yanzu haka nan da awa ukku za'a shiga da ita theater room d'in.
Cike da farin ciki tace naji dad'i sosai Aunty Meerah insha Allahu ummanmu zata tashi, ammah ya naga kina tafiya dak'yar ko fad'uwa kikayi?
Murmushin k'arfin hali tayi tace eh bugewa nayi.
Shine Aunty Meerah kikayi kuka ni bana kuka idan nabuge,,,cewar Sadeek.
Murmushi kawai tayi taja hannunshi tana takawa cikin dauriya suka shiga tsaye tayi wajen da ummah take kwance tana kallonta sai jin wasu sabbin hawaye tayi suna tsiyaya matsawa tayi daf da ita ahankali tace ummana kiyi hak'uri nasan ban kyauta makiba indai kikaji abinda na aikata ba zaki ta6a yafe min ba saidai babu yadda zanyi hakan shine mafita a garemu nan tafashe da wani sabon kukan, Safiya ce tamatso tarik'eta cike da tausayinta tace Aunty meerah kidaina kuka insha Allahu ummah zata warke.
Murmushi tayi tare da zame jikinta wajen kayanta tanufa tad'auko wata doguwar riga sannan tawuce tashiga toilet nan ma saida tasha kuka sannan tahad'a ruwa masu d'umi tagasa jikinta tayi wankar tsarki sannan tad'auro alwallah saida tasa kayanta sannan tafito.

Ganin su safiya sunyi shuru yasa tad'auki kud'i tabasu tace suje susiyo abinci suci nan suka kar6a suka fita, sallaya tashimfid'a tatayar da sallar azuhur cikin sallar inba kukaba babu abinda take nadama da tsoron abinda ta aikata duk suka taru sukayi mata yawa idan taduk'a sujjada sai ta dad'e tana neman gafarar ubangiji sannan tad'ago ahaka har tagama sallar lokacin tuni su safiya sun dawo suna cin abincinsu, cigaba tayi da zubar da hawaye tana tasbihi har lokacin sallar la'asar yayi tana jin nurse tashigo tana tambayarsu ba'a dai bata komai ba safiya tace ai bata ma farkaba tunda tayi bacci fita nurse d'in tayi, Meerah tatashi tagabatar da la'asar nan ma ta dad'e tana addu'a tana neman gafara tare da neman ma mahaifiyartasu lafiya, tana a zaune wasu nurse su ukku sukazo tafiya da ita kasa d'agowa tayi takallesu saida tahad'e kai da gwiwa tashiga yin kuka ahankali kukan da tarasa na farin cikine ko na bak'in ciki, Sadeek ne yafara kuka yana cewa ina zaku tafi da ummanmu, safiya rungumoshi tayi jikinta tace yanzu zasu dawo da ita.
Meerah cikin sauri tatashi tafita waje daga saman d'an dakali tazauna tacigaba da kukanta tana kallo aka wuce da ummansu theater room cikin sheshek'ar kuka tace na cuci kaina meyasa nabiye ma son zuciya na aikata abinda bai dace da niba, na tabbata ummah bazaki ta6a yafe min ba idan kikaji hakan, bansan da wane ido zaki kalleniba, dafe kanta tayi da hannuwa biyu tace kaicona ni Meerah, cigaba tayi da kuka har da sheshek'a batasan lokacin da Sadeek yazoba saidai ji tayi ya rungumeta ta baya yana cewa Aunty Meerah kije kice sumaido mana ummanmu sun tafi da ita.
rungumoshi tayi jikinta tana me cigaba da kuka itama.

Mik'ewa sukayi suka koma suka samu Sadeek har ya yi bacci safiya ce tatasheshi tagyara ma ummah gadonta suna nan zaune wata nurse tashigo tace ancanza masu ward zasu koma yanzu a chan za'a wuce da mahaifiyarsu toh sukace nan suka tattara kayansu sukabi bayan wata attender tarakasu ward d'in duka mutum ukku ne kwance duk wad'anda akayi ma theater ne sai masu jinyarsu sunji dad'in wajen sosai koda chan ma inda suka baro babu mutane sosai nan suka samu waje suka ajiye kayansu suka gyara ma ummah gadon da zata kwanta suna nan zaune wajen k'arfe shidda aka turo ummansu aka shigo da ita lullu6e take batada banbanci da matacce ahaka aka kwantar da ita, su duka ido suka zuba mata suna kallonta cike da tausayi ganin yadda duk takoma.

Bayan sallar magrib meerah tad'auko wayarta da tunda tadawo daga wajen Ammar ta ajiye tana dubawa kusan 15 missed call d'inshi tagani sai na Suwaiba biyu number d'in suwaiba tayi dialing tana d'auka nan suka gaisa suwaiba tatambayeta jikin ummansu tace dasauk'i anma yi mata aiki.
Cike da tausayi tace Allah sarki, Allah dai yabata lafiya insha Allahu yanzu zamuzo da Babansu musa ya dawo.
Toh tace nan sukayi sallama takashe wayar.

Wajen isha'i sukazo asibitin suwaiba da mijinta suka duba ummah nan suka kawo masu lemu da ayaba da zasu tafi mijinta yabasu d'ari biyar nan sukayi mashi godiya.


Wasa-wasa ummah saida tayi kwana biyu tana bacci sannan tafarka saidai azo ayi mata dreesing batasan inda kanta yakesa lokacin da tafarka sosai suka nuna farin cikinsu nan sukayi ta mata sannu saidai tabisu da ido tad'an d'aga kai cike da tausayin yarannata musamman ma Meerah da gabad'aya tafita hayyacinta saikace itace me jinyar.

Bayan awa d'aya meerah dakanta taje tasamo ma ummah tea wajen masu shayi takawo mata saida yad'an huce sannan takira nurse sukazo suka tataimaka mata tad'an jingine jikinta a gado sannan suka bata tad'an sha dakyar tasha rabin cup d'in sannan aka maidata aka kwantar.

Yau Meerah tun da safe da tatashi tattare kayansu tayi taje tawanke lokacin kowa yana bacci lokacin da tadawo ummah ta farka ganinta yasa tamatso cikin sauri tace sannu ummana ya jikin naki?

Ahankali tace toh Doctor.

Saida yayi mata allura sannan yatafi bayan tafiyarshi meerah rik'o hannun ummansu tayi da aka cire ma kanula yakumbura tashiga matsa mata ahankali kallonta umman take sai chan tace yanzu meerah ku kukatare a asibitin su Sadeek basu zuwa makaranta ai da Yaya binta kika kiramin ita tazo tazauna dan zaman asibiti baida dad'i duk sai ku takura.

Ummah ce tace kikayi shuru meerah tambayarki nake.

Murmushi tayi tace hakane Meerah ba wai na raina bane nagode sosai Allah yayi maku albarka.
Ameen ummana.


Bayan kwana biyu Meerah tana zaune a gefen ummah ta zuba ma waje d'aya ido tana kallo da kaganta kasan tunani take ummah da farka warta daga bacci kenan tace Meerah tunanin me kike haka?

Shuru ummah tayi sai chan tace Meerah wai ina muka samu kud'in da ake amfani da su sannan kud'in da akayi min aiki ina aka samo su?
Gaban Meerah saida yafad'i nan tayi kalar rashin gaskiya dakyar tasamu tasaita nutsuwarta sannan tace Ummah kud'in da muka tara ne nakwaso sai kuma nanemi taimakon wasu sannan Baba Muhammad shima ya taimaka mana tunda kud'in da akayi maki aikin ba wasu masu yawa bane aka kar6a.
Ajiyar zuciya ummah tasafke sannan tace naji dad'i da jin haka Meerah dan Allah kiyi k'ok'arin tsaya ma gaskiyarki akowane lokaci ubangiji shi zaici gaba da rufa mana asiri.
Dakyar tahad'iye yawu dan da ace batasan halin ummansu bane da nasiha da tace ta gane abinda tayi, muryar ummah taji ta ce toh ammah kud'in gado fa muna da su?
Eh ummah akwai sauran kud'i hannuna dasu muke cin abinci yanzu ma nakeso inje inbiya kud'in kwanakin da mukayi saboda kar sai anyi discharge d'inmu muga kud'i dayawa.
Toh shikenan Meerah hakan dai zaifi, har yanzu dai Yaya binta batazo ba ko?
Girgiza kai Meerah tayi tace bata zoba yanzu bari inje indawo ummah nasan yanzu su safiya zasu dawo.
Toh shikenan sai kin dawo.
Mik'ewa tayi tad'auki jakkar kud'insu tafita taje tabiya kud'i ahanyarta ta dawowa kawai sai had'uwa tayi da Ammar tunda tahangoshi taso tacanza hanya ammah ina tuni ya ganta dan haka tad'aure fuska tacigaba da tafiyarta, wani irin farin ciki ne yaziyarci Ammar cikin sauri yabiyota yana cewa haba Meerah bama zaki tsaya mugaisa ba.
Ko tankashi batayiba tacigaba da tafiyarta.
Ganin haka yasa yayi saurin shan gabanta yace wai ke meyasa kike irin haka yakamata ace kin canza yanzu baidace ace kina gudunaba.
Gefe d'aya takalla tace kaga malam kabani hanya inwuce dan yanzu banda lokacinka karabu da ni.
Murmushi yayi yace nikuma ina da lokacinki Meerah dan agaskiya bana ji zan iya rabuwa da ke saida yad'an duk'o saitin fuskarta sannan yace musamman ma yanzu da nasan wacece ke Meerah.
Wasu irin hawaye masu zafi suka gangaro mata kallonshi tayi tace Ammar daman na sani jikina kakeso kuma ka samu a yanzu na tsaneka ganinka kawai k'ara min tsanarka yake acikin raina kuma koda kaga na biye maka munsa6a ma ubangiji dande babu yadda zanyi ne ammah har yau har gobe ban ta6a samun nutsuwa ba a raina bana iya baccin kirki akowane lokaci ina da nasani akan abinda na aikata wannan abun kunyane kaima indai ka san kunyar toh ya kamata ace ka ji ta.
Cikin rashin damuwa yace toh menene a ciki ina ce taimakon juna mukayi kuma nine zan aureki kinga ai ba abin damuwa bane dan mun kasance da juna.
Tsaki Meerah taja cike da takaici taratsa ta gefenshi tawuce saida tagoge idanunta sannan tashiga takoma wajen ummah koda tashiga k'in yadda tayi suhad'a ido gudun kar ummah tagano halin da take ciki nan tad'auki abincin da su safiya suka ajiye mata tafara ci ba dan dad'iba turawa kawai take a ranta tana mamakin halayyar Ammar

Kamar daga sama sai jin sallamarshi tayi cikin sauri tad'ago takalleshi ko inda take bai kallaba nan yashiga gaishe da ummah tare da yi mata ya jiki, nan ta amsa mashi su safiya ne suka fad'a mata kowanene shi dan dama bata sanshiba wajensu take jin labarin shi nan tasa suka bashi kujera yazauna.
Meerah ko saboda bak'in ciki batasan lokacin da taja tsakiba tace ina amfanin naci, ummah ce takalleta tace Meerah me kikece?
Fuskarta a d'aure tace ba magana nayiba ummah.
Toh bazaki gaishe da shi ba, wai meyasa kike irin haka
Ammar dai shuru yayi yana dannar wayarsa
saida tawurga mashi harara sannan murya chan k'asa tace ina wuni?
Murmushi yayi tare da d'agowa yakalleta yace lafiya lau, ya me jiki?
Gatanan da sauk'i,,,tafad'a agadarance sannan tamik'e.
Su dukansu kallonta sukayi ganin haka yasa tace ummah bari inje insamo ruwa masu d'an sanyi insha.
Ruwan sanyi kuma meerah toh abincin fa da kika ajiye?
Murmushi tayi tace Allah ummah na k'oshi nafison dai shan ruwan, gudun kar ummah tatsaidata yasa tayi gaba batare da ta jira jin abinda zataceba.
ammar yana gani ta fita nan shima yamik'e cike da ladabi yace ummah zan tafi Allah yak'ara sauk'i.
Kallonshi tayi tace da ka bari ta kawo maka ruwa ka sha.
Murmushi yayi yace bakomai nagode ummah Allah yabaki lafiya.
Cikin jin dad'i tace Ameen nagode.
Naira dubu ukku yazaro yamik'a ma sadeek yace abokina ga wannan ka sha chocolate.
A'a kaiko har da d'awainiya haka kabarshi kawai ya gode,,,cewar Ummah.
Sadeek mik'a mashi kud'in yayi yace a'a abokina ni banaso.
Murmushi Ammar yayi yace toh ajiye min wajenka ni inason chocolate d'in kasiya min, saka cikin aljihu.
Bai musaba yasaka yace yaushe zakazo kakar6a?
Shafa kanshi yayi yace saidai zuwa gobe, yanzu bari intafi sauri nakeyi.
Godiya su ummah sukayi mashi nan yatafi cikin sauri, yana ko fita daga chan gaba kad'an yahango Meerah tsaye tana ta saffa da marwa ganinshi yasa tayi saurin isowa inda yake cike da tashin hankali tace wai kai me kake nufi da nine da zaka dinga bibiyata?
ta6e baki yayi yace toh menene dan na bibiyeki kuma ma ai ba wajenki nazoba wajensu ummah nazo.
Wani irin kallo tayi mashi tace bama buk'atar zuwanka dan haka kar kakuskura kak'ara tako inda muke na fad'a maka na tsaneka Ammar kadaina bibiyar rayuwata kabarni inji da abinda yadameni.
Girgiza kai yayi yace bana tunanin zan iya haka Meerah dan a yanzu kece kad'ai macen da nake jin zan iya cigaba da kasancewa da ita ki amince min kawai nikuma nayi maki alk'awali zan dinga baki kud'i masu yawa.
Faahewa tayi da kuka cike da takaici tace wai kai Ammar me kamayar da ni ne? Anfad'a maka ni irin 'yan iskan matan nan ne, kaima ka sani ko a wanchan lokacin *K'ADDARA CE* kawai tasa na amince maka ammah da badan hakaba wallahi bazaka ta6a samun jikina.
 Haka dai kikace Meerah ammah ni nasan akowane lokaci ni me sa'a ne zan sameki ko ta wane hali ne.
Nuna mashi hanya tayi da hannunta cikin kuka tace Ammar kabar wajen nan please na rok'ek'a kabarni inji da illar dakayi ma rayuwata.
Murmushi yayi yace shikenan naji zan tafi ammah kisani zan dawo akowane lokaci da nake buk'ata.
Kar kasaki kadawo min nan dan bazaka ta6a samun abinda kakeso ba.
Batare da yayi magana ba yajuya yatafi.
Meerah duk'ewa tayi wajen tana me cigaba da kuka cike da tsanar Ammar.

Tana gama kukan zuwa tayi tasiyo masu ruwa saida tawanke fuskarta sannan takoma tunda tashiga ummah take kallonta har ta iso kanta sunkuye a k'asa ta ajiye ruwan hannunta tare da d'auko yoghurt d'in da tasiyo tace ummah ga wannan nasiyo maki kisha.
A'a Meerah hana jin yunwa.
'Dagowa tayi takalleta tare da marairaicewa kamar zatayi kuka tace dan Allah ummah kisha kinga fa tun safe bakici komai ba.
Zan sha Meerah ki ajiye min sai zuwa anjima, ammah kuka kikayi ko?
Girgiza kai tayi tace ba kuka nayiba ummah.
Ga idanunki nan ai sunyi ja, akan Ammar ne kike kuka ko?
Shuru meerah tayi nan ummah tacigaba da cewa a kowane lokaci shawara d'aya nake baki kicire komai a ranki Meerah ba fa dauwama zakiyi a gida ba saboda ke d'iya macece dole wata rana kiyi aure.
Goge hawayen da suka zubo mata tayi sannan tace insha Allahu ummah zan gyara indai nasamu wanda nakeso ammah Allah ummah banson Am.ar shi.
ummah da tazuba mata ido tace saboda me, miye illarshi ba mutumin kirki bane?
Girgiza kai Meerah tayi alamun a'a.
Toh kinga bakida hujjar da zakice baki sonshi kicigaba da addu'a Allah yaza6a maki mafi Alkhairi.
Ameen ummah.
'Dan cize le6e ummah tayi tare da dafe gefen k'irjinta 
Meerah ce tace sannu ummah kidaina yawan magana kinga doctor ya hana.
Shuru tayi tare da runtse idanunta matsowa Meerah tayi tagyara mata rufar da tayi sannan tazauna daga gefen gadon tana jera mata sannu.

*PAGE 18*

Washe gari bayan Doctor ya zo ya duba ummah allura kala biyu yarubuta ma Meerah yace aje asiyo ayi mata yanzu kafin zuwa anjima idan yaga yanayin jikin zai rubuta drugs d'in da za'a siya mata.
kar6a Meerah tayi tad'auki jakkarta tafita dan tasiyo koda ta isa tamik'a takardar Naira dubu bakwai akace zata biya hankalinta ba k'aramin tashi yayiba dan a lokacin dubu takwas ce tarage masu babu yadda ta iya haka tacire dubu bakwai tamik'a tasiya injection d'in takoma wasu nurse suka kar6a sukayi ma ummah.
 
Meerah jan su Safiya tayi suka fita suka d'an zagaya cikin asibitin dan ummah tad'an huta lokacin da suka dawo har tayi bacci saidai suwaiba suka samu zaune ta zo dubata nan suka gaisa tatambayesu me jiki sukace da sauk'i bata dad'e sosai ba tayi masu sallama tatafi.

Bayan azuhur koda likita yazo yaduba jikin ummah drugs yarubuta mata yaba Meerah yace asiyo da k'arfe sha biyun dare akeso afara bata tasha, kar6a tayi cikin sanyin jiki a ranta tana addu'a Allah yasa basufi dubu d'ayaba dan iya kud'in da suke hannunta kenan, cema su Safiya tayi sujira taje tasiyo tadawo koda tafita taje chemist d'in da yake cikin asibitin total d'in kud'in sukayi mata sukace tabada dubu goma sha biyu Meerah rikicewa tayi jin kud'i masu yawa muryarta tana rawa tace yanzu baza'a rageba?
Me chemist d'in yace ai babu yadda za'ayi a rage hakane kud'inshi duk inda zataje, haka tajuyo jiki babu k'wari tatafo cike da tashin hankali taje office d'in Doctor tasamu office d'in a rufe dan haka takoma wajen Nurses d'in da suke a ward d'in cikin sanyin jiki tace dan Allah sister's baza'a iya canza mana drug's d'innan ba arubuta masu sauk'in kud'i wlh bamuda dubu goma sha biyu a yanzu indai akwai marassa tsada arubuta mana.
'Daya daga cikin nurse d'ince tace wa yafad'a maki idan Doctor yarubuta magani ana canzawa? Kar ma kibari yaji labarin nan sannan koda kikaga anrubuta maki wannan ai babu nak'asa da shi inma banda abunku ai magani me tsada ya fi aiki, kuje kusamu kuhad'a kud'in koda cikin dangi bazaku rasa wanda zai bakuba, d'aya daga cikin nurses d'ince tace haba Sis Halima kikyaleta kawai ke kika tsaya yi mata bayani mu bakiga shuru mukayi muka kyaletaba taje kawai idan ba zata siyoba ai sai tabarshi ba dole.
Girgiza kai sister d'in tayi tace a'a ba a haka kinga saisa ake cewa muna da wulak'anci kuma ma wannan duka nawa take bazata wuce 20yrs ba fa, Meerah tana jinsu suna tayi tajuya tafita dan hankalinta ba a kansu yakeba dakyar taja k'afarta taje bisa wani dakali tazauna daga gefenta ta ajiye takardar nan tafara hawaye cike da tausayin kansu tana tunanin inda zata samo ma ummansu kud'in magani chan dabara tafad"o mata tad'auki wayarta talalubo number d'in Baba Muhammad tana kiranshi baiyi picking ba har saida tatsinke akaro na biyu tasake kira saida takusan tsinkewa sannan yayi picking tare da yin sallama.
Meerah amsa mashi tayi tare da gaisheshi.
Bayan sun gaisa yace ban gane me maganaba wacece?
Muryarta tana rawa tace Meerah ce baba.
Au kece ya akayi ne?,,,yanayin yadda yayi magana yasa tagane a 6acin rai yayita ammah bata damuba dan bashine a gabantaba tace Baba daman magani ne aka rubuta ma ummah kuma bamuda kud'in da za'a siya.
Tun kan tarufe baki yace wai ke meyasa kin cika damune? Ina ummantaku bani ita a waya.
Bacci take yanzu.
Baku baro asibitin ba ko?
Girgiza kai tayi kamar tana gabanshi tace wallahi Baba jikin ne nata sai ahankali shiyasa muka barta har ma anyi mata aiki yanzu magani ne kawai yarage asiya.
Cikin fad'a yace watau kun raina maganata baku d'aukeni abakin komai ba da nace kubaro asibitin saida kuka zauna toh ai sai kuyi yadda kukeso nidai nan kin ganni banda wasu kud'i atare da ni dan wata ya ja sannan kud'in da suka rage min ne nadunk'ule nabaki da kikazo.
Fashewa Meerah tayi da kuka tace dan Allah baba kataimaka mana da ko dubu biyar ne inyaso sai musamo sauran.
Cikin d'aga murya yace wai ke bakisan babu ba ko sokike inyo sata inbaki ne? Tunda nace babu ai babu sai kibari sai na samu sai inbada,,,yana fad'in haka yakashe wayarshi.
Cikin kuka Meerah tashiga furta innalillahi wa innah ilaihiraji'un wannan wace irin rayuwa ce wa zai taimaka mana.
Number d'in hajiya Binta talalubo tayi dialing tayi ringing tagama batayi picking ba tak'ara yin wani dialing d'in at least saida tayi mata 5 missed ammah bata d'agaba wurgi Meerah tayi da wayar tare da dafe kanta tafara kuka cike da tausayin kansu tana cikin kukan tatuna da mahaifinsu cikin sauri tad'auko wayar tayi dialing d'in number d'inshi ammah bata shiga nan ta ajiye wayar tacigaba da kukanta tana cewa meyasa babu wanda zai iya taimaka mana ko dan basuga halin da ummana take ciki bane.
Duk wanda zai wuce ta wajen sai ya kalleta wasu sutsaya suna bata hak'uri duk atunaninsu mutuwa akayi mata wasu kuma saidai suwuce abunsu, dafe kanta tayi da duka hannuwanta tana me cigaba da kuka ahankali bataji zuwan mutum ba saidai taji k'amshin turarenshi cikin sauri tad'ago takalleshi.
Tsaye yake a gabanta ya zuba mata idanu yana kallo hannunshi rik'e da wata k'atuwar leda janye idanunta tayi gefe guda tana me cigaba da hawaye,
 ajiye ledar yayi yazauna daga gefenta yace Baby lafiya? Me yasa kike kuka wani abu yafaru da ummah?
Batare da tabashi amsaba tace Ammar kabar wajen nan banaso kana shiga rayuwata.
Takardar da yagani gefenta yad'auka yaduba murmushi yayi yace idan na fuskanci kukan da kike toh akan wad'annan kud'in maganin ne Meerah.
Banza tayi takyaleshi nan yacigaba da cewa Minene 12 thousand wannan ai ba wasu kud'i bane Meerah ammah bari infito, 'Daukar ledar yayi yanufi hanyar ward da idanu tabishi cike da takaici dan ko ba'a fad'aba tasan wajen ummansu zaije, tsaki taja tare da buga tagumi, yana shiga baifi minti biyar ba yafito yadawo yazauna gefenta yace na shiga umman ma ashe bacci take sai su Safiya da Sadeek.
Shuru tayi bata tankashiba ido yazuba mata yace wai meyasa kike irin haka baby haba sai kiban kunya akan wad'annan 'yan kud'in kiyi ta sa kanki damuwa, ni zan ma baki 20 thousand kizo muje gidana ki amsa.
Juyowa tayi tawurga mashi harara tace wai kai meyasa ka fiye son kankq dayawa Ammar? Meyasa bakada buri sai na ganin ka lalata min rayuwa fashewa tayi da kuka tace me kakeso kamaida ni ne? Meyasa kakeson shiga rayuwata shin bakaga yanayin yadda nakeba ko ance maka talauci haukane, katashi katafi kawai bana buk'atar komai naka Ammar.
Tunda tafara maganar kallonta yake har tagama sannan yace ko d'aya Meerah ni baso nake in6ata maki rayuwaba ke kanki kinsan ina sonki da ace ba sonki nakeba da bazan dinga bibiyarkiba, kuma da kike maganar bakison kud'in ina to zaki samosu kinsan dai babu wanda zai dunk'uli kud'innan yabaki a yanzu haka dan duniyar nan yanzu kowa nashi yasani sannan kafin kihad'a wad'annan kud'in na tabbata sai ankwana dan bazaki samesu a yauba, kuma tunda har Doctor yarubuta toh yau yakeso asiyo su, ko dan bakisan yadda ummah takejin jikintaba saisa kike haka na tabbata da ace kin sani da ta kowace hanya zaki iya bi kisamo mata kud'in.
Dafe kanta tayi da hannu biyu tana me cigaba da kuka me sauti cikin ranta ko tana gasgata zancen Ammar tabbas tasan babu inda zata samu dan dangin ummah ma kansu basu basuba babu wanda zai basu ta san cewa ummah itace gatansu daga inda suka rasata basuda sauran gata a duniya, 'Dagowa tayi takalli Ammar da yatsareta da manyan idanunshi batare da tayi maganaba, ganin yadda tayi sanyi yasa yace idan kin amince zamu iya tafiya ba dad'ewa zakiyiba kinga kafin ummah tafarka na maidoki.
Girgiza kai tayi tace Ammar bazan iyaba ina tsoro kaga abinda zamu aikata zunubine.
Toh menene a ciki ai sai mutuba daga baya Allahu gafururrahim ne.
"A'a Ammar ina amfanin irin wannan tubar ai bazata kar6uba idan munyi nidai kataimaka karanta min kud'in inyaso zanyi k'ok'ari insamo maka cikin kwana biyu inbaka.
Mik'ewa yayi tsaye yace bana bada bashi saidai kyauta ammah kyautar ba akowane lokaci take samuwaba idan dai har kin amince kitashi mutafi inkuma baki aminceba ni zan tafi.
Ido tazuba mashi ganin ya wuce zai tafi yasa tamik'e tare da d'aukar takardar maganin tabi bayanshi wani irin farin cikine yaziyarci Ammar da yaga ta biyoshi ammah yanuna kamar baisan tatafoba yana tafiya ahankali har ya isa wajen motarshi sannan yajuyo yana kallonta tana tafiya tana hawaye har ta iso tana isowa bud'e mata gaban motar yayi batare da yayi maganaba nan tashiga yamaida yarufe sannan yazagaya shima yashiga.
Meerah duk'ar da kanta tayi tana me cigaba da kuka tana ji kamar tabud'e tafita saidai gefe d'aya na zuciyarta tana fad'a mata kar tafita intabar Ammar babu inda zata samu kud'in maganin.
Ammar yana jin tana kuka tuk'inshi kawai yake daga k'arshe da yaga kukan ba me k'arewa bane yarik'o hannunta yafara shafawa da hannu d'aya, d'ayan kuma yana driving da shi cikin muryar lallashi yace Please Meerah kidaina kukan hakanan dawowa fa zakiyi ba wani dad'ewa zakiyi ba, shuru tayi kamar bada ita yakeba tacigaba da kukan.
Jin kukan yake har cikin ranshi waje yasamu yayi parking d'in motar, Meerah sai ji tayi ya rungumota jikinshi cikin sauri tad'ago takalleshi muryarta tana rawa tace menene haka Ammar kasakeni.
Shafa fuskarta yayi yace toh ai kece baby sai kuka kike kuma banason kukan nan naki yana rikitani.
Tureshi tayi tace nidai kasakeni ai na daina.
Murmushi yayi ganin yadda jikinta gabad'aya yake rawa yace toh shikenan na sakeki ammah kema kukan yatsaya haka inba hakaba sai mutsaya a nan har sai kingama kukan.
Shuru Meerah tayi dan tasan tunda yafurta toh dagaske yake  nan tajuya tana kallon window saidai hawayen da suka k'i tsayawa.
Ammar jan motar yayi yacigaba da tafiya saidai kad'an kad'an ya juyo yakalli Meerah da taketa hawaye.
Gaban wani makeken gida yayi horn nan megadi yazo cikin sauri yabud'e mashi gate yacinna kan motar.
Meerah bin gidan tashiga yi da kallo cike da mamaki a ranta tana cewa nan ko ina ne Ammar yakawoni tana cikin kallon gidan sai jin muryarshi tayi ya ce baby kifita mushiga bud'e motar tayi tafita tare da cigaba da kallon harabar gidan, Ammar zagayowa yayi inda take tsaye yace baby zamu iya shiga ciki.
Juyowa tayi takalleshi saida yawuce gaba sannan tabi bayanshi jikinta yana ta kyarma gabanta yana ta fad'uwa, suna zuwa wajen k'ofar hannu yasa a aljihu yad'auko key yabud'e gidan tsaye Meerah tayi tak'i shiga ganin haka yasa Ammar yarik'o hannunta yace kishigo man baby nan fa gidana ne ba kowa ciki.
K'wallah ce tacika mata idanu da kaga yanayinta ka san a tsorace take, cikin rawar murya tace Ammar bazan iyaba
katseta yayi yace kefa kinga matsalarki Meerah sai ayita fama da ke ko kin mance ummah fa tana chan kwance kar ta farka taga bakinan ba dad'ewa zamuyiba zan maidake.
Jin haka yasa tabi bayanshi suka shiga makeken falon ammah gabanta yana cigaba da dukan ukku ukku har a lokacin dukkan jikinta rawa yake ko da tsoron da take tare da shi ammah hakan baisa takasa ganin kyau da tsaruwar falon Ammar ba tsaye tayi tana kallon maka-makan frames d'insa da suke manne a bangon parlourn ya yi kyau sosai daman abu ga kyakkyawan saurayi, ganin tayi tsaye yasa Ammar yarik'o hannunta yajata suka shiga d'aya daga cikin bedrooms d'in d'akin suna shiga daga bakin k'ofa taja tatsaya maida k'ofar Ammar yayi yarufe sannan yajuyo yakalleta yace haba Baby ni narasa abinda yasa kikeyin irin haka dan Allah kisaki ranki kinsan dai ina sonki kuma aurenki zanyi.
'Dago kai tayi takalleshi koda tana a tsorace ammah hakan bai hana mamaki bayyana a fuskarta ba, ganin yadda take kallonshi yasa yace Eh dagaske aurenki zanyi dan banaji zan iya rabuwa dake so nake ma da parents d'ina sun dawo daga dubai inyi masu maganar aure kinga sai muyi aurenmu hankali kwance.
Girgiza kai Meerah tayi tace Ammar kadaina cewa haka dan bana tunanin aure a yanzu gabad'aya maza halinku d'aya mayaudara ne na tabbata ba sona kakeyi ba jikina ne kawai kakeso.
Rungumota yayi jikinshi yace kidaina cewa haka meerah dan indai kikace basonki nakeba bakimin adalciba dan sonki gabad'aya ya mamaye dukkan illahirin jinin jikina tallabo fuskarta yayi yace kiyarda da ni Meerah ina sonki, ganin wani irin kallo da yake jifarta da shi yasa tayi saurin runtse idanunta tana me cigaba da hawaye.

Ajiye hijab d'in yayi gefe tare da matsowa kusa da ita yace Meerah kar kija mu6ata lokaci please.

kasa bud'e idanunta tayi saboda takaici cike da tarin nadama ganin akaro na biyu ta mallaka ma Ammar jikinta, shi kanshi Ammar wani irin dad'i yakeji ganin yadda yak'ara samun Meerah a karo na biyu dan shi ji yake baita6a jinshi yanayin da yake shigaba idan yana tare da Meerah.

Bud'e idanunta tayi dakyar takalli Ammar da yazuba mata ido harara tawurga mashi sannan tasamu tatashi zaune.
Mik'ewa Ammar yayi yana cewa yau dai sai munyi wanka zakibar gidan nan baby.
Towel yaje yad'auko yakawo mata lokacin tana k'irk'irin saka rigarta k'in amsa tayi ganin haka yasa yafizge rigar yace kid'aura wannan kiyi wanka sannan kitafi.
Kar6a tayi tad'aura Ammar yace muje toilet.
'Dago rinannun idanuwanta tayi takalleshi shi kanshi saida yaga tsanarsa k'arara a cikin idanunta murmushi yayi tare da zama gefen gadon yace Meerah wai meyasa kuka baya yi maki wahala dan Allah kidaina hawayen nan hakanan ko yanzu ink'ara maimaita abun.
Jin haka yasa tamik'e dak'yar tanufi toilet mik'ewa Ammar yayi shima zaibita saidai kafin ya isa har ta maida k'ofar ta rufe.

Meerah batare da tayi magana ba tatsaida kukan taje tasakar ma kanta shower wanka tayi sosai sannan tayi na tsarki tana gamawa d'aura towel d'in tayi sannan tad'auko wani tayafa a kafad'arta tabud'e k'ofar.
Zaune tasamu Ammar a bakin gado ya shirya cikin k'ananun kayanshi ya yi gwanin kyau tana fitowa yazuba mata idanu, fuskarta a d'aure taje tatattaro kayanta takoma toilet d'in tasaka kayan tafito ko inda yake bata kallaba tanufi k'ofa ganin haka yasa Ammar yamik'e yabi bayanta suka fita yamaida gidan yarufe a jikin mota yasameta tsaye ta jingine kanta a motar tana jiranshi yana zuwa yabud'e yashiga sannan yabud'e mata ta ciki nan tashiga tare da juyawa gefe d'aya tana kallon window ahaka sukabar gidan Ammar ya yi mata magana ta fi ak'irga ammah babu wacce ta amsa daga ciki yayi juyin duniyarnan ammah tak'i tankawa takoma kamar kurma saidai hawaye kawai da suke zubowa tana gogewa ahaka suka isa asibitin suna isa tun a wajen chemist tace safkeni a nan.

saidai tace bakomai.

a ranar mak'abta suka dinga shigowa suna duba ummah da jiki,
