Yaron yace wani mutum ne yazo da mota.
Ahankali tace kaje kace ance bata nan.
Toh yace nan yajuya yafita daidai lokacin ummah tafito daga cikin bayi ajiye butar hannunta tayi tace wai Meerah ba kece akace ana nemaba?
'Dagowa tayi daga tankad'en da take tace ni kuma ummah?
Au kina nufin ba haka bane ko banji ba? Kedai Allah yarabaki da wannan halin naki ace duk wanda zaizo wajenki baki fita toh ai gaki ga gidan sai kiyi ta zama kitsinci abinda zaki tsinta.
Ita dai Meerah shuru tayi tacigaba da tankad'enta ummah kuma tayi ta mata fad'a har tagaji tashige d'aki.

Meerah dai saidai tayi murmushi cikin ranta tace indai Ammar zai shekara zuwa ba zan ta6a fitaba.

Ammar da aka je aka fad'a mashi batanan baiji dad'iba ahaka yashiga motarshi yabar anguwar asibiti yaje yak'ara dubawa duk wani female ward da yake asibitin ya zagaya ammah baigansuba dan haka kan dole yahak'ura yakoma gida.



Bayan kwana biyu ummah kallon Meerah tayi da take wasa da wani d'an tsinke a hannunta tace Meerah yau dai zakije islamiyya ko dan kin kwana biyu bakijeba anwuceki karatu sosai.
Kallon umman tayi tace a'a ummah ba zan je ba nafiso sai kin k'ara samun sauk'i sai inkoma.
Toh ai nasamu sauk'i dan garas nakejina kishirya kiyi tafiyarki islamiyya da anjima duka fa awa biyu ne zakuyi kudawo.
Murmushi tayi tace toh shikenan ummah yadda kikace haka za'ayi zanje insha Allahu.
Yauwa kokefa ai indai kikace kizauna wuceki za'ayi sosai kinga kuma ba dad'i.
Hakane ummah.



Bayan sallar la'asar shiryawa sukayi sukaima ummah sallama suka tafi islamiyya kafin malam yashigo tasa wata k'awarta tabiya mata karatun da aka wuceta har malamin tauhidi yashigo nan suka fara d'aukar darasi.


Da k'arfe shidda suka tashi islamiyya saida tabiya ta ajinsu safiya da sadeek taga duk sun tashi basunan dan haka tasan sun tafi gida itama takama hanyar gida tana tafiyarta a nutse har ta kusan kaiwa gidansu sai ganin mota tayi ta sha gabanta batare da ta d'ago ta kalli motarba tawuce , fitowa yayi cikin sauri ko rufe motar baiyiba yabi bayanta yana zuwa yasha gabanta, jin k'amshin turarenshi yasa Meerah tayi saurin d'agowa takalleshi lokaci guda gabanta yayi wani irin fad'uwa, Ammar zuba mata idanu yayi yana kallonta kamar wani maye fuskarsa d'auke da murmushi yace baby kinyi mamakin ganina ko?
'Daure fuska Meerah tayi sannan tace wai kai menene haka kakeyi Ammar? Meyasa kakeson shiga harkata ina ce ka samu abinda kakeso dan haka kafita daga rayuwata.
Murmushi yayi yace bana tunanin haka Meerah dan koda kin cireni daga rayuwarki nikuma akowane lokaci shiga tawa rayuwarkikeyi dan gaskiya bazan iya rabuwa da ke ba akowane lokaci muradin ganinki da son jinki nake akusa da ni dan kwana biyu da bamu had'uba bakiji yadda nakejiba ammah a yanzu nasan zaki bani ruwan shana inwarke daga k'ishirwar da nakeji.
Harara tabanka mashi irin ta baka isaba sannan tace kar kak'ara tunanin zaka k'ara samuna Ammar domin yanzu nayi maka nisa jikina yafi k'arfinka wanchan karan ma dakaga na biye maka har sau biyu toh abisa kuskure ne na'aikata haka kuma akowace rana ina nadama akan hakan sannan yanzu ummana Alhamdulillah tasamu sauk'i kar kak'ara tunani zan k'ara neman wani abu a wajenka kai koma bata samu sauk'in ba bazan k'ara yadda kak'ara samun galaba akainaba,,,girgiza kai tayi tare da k'ok'arin maida k'wallar da tacika mata idanu tace ko d'aya Ammar baka dace da rayuwataba na tsaneka saidai ina tayaka murna da kaci galaba akaina ta hanyar ruguza min farin ciki karabani da mutincina wanda har abada ba zai ta6a dawowa agareniba.
Shafa lallausar suman kanshi yayi yace wai Meerah wace irin maganace haka kikeyi yakamata ace zuwa yanzu kin waye amatsayinki na 'yar birni baidace ace kina abu kamar bagidajiba dan Allah ki amince min mucigaba da faranta ma juna kinga zaki taimaka min nima intaimaka maki indai kika kasance tare da ni insha Allahu dake da danginki zaku warke daga talauci....
Dakata malam na lura kamar kanka ya fara ta6uwa bakasan kalaman da kake fad'aba da alama sai kaje anduba maka k'walwarka kakalleni sama da k'asa kaga nayi maka kama da 'yan iskan matan nan? Kai kanka ka sani ba halina bane wanchan karonma abisa kuskure na amince maka a yanzu na shafe wannan babin a rayuwata kai hatta kai na rufe babinka kar kak'ara nuna ka san wata Meerah bare har katako k'ofar gidanmu.
Jinjina kai yayi tare da yin murmushi yace Meerah kenan banyi mamakiba da jin kalamanki yanzu dan kinga ina bibiyarki saisa kike fad'a min magana son ranki,,,d'aga kafad'a yayi yace saidai ba yadda zanyi tunda ni nake sonki kuma kema dolene kisone sannan ki amince min kamar yadda nabuk'ata domin Ammar akowane lokaci me sa'ane duk abinda yanema dole yasameshi inko ba hakaba komai zai iya faruwa.
Hararar sama da k'asa tayi mashi tace toh ai bismillah sai inga abinda zai faru nidai nasan nafi k'arfinka na gargad'eka da zuwa gidanmu idan kasake tako k'afafuwanka a k'ofar gidan nan toh sai kayi nadamar da baka taba yi ba a rayuwarka,,ta gefenshi taratsa zata shige gida cikin sauri Ammar yafizgota tafad'o jikinshi tallabo fuskarta yayi idanuwanshi sunyi jawur kamar garwashi cikin 6acin rai yace me kike tak'ama da shi da har zaki ce zaki wulak'anta ni a cikin garin nan?
Fizge jikinta tayi tare da ja dabaya tace kar kakuskura kasake ta6a min jiki.
Murmushi yayi tare da zura hannuwanshi cikin aljihun wadon jeans d'in da yake sanye da shi sannan yace Meerah duk wani kurarinki dole kiyi yadda nakeso ammah kisaurareni zuwa nabiyu zan baki mamaki.
Harara Meerah tawatsa mashi tare da jan tsoki tace aikin banza sannan tashige gida.
Binta yayi da kallo tare da cize le6e har tashige gida sannan yace Meerah dasannu zakisan wanene Ammar juyawa yayi yaje yashige motarshi yabata wuta.

Meerah tun a zaure tatsaya tagoge hawayenta tare da saita nutsuwarta sannan tashiga gida bakinta d'auke da sallama.
Suwaiba da take kitchen tana tuk'in tuwo ta amsa mata nan tashigo tawuce d'akinsu tana shiga su Safiya tasamu zaune tatambayesu ina ummah sukace ta shiga bayi dan haka tacire hijab d'inta tahau saman katifarsu takwanta kanta yana kallon ceiling tunanin maganganun Ammar tafarayi cikin ranta tace mutumin nan ya cika jaraba dama na sani tun farko ban amince mashiba da bazaidinga min hakaba.
Aunty Meerah mi akayi naji kina yin tsaki,  wani abu akayi maki a makaranta?
Runtse idanunta tayi batare da ta kalli safiya ba tace babu komai safiya kawai dai bacci nakeji kuma magrib tayi.
Sadeek ne yace Aunty Meerah kiyi baccin zan tayardake idan ankira sallah.
Juyawa tayi d'ayan side d'in tace a'a Sadeek ai babu kyau bacci a wannan lokacin bari dai sai nayi sallah zanyi.
Ummah ce tashigo d'akin tana cewa Meerah har kin dawo?
Tashi tayi zaune tace eh ummah na dawo ya k'arfin jiki?
Jiki Alhamdulillah ya yi sauk'i Su safiya sai suka tafo suka baroki ko?
Murmushi tayi tace ai anriga tadasu saisa suka tafo.
Toh ai ni nan da na fara masu fad'a na ce sudinga bari kudawo tare.
Safiya tace Allah ummah saida muka biya ta wajen ajinsu Aunty Meerah ba'a tashesuba.
"Eh lokacin bamu tashiba".
Hijab ummah tasaka sannan tace toh shikenan ai na d'auka hakanan kuka dawo.
Mik'ewa Meerah tayi tace bari inje inyo alwallah nima naji anfara kiran magrib.


Da dare koda suka ci abinci wajen tara suka kwanta dan su safiya ma tun takwas ummah tasasu sukayi bacci saboda gobe akwai makaranta da safe, Meerah tunda takwanta juyi kawai take abun duniya duk ya isheta ita bakomai yafi tadamata hankaliba sai yadda Ammar yake bibiyar rayuwarta akowane lokaci burinshi d'aya tabashi kanta yakwanta da ita, Girgiza kai tayi tace hakan ba zai ta6a yuwuwaba Ammar na gama amince maka in aikata sa6o, shuru tayi lokacin da tatuno a fili tayi maganar dan ma Allah ya taimaketa ummah ta yi bacci nan tacigaba da tunane tunanenta har daga k'arshe bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.


Washe gari tun da safe tatashi tayi ayyukanta tasoya awara da wainar fulawa wajen tara tagama almajirinsu yazo yad'auka yatafi da su, wanka tashiga tayi sannan tad'auko masu awarar da tad'ibar masu sukaci ita da ummah suna gamawa ganin goma tayi yasa tashiga kitchen tad'aura masu girkin rana.

Wajen k'arfe ukku suna zaune tana taje ma ummah gashinta suka jiyo muryar wani yaro yana cewa wai ance ana sallama da Meerah gabad'ayansu shuru sukayi babu wanda yayi magana saida ummah tace ke bake ake nemaba?
6ata fuska tayi sannan tace ummah aiki fa nakeyi.
Koma me kikeyi na dai fad'a maki kidaina wulak'anta mutane dan duk wanda zai tako yace yana sonka ai masoyine agareka ya wuce wulak'anci.
Meerah kallon Sadeek tayi da yake homework d'inshi tace sadeek jeka kagano min wanene?
Ummah ce tace babu inda zaije indai bazakijeba kizauna.
Babu yadda ta iya d'aure ma ummah gashinta tayi sannan tamik'e tad'auko hijab d'inta tasaka tafita cikin ranta tana cewa yanzu haka wanchan natattan ne, tana zuwa ko shi tasamu tsaye a zaure hannunshi rik'e da waya yana kallo yana murmushi ganinta yasa yad'ago yakalleta yace baby har kin fito?
Wata uwar harara tawurga mashi tace wai kai wane irin natattan mutum ne ace kullum ana korarka ammah sai ka zo.
Ta6e baki yayi yace kema kin sani dan nadamu da kene ammah da badan hakaba da ko kallo baki isheniba kinyi sa'a sosai da kika kasance atarihin rayuwata mace tafarko da take min yadda taga dama dan ni saidai inwulak'anta ba dai awulak'anta niba sannan saidai abini ba dai inbi ba, lallai gaskiya natayaki murna akan wannan sa'ar da kika taka.
Wannan matsalarkace malam dan ni baka kai matsayin da zan bikaba haba Mutum sai nacin tsiya gabad'aya ka takura ma rayuwata kabarni inji da abinda yadameni.
Matsowa yayi kusa da ita yace meerah ba zan ta6a barinkiba har sai kin amince kin biya min buk'atata.
Cike da raini take kallonshi tace lallai Ammar dasauranka watau a nan kana tunanin zan k'ara abince maka ko?
Murmushi yayi cikin rashin damuwa yace sosai ma kuma dolenki ne hakan indai kinason kanki da arzik'i.
A fusace tace inkuma banaso fa me zakayi?
Abinda nayi niya shi zanyi, wasu hotuna ne yamik'a mata yace amsa kiduba wannan, k'in kar6a tayi saida yace ki amsa man sannan takar6a cikin sanyin jiki tafiddo su daga cikin d'an gidansu tare da kallo, zaro ido tayi cikin sauri tad'anja da baya cike da tashin hankali take kallon hotunan guda biyu d'aya bayan d'aya hankalinta ba kad'an yatashiba nan gabanta yashiga dukan ukku ukku bakomai bane yad'aga mata hankali sai ganin itace a jikin hotunan d'aya tana kwance saman gadon Ammar ba hijab da kallabi a jikinta idanuwanta a rufe gashinta a baje saman gadon, d'ayan kuma tana d'aure da towel d'inshi a jikinta lokacin zata shiga wanka.
 lokaci guda idanuwanta suka rine sukayi jawur nan hawaye suka cika idon gabad'aya illahirin jikinta rawa yake d'ago kai tayi takalleshi cikin rawar murya tace Ammar wannan hoton fa ina kasamesu? Yaushe kayi min su batare da na saniba?
Murmushi Ammar yayi yace toh menene abun tashin hankali aciki Meerah? Kawai nayi makine dan alokacin kin min kyau sosai.
Hawayen da suka cika mata idanune suka gangaro tace ammah ai kasan hakan ba daidai bane kayi.
ta6e baki yayi yace ni awajena daidai ne tunda kece kin cika taurin kai yanzu hakama zuwa nayi nawankosu da baki fitoba da aika maki zanyi da su cikin gida.
Cikin muryar kuka tace Ammar bakada hankali idan ummana tagani fa?
Bazan damuba tunda kece kike min yawo da hankali nima kaina zanfi son tagani.
Keta hotunan tashiga yi gutsun gutsun tace wallahi hakan ba zai ta6a yuwuwaba Ammar me natsare maka da kakeso kaga ka sakani cikin k'unci?
Fuskarshi d'auke da murmushi yace idan kin keta wannan babu abinda kikayi dan ina da wasu gida ke hatta a wayata ina da shi nan yanuno mata yace kingani?
Hannu takai zata amshi wayar yajanye yace bazan baki wayataba Meerah.
Fashewa tayi da kuka tace Ammar ya kakeso inyi da rayuwata me kakeso duniya tad'aukeni? Ka san halin da ummana zata shiga idan tagani?
Ban saniba Meerah ammah ai kece kikaja dan haka idan har baki amince min ba zan aika ma ummanki hoton tagani.
'Dagowa tayi takalleshi cike da tashin hankali tace ummana fa kace?
'Daga kai yayi alamun Eh.
Duk'ewa tayi k'asa tare da rage sautin kukanta tace dan Allah Ammar kataimaka min kar kayi min haka na rok'eka wallahi ummah bazata ta6a yafemin ba idan taji abinda na aikata.
Ido yazuba mata yana kallon yadda take kuka kamar wadda aka aiko ma da sak'on mutuwa chan yanumfasa yace baby kiyi shuru hakanan kar sujiyoki kina kuka sud'auka wani abu nayi maki.
Cikin sauri tagoge hawayen fuskarta tare da waigawa takalli k'ofa ganin ba kowa yasa tayi k'asa da murya tace Ammar kataimaka min kagoge hotunan dan Allah.
Murmushi yayi yace naji zan goge ammah dole sai kin min abinda nakeso.
Shuru tayi tare da zuba mashi idanu tanaso tatambayeshi abinda yakeso tayi saidai gudun kar taji ya fad'i abinda tatsana yasa taja bakinta tayi shuru ganin batada niyar yin magana yasa yace nasan dai kin gane abinda nake nufi Meerah dan haka za6i ya rage gareki nan yazuba mata idanu yana jira yaji abinda zatace.

_*WANENE AMMAR*_

Alhaji Nura da Hajiya Saudat 'yan asalin garin Katsina ne sunyi aurensu acikin garin katsina Alhaji nura attajirin me kud'i ne d'an boko yana da companynshi da yazuba ma'aikata sosai suna aiki a k'ark'ashinshi, tunda sukayi aure Allah bai basu haihuwaba sai bayan shekara biyar sannan saudat tasamu ciki sunyi murna sosai dan Allah yayi su da son yara  cikinta yana shiga wata tara tahaifi shantalelen d'anta kyakkyawa dan suma kansu ba bayaba wajen kyau ranar suna yaro yaci suna Ammar.

Alhaji Nasir watansu tara da aure Allah ya azurtasu da samun d'a namiji lokacin matar Alhaji Nura watanta ukku da haihu  sosai suma suka nuna farin cikinsu ranar suna yaro yaci suna Haisam kyakkyawa ne sosai dan kamanninsa sak na mahaifiyarsa yakwaso hasken ne na mahaifinsu yad'auko dan ita Saudat Farace sosai yana da shekara ukku mahaifiyarshi tak'ara samun wani cikin tahaifi d'iyarta mace Khadija bayan ita ta k'ara haihuwa saidai d'iyar batazo da rai ba toh tundaga nan haihuwa tatsaya mata.
Haka Haisam shima yataso cikin gata a wajen iyayenshi hatta Alhaji Nura duk abinda zaiyi ma Ammar sai yayi ma Haisam ya aiko mashi da nashi har Abuja dan yanason yaron sosai.

Ammar lokacin suna da shekara biyar sukayi wani irin sabo shi da Haisam dan indai zasuje Abuja toh kullum suna tare haka kuma idan suma sukazo katsina a duk lokacin da zasu rabu kuka suke sosai su kansu iyayensu har mamaki suke akan irin sabon da sukayi, har so suke ayi hutu makarantar boko dan kawai d'aya yaje ma d'an uwanshi hutu.
Lokacin da suka gama primary School d'insu Daddyn Ammar yaso Abban Haisam yabashi Haisam yarik'e ammah yanuna sai ya gama secondary school sannan zai bashi shi, dan haka yahak'ura yabarshi.

Meerah tunda tatashi dawani irin matsanancin ciwon kai tatashi saboda duk jiya bata samu tarintsa ba ga kukan da tasha, ummah hanata yin awara tayi aranar dan yanayinta kawai zaka kalla kagane cewa batada isasshiyar lafiya, koda me d'aukar tallar yazo ummah cemashi tayi yatafi sai gobe yau ba'ayiba, Meerah ganin ummah zatayi girki yasa tamik'e cikin dauriya tanuna kamar ta samu sauk'i dakyar tasamu ummah tabarta tayi masu girkin shima d'an wake ne tajefa masu, ummah ita tayi masu wanke-wanke.
Tana gama girkin wanka tayi tashirya tana sallar azuhur d'an shingid'awa tayi a saman darduma ummah matsa mata tayi tazuba abinci tad'anci tasha magani sannan takoma takwanta har su Safiya suka dawo daga makaranta tana nan kwance taso tad'an rintsa saboda duk jiya bacci k'aurace ma idanunta yayi ammah hayaniyarsu tahanata baccin.

Ammar koda ya iso k'ofar gidan parking d'in motarshi yayi yafito yana kallo yaga ta inda zai samu yaro ya aika yakira mashi Meerah, wani yarone da yadawo daga makaranta yakira yace yashiga gidan yace ana sallama da Meerah
 yaron toh yace yashiga gidan yayi sallama lokacin duk suna cikin d'aki yaron yace wai ance ana sallama da Meerah.
Gaban Meerah wani irin mummunan fad'uwa yayi dan ko ba'a fad'aba tasan cewa Ammar ne, Safiya ce tace Aunty Meerah ince baki lafiya ko?
Tashi tayi zaune dakyar tace a'a Safiya bari inje.
Ummah da take saurarensu tace keda bakida lafiya taya zaki iya fita.
Murmushi tayi tace zan iya ummah 
Mamakine yakama ummah ganin yau Meerah dakanta take cewa zata fita.
Meerah ko badan komai ta amince zata fitaba sai dan gudun kar tak'i fita Ammar ya aiko da hotunan su ummah sugani, tashi tayi tad'auko hijab d'inta tasaka.
Ummah tace indai baki iyawa kibari aje ace bakida lafiya 
Girgiza kai tayi tace zan iya ummah. 
Toh shikenan sai kin dawo.
Wucewa tayi tana tafiya ahankali har tafito a zaure tasameshi tsaye yana jiranta ganin yadda take tafiya dakyar yasa yayi saurin isowa inda take tare da rungumeta yace baby lafiya naganki haka?
 So tayi tak'wace ammah rashin k'warin jiki yasa takasa 
K'in janyeta yayi daga jikinshi yace baby bakida lafiya?
Ahankali tabud'e baki tace Ammar kasakeni hakan bai daceba kadinga ta6ani
K'ara matseta yayi a jikinshi yace toh menene aciki Meerah muna son junanmu kuma aure zamuyi kikwantar da hankalinki ni zan aureki yanzu kin amince mutafi gidana ko?
Wasu hawayene masu zafi suka gangaro mata tallabo fuskarta Ammar yayi yakai bakinshi asaman kumatunta yafara lashewa yana cewa Please baby kidaina kukan nan kinsan banason ganin hawayenki idan akwai abinda kike buk'ata kifad'a min inma kud'ine konawane kifad'a zan maki.
 samu tayi tatureshi da dukkan k'arfinta tare da d'an ja da baya tajingina da bango cikin muryar kuka tace wai meyasa kake min irin haka Ammar kasan wannan ba abu bane me kyau ammah kadinga abu saikace dabba.
Cike da mamaki yake kallonta yace Meerah nine kamar dabba?
Cigaba tayi da kukanta batare da tayi maganaba.
Jinjina kai yayi yace nagode sosai Meerah watau dan kinga na damu dake saisa kike min haka ni zaki kallah kice kamar dabba ni Ammar.
Murmushi yayi yace sa'arki d'aya bakida lafiya ammah da badan hakaba babu abinda zai hana yau intafi dake gidana.
Cikin d'aga murya tace wallahi baka isaba Ammar ai ba aurena kakeba da zaka maidani kamar matarka bazaka k'ara samun jikina ba dan ya yi maka nisa.
Murmushin mugunta yayi yace haka dai kikace ammah baki isa kimin katanga da jikinki ba tunda inaso ke bama abin farin ciki bane agareki kiga shantalelen saurayi kamata ina bibiyarki, kinsan dai ina da kud'i indai kika cigaba da faranta min kema kanki zakiji dad'i keda iyayenki sai kun more.
Girgiza kai tayi cike da takaici tare da rage sautin kukanta tace Ammar narasa abinda yasa kwata-kwata baka aiki da k'walwarka shin wai ka d'auka kud'inka nakeso? Ko ance maka ko wanchan lokacin da nabiye maka akan son raina nayi?
Matsowa yayi daf da ita har suna jin numfashin juna fuskarsa yakai saitin tata cikin sauri tajanye kanta gefe tare da runtse idanunta ya d'an dad'e yana kallon fuskarta sannan ahankali kamar me rad'a yace Meerah kar kimance da akwai hotunanki awajena ke bama suba hatta video d'inki akwai cikin wayana bandai gwada makiba dan ke kanki idan kika gani sai yasa kinzubar da hawaye sosai.

Cikin sauri tabud'e idanunta tasafkesu akan fuskarshi muryarta tana rawa tace Amm Ammar meyasa kake min irin haka? Wai kai baka tsoron ubangiji ne?

Murmushi yayi yace idan baki yarda ba zan nuna maki kigani.

Dafe kanta tayi da yawani irin sara mata tare da furta wash, rik'e hannunta Ammar yayi yace lafiya baby ko zamuje asibiti?
Fizge hannunta tayi tare da ja dabaya tace Ammar kayi yadda kakeso nalura dakai bakada mutunci bakasan hanyar inda mutunci yakeba bakada buri sai na ka6ata min rayuwa na tabbata babu sona aranka jikina ne kawai kakeso akowane lokaci.

Kwantar da murya yayi kamar wani mutumin kirki yace Meerah kidaina cewa haka ke shedace cewa ina sonki kuma zan aureki.

Goge hawayen fuskarta tayi tace kaidai kasani Ammar nidai bazaka k'ara cin galaba akainaba dan haka katafi kabar gidan nan kabarni inji da abinda yadameni, tana fad'in haka tajuya zata shige gida bataji tahowarsaba kawai sai ji tayi ya rik'e mata hannu, tsayawa tayi batare da ta juyoba ido yazuba ma bayanta kusan minti d'aya sannan yace koma me kenan zan nemeki zuwa gobe amince min ya zama dole dan inba hakaba zakiyi nadamar da zatasa kiji dama bakiyi jayayya da ni ba,,,, yana fad'in haka yasaki hannunta yajuya yafita yabar gidan 

Meerah tsaye tayi takasa ta6uka komai saidai hawaye da suka cigaba da kwarara daga idanunta ahankali tafara furta innalillahi wa'innah ilaihiraji'un wannan wace irin rayuwa ce, jingina tayi da bango tare da duk'ewa k'asa tadafe kanta da hannu biyu ta dad'e tsugunne a k'asa kafin daga baya tagoge hawayen fuskarta tamik'e tashiga cikin gida ruwa tazuba a buta tad'auki soap tashiga bayi, wanke fuskarta tayi tas sannan tafito takoma d'aki lokacin ummah ta d'an kishingid'a a saman katifa, su safiya kuma suna cin abinci daga chan bayan ummah takwanta batare da tayi ma kowa magana ba zuciyarta tana wani irin tafarfasa cikin ranta tace ina ma ace inmutu da incigaba da ganin wannan tashin hankalin da Ammar yake jefani, 6angare guda na zuciyartane tace toh idan kin mutu kina da hujjar da zaki kare kanki a wajen ubangiji akan Zina da kika aikata? cikin sauri tace Astagfirullah wa atubu ilaik ya ubangiji kayafe min ba dan halina ba, ya ubangiji kashiga tsakanina da Ammar kakareni daga sharrinshi, jin yadda zuciyarta take k'una yasa tatashi tad'auko alk'uri takaranta dan samun sauk'in abinda takeji.



Ammar gudu kawai yaketa shararawa a motarshi gefe d'aya yana mamakin halin Meerah da kwata-kwata takasa amince mashi yana binta tana guje mashi abun har mamaki yake bashi ganin yadda 'yanmata suke binshi suna bashi kansu ko bai nemaba ammah shi yake binta tana tak'i, cize le6e yayi yace yarinyar nan kin cika taurin kai zanyi maganinki.

Yana isa gida cire kayan jikinshi yayi yafad'a wanka bayan ya fito ya shirya saman gadonshi yafad'a yakwanta wayarshi ce datafara ruri yasa yajawo yayi picking 
batare da ya duba suna ba muryar Mufida yaji ta ce hallo, saida yad'an yamitsa fuska sannan yace ya dai Mufida?

Ka amince goben inzo?
"Zamuyi waya kisaurareni zuwa goben".
Toh shikenan sai najika.
"Ohk",, bai jira jin abinda zata ceba yakashe wayar tare da yin wurgi da ita tsaki yaja yace Mufida kina matsamin dayawa ke kina ta bibiyata nikuma wadda nake bibiya daban, murmushi yayi yace lallai Meerah ke tadabance kuma dole ki amince min. 



Haisam saida yayi sallar la'asar sannan yadawo gida wanka yayi yashirya cikin k'ananun kaya jin shuru baiji motsin Ammar ba yasa yawuce d'akinshi ahankali yatura yashiga yaganshi kwance yana ta kwasar bacci, Murmushi yayi yamaida k'ofar yarufe yakoma parlour yazauna yajanyo laptop d'inshi yakunna yafara wani aiki.



Bayan sallar isha'i zaune take saman darduma tayi zugum ta zuba ma waje d'aya ido tana kallo da kaganta kasan hankalinta baya tare da ita, Ummah ce da take tasbihi tad'ago takalleta tace Meerah wai lafiyarki?
Ajiyar zuciya tasafke tace lafiya lau ummah.
Toh kuma kike tunani, me yake faruwa ne?
Juyowa tayi takalli ummah tarasa abinda zatace mata ganin yadda tatsareta da ido yasa tace ummah kawai ina tunanin su Aunty Binta kiduba kiga tunda kika kwanta asibiti basuzo suka dubakiba har aka sallamomu ko waya basu kiraba.
'Dan guntun murmushi ummah tayi tace daman haka rayuwar take Meerah ba kowane d'an adam bane yake iya xumunci domin shi zumunci sai an jajirce anyi yak'i da zuciya domin zumunci shi kanshi yana kaiwa ga Aljannah, saisa kikaga ban ta6a damuwa da maganganunsu ko kallon da sukemin na ganin ko maula naje wajensuba saidai basu saniba zuciyata fes take kawai dai ina son 'yan uwana saboda Allah dan banda wasu 'yan uwa a duniyar nan sai su.
Meerah da tazuba ma Ummah ido cike da tausayinta murmushi tayi tace hakane Ummah saisa akowane lokaci nake addu'a Allah yasa inbiyo irin hak'urinki da halinki me kyau, saidai k'addara ta riga fata.
Kallonta ummah take cikin rashin fahimtar maganarta tace ban gane k'addara ta rigayi fata ba, me yasa kikace haka?
Girgiza kai tayi tare da kallon gefe d'aya tace kokad'an ummah ban d'auko halinkiba.
Murmushi ummah tayi tace kinjiki da wata irin magana ai Meerah halinki yana da kyau sosai akowane lokaci ina godiya ga ubangiji da yabani 'ya irinki gabad'aya halinki yayi saisa kullum addu'ata d'aya Allah ubangiji yaza6a maki miji nagari wanda zai kular min da ke.
 hawaye ne suka gangaro mata a kumatu har cikin ranta ta ji dad'in addu'an ummah saidai me ta riga ta 6ata rayuwarta bata tunanin hakan zai kasance tare da ita.



Washe gari koda suka tashi tun dawuri tad'aura Awara dan kar ummah tahanata nan da nan tagama dafawa tad'ibo wata tasoya ma su safiya suka tafi da ita makaranta ummah tana chan d'aki tana bacci batasan wainar da ake toyawaba saida tafarka tafito nan tasamu Meerah a kitchen tana ta soya awara cike da mamaki ummah take kallonta tace kekuma duk yaushe kika fara batare da na saniba.

Dariya Meerah tayi tace ummah lokacin kina bacci.
ammah keda bakida lafiya memakon kibari sai kin warke sannan.

toh shikenan tunda haka kikace Allah dai yataimaka.

"Eh na kaimata"

"Eh ummah na kusa yin k'arshe ne zanyi nasan yanzu ma zaizo yad'auka"


tana gamawa gyara masu d'akinsu tayi tawanke duk abinda ta6ata sannan  tazauna tayi kalaci shima saida ummah tamatsa mata sannan taci awara tana gamawa tad'an kishingid'a tayi bacci sai wajen 11am tafarka.



Haisam ne yaturo k'ofar office d'in Ammar yashigo rik'e da wasu files ganin Ammar yayi ya kife kai a saman table d'in da yake gabanshi kamar me bacci jin anshigo yasa yad'ago kai dan ganin wanene ganin Haisam yasa yace ashe kaine.
Kujera haisam yaja yazauna tare da mik'a mashi files d'in da suke hannunshi yace ga wannan sai kayi signing d'azu aka kawo su kayane akeso a chan Ghana zamu tura masu .
Kallon files d'in yayi sannan yace ai kai yakamata kayi sign ba niba bro.
Eh nayi nawa ni ai.
Kar6a Ammar yayi ya ajiye yace toh zanyi.
Ido Haisam yazuba mashi yace wai Ammar lafiyarka kuwa?
Shima kallonshi yakeyi yace me kagani?

Mik'ewa Haisam yayi yace shikenan bari inje in ida aikina.

kallonshi Haisam yakeyi yace gida kuma, har ka gama aikin?

Toh shikenan ba matsala.

Haisam yana fita Ammar cikin sauri yayi sign a files d'in yatattarasu yasaka cikin k'aramar drawer d'in da take office d'in yad'auki key d'in motarshi yaficce.

saida yabiya yayo takeaway sannan yawuce gida yana komawa wanka yayi yashirya yaci abinci bayan ya gama bedroom d'inshi yashiga yakwanta nan yad'auko wayarshi yayi dialing d'in number d'in Meerah ya yi sa'a ko yana kira tashiga ammah tayita ringing ba'ayi picking ba har tatsinke, k'ara kira yayi nan ma ba'a yi picking ba wani irin haushine yakamashi yace watau bama zatayi picking d'in wayana ba dan ta rainani, zanyi maganinki ko.
Message yarubuta yatura mata yana shirin ajiye wayar nan wayar tafara ringing cikin sauri yakalli screen d'in duk atunaninshi Meerah ce ammah sai ganin sunan Mufidah yayi ya bayyana picking yayi tare da cewa Mufidah ya dai?
My ganinan na iso kazo kabud'e min gidan.
Tashi yayi yace ohk ganinan.

ratsawa tayi ta gefenshi tashigo tana taku d'aid'aya rufe k'ofar yayi yabiyo bayanta a saman kujera tazauna nan shima yazauna daga gefenta yace shine baki sanar da ni zaki zo ba?

Haba Ammar dan mi zakace haka.
tarar numfashinta yayi yace koma ban fad'aba kekanki kinsan hakane Mufi...
Turo k'ofar da akayi aka shigone yasa gabad'aya suka maida kallonsu ga k'ofan Ammar cikin sauri yatureta daga jikinshi tare da mik'ewa tsaye gabad'aya rikicewa yayi Muryarsa tana rawa yace Haisam ka dawo.
Haisam da yake tsaye mamaki ya cikashi kallon Ammar yake yana kallon Mufidah da take zaune tana kallonshi hannunta yana akan gashin kanta tana wasa da shi.
Kallon Ammar yayi nad'an lokaci sannan yace Ammar wacece wannan, me tazo yi gidan nan?
Cikin inda inda yace wannan Friend d'ina ce Mufidah tazo ne tagaisheni kwana biyu bamu had'uba.
Haisam shuru yayi yaje saman kujera yazauna tare da d'auko wayarshi yana dannawa.
Mufidah idanu tazuba mashi sosai taji ya burgeta, d'ago kan da zaiyi ganin yadda tatsareshi da ido yasa yawurga mata harara ba shiri tasafke kanta k'asa.
Ammar ne yace Mufidah wannan bro d'ina ne kugaisa.
Kwantar da murya tayi tare da shak'eta tace sannu ina wuni?
Shuru Haisam yayi yakyaleta kamar bai ji ba,
Ganin haka yasa Ammar yace kitashi kitafi zamuyi waya.
Bud'e baki tayi zatayi magana cikin sauri yagirgiza kai alamun kar tace komai dan haka tamik'e tad'au handbag d'inta tafita kallon Haisam yayi ganin ba kallonshi yakeba yace bari inmiki rakiya nagode sosai da ziyarar da kika kawo min, Murmushi tayi tare da kallon Haisam sannan tace bakomai ai daman zaman taren kenan idan anji shuru kwana biyu ba'a had'uba sai akawo ziyara.
Hakane kinyi magana me kyau muje inrakaki, nan suka fita daga gidan.





Meerah bayan ta fito wanka ta shirya zama tayi tana jiran su safiya sudawo daga makaranta dan alokacin ita kad'ai ce ummah ta shiga mak'wabta anyi haihuwa.

Wayarta tajanyo dan tayi game nan taga Missed call d'in Ammar har guda biyu tsaki taja tace maye kawai bai ajiye komai ba sai damun mutane, Meesage d'in da tagani ne tashiga dan ganin na menene ganin number d'in Ammar yasa tabud'e tafara karantawa

_Meerah ina fata yanzu kin warke nakiraki shine kika k'i yin picking hmm duk abunki bazaisa inrabu da keba, na lura bakisan wanene Ammar ba har kina gani kamar wasa nake bazan iya aiko da hotunankiba a gidanku, zanzo da anjima ind'aukeki rashin amincewarki zaisa inyi abinda zai baki mamaki wanda zakiji daman baki min jayayya ba dan sai kowa yasan abinda kike aikatawa, wane yanayi kike ganin zaki shiga idan 'yan gidanku suka gane abinda kike aikatawa? Kizauna kiyi tunani, yanzu dai kisaurareni nan da bayan la'asar zanzo cikin shirina dole cikin biyu ayi d'aya dan idan takama dakaina zan shiga inkai ma iyayenki sugani dan suyarda saboda akwai wanda nad'aukemu ni da ke, inkuma kika amince min na miki alk'awali zan baki wayata dakanki kigoge hotunan_
      _bissalam._


Tunda tafara karantawa tafara hawaye lokaci guda tafashe da kuka bayan ta gama karantawa ajiye wayar tayi tace narasa meyasa kake min irin haka Ammar, narasa meyasa bakason ganina cikin farin ciki, wayar tad'auko tayi dialing d'in number d'inshi.

Haisam da yake zaune yana dannar wayarshi jin ringing d'in waya akusa da shi yasa yakalla ganin ta Ammar ce yasa yad'auko sunan MEERAH ne yabayyana a jikin screen d'in wayar tsaki yaja tare da ajiye wayar cike da takaici yace aikin kenan shidai 'yanmata, duk da ya ajiye wayar ammah ido yazuba mata har tatsinke chan kuma wani kiran yasake shigowa.

*PAGE 24*


Har saida wayar takusan tsinkewa sannan yayi picking yakanga a kunnenshi shuru yayi batare da yayi magana ba.
Meerah naji anyi picking ita kanta bata jira anyi maganaba cikin kuka tace Ammar meyasa kake min irin haka? Meyasa bakada buri sai nason ganin ka 6ata min rayuwa, meyasa baka amfani da k'walwarka kayi tunanin cewa tun farko ba dason raina na amince nabaka kaina ba wallahi duk abinda na aikata k'addara ce tasa haka ba wai da son raina na amince makaba.

Haisam rintse idanunshi yayi yana sauraren maganarta, bata damu da shirin da yayiba tace da ace tun farko na samu kud'in da za'ayi ma ummana aiki taya kake tunani zan amince maka inbaka kaina? Kai kanka kasan cewa ba halina bane ban ta6a aikata zina ba kaine mutum nafarko da kafara sanina kuma ina nadama akan haka,,, k'ara sautin kukanta tayi tace dan Allah Ammar na rok'ek'a kar ka aiko ma ummana da pictures d'innan wallahi zata iya shiga mawuyacin hali tadalilin haka zata iya rasa ranta, inkuma haka tafaru bazan ta6a yafe ma kaina ba na tabbata 'yan uwana bazasu ta6a yafeminba suma dan gabad'aya itace gatanmu, Ammar indai dagaske kakr sona toh na amince katuro ayi maganar aurenmu zan aureka dan bazan k'ara amincewaba in aikata k'azamammiyar rayuwa ba wannan tabon da kayi min bazai ta6a gogewaba acikin zuciyata,, rage sautin kukanta tayi tace Ammar ka yi shuru bakace komai ba please kayi magana.
Haisam saurare kawai yakeyi ya kasa cewa komai cikin ranshi mamakin d'an uwan nashi kawai yake yi, Meerah jin shurun yayi yawa yasa takashe wayar tare da kife kanta a saman katifa tacigaba da rusa kuka me k'arfi cikin kukan tace meyasa bazakayi maganaba Ammar ka kasa gane cewa abinda kakeso muyi ba abu bane me kyau.


Haisam baisan lokacin da takashe wayarba jin shurun da yayi yasa yajanye wayar daga kunnenshi ya ajiye tare da girgiza kai yace Ammar bansan yaushe zaka daina abinda kakeyi ba.
 Ahankali Ammar yaturo k'ofa yashigo duk yayi kalar rashin gaskiya kallonshi Haisam yayi yad'auke kai, kujerar da take opposite d'in wadda Haisam yake yazaune yaje yazauna shuru yayi nad'an lokaci jin Haisam baice komai ba yasa yace bro wannan da tazo tare mukayi primary da secondary school k'awatace sosai.
Wani irin kallo Haisam yayi mashi yace toh cewa nayi inason jin yadda kuke da ita? Ammar kana bani mamaki wallahi indai kace wannan rayuwar zaka cigaba da yi toh baka d'auko ma kanka hanya me 6ulewaba ace kai kullum bakada aiki sai 6ata yaran mutane idan aure kakeso toh kayi man.

Haba Haisam meyasa zakace haka kasan fa na daina wannan rayuwar tun a U.K ko ziyarar da Mufidah takawo min ce yasa kake zargina? Toh abinda kake tunani ba haka bane.

Tsaki haisam yayi yace kaidai kasani nidai shawarace nabaka idan kaga dama kad'auka inkuma bakaga damaba toh kawatsar ammah kwatakwata wannan ba hali mekyau bane ace dan kana da dukiya kadinga amfani da su kana lalata 'ya'yan mutane ba zaka iya taimakonsuba saboda Allah sai sun baka kansu sannan zaka basu kud'i.

Ammar baki yasaki yana kallonshi cike da mamaki sai chan yace Haisam wa yafad'a maka ina amfani da dukiya inlalata 'ya'yan mutane?

Mik'ewa Haisam yayi yace wannan kuma kai za'a tambaya nidai shawarar da zanbaka shine kayi amfani da damarka tun kafin rashin dama tarisk'eka,,, yana fad'in haka yawuce yashige bedroom.

Mik'ewa Ammar yayi yafara zagaye parlourn yana tunanin maganganun Haisam magana d'aya ce tafi mashi yawo a kwakwalwa da yace yana amfani da dukiya dan yalalata 'ya'yan mutane ba zai iya taimakonsu saboda Allah ba, cize le6enshi yayi ahankali yace taya Haisam yasan haka, wa yafad'a mashi? Ammah ai su suke bibiyata bani nake binsuba mutum d'aya ce nake bi, saidai kayi hak'uri Haisam bana ji zan iya dainawa,, zuwa yayi yad'auki wayarshi shima yashige bedroom d'inshi yakwanta duk sai yaji ba dad'i dan yasan kafin Haisam yasafko daga fushin da yake da shi sai ansha daga.


Haisam ma yana shiga kwanciya yayi saman gado kanshi yana kallon ceiling yayi matashin kai da hannuwanshi maida idanunshi yayi yalumshe kamar me shirin yin bacci lokaci guda yaji maganganun da Meerah tayi suna dawo mashi a kunne tsaki yaja tare da bud'e idanunshi yace na rasa meyake burgesu atare da Ammar suke siyar mashi da mutuncinsu tashi yayi zaune dan ji yayi baya iya yin baccin da yakeji mik'ewa yayi yafad'a toilet wanke yayi yafito yashirya yazari key d'in motarshi yaficce yabar gidan.

Ammar baiji fitarshiba saida yatada mota sannan yajiyoshi murmushi yayi yace ashe akwai babban aiki agabana.

Tun daga ranar Haisam yarage kula Ammar indai ba magana ta zama doleba toh baya yi mashi saidai shi Ammar ne yake kulashi ganin yadda yad'au fushi da shi sosai yasa jikinshi yayi sanyi yasameshi a office yabashi hak'uri yace insha Allahu bazai k'ara neman matan banza ba.

Kallonshi kawai Haisam yayi ba dan ya yarda da abinda yaceba ganin yadda yake mashi kallon rashin yarda yasa Ammar yace dagaske nake Haisam.

Ta6e baki yayi yace ba matsalata bace Ammar in ka daina dan kanka inma baka dainaba bazan damuba kaje kayi yadda kakeso kana da damar yin koma miye.

Shuru Ammar yayi nan Haisam yacigaba da aikinshi acikin system d'inshi ganin haka yasa Ammar yatashi yakoma office d'inshi sai chan wajen azuhur yafito sukaje sukayi sallah bayan sun dawo aka kawo masu takeaway a chan office d'in Haisam yazauna yaci koda Haisam bai wani sakar mashi fuska ba.

Ak'alla saida Ammar yayi kusan sati d'aya bai k'ara neman wata ba Mufidah ko kullum sai damunshi take shi kanshi daurewa kawai yake daga k'arshe da yaji bai iya cigaba da daurewa kiranta yayi awaya yace suhad'u a chan mekud'i hotel dan chan ne kawai zasu more hankali kwance batare da Haisam ya kawo masu matsala ba.





Meerah ko saboda fargaba da tashin hankalin da tashiga abinci kanshi ba sosai take ciba ummah tasha tatambayeta abinda yake damunta kullum dai amsar d'aya ce bakomai, ba dan ta yardaba saidai kawai tabarta dan ita har mamaki take ganin yadda take rama saidai kawai tabita da addu'a.

Zazza6i da ciwon kai ne tafara ammah bata nuna ba saboda dauriyar da take da ita saidai yanayinta kawai zaka kalla kagane batada isasshiyar lafiya abinci ma kanshi dakyar take samu tad'an ci hatta girki a 'yan kwanakin nan ummah takeyi masu ganin yadda take jin jiki yasa ummah tace tashirya gobe litinin tadaure taje asibiti taga likita.
Meerah amsawa tayi da toh dan a yadda take jin jikinta itama zataso taje asibiti ko magani ne arubuto mata.


Washe gari tunda safe tashirya tare suka fita tare da su safiya tasamu abun hawa tawuce general hospital.
koda ta isa a G.O.P.D tasafka ba laifi ta tarar da mutane nan itama ta amshi number d'in da ake badawa taganin likita sannan tasayi folder akayi masu B.P nan suka zauna jiran likitoci suzo.

Suna nan zaune likitoci suka fara zuwa nan aka fara d'ibar folders ana kaimasu suna duba patients ahaka har layi yazo kanta aka kirata tashiga nan likitan yatambayeta abinda yake damunta tafad'a mashi yarubuta acikin folder d'inta sannan yatambayeta tana da aure tace a'a.
Test yarubuta mata yace taje tayo takawo mashi result sai yaga abinda yadace yayi mata.
amsa tayi tatafi lab dan daman ummah saida tabata dubu biyu tace tarik'e koda za'a nemi wani abu, tana zuwa tasamu aka d'ibi jininta sannan aka bata 'yar roba taje tayi fitsari aciki takawo masu akace taje tazauna tajira sai nan da minti talatin za'a bata sakamakon.

Waje tasamu tazauna tana jira har wajen minti talatin sannan aka kirata aka bata result d'inta tawuce wajen Doctor tana zuwa wani patient na fitowa tayi saurin shigewa takaima Doctor kar6a yayi yaduba sannan yad'ago yakalleta yace keda kikace bakida aure ya akayi kike da ciki har na tsawon sati hud'u?
Gabanta wani irin mummunan fad'uwa yayi batasan lokacin da tazamo k'asaba daga saman kujerar da take zaune ahankali tafara furta innalillahi wa'inna ilaihiraji'un wasu irin hawaye ne masu zafi suka gangaro mata duk yadda taso tayi kuka kasawa tayi gabad'aya wuta tad'auke mata tunani tafara yi taya tasamu ciki? Ya akayi take da ciki bata saniba toh wai ma wa yayi mata shi? Chan tatuno da Ammar tabbas tasan shine yayi matashi dan da shi kawai tata6a huld'a,
 magana kawai likita yakeyi mata ammah batasan yana yi ba, saida yad'an buga table d'in da yake gabanshi sannan tadawo cikin hayyacinta yace lafiya ina ta magana kinyi shuru?

Hawaye ne suka cigaba da tsiyaya daga idanunta nan tashiga girgiza kai batare da tayi magana ba, cigaba yayi da agaskiya sai kin rage yawan sa damuwa atare da ke dan zai iya haifar ma babyn da kike d'auke da shi da ke kanki wata matsala, zan baki folder d'inki kitafi da ita chan Maternity word achan ne za'a dinga kula da lafiyarki da ta babynki zasuyi maki bayanin komai idan kinje chan, wasu drugs yarubuta mata yace wannan kuma kije kiyi amfani da su na Malaria ne da kike fama da ita, 
kar6ar kawai tayi tamik'e dak'yar jiki ba k'wari taficce tana fitowa waje tasamu tazauna batadamu da mutanen da suke wucewa ba tashiga rusa kuka cike da tashin hankali cikin kukan take cewa nashiga ukku ina zansa rayuwata ina ma ace inmutu inhuta da wannan rayuwar, me zance ma ummah idan nakoma gida? Meyasa nabiye ma rud'in shed'an naba Ammar kaina shikenan nasan ummah bazata ta6a yafemin ba ina ma ace wannan duk a mafarkine yafaru, tunowa tayi da inda take zaune yasa tamik'e dak'yar tafara tafiya ahankali ammah tana cigaba da kukanta ahaka har takusan fita gate sannan tasamu me napep tatsaida tarasa ina zatace yakaita tsoronta d'aya taje gida batasan me zatace ma ummah ba tsintar kanta tayi da cewa yakaita chan unguwar da Ammar yake bata tsaya cinikiba tashiga.

Koda suka kama hanya kuka kawai takeyi ahaka har suka isa kasancewar gidan nashi abakin hanya yake yana tsayawa dasauri tafita har ta mance zata wuce bata bashi kud'inshiba saida yace hajiya kud'ina sannan tazaro tabashi tawuce tanufi gidan tana zuwa tura k'ofar gate d'in tayi tashiga ta yi sa'a gateman d'in baya nan hango motar Ammar da tayi yasa tad'anji sanyi a ranta dan tasan yana gida tana zuwa tana tura k'ofar tabud'e dan haka tawuce direct, Ammar yana zaune a parlour yana waya da Mufidah sai ganin Meerah yayi kamar daga sama k'walo ido yayi waje dan yatabbatar da ko itace dagaske ganin itacen yasa yayi saurin kashe wayar tare da mik'ewa tsaye yace Baby kece yau dakanki kinyi missing d'ina ko? Daman nace bazaki iya dad'ewa irin hakaba batare da kinganni ba.
Batabi ta kan maganar da yakeba tanufi inda yake tana ko zuwa kamar jira yake nan yarungumeta tare da safke ajiyar zuciya yace baby ya naji jikinki dazafi haka ko har yanzu bakida lafiya?
Sake fashewa tayi da kuka tare da tureshi tace Ammar kasakeni na fad'a maka kar kasake ta6a min jiki.
Haba Meerah wai meyasa kike irin haka yanzu dai nasan abinda yakawoki mushiga ciki inmungama sai inbaki kud'in.
Wani irin kallon ukku saura k'wata tayi mashi koda tana cikin kuka ammah hakan baisa takasa maganaba tace wallahi Ammar kwatakwata hankali bai gama zaman ma k'walwarka ba kai atunaninka zan k'ara dawowa agareka koda a mafarkine? Takardar hannunta tawurga mashi tace Ammar sai kaduba kagani abinda yake ciki,,, duk'ewa tayi k'asa tafara rusa kuka.
Ahankali yabud'e takardar lokaci guda yazaro ido tare da kallon Meerah yace Meerah ciki kuma? Ina kika samo ciki?
Kukan da takene yatsaya mik'ewa tayi tsaye tace tambaya ma kake ina nasamo ciki alhalin kasan ko nawanene?
 Kallon rashin fahimta yayi mata yace ban ganeba toh cikin nawanene?
Kallon cikin idanunshi tayi tace kai shedane ban ta6a ba ma kowa kaina ba inba kaiba kaine mutum nafarko da kata6a sanina dan haka kaine uban wannan cikin nawa.

Dariya Ammar yafara irin ta rainin wayau saida yad'an tsagaita daga dariyar da yake sannan yace wai ni kike nufi Meerah? Ni zaki lik'a ma cikinki bayan duk mazan da kike mu'amala da su kima rasa wanda zakice sai ni.

Kallonshi take cike da mamaki nuna kanta tayi da yatsa tace Ammar yanzu ni zakace ina mu'amala da wasu maza, sharri zaka ja min bayan kasan kaine kad'ai kata6a sanina,,, fashewa tayi da kuka me kar6i tace toh wallahi ba zan yarda ba, me kakeso infad'a ma ummana idan nakoma gida bayan batasan abinda na aikata ba.
Shuru Ammar yayi yana kallon yadda take risgar kuka chan dai da yaga kukan ba mai k'arewa bane rik'ota yayi yace haba Meerah menene abun kuka kuma kikwantar da hankalinki kibari muyi magana.

Kallonshi tayi da idanuwanta da suka fara k'ank'ancewa saboda kuka tace Ammar kace inkwantar da hankalina? dan ba kai abun yafaru da kaiba saisa kake cewa haka.

K'ara matsota yayi a jikinshi yace ba fa haka nake nufiba Meerah kifahimceni, ni abinda nake nufi shine yanzu zan baki kud'i sai kije kizubar da cikin kinga kenan mahaifiyarki bazata ganeba muma sai mucigaba da gudanar da rayuwarmu sai musan ta hanyar da zamubi batare da ciki ya k'ara shigaba.

Wani irin mugun kallo tayi mashi tare da fizge jikinta tace wallahi bansan bakada mutunciba Ammar sai yau yanzu bayan laifin da mukayi ma ubangiji muka aikata zina muka samu cikin shine kuma kace inzubar da shi, wallahi ba zan iyaba da me zanji da zunubin yin zina ko da na zubar da ciki,,, girgiza kai tashiga yi tare da rage murya tana me cigaba da zubda hawaye tace bazan iyaba koda duka duniya zata kalleni tazageni wannan ya fiyemin da ace in aikata wannan laifin awajen ubangiji bayan wanda nayi ina neman gafararshi.

Tsaki Ammar yaja yace kefa matsalarki kenan Meerah bakida ganewa toh wallahi indai baki zubar da cikin nan ba saidai kinema mashi uba ammah ba dai Ammar ba dan Ammar baiyi kama da wanda zai haifi d'an shegeba baki isa kizubar min da mutunci a idon jama'a ba dan haka ke zakije kiji kunyarki ammah ni babu abinda zai dameni.
 
'Dan guntun murmushi tayi wanda yafi kuka ciwo tace daman duka maza halinku d'aya musamman masu kud'i akowane lokaci kanku kad'ai kuka sani baku damu da rayuwar kowaba sai taku, indai zaku samu yadda kukeso babu ruwanku da yanayin da kowa zai shiga saisa koda sau d'aya ban ta6a cire mutum d'ayaba daga cikinku.

Dafa goshin shi yayi cikin ranshi yace yarinyar nan fa ta cika taurin kai dayawa gashi ina buk'atarta nasan bama zata amince min ba ammah bari muga, murmushi yayi wanda iyakarsa le6e yace Meerah meyasa wai kike irin haka kinga ni bafa wani abu nake nufiba kingane yanzu kizo muje ciki sai inbaki kud'i kije kizubar da cikin idan parent d'ina suka dawo daga Dubai sai inmasu magana suzo sunema min aurenki.

Tarar numfashinshi tayi tace kar kasaki kayaudareni Ammar karma kayi tunanin zan k'ara gigin baka kaina.

Matsowa yayi yarik'ota yace kinga kukan ya isa haka ni bafa wani abu nace kibani ba kinsan dai ina sonki sosai batasan lokacin da takife kanta jikinshi tana me cigaba da kukaba, hannu yakai yafara shafa cikinta yace ammah kina ganin za'a barmu muyi aure wannan cikin yana a jikinki?

'Dagowa tayi daga jikinshi tana k'ok'arin janye hannunshi daga cikinta, k'ara matseta yayi cikin wata irin murya kamar me jin bacci yace kitaimaka min Meerah just 1 hour kawai zamuyi.

Wani irin k'ululune yatsaya mata a mak'ogwaro ji take kamar ta had'iye zuciya tamutu, da duka k'arfinta tanemi tatureshi ammah takasa muryarta tana rawa tace Ammar wai kai meyasa bakada hankali na fad'a maka bazan k'ara aikata zina ba ammah ka kasa fahimta da me kakeso inji.

Shafa fuskarta yayi tare da kai bakinshi yafara lashe mata hawayen da suke gangaro mata yace saidai kiyi hak'uri tunda ina buk'ata kuma kema nasan kinaso.

Fashewa tayi da kuka tare da k'ok'arin k'wace kanta ammah rik'on da yayi mata yasa takasa, hannu yakai yafincike mata hijab d'in da take sanye da ita wata irin k'ara tasaki tare da cewa wai minene haka kakeyi Ammar meyasa bakada tausayi nace maka bazanyiba bazanyiba ammah ka k'i k'yaleni wallahi yau saidai idan kasheni zakayi kakasheni ammah baka isa kasamu jikinaba.

Kasa magana yayi nan yanemi yarabata da rigarta ita kuma tana k'ok'arin fincike kanta tana kuka da dukkan k'arfinta.

Haisam da tun shigowar Meerah ya iso gidan rik'e da ledar da yayo masu takeaway har zai d'age labulen parlourn yashiga yajiyo maganarsu dan haka yatsaya yana saurarensu jingine kansa yayi jikin bango tare da rintse idanunshi yana jin wani iri akan rayuwar da d'an uwan nashi yake aikatawa duk maganar da sukayi akan kunnenshi akayita jin yadda Meerah take kuka yasa yayi saurin d'age labulen yashiga ganin yadda Ammar yafita hayyacinshi yana neman rabata da rigarta yasa yadaka mashi tsawa yace Ammar menene haka kakeyi.

Lokaci guda Ammar yayi wurgi da ita tare da ja ba, ta baya tatafi zata fad'a ma wani center table da yake gaban Haisam cikin sauri Haisam yayi wurgi da ledar da take hannunshi yajanyo Meerah da idanuwanta suke a rufe tana kuka tafad'o jikinshi
 Rungumeshi tayi cike da tsoro tana me cigaba da kuka idanuwanta suna a rufe.
Haisam kallon Ammar yake da gabad'aya yasha jinin jikinshi.
Ganin irin kallon da yakeyi mashi yasa Ammar yayi k'ok'arin saita nutsuwarshi tare da shafa kanshi sannan yace Haisam kabarni da yarinyarnan yanzu in 6a66allata cikin gidan nan yanzu saboda Allah ace mutum dan yana da kud'i baida sauran kwanciyar hankali kowa yakwaso saidai yayo wajenshi, kaduba kaga dan ta raina min wayau ta je tayi cikin shegenta shine yanzu tazo wajena wai nawane inbata kud'i tazubar bayan ni ba ni nayi mata cikinba ada munta6a soyayya har nace zan aureta ban saniba ko dan taga asirinta zai tonu infasa aurenta saboda tana d'iyar talakkawa shine talik'a min cikin,,,shuru yayi da yaga Haisam yana cigaba da jifarshi da wani irin kallo.

Meerah ko k'ara sautin kukanta tayi tana jin abinda Ammar yace ammah gabad'aya tarasa me ma zatace.

Nunata Ammar yayi yace kalli fa bro yadda kawani rungumeta a jikinka ko bakasan karuwa bace kasaketa kawai tafita tabar mana gida.

Haisam bai musaba yajanyeta daga jikinshi yatsayar da ita gefenshi har a lokacin baiga fuskar kowaceceba cikin 6acin rai yace karuwarka ba.
Da mamaki Ammar yake kallonshi yace karuwata fa kace?
'Daga kai Haisam yayi yace sosai ma ko nayi k'arya?.
'Daga mashi hannu Ammar yayi yace kaga malam kar kalik'a min abinda banyiba wai me kamayar da ni dan kawai na ta6a aikata kuskure a baya shine har yanzu kake kallona da kuskuren banason irin haka Haisam kadaina.
Murmushi Haisam yayi yace duk abinda zakace bazan ta6a yardaba saboda duk maganganun da kukayi da ita a kunnena kukayisu, gabad'ayansu kallonshi sukayi dan ita Meerah tsayawa tayi daga kukan da take tazuba mashi ido ko inda take haisam bai kallaba yace akwai ma abinda kai bakasan na saniba ta ta6a kiranka a waya alokacin da ka ajiye wayarka ni nad'auka a nan naji ta fayyace komai akan wasu hotuna da kace zaka tura ma mahaifiyarta idan bata amince maka tabaka kantaba hatta yadda akayi kafara nemanta duk naji toh wannan ma sharri akayi maka?

Zuface tafara kwarara daga fuskarshi yarasa abinda zaice ma Haisam sai kallon Meerah yayi yawurga mata harara.
Meerah batasan yanayiba dan ita kallon haisam kawai take mamaki duk ya cikata, sai a lokacin haisam yakallo inda take tsaye.

'Dan ja tayi da baya kad'an tana me cigaba da zubar da hawaye tana kallon Haisam da shima yake kallonta tace tabbas duk abinda kaji na fad'a wallahi gaskiya ne nafad'a Ammar shine yanemeni nikuma na amince mashi alokacin da nake ganin idan ban samu kud'in da za'ayi ma ummana aikiba zata iya rasa ranta, ni kaina sai bayan na amince mashine nazo ina da nasani na zubar da hawaye sosai, har yak'ara nemana akaro nabiyu na amince mashi shima alokacin da naga banda kud'in magani, wallahi ba halina bane haka kuma nayi nadama akan abinda na aikata bakomai bane yajamin haka sai k'uncin rayuwa dan gabad'aya masu kud'i basu taimakon marassa shi inma sun taimaka toh akwai dalilin da yasa duk inda nake tunani naje ban samo kud'inba, girgiza kai tayi tace kawai dan Allah yayo mu talakkawa shine sai kowa yak'imu? Juyawa tayi takalli Ammar tace ka ban mamaki Ammar saidai hakan ba abin mamaki bane saboda tun farko daman nariga da duk maza na ajiyeku a waje guda ina maku kallon hali d'aya gareku kuma ban ida tabbatar da hakanba sai akanka Ammar dan gabad'aya ka gama da rayuwata ayanzu batada sauran amfani tun tuni nayi da nasani akan amince maka da nayi akowane lokaci kana yaudarata da zaka aureni inbaka kaina baka ta6a tunanin katausaya min ba amatsayina na k'arama kuma talakka, fashewa tayi da sabon kuka tace kasan halin da ummana take ciki Ammar ammah kaidai burinka d'aya ka ida nak'asa mana rayuwa, kaje duk abinda kayi dan kanka dasannu ubangiji zai min sakayya ni zan tafi zan nema ma kaina mafita zanyi abinda yadace da rayuwata saidai bazan ta6a zubar da wannan cikin ba kasa a ranka komai daren dad'ewa akwai cikinka atare da ni, tana fad'in haka tajuya tabar Ammar tsaye kamar ruwa ya cishi sai zufa yake bakinshi nauyi yayi mashi yakasa bud'ewa yayi magana dan yana cikin tashin hankali kuma ba komai yatada mashi hankaliba sai ganin Haisam da yaji kuma yasan abinda ya aikata.

Tana fita Haisam kallon Ammar yayi cike da takaici yace wannan kad'an ma kafara gani indai kace irin wannan rayuwar zakayi, wallahi ina bak'in ciki ace wai d'an uwanane yake wannan k'azamar rayuwar, kaduba kaga wannan 'yar k'aramar yarinyar kalalata ma rayuwata har kad'ura mata ciki ko kunya baka jiba, yanzu ka san halin da tashiga ko kasan halin da iyayenta zasu shiga idan sukaji abinda yafaru da d'iyarsu?

6ata fuska yayi yace nifa ba abinda nayi mata  kuma ai maza dawaya take huld'a dasu cikinsu tarasa wanda zata lik'a ma cikin sai ni da taraina.

Harara Haisam yawurga mashi yace  ammah kaima ai kana cikinsu inma ba kai kad'an bane, wallahi kayi ma kanka karatun ta nutsu tun kan su Daddy susan abinda kake aikatawa sannan wannan yarinyar kabita kabata hak'uri inma son samune ka aureta.

Ammar bai bari yarufe bakiba yatari numfashinshi yace in aureta fa kace wannan 'yar iskan? Duk iskancin da takeyi zakace in aureta bayan bani nafara lalata da itaba.

Girgiza kai Haisam yayi cike da takaici yawuce yaficce yabar gidan.

Yana fita Ammar zama yayi saman 1 seater d'in da take gefenshi yakai hannu yagoge zufar da take fuskarshi cike da damuwa yace lallai Haisam ba wasa na d'ebo ma kaina ruwan dafa kaina inma banda abun Haisam ya za'ayi in auri wannan yarinyar, ni kawai sha'awarta nake ba sonta nakeba, mik'ewa yayi tsaye yafara zagaye parlourn yana tunanin yadda zai 6ullo ma Meerah akan cikin da take d'auke da shi.

Tunda tafita tafiya kawai take tana kuka cike da tashin hankali da fargaba tafiya takeyi ita kanta batasan inda take jefa k'afafuntaba tsakiyar titi tahau tana cigaba da tafiya kokad'an bata damu da tatsaida abun hawaba bama abun hawan da zata tsaida ta d'an dad'e tana tafiya chan tad'an jiyo horn d'in mota kad'an kad'an abayanta saidai kokad'an batayi niyar matsawaba tun tana jiyo horn d'in kusa da ita lokaci guda tadaina ji chan kuma sai tajuyo ta wata motar ta tafo dagudu still dai tashare tacigaba da tafiya tana jin motar tana matsowa kusa da ita tana horn ammah tashare bata ko waigaba kawai sai ji tayi anfizgota dak'arfi ta fad'o jikin mutum, ana fizgota motar tana wucewa dagudu d'ago kanta tayi dak'yar takalleshi shi kanshi kallonta yakeyi yace ke bakiji ana maki horn yanzu inda anbankeki fa koni sau nawa ina miki horn kika k'i matsawa ko bakiga saman titi kika hau bane?.

K'wacewa tayi daga rik'on da yayi mata cikin muryarta da tafara dashewa saboda kuka tace meyasa kajanyeni ai da ka bari na mutu na huta da wannan rayuwar dan rayuwata batada sauran amfani mutuwa tafi dacewa da ni,, tana fad'in haka tajuya zata cigaba da tafiya tana me cigaba da kukanta cikin sauri yasha gabanta yace wannan ba daidai bane kice kimutu kihuta banga amfanin tada hankaliba akan haka bayan kece kika kai kanki akayi maki cikin yanzu ne kike da dama kije kinemi gafarar ubangiji idan har kinyi nadama dagaske kin daina aikatawa.

Jinjina kai tayi tace daman nasani haka kowa zaimin kallon 'yar iska shin wai d'an adam baya kuskure ne? Ko ance maka nima da son raina na amince ma Ammar.

Tarar numfashinta Haisam yayi yace toh su sauran mazan da kikebi duk ba a son ranki kike biye masuba?.

Fashewa tayi da sabon kuka tace wayace maka ni 'yar iskace ina bin maza? wallahi ni babu wanda nata6a tarayya da shi sai Ammar shima d'in k'addarar rayuwace tasa na aikata haka duk dan inceto ummana ne bansan hakan da nayiba masifa zai jaza ma rayuwata ba, meyasa ku masu kud'i duk halinku d'aya bakuda tausayi kokad'an? Ko ance maku dukiyar da kuka samu dan ubangiji ya fi sonkune yabaku ita.

Ammar da yazuba mata idanu yana kallonta ganin yadda take magana kamar zararra yasa yace wai menene haka kikeyi baidace ace dan mutum d'aya ya yi maki laifiba kihad'e gaba d'aya kice duk halinmu d'aya.

Cikin d'aga murya tace eh d'in na fad'a halinku d'aya ba k'arya nayiba duk ba tadalilin kud'in bane mahaifinmu yak'imu, sannan duk ta dalilin kud'in ne dangin mahaifiyata suka k'ita da mu kanmu suka kasa taimaka mata alokacin da take cikin mawuyacin hali, shi kanshi Ammar d'in ai nunawa yayi yanasona ammah daga k'arshe yanuna jikina yakeso ba ni ba alokacin naso ink'i amince mashi saidai banda za6i dan lafiyar ummana ta fiye min komai, yanzu gashinan ta dalilin kud'in na kwaso ma ummana abun kunya wanda nasan sai ta k'wammace mutuwarta akan abinda na aikata, toh me zakace anan zaka k'aryatani ne ko ban fad'i gaskiya ba zakace? 

Ido Haisam yazuba mata yakasa cewa komai, rage sautin kukanta tayi tace kagani ko rashin tunani da hangen nesa ne suka ja min na amince ma Ammar ban ta6a tunanin irin wannan ranar zatazoba, katafi kawai kabarni ayanzu bana buk'atar taimakon kowa.

Haisam batare da yayi maganaba yajuya cikin sanyin jiki zai tafi, Muryar Meerah yaji ta ce kafad'a ma abokinka indai Allah yasa rayuwata takai toh d'anshi yana nan dawowa gareshi idan nahaife mashi.
Tsaye Haisam yayi batare da ya juyoba yarintse idanunshi yana jin ba dad'i acikin ranshi ya dad'e tsaye a haka sannan yajuyo ganin Meerah yayi ta samu waje ta zauna ta had'e kai da gwiwa tana me cigaba da kuka. 
Juyawa yayi zai cigaba da tafiyarshi sai kuma yaji baya iya barinta acikin wannan halin amatsayinta na yarinya k'arama, zuwa yayi yasameta yatsugunna daga gefen da take zaune jin mutum kusa da ita yasa tad'ago cikin sauri ganin Haisam ne yasa tace baka tafiba? Me zakayi a nan Ko kaima sokake kagwada min irin naka halin kamar yadda d'an uwanka yagwada min.

Ta6e baki yayi yace ko d'aya ni ba wannan yakawoniba kawai gani nayi bai dace kizauna a nan ba dan ba mutane sosai masu wucewa sannan kuma abun hawa zaiyi wuya a wajen tunda ba cikin gari bane zaki dad'e baki samu abin hawa ya wuce nan ba yanzu kizo muje inrage maki hanya.

Girgiza kai tayi tace a'a banaso katafi kawai ni yanzu idan natafi bansan inda zanjeba dan bana iya komawa gidanmu inkalli ummana da wannan abun kunyar katashi katafi kawai.

Wani irin haushine yakama Haisam ganin yadda yau shine yake nuna damuwa ga wata mace wadda kwatakwata rayuwarta bata ma shafeshiba, tsaki yaja sannan yamik'e batare da ya yi maganaba yajuya yaje yashige motarshi.

Ido Meerah tazuba mashi har yashige motar sannan tamaida kanta takife tacigaba da kuka.

Haisam kasa kunna motar yayi yatsaya yana kallonta dan baya ji zai iya tafiya yabarta dan shi ya san tausayinta yakeji musamman ma da yasan ta dalilin d'an uwanshi tashiga wannan halin, bud'e motar yayi yafito yatsaya jikin motar yana kallonta ganin batada niyar daina kukan yasa yataka yakoma wajenta yana zuwa tsaye yayi daga gefenta yace ammah kinsan wannan kukan babu amfanin da zaiyi miki ko?
Jin maganarshi yasa tad'ago takalleshi da mamakinshi sai gani yayi ta yi murmushi cikin kukan sannan tace kar kadamu da duk halin da nake ciki kaidai katafi kawai 
Cikin rashin damuwa yace daman ai ba wai na damu bane kawai ina ganin rashin dacewar kukan da kike alhali agidanku ana chan ana jiranki ayanzu gida yafi dacewa kije.
"Toh ya zanyi in naje gidan me zan fad'a masu tada masu hankali suma zanyi? kaga kenan zuwana gidan baida amfani"
  Shuru yayi nad'an lokaci sai chan yace baizama dole kitada masu hankaliba akwai ta hanyar da kinbi zasu saurareki.
Girgiza kai tayi tace bana tunanin haka, hankalin ummana zai tashi sosai gashi ciwonta baya son tashin hankali ina tsoron tadalilina ciwonta yatashi gabad'aya ummah itace gatanmu ni da k'annena ita kad'ai garemu babu wanda muke kallo muji dad'i sai ita babu wanda yake k'aunarmu sai ita idan wani abu yasameta k'annena su kansu bazasu ta6a yafe min ba wannan daliline yasa bana iya tunkarar ummana da wannan maganar sannan banaji zan iya rabuwa da cikin nan.
Ajiyar zuciya Haisam yasafke cikin zuciyarshi yace wannan itace tsaka me wuya,, chan yanisa yace ammah wannan ai ba wani abu bane insha Allahu babu abinda zai sameta na tabbata mahaifiyarki zata saurareki tafahimceki idan kika fad'a mata komai ni kaina ban goyi bayan kizubar da cikin ba.
Shuru Meerah tayi tana nazarin maganarshi hadarin da yafara had'uwane  yasa Haisam yace taso muje inkaiki gida kafin afara ruwa kinga garin hadari ke akwai sosai dan in kika zauna nan bazaki samu abun hawaba.
Ido tazuba mashi tana kallonshi ganin bataga alamun zai cutar da itaba yasa tamik'e dak'yar tare da d'aukar takardunta tabi bayanshi, Haisam gaba yawuce tana biye da shi har wajen mota suna zuwa yabud'e yashiga tsaye tayi tana jira yabud'e mata taciki ammah bai bud'eba sai ma jira da yakeyi tashigo ganin haka yasa tabud'e tashiga
 tada motar yayi yajata dagudu sukabar anguwar Meerah zugum tayi tana kallon titi still dai tana hawaye ganin bai tambayeta anguwar da zai kaitaba yasa tajuyo takalleshi tace ka san inda zaka kaini ne?
Batare da ya juyo ya kalletaba yace ai ke nake jira kifad'a min.
Jingine kanta tayi jikin kujerar saboda wani irin ciwon kai da take ji sannan tafad'a mashi anguwarsu.
Cigaba yayi da driving batare da yayi maganaba itama nan tad'an lumshe idanunta tana tunanin yanayin da ummanta zata shiga, muryar Haisam taji ya ce wace hanya zamubi?
Bud'e idanunta tayi ganin har sunzo anguwarsu yasa gabanta yacigaba da fad'uwa gabad'aya illahirin jikinta rawa yakeyi dakyar tabud'e baki tace kwanar dama, suna shiga tun kan a ida kai wajen gidansu tace safkeni nan ya yi.
tsayar da motar yayi tare da juyowa yakalleta a maimakon tafita sai tayi zaune tazuba ma waje idanu tana kallo ganin haka yasa Haisam shima yashareta, sunyi kusan minti biyar a haka sannan tajuyo takalleshi sai a lokacin yagane kuka takeyi, cikin muryar kuka tace nagode sosai da taimakonka agareni Allah ubangiji yasaka maka da alkhairi kayi min abinda bakowa zai iya min ba ka fahimceni alokacin da ba kowa zai iya fahimtata ba.
Hannu yazura cikin aljihu yad'auko kud'i yamik'a mata yace ga wannan sai kisayi maganin da aka rubuto maki.
Kallon kud'in tayi batare da ta k'ar6aba tace a'a kabarshi kawai nagode ina da kud'in da zan siya.
Ajiye mata kud'in yayi saman cinyarta yace bana fiddo abu inmaida indai naba mutum saidai yazubar idan baya so, zaki iya tafiya idan kin gama.
Bud'e motar tayi dakyar tafitar da k'afafuwanta waje tana shirin fita sai jin muryarshi tayi ya ce kitabbatar kin fad'a ma mahaifiyarki gaskiya dan duk wani 6oye-6oyen da zakiyi akwai ranar da dole gaskiya tabayyana.

Ahankali tace nagode sosai saida ta goge hawayen da suke a fuskarta sannan ta ida fita daga motar dakyar take jan k'afafuwanta tana tafiya gabanta yana cigaba da dukan ukku ukku tsoro da fargaba duk sun baibaye mata zuciya ahaka ta isa gida a zaure tatsaya tana tunanin yadda zata fara ce ma ummah idan tashiga.
Tayi kusan minti ukku a tsaye sannan tashiga gidan muryarta chan k'asa tayi sallama.


Haisam saida yaga shigarta gida sannan yatada motar cikin ranshi haushin Ammar yakeji ganin duk shine silar faruwan komai danshi har ga Allah ya taimaka matane saboda Allah da kuma gudun kar tadalilin haka tajefa rayuwarta cikin wani hali amatsayinta na yarinya k'arama, tsaki jaya tare da girgiza kai yace Ammar bansan yaushe zaka nutsu ba.

Meerah jin shuru ba'a amsaba yasa tafad'a cikin gidan, k'ofar d'akin Suwaiba takalla taga a rufe daman ita Mariya tunda taje garinsu bata dawoba, wucewa tayi tanufi d'akinsu tsaye tayi bakin k'ofa takasa d'age labule tashiga daga chan cikin d'aki ummah jin motsin mutum yasa tace wanene.
'Dage labulen tayi tashiga batare da tayi maganaba.
Ummah da take zaune tana linke masu kayansu da takwaso saman igiya lokacin da taga hadari garin kallon Meerah tayi tace ashe da kece ni naji motsi tun d'azu na ma d'auka su Safiya ne ina nan ina jiransu daga cewa zataje ta aro note book d'in k'awarta shine shuru basu dawoba,  har kin dawo daga asibitin?
Cikin sanyin jiki taje tazauna saman katifa sannan muryarta tana rawa tace  Eh na dawo.
Yanayin yadda takeyi yasa ummah tazuba mata idanu tace lafiya Meerah ko jikin ne yasa duk kika bi kika susuce, anrubuto maki magani ko?
Hawaye ne suka cika mata idanu ganin zasu gangaro yasa tayi saurin sadda kanta k'asa tare da k'ok'arin goge hawayen.

Baki ummah tasaki tana kallon ikon Allah.
'Dago kai tayi takalli Ummah suna had'a idanu kamar jira takeyi nan tafashe da kuka.
Jikin ummah sanyi yayi tace Meerah wai me yake faruwa ne kifad'a min kinga kinfara tayar min da hankali.
Tashi tayi daga inda take zaune takoma kusa da ummah tazauna tana me cigaba da kuka dakyar tabud'e baki tace Ummah.
Ido ummah tazuba mata tace ina saurarenki Meerah me yake faruwa?.
Cikin kuka tace Ummah ina cikin matsala,,, tana fad'in haka tad'aura kanta saman cinyar ummah tacigaba da kuka gabanta yana me cigaba da fad'uwa, tana so tafad'a ma ummah ammah tana tsoron halin da ummansu zata shiga.

*WRITTEN BY SIS NERJA'ART✍*




*INTELLIGENT WRITERS ASSO...®📚*
{On Ward Together}



*PAGE 27*

Ajiyar zuciya ummah tasafke tare da shafa kan Meerah da take ta faman kuka tace kinga Meerah kitsaidar da kukan nan hakanan kiyi min bayani wai me yake faruwa ne?

'Dagowa tayi daga jikin ummah tarage sautin kukanta kasa had'a ido tayi da ummah nan tayi k'asa da kanta.

Ido ummah tazuba mata tace toh kifad'a min abinda yake faruwa nima kinga kin tada min hankali.

Bud'e baki tayi ahankali tace ummah wallahi k'addara ce tasa na  aikata ba da son raina ba dan Allah kiyafe min. 

"Meerah kikwantar da hankali kimin bayani menene kika aikata? Wani abu ne yafaru?

Cikin sheshek'ar kuka tace Ummah cikine da ni,, bata rufe baki ba taji saukar mari asaman k'uncinta cikin sauri tad'ago takalli ummah da take mata kallon mamaki tace ke kinsan abinda kike cewa kuwa? Watau shegantakar naki har ya kai na kice haka banason shashanci ni bayanin kukan da kikeyi nace kiyi min.

Dafe k'uncinta tayi cike da tsoro tace kiyi hak'uri ummah wallahi nima ba a son raina hakan tafaru ba a lokacin na rasa yadda zanyi ne insamo kud'in da za'ayi maki aiki Aunty binta da Baba Muhammadu duk naje garesu ammah basu bada ba duk inda yakamata naje ban samuba saidai Ammar shine yanemeni yace indai na amince mashi zai bani kud'in naira dubu d'ari da arba'in da fari kamar ba zan aminceba sai daga baya da naga wannan itace hanyar da kawai zan bi insamu kud'in yasa na amince mashi.

Tana rufe baki wasu irin hawaye masu d'umi suka zubo ma ummah da tazuba mata idanu tana kallonta wani yawu tahad'iye dak'yar sannan tace Meerah dagaske kin amince ma Ammar kin siyar mashi da mutuncin ki? Kuma kina d'auke da cikinshi?

Had'e hannuwanta tayi waje guda tana me cigaba da kuka tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri nima nayi nadama daga baya wallahi bazan sakeba.

Ahankali ummah tafurta innalillahi wa'innah ilaihiraji'un wannan wace irin rayuwace shikenan Meerah kin gama da ni yanzu abun kunyar da kika aika min kenan shikenan ni akowane lokaci banda hutu sai shiga damuwa daga wanchan sai wanchan.

Durk'ushe k'asa tayi tace dan Allah ummah kiyafe min wallahi sharrin shaid'an ne.

Wani irin kallo ummah tayi mata tace sharrin shaid'an kuma? Wallahi Meerah kinban mamaki kwatakwata bakiyi amfani da iliminkiba yanzu rayuwar da kika za6a ma kanki kenan,,, fashewa da kuka ummah tayi cike da tashin hankali.

Ganin ummah tana kuka yasa hankalin Meerah ya ida tashi nan tacigaba da rusa kuka itama gabanta yana me cigaba da dukan ukku ukku ji take ina ma ace tamutu tahuta da dai taga ummanta tana zubar da hawaye saboda ita duk ciwon da takeji sai tanemeshi tarasa saboda tashin hankalin da tashiga naganin ummanta tana zubar da hawaye saboda ita, rik'o hannun ummah tayi tace dan Allah ummana kidaina kuka saboda ni hankalina tashi yakeyi idan naga kina zubar da hawaye wallahi sharrin shaid'an ne yasa na aikata.
Cikin kuka ummah tace dakata Meerah bana son jin komai daga gareki ke baki yarda da k'addara ba? Meyasa tun lokacin baki barniba in ma ubangiji ya kar6i rayuwata ai daman haka yatsara min ita k'arshen kwanakina kenan inko lokacin tafiyata baiyiba ko ba'ayi aikinba sai na tashi kice duk ganin da nakeyi maki da yaron nan abinda kuke aikatawa kenan duk k'ok'arin da nayi akanki ya zama na banza kenan tunda ke kin za6a ma kanki yadda rayuwarki zata kasance kin biye ma son zuciya kin aikata zunubi, yanzu gabad'aya kin zubar min da mutunci da k'ima a idanun mutane ace d'iyar cikinace take d'auke da cikin shege wannan wace irin *K'ADDARA CE* innalillahi wa'inna ilaihiraji'un ya ubangiji kana kallon halin da nake ciki naga jarabawa iri-iri ya ubangiji kakawo min k'arshenta.

Meerah wani irin kuka takeyi ji take ina ma ace bata aikataba da ummanta bata shiga damuwaba saboda ita, ganin ummah bata da niyar daina kukan yasa tarik'o hannunta akaro na biyu tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri bazan sake aikatawaba tsotsai ne.
Runtse idanunta ummah tayi tace Meerah banason ganinki dan ganinki ba k'aramin d'aga min hankali yakeba bana fata ubangiji yakaini lokacin da zaki haifi wannan cikin naki.

Sakin hannun ummah tayi tare da d'an ja da baya tana me cigaba da kuka tace kiyi hak'uri ummana ni kaina bazanso inkai wannan lokacin ba gafararki kawai nake nema.

Mik'ewa Ummah tayi batare da tayi maganaba taficce tabar d'akin waje tasamu tsakar gida tazauna tana me cigaba da kuka ahankali tana cewa wannan wace irin jarabawace tunda nake a rayuwata ban ta6a shiga tashin hankali irin wannan ba ya zanyi da rayuwata ace ta ko'ina ba sauk'i shikenan Meerah ta zubar min da mutuncina a idon kowa na duniya. 

Meerah duk'ewa tayi tana me cigaba da kuka tana jiyo kukan ummanta daga inda take ji take ina ma ace dukkan wannan abun da yafaru yakasance mafarki ne

Jin motsi kamar za'a shigo yasa ummah tatashi tad'auki buta tashige bayi nan ma tacigaba da kukan daga k'arshe da taga kukan baiyi mata nan takama ruwa tad'auro alwallah tafito koda tashiga d'aki ko kallon inda Meerah take zaune tana kuka batayiba tashimfid'a darduma tad'auko alk'ur'ani tafara karantawa dan jin zuciyarta tayi tana harbawa ga wani irin bak'in ciki da yatokare mata mak'ogwaro.

Ganin haka yasa Meerah tak'ara sautin kukanta tana jin wani irin tsanar kanta.

Su safiya ne suka dawo gidan tun daga zaure suke sallama har suka shigo ciki Sadeek ko dagudu ya ida shigowa d'aki yana cewa ummanmu ko bakunan muna ta sallama baku amsaba ganin ummah tana karatun alk'ur'ani yasa yakalli Meerah da tatura kanta tsakanin cinyoyinta tana k'ok'arin maida kukan da takeyi, safiya ce tace Aunty Meerah jikin nan har yanzu ko allura zakije wajen malam musa yayi maki, ummah tsagaitawa tayi daga karatun da takeyi tace safiya kuyi min shuru karatu nake inkuma surutu zakuyi kufita waje.
Toh Ummah nan taja Sadeek suka fito tsakar gida suka zauna.

Meerah naji sun sufi nan ta ida kwanciya saman tabarmar da take shimfid'e tsakiyar d'akin tana jin kanta yana juya mata har aka fara kiran sallar azuhur tasamu tamik'e dak'yar tafita, koda tashiga bayi saida taci kuka har tagode ma Allah sannan tawanke fuskarta tafito tad'aura alwallah tashiga d'aki.
Koda tayi sallah ta dad'e a zaune tana kaima ubangiji kukanta har aka kira sallar la'asar tana saman darduma ummah ko bayan tayi azuhur tashi tayi tazuba ma su Safiya d'an wanken da tayi nan suka ci sukayi bitar karatunsu na islamiyya ita dai zama tayi tai zugum abin duniya duk ya isheta ana kiran sallar la'asar tasasu sukayi sallah suka tafi makaranta ko ta kan Meerah bata sake bi ba tacigaba da hidimunta, abun ba k'aramin d'aga ma Meerah hankali yayi ba dan kasa tashi tayi daga saman dardumar inba kuka ba babu abinda takeyi ko abinci bata samu tasaka ma bakinta ba kuma ummah bata damu da hakan ba dan itama kanta bataci abincin ba.

Haka suka zauna babu wanda yatanka wani dan Meerah ko tad'ago dazumar tayi ma ummah magana da taga yadda tasha k'amshi tahad'e fuska kan dole take hak'ura tayi shuru, daga k'arshe kwance tayi a nan k'asa kukan ma kasawa tayi saidai hawaye da suke bin kumatunta ga wani irin zazza6i da takeji ga jiki ba k'wari saidai ita duk ba wannan ne damuwartaba matsalarta d'aya cikin da take d'auke da shi da ummanta.

Har su Safiya suka dawo daga islamiyya babu wanda tatanka mawa daga cikinsu ganin haka yasa duk sukaja bakinsu sukayi shuru dan sunsan ba k'asafai tacika son magana ba.

Har akayi sallar isha'i Ummah batayi mata maganaba hankalin Meerah ya tashi sosai musamman da taga har lokacin ummah bataci komai ba, d'ago kai tayi takalli ummah da take zaune gefen katifa ta jingine kanta da bango dak'yar tabud'e baki muryarta tana rawa tace Ummah.
Shuru ummah tayi tashareta duk atunaninta bata jiba dan haka tak'ara kiran sunanta.
'Dagowa ummah tayi tai mata wani irin kallo da yasa dole tashiga hankalinta.
Shuru Meerah tayi dan ba kad'an tatsorata da kallon da ummah tayi mataba kan dole taja bakinta tayi shuru, wani irin tsanar Ammar da cikin ne takeji ji take daman tun farko ta zubar da cikin ta huta batare da ummah ta san abinda yafaru ba.

Sai wajen k'arfe tara sannan ummah tasha ruwan lipton takwanta, Meerah ko a nan saman darduma tayi kwanciyarta tana me cigaba da kuka k'asa-k'asa, gabad'ayansu babu wanda yasamu yarintsa.


Haisam bayan ya safke Meerah gida yakoma yana shiga a parlour yasamu Ammar zaune ko inda yake bai kallaba yaratsa zai wuce cikin sauri Ammar yatashi  yasha gabanshi yana cewa haba Bro ina kashiga tun d'azu ina ta kiran wayanka ammah bakayi picking ba.
Wani irin kallo Haisam yayi mashi yace akwai abinda kabani ajiya ne?
Girgiza kai Ammar yayi yace babu ammah ai dole indamu idan naga ka dad'e baka dawoba, yanzu dai kayi hak'uri da abinda yafaru komai yawuce please. 
"Wannan kuma matsalarka ce Ammar kai yashafa baniba dan rayuwarkace kamar yadda kafad'a kana da right kayi abinda kakeso, saidai ina gargad'inka da kakiyayi ranar nadama dan kwatakwata baka za6a ma kanka rayuwa mekyau ba"

"Haba Haisam na fa fad'a maka na daina kuma indai akan Meerah kake magana kakwantar da hankalinka ni bana binta kawai dai sharri ne zatayi min sau d'aya nasan na ta6a nemanta"

"Wannan ba damuwata bace Ammar duk abinda kake aikatawa dan ka 6oye min toh aikin banza kayi saboda ubangiji yakamata ace kaji kunyarsa ba wai ni ba, kaje kad'auko min key d'in gidana kabani.

Zaro ido Ammar yayi yace me zakayi da shi?

Cikin idanunshi yakalla sannan yace chan zan koma inzauna ko akwai wani abu?.

Haba Haisam saboda me zakayi haka nan gidan me yayi da bazaka zauna ba?
Ta6e baki yayi yace baiyi komai ba bana dai buk'atar cigaba da zama a nan d'in saboda banison k'ara ganin wani tashin hankalin kaima zaka fi sakewa idan kakasance kai kad'ai kaga zaka samu kayi abinda kakeso batare da wani ya shiga rayuwarkaba.

Wannan wace irin magana ce kakeyi Haisam wai meyasa bazaka yarda da maganataba na fa fad'a maka na daina me kakeso inyi kayarda da haka.

Tun kan yarufe baki yace auren Meerah nakeso kayi.

Ido Ammar yazuba mashi yayi shuru yakasa cewa komai.
Shima Haisam kallonshi yake ganin baida niyar yin magana yasa yace ko bakaji abinda naceba nace ka auri Meerah.
Haisam meyasa kace haka kasan abinda take aikatawa kuwa?

"Ni ko nasani ta hakane kawai zai sa inyarda da ka daina aikatawa inaso tagane cewa abinda take tunani ba haka bane.

Ammah ai kasan ba mutuniyar kirki bace kuma ko kai bazaka iya auren me irin halintaba.

Shuru Haisam yayi sai chan yace idan har nine nafara lalata mata rayuwa toh zan iya aurenta dan haka ita kai ne ka6ata mata rayuwa babu wanda yadace ya aureta sai kai.

Girgiza kai Ammar yayi yace wannan ba gaskiya bane Haisam kayi hak'uri banaji zan iya aurenta dan bata cikin kalan matan da nakeso in aura bakaga da banbanci ba a tsakanin ni da ita.

Ammah ai tana cikin kalan wad'anda kakeso kalalata ko? A wannan 6angaren bakuda banbanci, Wallah kabi duniya ahankali Ammar idan ka san farkon rayuwa toh bakasan k'arshentaba kadinga tunani kafin ka aikata komai,,,, yana fad'in haka yawuce d'akinshi kayanshi yahad'a yad'auko key d'in da yake wajenshi yafito duk yadda Ammar yaso yatsaidashi ammah k'in tsayawa yayi har wajen mota sukaje yana jan jakkar kayan shima yana ja magiya yayi mashi sosai ammah yanuna sam ba zai zaunaba babu yadda ya iya dole yahak'ura yana kallonshi yaja motarshi yaficce yabar gidan, cike da jin haushi Ammar yakoma cikin gidan ganin duk akan Meerah ne d'an uwanshi yacanza mashi.

Haisam koda yakoma gidanshi k'ara sawa yayi aka gyara mashi tas dan dama tun da yadawo daga Abuja yasa aka gyara saidai bai zauna a gidanba, shima flat house ne mekyau babba irin na Ammar, bedroom yashiga ya ajiye kayanshi yafad'a toilet yawatsa ruwa bayan ya fito ya shirya saman gadonshi yahau yakwanta runtse idanunshi yayi yanaso yai bacci ammah yakasa tunanin maganganun Meerah kawai yake lokacin da take kuka tana fad'a ma Ammar tana d'auke da cikinshi, bud'e idanunshi yayi ahankali wani irin tausayintane yaji tsaki yaja tare da tashi zaune yadafe kansa da hannuwa biyu kukan Meerah ne yafara yi mashi yawo acikin kunne cikin sauri yajanye tunanin tare da janyo laptop d'inshi yakunna yafara aiki aciki duk dan yamance da abinda yafaru.



Meerah washe gari har sukayi sallah tagaishe da ummah ammah bata kalletaba bare ma tasa ran zata amsa mata koda ummah tatashi zata had'a breakfast zuwa Meerah tayi tace tabata tayi wani irin kallo da ummah tayi mata yasa ba shiri tabar wajen hatta su safiya saida suka fara ganowa ummah bata shiri da Meerah, haka tahad'a breakfast d'in saidai su Safiya da Sadeek sukayi ammah su babu wanda yaci.

Tsakanin jiya zuwa yau Meerah ta fita hayyacinta sosai ga damuwar rashin kulawar ummah agareta ga ta cikin Ammar ga yunwa da take galabaitar da ita ammah bata ji zata iya cin wani abu, abinda ma yafi bata mamaki ummah tana sane da rashin cin abincin da batayi ammah bata nuna damuwarta akai kullum cikin kuka take tana ba ma ummah hak'uri ammah ummah bata saurarenta dan ita kanta tana yawan 6oyewa tasha nata kukan.
