A ranar da tacika kwana ukku tayi wata irin rama dan ruwa kawai take sa ma bakinta ummah da taga abun Meerah ba me k'arewa bane da rana bayan ta gama abincin rana tazuba ma Safiya a plate tace taje takai ma Meerah tace taci, tana zuwa takai mata kar6a kawai Meerah tayi ta ajiye agabanta tatisashi agaba tana kallo har ummah tagama abinda takeyi tashigo d'akin ganin bata ciba yasa tace ke watau da na aiko maki da abincin shine kika tisashi a gaba kina kallo ko.
?
'Dagowa tayi takalli ummah da fuskarta kwatakwata babu fara'a dan indai kaga dariyarta toh da su safiya ko sadeek ne, nan idanun Meerah suka cika da k'wallah tace kiyi hak'uri ummah bana iya ci dan fushin da kike da ni ya fi d'aga min hankali dan yunwar banajinta koda ace zanyi wata d'aya banaji zan iya neman abinci indai har kina fushi da ni dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri da abinda yafaru wallahi nima ba a son raina na aikata hakanba inkin amince min yau sai inje inzubar da cikin indai har hakan zaisa hankalinki yakwanta.

Murmushi ummah tayi tace yanzu ke Meerah kina tunanin cikin shine babban abun tashin hankali agareni? Kod'aya ba haka bane koda ace kin zubar toh ana dai nan tunda kin siyar ma wani da mutuncinki wannan abun shi yafi d'aga min hankali akan komai ke yarinyace saisa bazaki ta6a gane illar da ke cikin hakan ba, akowane lokaci hankalina yana tashi sosai idan natuna da wannan rayuwar da muke ciki nasan wannan ya zama abun gori gareni ga duk wanda yasan abinda kika aikata kinsa bacci ya k'aurace ma idanuna saboda tsoro da fargabar ranar da cikinki zai fito na tabbata mutane zasuyita yi da ni sud'auka cewa nice nakasa rik'eku kokuma son abin duniyane yasa naturaki kika siyar da mutuncinki saidai ga duk wanda yasan halina ya san ba zan aikata hakan ba.

Meerah fashewa tayi da kuka dan ayanzu ba ma tausayin kanta takejiba na ummansu takeji da tad'aga mata hankali cikin kuka tace kiyi hak'uri kiyafe min ummah wallahi nima kaina nayi nadamar hakan insha Allahu bazan sake aikatawaba kuma zanje acire min cikin ko da kad'anne nasan hankalinki zai d'an kwanta.

Tun kan tarufe baki tatari numfashinta tace kul kar kikuskura inji kinje kinzubar da cikin nan bakisan aikata hakan wani zunubi bane zaki k'ara d'auko ma kanki ba indai har kika zubar da shi tamkar kin kashe raine dan haka kije kiyi ta neman gafarar ubangiji shine yafi cancanta kinemi yafiyarshi,, ummah tana fad'in haka tajuya taficce tabar d'akin.

Meerah duk'ewa tayi tacigaba da kukanta ahankali saida tayi me isarta sannan takwanta a nan inda take ahaka bacci yad'auketa batare da taci abincin ba.

Koda ummah tashigo ganin tayi bacci yasa tad'auko plate tarufe mata abincin tawuce tacigaba da hidimarta.

Da tafarka ganin abincin kusa da ita yasa taje tayi alwallah tayi sallar la'asar tana gamawa tajanyo kwanon tabud'e tafara ci kad'an kad'an tana yatsina fuska duka loma ukku tayi nan taji wani amai ya taso mata dagudu tafita taje tsakar gida tafara kwarara amai Ummah da Mariya suna zaune tsakar gida suna hira kasancewar jiya Mariya tadawo daga k'auyensu, mariya ce kawai takeyi mata sannu ummah ko sharewa tayi kamar bata ganiba, nan tayita yunk'urin amai bayan ta amaye d'an abincin da taci lokacin su Safiya suna islamiyya saidai mariya tataso tazuba mata ruwa a buta tamik'a mata takuskure bakinta dakyar takoma d'aki takwanta.
 Mariya kallon ummah tayi da taketa sauraron radio tace Maman sadeek anya baza'aje asibitiba kinga tun jiya da nadawo naga Meerah akwance ba lafiya jikinta yanzu bakijiba akwai zazza6i sosai.
Murmushi ummah tayi tace ai taje asibiti kuma tana shan magani kinsan yanayin zazza6in nan na wannan lokacin haka ake fama da shi.
"Eh kuma hakane gaskiya toh ko na hausa za'a koma mawaba aga ko andace?"
Ummah cike da k'osawa da maganar tace toh bari mugani ad'an k'ara kwana biyu idan anga ba wani cigaba sai akoma ma na hausan, yanzu bari intashi inshiga ciki inji ko akwai abinda takeson ci.
Toh Mariya tace 


Ummah tana shiga tasameta tayi kwance k'asa hannunta dafe da ciki, janye idanunta tayi daga kallonta tace ko akwai abinda kikeson ci.

Girgiza kai tayi agalabaice tace a'a ummah ko naci amai zanyi.

Toh ai sai kishirya kije asibiti ko maganin amai ne arubuto maki dan gaskiya bazanso kidinga amaiba har 'yan gidan nan su fahimci wani abu d'an mutuncin da suke gani na da shi yazube koda a yanzu ma badad'i nakejiba akan cemasu da nake zazza6i kikeyi alhali ba haka bane.

Ahankali tace toh ummah sai zuwa anjima yanzu cikina ciwo yakemin.
Wucewa ummah tayi batare da tace komai ba tacigaba da hidimarta.
Meerah juyi tayi tayi a saman katifa daga k'arshe tashi tayi zaune dan duk yadda takwanta ji takeyi babu sauk'i ummah ko duk shigowar da zatayi d'akin bata kallon inda take zaune bare ma tace mata sannu.
Su Sadeek ne da suka dawo daga islamiyya ganin halin da take ciki yasa suka tisata agaba suna ta jera mata sannu saidai kawai tad'aga masu kai dan a lokacin maganar ma kasawa tayi.
Suna a cikin haka wayarta tafara ringing cikin sauri Safiya tad'auko tamik'o mata ahankali tace safiya ajiye wayar kawai.
A kusa da ita ta ajiye mata wayar har tatsinke nan wani kiran yasake shigowa sai a lokacin takai idanunta a screen sunan Ammar ne tagani tsaki taja tacillar da wayar chan gefe.
Safiya ce tace Aunty Meerah ko ind'aga ince bakida lafiya kinga wannan kira na ukku ne yashigo shuru Meerah tayi takoma takwanta tare da janyo pillow tad'aura saman cikinta.
Ganin haka yasa Safiya tajanyo wayar tayi picking idanuwan Meerah a rufe suke saidai ji tayi tana cewa Hello me wayar batada lafiya kwance take, cikin sauri tabud'e idanunta tare da wurga mata harara ba shiri Safiya takashe wayar ta ajiye.
Jawo wayar Meerah tayi takashe gabad'aya cikin ranta tana k'ara jin tsanar Ammar da yayi silar jefata cikin halin da take ciki.

A chan 6angaren Ammar daman kiranta yayi dan yayi mata dad'in baki yasamu tahak'ura tasafko daga fushin da take da shi dan yasan ta hakane kawai zai sa yasamu tasake mashi shikuma yasamu damar k'ara tura kanshi agareta, ganin ta kashe wayar yasa shima ya ajiye tashi wayar tare da d'aukar key d'in motarshi yafita yabar gidan.



Alhaji Ahmad k'ibarshi kawai yakeyi hankali kwance kud'i suna shigo mashi dan banda aikin da yakeyi daga baya har harkar motoci yafara shida wani abokinshi ana kawo masu suna siya suna siyarwa nan arzik'i yak'ara ha66aka, hannatu ma abun ba'a magana dan ta samu yadda takeso saidai taci tasha ita kad'ai cikin gida takoma hajiya guda inkaganta sai kad'auka wata matar wani Alhajine na daban.

Ahmad kokad'an baya tunawa da wasu 'ya'yanshi dan aganinshi har yanzu bai isa yafara ajiye 'ya'yaba sai nan gaba idan yak'ara tara kud'in, matarshi ce ma yake d'an tunawa saidai ba wai dan tadawo mashi gidaba dan ayanzu gani yake ya wuce ajinta.



Meerah haka takwana da wannan ciwon ciki wasa-wasa saida tayi kwana ukku kwance tana fama ruwan tea ne kawai take d'an daurewa tasha dan ummah tun tana shareta ganin halin da take ciki yasa tad'an safko take had'a mata tea taba safiya takaimata daman duk wata hidimar gida a yanzu ita take abunta hatta awara da wainar fulawa da sukeyi na siyarwa cikin ikon Allah suna samun ciniki yadda yakamata dan wani lokacin kafin afita talla 'yan unguwa ma suna zuwa susiya dan haka tak'ara yawan wadda takeyi.

Bayan kwana ukku Meerah tana kwance tana ta juyi dafe da ciki lokacin ummah batanan ta je kasuwa daman tun kan tatafi ganin yadda take ta juyi saman katifa dafe da ciki yasa tace tatashi tashirya tatafi asibiti da toh ta amsa mata dan ita kwata-kwata a yanzu ta tsani abinda zai kaita asibiti musamman ma idan taga tadalilin cikin shege zataje, ciwon ne da taga yaci k'arfinta sosai yasa tamik'e dakyar tana ko mik'ewa nan jini yafara binta kad'an-kad'an hankalinta ba k'aramin tashi yayiba cikin sauri taje tad'auko pad tasaka a dudduk'e tagyara inda ta6ata sannan tad'auki kud'i tafita
 tsaye take a k'ofar gida tana neman me napep tsayuwar ma dakyar takeyinta gashi bata iya isa zuwa titi saboda ciwon da takeji motace taga ta tsaya a k'ofar gidansu ko inda motartake bata kallaba bare ma tasan ko wanene har saida yafito ya iso inda take k'amshin turarenshi kawai da taji yasa tayi saurin d'agowa takalleshi Ammar ne tagani tsaye ya zuba mata idanu yana kallonta saida gabanta yafad'i da taganshi cikin sauri tajuya zata koma cikin gida, rik'o hannunta yayi yace baby ina kuma zakije kitsaya mugaisa man saikace kinga dodo zaki gudu.
Kasa k'wacewa tayi daga rik'on da yayi mata dan jikinta ba k'wari dakyar tabud'e baki tace Ammar kasakeni wai meyasa kakeson shiga rayuwata, meyasa bakada buri sai ganin ka jefani cikin matsala, wai kai bakada zuciya ne?
Murmushi yayi yace baby indai akanki ne banda zuciya ban san ma inda zuciyartakeba duk abinda kikaga inayi maki sonkine sila yakamata kiyi hak'uri da abinda yafaru, d'an cize le6e tayi kad'an tare da sa d'ayan hannun tadafe cikinta, ganin haka yasa Ammar yace subhanallah baby bakida lafiya, folder d'in hannunta yakalla yace hospital zakije ma, muje inkaiki.
Girgiza kai tayi alamun a'a dan maganarma kasawa tayi, Ammar yana rik'e da ita yace muje man baby kishiga motar in baki iyawa sai inkaiki cikin motar.
Cike da dauriya tace Ammar kasakeni bazan shiga motarkaba please kabarni da abinda yadameni.
Shi kanshi kallonta yake dan ya ji tausayinta ganin yau ko fad'an da tasaba yi mashi bata iya yi, ajiyar zuciya yasafke yace kidaure muje baby Allah bazan barki kishiga napep ba ni zan kaiki wallahi ba cutarki zanyiba dagaske asibiti zamuje babu abinda zanyi maki, inko baki amincaba saidai mukwana a nan.
Ganin dagaske yake ga ciwo da yadameta yasa tayi shuru, ganin haka yasa Ammar yace muje please ko ind'aukeki?
Bata musaba tabishi tana takawa ahankali har wajen motar nan yabud'e mata tashiga shima yazagaya yashiga mazaunin driver,  jingine kanta tayi jikin kujerar tana cize le6e jan motar yayi dagudu yanufi hospital da ita cikin k'ank'anin lokaci ya isa wata private hospital suna isa tabud'e tafito dak'yar tafara takawa nan tafara ganin jiri Ammar ne da yafito yayi saurin rik'eta taso tak'wace ammah bata iyawa ahaka suka shiga ciki suna shiga nurses suka rik'eta suka wuce da ita office d'in Doctor saman kujera tazauna nan suka bashi folder d'inta da Ammar yabasu, Doctor d'in dubawa yayi sannan yayi mata tambayoyi akan yadda take jin jikinta tafad'a mashi har bleeding d'in da takeyi, sawa yayi nurses sukayi mata scanning bayan anfitar da sakamako aka bashi yaduba yana dubawa yace saidai kiyi hak'uri cikin da ke gareki ya zube daman k'aramine baiyi k'wariba tunda duka sati bakwai ne, tunda yace cikinta ya zube tafara hawaye dan kwatakwata ba haka tasoba tana ji yana fad'a ma nurses sutafi da ita abata d'aki akwantar da ita akwai injections d'in da za'ayi mata sannan za'a sanya mata drip dan babu ruwa a jikinta, tashi tayi dak'yar suka rirrik'eta aka fito da ita nan wajen taga Ammar tsaye yana waya wani irin tsanarshice tak'aru a cikin zuciyarta, kallon nurses d'in yayi yace ya dai har angama?
'Daya daga cikinsu ce tace za'a kwantar da itane kallon Meerah yayi da take hawaye ammah ita bata kalli inda yakeba ahaka aka wuce da ita aka bata room d'aya aka kwantar da ita.
 Doctor rubuta drip da drugs sai injections yayi yaba wata nurse takai ma Ammar da yake zaune kusa da d'akin nan yakar6a yaje yasiyo, Doctor d'in saida yayi mata injections kusan kala ukku sannan yasa mata drip 
Nan bacci yayi awon gaba da ita yana ganin tayi bacci yafito yace ma Ammar  yabiyoshi office suna zuwa kujera Doctor yanuna mashi  yazauna sannan yace kaine mijinta?
Ammar samun kanshi yayi da cewa Eh nine doctor
Rubutu doctor d'in yayi acikin file d'inta sannan yad'ago yakalleshi yace Agaskiya matarka tana buk'atar kulawa sosai sai anrage barinta tana shiga cikin damuwa da rashin cin abinci saboda yanzu gashinan tadalilin damuwar da take shiga da rashin kula da cikin da yake jikinta yasa cikin ya zube.
Tun kan yarufe baki Ammar yatari numfashinshi yace cikin da yake jikinta ya zube?
'Daga mashi kai Doctor yayi tare da cewa Eh saidai kuyi hak'uri.
Wani irin farin cikine yaziyarceshi cikin ranshi yafurta Alhamdulillah dan sosai yaji dad'i saboda daman bayaso tahaifi cikin, k'ok'arin 6oye farin cikinshi yayi agaban Doctor sannan yace bakomai Doctor ammah ita ina fata tana cikin k'oshin lafiya.
"Eh tana cikin k'oshin lafiya dan ayanzu haka ta samu bacci zuwa anjima idan tafarka mukaga jikin dasauk'i zamu iya sallamarku.
Toh shikenan doctor bari inje indawo kafin tafarka inma tafarka ana buk'atar wani abu ayi idan nadawo sai afad'a min zan biya.
Bamatsala za'ayi yadda kace sai ka dawo.

Ammar cike da farin ciki yafita daga asibitin dan daman burinshi kenan saboda koda ba'ace cikin ya zubeba shima yana da niyar yasa azubar da shi, gida yakoma yayi wanka yad'auko wasu files yaje office yakaima Haisam dan daman tun tuni Haisam yake kiranshi akan batun files d'in yazo yakawo mashi, koda yaje shi kanshi Haisam saida yalura da Ammar yana cikin farin ciki sosai dan bakinshi kasa rufuwa yayi saboda murna shareshi yayi bainuna ya kula da hakaba nan yazauna suka cike files d'in atare bayan sun gama Ammar yatashi yana sosa k'eya yanaso yatafi saidai bayaso Haisam yayi mashi wata fassarar, ganin haka yasa Haisam yafahimci nufinshi cigaba yayi da aikin da yakeyi sai shikuma Ammar yayi tsaye yakasa cewa komai dan baima san abinda zaice mashiba chan dai da yaga tsayuwar batayi mashi yakalli agogon hannunshi ganin kusan awa biyu yaci a office d'in yasa yace Bro bari ind'anje wani waje indawo please. 
Batare da yad'ago ya kalleshiba yace badamu.

Juyawa yayi cike da farin ciki yaficce yabar office d'in saida yabiya ta restaurant yayo masu takeaway sannan yawuce hospital d'in.

Meerah awanta biyu tana bacci saida aka sa mata drip leda biyu, farkawarta ya yi daidai da dawowar Ammar nurse d'in da take tsaye wajentane taje takira doctor nan yazo yak'ara dubata yatambayeta akwai inda yakeyi mata ciwo.
Girgiza kai tayi alamun a'a dan taji sauk'in jikinta ba kamar d'azuba saidai ciwon cikin da bai ida sakintaba ammah gudun kar ak'i discharging d'inta yasa tanuna batajin ciwon komai.
Fita Doctor d'in yayi yasamu Ammar da yake zaune yana waya yace gatachan ta farka zaka iya bata wani abu taci dan anaso tasha magani yanzu.
Ammar amsawa yayi da toh nan yakashe wayar yamik'e yashiga d'akin, Meerah tana ganinshi tayi saurin janyo kallabinta tad'aura akanta tare da gyara abinda tayi rufa da shi.
Murmushi Ammar yayi yaje daga gefenta yazauna yace baby sannu ya jikin?.
Banza tayi mashi takyaleshi
bai damu da hakanba yace yauwa tashi kici abinci ko kin samu k'arfin jikinki.
Bud'ar bakinta sai cewa tayi bazanciba kaje kace ma doctor yasallameni intafi gida.
Bud'e takeaway d'in yayi ya ajiye daga gefenta yace toh ai doctor d'in shine yace abaki abinci kici zasu baki magani sannan su sallameki.

Kallonshi tayi nan yad'aga mata kai alamun eh, janye idanunta tayi daga kallonshi nan tayi yunk'urin tashi zaune.
Ammar ne yayi sauri mik'ewa yakai hannu zai tasheta.
'Daga mashi hannu tayi tace kar kata6ani kabarni zan tashi dakaina.
Ammah baby keda jikinki babu k'wari kibari intaimaka maki.
Ganin yana shirin ta6ata yasa cikin d'aga murya tace Ammar nace kar kata6ani banaso wai kai meyasa kake abu saikace ba musulmiba.
Komawa yayi yazauna yace ammah ai ansan musulmin ne kawai daga zan taimaka maki
Shareshi tayi tayunk'ura dakyar tatashi zaune hannu takai tajanyo hijab d'inta tasaka takeaway d'in yad'auko yamik'a mata bata musaba takar6a bismillah tafarayi sannan tad'ebo fried rice d'in taci dakyar tayi
cokali biyar ta ajiye, Ammar da yake cin nashi kallonta yayi yace ba dai kina nufin  kin k'oshiba.
Eh na k'oshi kaje kakira doctor yasallameni.

"Ammah ai ba wani dayawa kikaciba kidaure kici man"
Batare da ta kalleshiba tace wannan ba damuwarka bace Ammar kabarni inma mutuwa zanyi inmutu nasan kana farin ciki da zubewar cikin nan"
Tarar numfashinta yayi yace kema ai kina cikin farin ciki baby bazakiso kihaifi d'an shegeba.

Kallonshi tayi a wulak'ance tace ko d'aya bana farin ciki da zubewarsa naso in haifeshi duniya tasan cewa d'an kane dan ni bazan damu da kowane zagi za"ayi min ba.

Murmushi Ammar yayi yace kice Allah ya taimakeni ba damar kizo kina yayatani ga jama'a kina cewa ni nayi maki ciki.

Tsaki Meerah taja cike da takaici sannan takoma takwanta tare da juya mashi baya tana ji Ammar yana cewa bazaki k'ara cin abincinba, ammah tashareshi tayi shuru.

Ganin haka yasa yajuya yafita yana cewa bari yakira doctor yazo yadubata nan ma bata tanka mashiba.

Yana fita office d'in doctor yaje yashaida mashi da taci abinci nan yatura wata nurse taje tabata magani tasha yace nan da awa d'aya zai sallamesu idan tad'an k'ara hutawa.

Ammar cikin motarshi yakoma yazauna yana jira lokacin yayi asallami Meerah sutafi.

Meerah ganin rana tayi nan hankalinta yatashi dan tasan dole ummah sai ta nemeta so tayi tatashi tagudu gida ammah batajin k'arfin jikinta dan ko mik'ewar ji tayi bata iyawa dan haka tahak'ura takwanta tunani tashigayi yadda zatace ma ummah idan takoma gida ba cikin dan tasan dole sai ummah ta tuhumeta a 6angare d'aya kuma farin ciki takeyi akan zubewar cikin ko bakomai tasan ummanta hankalinta zai d'an kwanta.

Bayan awa d'aya doctor yadawo yak'ara dubata sake tambayarta yayi idan akwai inda yakeyi mata ciwo nan ma tace babu dan haka yarubuta mata sallama yana cikin rubutun Ammar yashigo ko inda yake Meerah bata kallaba nan Doctor yarubuta mata wasu drugs yace asiyo adinga bata shi zai taimaka mata wajen samun k'arfin jiki, amsa Ammar yayi yace toh.

Bayan fitar Doctor yunk'urawa tayi zata mik'e tsaye takasa ganin haka yasa Ammar yazo yarik'eta wani irin kallo tayi mashi tace wai kai meyasa kakeson ta6ani na fad'a maka kadaina ta6ani.

Rik'o hannunta yayi yace toh ai taimaka maki nayi baby nasan bazaki iya tafiyaba kinga jikinki babu k'wari.

Neman tureshi taso tayi tace kaga kasakeni banason iskanci. 

Shuru yayi yak'yaleta memakon yasaketa sai ma yak'ara rik'eta sosai yace muje.

Tsaye tayi tak'i tafiya tace banason haka wai kai meyasa bakada zuciya ni fa ba zan bikaba napep zan shiga katafi kawai.

 'Daure fuska yayi yace daman wa yace maki ina da zuciya ai ko inda zuciyartake ban saniba dan haka mutafi malama inko ba hakaba wallahi sai ind'aukeki infita da ke.

Kallonshi Meerah take cike da mamaki dan a yadda ma yayi maganar bataga alamun wasaba batare da tayi maganaba tabishi suka tafi yana rik'e da ita ahaka suka fita, suna fita taja tatsaya tace saidai yasaketa tahau napep bazata shiga motarshiba.
kafewa Ammar yayi yace shi kuma bazai bari tashiga napep ba shi da yakawota shi zai maidata me napep d'in da tazo wucewace Meerah tayi saurin tsaidawa tun kan ya ida tsayawa Ammar yace katafi malam ba shiga zamuyiba da motarmu, tana kallo me napep d'in yaja yatafi.
Tsaye tayi wajen tarasa yadda zatayi ga tsayuwar bataso saboda jikinta ba k'arfi.
shi kanshi Ammar ya lura da haka dan haka yace Meerah kizo muje inba hakaba sai mukwana a nan dan bazan barki kihau abun hawaba ni ina nan gida fa zan kaiki ba wani wajeba.
cike da k'osawa tace bazan jeba kana tunani zan k'ara amincewa inbika.
Shikenan idan bazaki jeba ni sai ind'aukeki inkaiki bari kiga zan iya.
Ganin dagaske yake yasa tace wai kai meyasa bakada kunya bakaga mutaneba a wajen.
ta6e baki yayi yace ke kikasan kunyar ni bandamuba kowa ma yaganmu.
ji tayi k'afafuwanta sun mata nauyi tsayuwar ta gagareta nan tayi baya kamar zata fad'i cikin sauri Ammar yatarota yarungumota a jikinshi yace na fad'a maki bazaki iyaba muje inkaiki gida, babu yadda ta iya haka tabishi yana rik'e da ita suka isa wajen motarshi yabud'e mata tsaye tayi tak'i shiga yace kishiga man ko intaimaka maki.
Ganin yana shirin sake ta6ata yasa tashiga.
Kulle k'ofar yayi yazagaya shima yashiga had'e kai tayi da gwiwa tafara hawaye cike da takaicin yadda Ammar yake nuna mata fin k'arfi.
Fara tuk'i yayi ganin ta k'i d'agowa yasa yace Meerah ba dai kuka kikeyi ba ko?
'Dagowa tayi takalleshi fuskarta sharkaf da hawaye tace Ammar kana takura ma rayuwata narasa meyasa kake min irin haka wai ya kakeso inyi ne shin idan sona kake da gaske kafito muyi aure.
Jan burki yayi yai parking d'in motar sannan yajuyo yakalleta.

abun ya so yabashi dariya ganin ita tayi maganar ammah tana harararshi baiyi maganaba yakai hannu yajanyota jikinshi sai ji tayi ya rungumeta bud'e idanunta tayi a tsorace muryarta tana rawa tace Ammar menene haka please kasakeni banason iskanci

Wani irin haushine yakama Ammar lokaci guda idanuwanshi suka kad'a cikin 6acin rai yace Meerah nine d'an bunsuru?

A  wulak'ance tace eh d'in kai ai kafi ma bunsuru bata rufe bakiba sai ji tayi ya kwantar da sit d'in da take zaune tayi baya tsorone ya ida kamata tana yunk'urin tashi Ammar yakwanto jikinta, gabanta dukan ukku ukku yashigayi tace banason haka Ammar kasakeni.

K'ok'arin rabata da hijab d'inta yayi yace bazan sakekiba ba dai nine bunsuruba sai kinsan kince min bunsuru.

Muryarta tana rawa tafara furta na shiga ukku Ammar dan Allah kayi hak'uri wallahi dawasa nakeyi maka kar kayi min haka.

Bai kulataba yacire mata hijab duk yadda taso tak'wace kasawa tayi saboda jikinta babu k'wari

Wasu irin hawaye ne masu zafi suka gangaro mata tace meyasa bakada tausayi ko kad'an a rayuwarka dan Allah kasakeni kaga fa banda lafiya. 

Hannunshi yake k'ok'arin turawa a cikin rigarta yace bafa wani abu zanyi maki ba baby kawai dai zan ji d'uminki ne.

Kuka tasa mashi tace babu kyau Ammar kadinga tunawa da zunubi ne kake aikatawa dan Allah kasake....bakinshi ne yahad'e da nata yafara kissing d'inta.

Da dukkan k'arfinta tafara tureshi tanaso tak'wace ammah takasa daga k'arshe da tagaji saidai tayi lamo tana jin yana k'ok'arin tura hannunshi a cikin rigartata saidai tayi saurin rik'e hannun kyaleta yayi yacigaba da kissing d'in bakinta daga k'arshe yagangaro zuwa wuyanta tanaji ammah takasa hanashi saidai hawaye kawai da suke gangaro mata ya yi kusan minti goma ahaka sannan yadaina kissing d'inta janye hannunta yayi ds tarik'eshi yana neman rabata da rigarta kuka tasa mashi me k'arfi tana cewa dan Allah Ammar kar kayi min komai kaga fa banda lafiya ganin yadda take kuka yasa yafasa abinda yayi niya nan yamaida bakinsu yahad'e waje guda kan dole yasa tahad'iye kukanta sannan yabarta yad'ago mata sit d'in yakoma mazauninshi yazauna.

Kuka kawai takeyi ahankali muryarta har bata fita sosai saboda gajiya da zafin ciwo da bai saketaba ido Ammar yakafeta da shi yana kallon yadda take kukan a gefe d'aya na zuciyarshi kuma yana jin d'an dad'i akan abinda yayi dan ko bakomai yasan ya d'an rage abinda yakeji game da ita chan da yaga kukan ba me k'arewa bane yace Meerah bafa wani abu nayi makiba ya kamata kidaina kukan hakanan.

Kallonshi tayi fuska sha6e-sha6e da hawaye cike da tsana tace ai ban d'auka zaka k'yaleni a hakaba mezai hana kayi abinda kayi niya gawata naso kabari ba wai ni ba wallahi na tsaneka bana k'aunarka kuma sai Allah ya saka min akan abinda kakeyi min.

Toh wai me nayi maki koma menene ai aurenki zanyi Meerah kin san haka kisaki jikinki dani zakiji dad'in kasancewa da ni dan zan maki duk abinda kikeso.

Takaici ne yahanata magana takai hannu zata bud'e murfin motar taji anyi lock d'inta juyowa tayi tawurga mashi harara tace dalla malam kabud'e min infita.

Shima kallonta yayi yace saboda me zaki fita kibari in ida dake gida sai ma mun biya chemist ansiya drugs,  kaga Ammar bana buk'atar komai naka ganinka ma banaso dan na tsaneka kadaina shiga rayuwata.

Murmushi yayi yace dakyau  'yan mata saidai kiyi hak'uri tunda ni ina sonki kuma da kike maganar kin tsaneni ni har yanzu banga tsanarba bari muje inkaiki gida kar magrib tayi.

Dafe kanta tayi da hannu biyu saboda wani irin sara mata da yayi.

Jan motar yayi suka cigaba da tafiya kad'an kad'an ya juyo yakalleta ta jingine kanta da sit hawaye kawai takeyi saidai yagirgiza kai a gaban wani babban pharmacy yayi parking juyowa yayi yakalleta yace bari inje indawo.
Ko kallon inda yake batayiba bare ma tasan da ita yake nan yabud'e mota yafita  yana fita itama takai hannu zata bud'e tafita sai jin motar tayi a datse ba haka tasoba taso ace bud'e take ko dan tafita takama gabanta dole tahak'ura tazauna chan sai gashi ya dawo da ledar drugs bud'e motar yayi yashigo ya ajiya sannan yatada suka cigaba da tafiya.

Ganin yadda tahad'e rai yasa yace Meerah fushi kike da ni ko?
Banza tayi mashi kamar bada ita yakeba.
Saida yamaida kallonshi ga titi sannan yace kiyi hak'uri nima ba da son raina hakan take faruwaba duk abinda kikaga inayi sonki ne sila akowane lokaci in naganki ina kasa controlling d'in kaina.

Wani irin kallo tawurga mashi tace wannan duk zancen banza ne Ammar da ace dagaske sona d'in kake ai da bazakayi min abinda kake min ba a yanzu kaidai kawai jikina ne kakeso kuma kayi nasarar samunshi a karo nafarko kuma bana fata kak'ara nasara a karo nagaba saidai idan bana numfashi yau d'inma dan kawai bana da lafiya saisa kayi yadda kakeso.

Hmm Meerah kenan kina burgeni sosai dan na yaba da halayyarki kina da tsada kamar yadda komai naki me tsada ne gaskiya kin ciri tuta acikin mata saboda ba kowace mace bace take da irin damar da kike da ita sannan tak'i amfani da damarta lallai zuciyarki me kyau ce.

Tarar numfashinshi tayi tace kamar yadda ba kowane namiji bane yake da damar da ubangiji yabakaba ammah kawatsar da damarka kake yadda kaso da rayuwarka kana tak'ama da dukiya ai koda ubangiji yabaka dukiyar ba wai dan kabanzatar da ita yabakaba yakamata kazauna kayi tunani abinda kakeyi kwatakwata bai daceba.

Jinjina kai yayi yana murmushi yajuyo yakalleta sannan yamaida kallonshi ga tuk'in da yakeyi kamar ba zaiyi magana sai chan yace dakyau 'yan mata.

Tsaki taja tajuya tana kallon titi itama, daganan babu wanda yak'ara magana har suka isa anguwarsu Meerah suna shawo kwana tace safkeni a nan, juyowa yayi yakalleta yace akan me zan safkeki kibari in k'arasa da ke k'ofar gida man keda bakida lafiya.

Cikin sauri tace a'a Ammar kasafkeni zan iya takawa in isa gidan.

Shareta Ammar yayi yad'an k'ara matsawa saida yakusan kai wajen gidan sannan yasamu waje yayi parking d'in motar yana cewa toh ba gashiba nan kawai saidai kishige gida.

Karshen k'ulewa Meerah ta k'ule harara tawurga mashi tace ni bud'e min infita.


Ummah da tunda tadawo ganin Meerah bata gidan yasa tagane taje asibitin dan haka tacigaba da hidimunta ganin har la'asar Meerah bata dawoba nan tafara tunanin dad'ewar da tayi waya ta ara wajen mariya takirata sai jin wayar tayi a kashe nan hankalinta ya ida tashi ganin har biyar ta yi yasa tad'auko hijabinta tasaka tace bari taje general taga ko lafiya Meerah tadad'e haka dan tasan chan asibitin zataje tana fitowa tacikaro da mota tsaye k'ofar gidan
juyowar da Meerah tayi zata bud'e tafita sai ganin Ummah tayi tsaye k'ofar gida lokaci guda suka had'a ido wani irin tsoro ne yad'arsu acikin zuciyarta zaro ido tayi tare da dafe k'irji tace shikenan na shiga ukku Ammar ka gama da rayuwata ummana ta ganmu nan hawaye suka fara tsiyaya daga idanunta.

Ummah mutuwar tsaye tayi tana kallon Meerah cike da mamaki tayi kusan minti biyar a tsaye nan hawaye suka cika ma idanu taf ji tayi k'afafuwanta sun kasa d'aukarta dan haka tajuya takoma cikin gida tana jin zuciyarta ta cunkushe mata nan tafara furta innalillahi wa'inna ilaihiraji'un a cikin zuciyarta.

Meerah naganin ummah ta koma gida nan ta ida rikicewa tafashe da wani irin kuka.
Ammar da yake kallonta cike da mamaki yace lafiya Meerah me yake faruwa? Wani abu ummah zatace idan taganmu a tare ni banga abun tashin hankali a nan ba?.

Cikin kuka tace nabani Ammar ka gama da rayuwata akowane lokaci bakada buri sai na kajefani cikin matsala me kakeso ince ma ummah wannan ganin da tayi mana a tare na tabbata dole tazargeni wallahi duk muguntar da kake min kanka kakeyi mawa kaje ban yafe makaba sai Allah ya sakamin kwata-kwata had'uwata da kai babu alkhairi aciki dan Allah kadaina shiga rayuwata hakanan dan na tsaneka fiye da yadda kake tunani.

Meerah me nayi maki da zakice haka dan ummanki ta ganmu ai ba wani abu bane.

Girgiza kai tayi tace awajenka ne ba wani abuba ammah ni a wajena wani abu ne haba wannan wace irin rayuwace ace akowane lokaci ba sauk'i kullum cikin sani cikin matsala kakeyi.

Haba Meerah wai wace irin magana ce kikeyi idan kina ganin ganinmu da ummanki tayi matsalane toh kibari ni zanje inmata bayani dan tasan asibiti mukaje ba wani wajeba.

Tun kan yarufe baki tatari numfashinshi tace ban sakaba kar kakuskura kaje babu ruwanka da rayuwar gidanmu kawuce katafi kar kakuskura kak'ara tako k'ofar gidan nan dan na tsaneka ban k'aunar sake sakaka acikin idanuna wannan yakasance gani nak'arshe da zanyi maka a rayuwata,, bud'e motar tayi har ta safke k'afafunta zata fita tajuyo afusace takalli Ammar da yazuba kata idanu yana mata kallon mamaki nunashi tayi da yatsa tana me cigaba da kuka tace na fad'a maka karufe babina acikin rayuwarka kar kakuskura kasake tuna ka san wata me suna Meerah dan ni tuni na rufe shafinka a rayuwata sannan abinda kayi min kaje Allah zai min sakayya dan duk matsalar da nashiga kaine kajefani.

Ammar dafe goshinshi yayi yace wai Meerah me kike magana akai nakasa fuskantar inda maganarki ta nufa kar fa kimance aure fa zamuyi nan ba da dad'ewaba.

kashe mata baki yayi tarasa me zatace mashi saidai hawaye da suke cigaba da zuba daga idanunta tunowa tayi da ummanta dan haka taficce daga motar tare da buga k'ofar dak'arfi tarufe cikin sauri tanufi gidansu, Ammar kiranta yafarayi tana jinshi bata saurareshiba tashige gida.

Jingine kansa yayi jikin sit d'in da yake zaune tare da furzar da iska daga bakinshi shi kanshi yana mamakin yadda Meerah takeyi mashi yadda taso ammah yakasa rabuwa da ita kuma yakasa d'aukar mataki akanta ammah sauran matan da yake mu'amala dasu su suke lalla6ashi suna bibiyarshi yana masu yadda yaso, ahankali yace Meerah kin cika matsala dayawa akowane lokaci ace ba'a abun arzik'i da ke, girgiza kanshi yayi yaja motar yatafi yana tunanin maganganun da tafad'a mashi.

Tsaye Meerah tayi a zaure takasa k'arasawa cikin gidan ba k'aramin tashin hankali tashigaba ji take ina ma ace bata fitaba da duk hakan bai faru da itaba tunaninta d'aya tasan sai ummah ta zargi wani abu kuma taya zata yadda da abinda zata fad'a mata dan tasan mawuyacine tayadda da asibiti taje dan gabad'aya maganin da folder d'inta suna cikin motar Ammar tabaro bare tasamu hujjar da zata kare kanta da ita dafe kanta tayi da hannu biyu tace na bani bansan yadda zanyi ba shikenan ummah dole tazargeni tad'auka wajen Ammar naje.
Rasa inda zatasa kanta tayi ji take kamar kar tashiga gidan gudun kar taga 6acin ran ummah saidai babu yadda zatayi dan dole dai tashiga, gabad'aya jikinta rawa yake dakyar tad'aga k'afarta tafara tafiya tanufi cikin gidan muryarta tana rawa tayi sallama ahankali, Mariya ce daga d'aki ta amsa mata tace Meerah sannu har kin dawo, 
meerah batama san tana yi ba dan gabad'aya hankalinta baya tare da ita tawuce tanufi d'akinsu tana zuwa wajen labule tsaye tayi takasa d'agawa tashiga da ka ganta kasan batada gaskiya ta yi kusan minti ukku a tsaye sannan tad'aga tashiga idanunta akan ummah suka safka da take zaune a k'asa ta zuba tagumi tana hawaye nan Meerah hankalinta ya ida tashi a nan bakin k'ofar ta gangare k'asa tazauna durshen itama tana me zubar da hawaye .

Ummah rufe idanunta tayi da hannunta tana me cigaba da zubar da hawaye, murya chan k'asan mak'oshi Meerah tace ummah.

Ummah shareta tayi tacigaba da hawayenta, rarrafowa Meerah tayi wajen ummah tamatso kusa da ita cikin muryar kuka tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri wallahi babu abinda na aikata asibiti naje shine yamaidoni gi....katseta tayi tace babu abinda zakice min Meerah k'aryar banza da wofi kin kyauta da abinda kike aikatawa yai miki kyau babu abinda zance maki tunda wannan rayuwar kika za6a ma kanki kije kiyi Allah yabada sa'a inkin tashi sai kisan inda zaki kai 'ya'yan shegun naku dan ba'a nan gidanba.

Girgiza kai Meerah tayi tare da k'ara sautin kukanta tace kiyi hak'uri ummah wallah abinda kike zargi ba haka bane bana iskanci wanchan karon ma tsautsayine yagifta min nasan kinsan ba halina bane.

Idan ba halinki bane toh na uban wanene idan ban zargekiba wa zan zarga bayan gaki da ciki kuma na ganku a tare yanzu bansan abinda kukaje kuka aikataba.

Meerah kuka takeyi sosai har wajen magrib su Safiya da suka dawo daga islamiyya suna shigowa d'akin hankalinsu tashi yayi sosai ganin yadda Meerah take kuka dasauri suka isa inda take Safiya tarungumeta jikinta Kamar zatayi kukan itama tace Aunty Meerah me yake faruwa ko ciwon ne?
Shuru Meeeah tayi takasa magana dan kasa tsaida kukan tayi Sadeek ko lokaci guda shima yasa kuka.
Safiya ma hawaye tafara tace dan Allah Aunty Meerah kidaina kuka insha Allahu zaki samu sauk'i.
Meerah goge hawayen tafara yi tare da matsawa gefe guda tace Sadeek kadaina kuka jikin Meerah yafad'a cikin kuka yace Aunty Meerah wa yabugeki kike kuka ko ummah ce?
K'ok'arin 6oye damuwarta tayi tace a'a ummah bata bugeniba.
Toh wanene inje inrama maki?
Tausayin k'anen nata yakamata tace bakowa Sadeek kawai dai bigewa nayi kadaina kuka ka ji.
Toh Aunty Meerah kema kidaina kuka idan kin bige indai kinaso kizama soja.
Murmushi tayi tare da goge mashi hawayen da suke kwance saman kumatunshi tace toh shikenan na daina kaji d'an k'anena.
Shima murmushin yayi yace yauwa Aunty Meerah bari ind'auko maki tsarabar da nayi maki ta biscuit d'azu ummah taban kud'i nasiyo a islamiyya.
Shafa kanshi tayi cike da k'osawa da surutunshi tace je kaci nima na ci d'azu ga su Musa chan na jiyo sun fara wasa kaje kuyi.
Mik'ewa yayi dagudu yanufi k'ofa yana k'wala ma musa kira.
Murmushi Meerah tayi tare da kallon Safiya da tatsareta da ido tace ya dai Safiya?
Girgiza kai Safiya tayi tace bakomai Aunty Meerah saidai gabad'aya kwanakin nan kin canza kamar ba ke ba dan Allah kifad'a min damuwarki ni 'yar uwarkice wallahi ganinki cikin irin wannan yanayin yana d'aga min hankali musamman ma da naga kamar baku shiri da ummah ku dukanku kuna da hak'uri ban ta6a tunanin akwai abinda zakiyi ma ummah ba ki6ata mata rai dan mune masu yin laifi keko akowane lokaci gudun 6acin ran ummah kikeyi.

Hawaye ne suka gangaro daga idanunta tace shi d'an adam daman ai me laifine dole asamu sa6ani ko kad'an ne tsakanin hak'ori da harshe ma ana sa6awa bare tsakanin mutane tabbas nayi ma ummah laifi saisa take fushi da ni.
Cike da mamaki safiya tace Me kikayi mata Aunty Meerah.
Girgiza kai Meerah tayi tace bazaki ganeba Safiya ammah ina cikin matsala sosai kitayani da addu'a kawai.
Insha Allahu Aunty Meerah zan cigaba da tayaki ammah me yahad'aku da ummah?
Murmushi kawai Meerah tayi batare da tayi magana ba dan ita kanta batasan me zatace ma safiya ba.
Har safiya ta bud'e baki zatayi magana sai ga ummah ta shigo tace ke tashi kicire uniform d'in kizo in aikeki kafin ashiga sallah.
Toh safiya tace nan tamik'e tanufi wajen kayansu.

Ummah abin sallah tashimfid'a tazauna tana jira akira sallar magrib, Meerah ko had'e kanta tayi da gwiwa taruntse idanunta abin duniya duk ya taru ya yi mata yawa tana jiyo ummah ta ba Safiya kud'i ta ce taje chemist tasiyo mata magani cikin sauri tad'ago kai takalli umman tace ummah bakida lafiya ne?
Shuru ummah tayi takyaleta, safiya bataji dad'in shurun da ummansu tayiba dan haka tace ummah Aunty Meerah tana tambayarki.
Batare da ta bata amsaba tace kiyi sauri kije kisiyo kikawo min kafin sushiga sallah dan banason fitarku da dare.
Toh safiya tace nan tawuce tatafi.

Meerah bakinta taja tayi shuru tana nan zaune safiya takawo ma ummah maganin kar6a ummah tayi ta ajiye gabanta sannan tatashi takabbara sallar magrib dan a lokacin ana ta kiraye kirayen sallah

Tana kallon ummah bayan ta gama sallah maganin tasha nan takwanta saman darduma har aka kira isha'i tayi a nan tayi kwanciyarta, Sadeek da Safiya Home work d'in da aka basune suka d'auko sukayi bayan sun gama suka kwanta, tana nan a zaune k'asa wayarta tafara ringing kallonta ummah tayi nan duk tasha jinin jikinta takasa tashi tad'auko wayar dan ita kanta batayi niyar d'aga wayarba dan tasan me kiran bai wuce Ammar ne
 itama ummah shareta tayi batace komai ba haka wayar tak'arashi ringing d'inta chan wajen k'arfe tara sai ga yaro ya yi sallama cikin gidan ya ce wai gashi ance aba Meerah.
Mariya ce daga cikin d'aki tace ga d'akin chan je kakaimata Meerah tunda taji ance ana nemanta nan idanu suka raina fata tayi shuru kamar ruwa ya cita haka yaron yayi sallama babu wanda ya amsa mashi yace wai ance gashi aba Meerah, 
Meerah kallon ledar hannun yaron tayi ganin ta maganin da Ammar yasiya matane yasa tagane shi ya aiko saida takalli ummah da tajanye idanunta gefe sannan tace maganin ne aka kawo min ummah, shuru ummah tayi takyaleta ganin haka yasa tamik'a hannu yaron yashigo yamik'o mata ledar yafita.
Tashi tayi tanufi inda ummah take kwance saman darduma tace ummah kin gani maganin ne da namanta yakawo min zazzage ledar tayi  nan magunguna suka zubo daga k'asa sai ga 5k gaban Meerah fad'uwa yayi nan ta ida dabarbarcewa kallon ummah tayi da idanuwanta suka kai akan kud'in muryarta tana rawa tace ina ji mancewa yayi yasako min kud'in nan, maimakon ummah tayi magana sai ma mik'ewa da tayi tawuce saman katifa takwanta, Meerah zaune tayi wajen tana bin kud'in da kallo takasa ta6uk'a komai dan koda ummah bata fad'aba taga 6acin rai afuskarta, jikinta sanyi yayi sosai dan tasan dole ummah tazargi wani abun ahankali tace kiyi hak'uri ummah wallahi bansan yadda akayi yasako kud'inba nidai har ga Allah ban aikata komai ba kuma ban rok'eshiba

Bud'ar bakin ummah sai cewa tayi idan kin gama kikashe mana hasken mu bacci zamuyi.

Babu yadda ta iya hata tamik'e taje takashe glove d'in sannan tadawo tazauna tayi jugum dan kwata-kwata ita bata jin baccin ahankali tace meyasa Ammar yakeson jefani cikin matsala ya yakeso inyi da rayuwata ace daga wannan sai wannan hawaye ne suka cigaba da gangarowa daga idanunta tace ya Ubangiji yashige min gaba kakawo min mafita.
Nan wajen tayi zaune har kusan k'arfe d'aya sannan takwanta nan k'asa shima ba dan tayi bacciba abun duniya duk yataru yayi mata yawa.

Ummah ma idonta biyu tashin hankalin da take ciki yahanata tarintsa tunaninta d'aya ganin yadda d'iyarta da take so take ma kallon mutuniyar kirki ba taji akwai inda bazata iya shedar halinta na k'warai ba ammah lokaci guda ta6ata rayuwarta itace take bin maza har tayi cikin shege saboda kud'i, wasu irin hawaye ne masu zafi suka gangaro ma ummah a gefen idonta nan tamaida idanunta taruntse tana jin gefen zuciyarta yana harbawa ahankali tafurta ya ubangiji kakawo min sassauci acikin wannan bak'ar rayuwar da nake gani dafe wajen da yake harbawa tayi tare da cize le6enta duk yadda taso tarintsa kasawa tayi tana jin motsin Meerah da tasan itama ba bacci takeyi ba.

Sai wajen k'arfe ukku sannan Meerah tasamu tad'an rintsa shima saidai baccin yasaceta ba dan tasoba, ummah ko a cikin kunnuwanta aka fara kiran sallar asuba dakyar tasamu tamik'e tad'auro alwallah tadawo kasa yin sallar tayi daga tsaye saboda wani iri da takeji k'irjinta yana mata zafi ga jiri da yake d'ibarta saidai tazauna tayi sallah daga zaune bayan ta gama ne tatashi safiya tayo alwallah tayi sallah wajen k'arfe shidda ummah taso tafita tahad'a masu karin kumallo saboda su Safiya da zasuje makaranta ammah takasa dan haka tayi kwanciyarta saman darduma sai da gari yak'ara haske bayan safiya ta fito wanka nan tasa tayi ma Sadeek bayan sun shirya kud'i tabata tace taje tasiyo masu kunun tsamiya da k'osai ganin yadda ummah take magana dakyar yasa safiya tace ummah ko bakida lafiya, girgiza kai ummah tayi tace lafiya ta lau safiya yi sauri kisiyo kuzo kusha kutafi makaranta.
Toh tace nan tawuce tafita.
Meerah da tafarka bacci jin safiya tana tambayar ummah ko batada lafiya, mik'ewa tayi dak'yar tafita daga d'akin dan ita kanta batajin k'arfin jikinta sai chan tadawo takalli ummah da take kwance ta runtse idanunta tace sannu ummah ina kwana?
Shuru ummah tayi tak'yaleta koda tana jinta shareta kawai tayi dan ba kad'an takaita bangoba abu dama ga me hak'uri bai iya fushiba, komawa Meerah tayi tazauna tana jin duk ba dad'i acikin ranta dan fushin da ummah takeyi da ita yana soya mata zuciya ta rasa tayadda zata fahimtar da ummah tayadda da ita, daga k'arshe tashi tayi dak'yar tacire kayanta taje tawatsa ruwa lokacin da tafito wanka su Safeeya sun gama kalaci zasu tafi makaranta ummah ce tabud'e idanunta tace Safiya duba cikin jakkata kid'auko maku naira ashirin ashirin kun sayi wani abu a makaranta. 

Toh ummah yau baza'ayi awara ba?
"Eh bazan iya yiba saidai gobe idan Allah ya kaimu da rai da lafiya"

A'a ni lafiyata lau kuyi sauri kutafi kar ku makara naga sadeek ma ya fita d'aukar mashi school bag d'inshi.
'Dauka Safiya tayi tace mun tafi.
Ummah ce tace adawo lafiya
Nan tawuce tatafi.
Tana tafiya ummah maida idanunta tayi tarufe dan ita kad'ai tasan abinda takeji dauriya kawai take dan tasan idan tabari su safiya suka gane batada lafiya bazasuje makarantaba.

Meerah zugum tayi tana kallon ummantasu dan ko ba'a fad'a mataba tasan batada lafiya, ummah ce tatashi zaune dak'yar saida tad'an dad'e zaune sannan tamik'e tsaye jiri ne taji ya d'ebeta cikin sauri takoma tazauna, Meerah ce tamik'e tsaye a rikice tace sannu ummah me kikeso inyi maki?
Nuna mata maganin da Safiya tasiyo mata tayi cikin sauri taje tad'auko maganin takawo mata sannan taje tad'ebo mata ruwa, kar6a tayi tasha nan tazauna tadafe kanta saboda juwa da take d'ibarta tsaye Meerah tayi kusa da ita cike da tausayi tace sannu ummah ko zamuje asibiti?
Girgiza kai kawai ummah tayi batare da tayi maganaba tasa hannu tadafe gefen zuciyarta.
Ganin haka yasa Meerah ta ida rikicewa nan tafara hawaye tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri wallahi na daina.
Bata tanka taba nan tayi tsaye tana kallonta chan ummah takoma takwanta tarufe idanunta kamar zatayi bacci.
Ganin haka yasa Meerah tazauna gefenta duk a tunaninta bacci zatayi har wajen k'arfe sha d'aya tana zaune idanuwan ummah a lumshe, 
Dakyar ummah tabud'e ido ahankali tace d'ebo min ruwa a kofi, tun kan ummah tarufe baki Meerah tamik'e cikin sauri taje tad'ebo takawo mata, kar6a ummah tayi nan tafara addu'a dakyar takeyi acikin ruwan chan kuma sai ta ajiye kofin takoma takwanta tasa hannu tadafe inda yake mata ciwo tare da cewa wash,  ida rikicewa Meerah tayi nan tafara kuka tace sannu ummah dan Allah kidaure muje asibiti idan wani abu yafaru da ke ba zan ta6a yafe ma kainaba dan Allah kiyarda muje asibiti
Bud'e baki ummah tayi tanaso tayi magana ammah takasa ganin haka yasa Meerah tamik'e cikin sauri taje tad'auko hijab d'inta tasaka tace ummah bari insamo abun hawa mutafi asibiti bata jira jin abinda ummah zata ceba tafita dasauri tabar gidan cike da tashin hankali tanufi titi.

Tana isa titi tsaye tayi a kid'ime tama rasa yadda zatayi tatsaida abun hawa duk me napep d'in da yazo wucewa da mutane a ciki nan ta ida rud'ewa duk napep d'in da tagani sai tafara tsaidata ko da da mutane a ciki duk wanda yaganta a lokacin sai yad'auka ta zare dan tuni ta fita hayyacinta, ahankali yake tafiya da motar kid'i yana tashi inda sanyin A.C  da k'amshin da motar takeyi suna ratsa shi wucewa da yazoyi kawai sai yajuya yakalli kwanar su Meerah dan ya san tabbas a nan ne yata6a ajiyeta kallon da yayi ya yi daidai da lokacin da Meerah take k'ok'arin tsaida wani me napep da yake gabanshi ammah me napep d'in bai tsayaba, kallonta yayi dan tabbas ya ganeta har zai wuce sai kuma yagangaro daga saman titin yayo baya yatsaya saitin da take tsaye cike da mamakin ganin yadda take zubar da hawaye, Meerah tana ganin ya tsaya matsawa tayi gefe dan atunaninta tare mashi hanya tayi, wane glass d'in motar yayi yana kallonta sai kuma chan yabud'e motar yafito matsawa yayi gefen da take tsaye har lokacin bata kalli inda yakeba hankalinta yana ga titi cikin muryarshi me sanyi yace lafiya naganki a nan kina kuka?
Ji tayi kamar tasan me muryar nan tajuyo dasauri takalleshi tabbas ko ba'a fad'aba ta ganeshi d'an uwan Ammar ne, cikin muryar kuka tace dan Allah kataimaka min insamu me napep inkai ummana asibiti kar tamutu sai tsaidasu nake sun k'i tsayawa na rasa meyasa ko basuso sud'aukemu ne.

Waigawa yayi baiga mutum ba yace ina ummantaki?
Tana gida ina tsoron in rasata dan indai narasata nima na tabbata binta zanyi, ganin yadda take magana kamar bata cikin hayyacinta yasa yace muje inkaita asibitin.

Ja tayi da baya tare da girgiza kai tace a'a bazan iyaba ummah zata k'ara zargina idan taganmu tare zata cigaba da min kallon 'yar iska ni napep zan shiga na gama amincewa da kowa sai kaina babu me taimako saboda Allah a yanzu kowa kansa yasani katafi kawai bana buk'atar taimakonka indai ba irin na d'an uwanka kakeso kayi min ba.

Haushine yakama Haisam ganin daga zai taimaka mata ammah sai jifarshi take da maganganu baice komai ba yajuya zai tafi nan tacigaba da kuka me sauti wata adaidaitace tazo zata wuce ganin ba kowa ciki yasa yatsaidata aikuwa akayi sa'a yatsaya Meerah tana ganin ya tsaya cikin sauri tamatso wajen tace dan Allah muje ind'auko ummana inkaita asibiti?
Me napep d'in yace ina zaki d'aukota?
Nuna mashi kwanar gidansu tayi dan kasa magana tayi saboda kukan da takeyi
yace toh shikenan ba matsala hawo muje.
Ko ta kan Haisam bata sake bi ba tashiga suka tafi
Tsaye yayi wajen yana mamakin maganganunta watau Ammar shine silar faruwan komai duk sai yaji takaici yacikashi dan ko ba'a fad'aba yasan akwai abinda yasake faruwa dan ba banzaba tace haka chan kuma yayi tunanin ko tun wanchan abun ne da yafaru a tsakaninsu ita da Ammar yasa tace mashi haka, motarshi yashiga yatada yatafi.


A daidai k'ofar gidan tasu yatsaida napep d'in tafito dagudu tashiga gidan tasamu ummah kamar yadda tabatta hijab tad'auko takamota tace ummah taso muje asibiti ga me napep nan na samu bud'e baki tayi tanaso tayi magana ammah takasa meerah duk yadda taso tatasheta kasawa tayi dan haka tasaketa tafita dagudu tanufi d'akin mariya tana shiga ba ko sallama tace dan Allah kizo kikama min ummah mukaita cikin napep

Meerah me yasamu ummantaku batada lafiya ne?
'Daga mata kai tayi alamun Eh 
Tashi Mariya tayi tafito suka shiga d'akinsu Meerah nan takar6i hijab d'in tasaka ma ummah sannan suka tadata tamik'e tsaye dakyar girgiza masu kai tayi tare da d'aga masu hannu alamun suk'yaleta, Meerah ce tafahimci abinda take nufi tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri muje asibitin
Mariya ma tace haba Maman Sadeek kuje asibiti kinga kina jin jiki sosai muje ashiga napep

 Rik'eta sukayi tana takawa ahankali ahankali ahaka suka fito suka sakata cikin napep d'in sannan Meerah takoma ciki tashiga d'akinsu kud'in da suke tarawa tad'auko tafito dasauri taje tashiga napep d'in.

Mariya tace toh Allah yakiyaye Allah yabata lafiya sai kun dawo
Me napep d'in yaja Meerah tana rungume da ummah a jikinta ahaka suka tafi, me napep d'inne yace wace asibitin zamuje?
Meerah rasa wadda zatace tayi sai chan tace muje general.

Suna isa a daidai emergency tace atsaya yana tsayawa nan tafara k'irk'irin son kama ummah sufita me napep d'inne yace da kin samu wata ta kama maki ita bazaki iyaba ke kad'ai, lek'awa tayi taga wasu mata biyu zasu wuce tace dan Allah kutaimaka kukama min ita infita da ita, 
Matan basu musaba nan suka fito da ita Meerah tabiya me napep d'in kud'inshi sannan suka shiga da ita ciki, direct inda likitoci suke zaune suka wuce da ita nan suka ce aje akar6o mata folder,  zuwa tayi tabada k'aramin katinta aka fiddo mata babban dan dama already tana da kati ana fiddowa takawo ma likitan nan tasameshi yana tambayar ummah abinda yake damunta  daga gefen k'irjinta tanuna mashi, nan yashiga dubata chan yakalli Meerah yace daman tana da wannan ciwon?
Meerah d'aga mashi kai tayi batare da tayi maganaba.
Rubutu yayi a file d'inta yace za'a kwantar da ku.
Meerah tace toh
Wasu atendoji yakira yace sud'auko wheelchair sukai ummah ward gashinan zuwa.

Suna d'aukowa aka d'aurata sama aka wuce da ita cikin ward d'in da yake cikin emergency d'in

Kwantar da ita sukayi saman gado nan Meerah tayi tsaye tazuba ma ummah ido tana kallo tana hawaye kusan minti biyar sai ga likita da nurses nan sukazo suka tsaya saman kanta suna dubata chan likita yarubuta drip biyu yace ma Meerah taje tasiyo takawo asa mata, kar6a tayi cikin sauri tafito tanufi pharmacy tana zuwa tamik'a masu kud'i suka bata a hanyarta takomawa tana sauri saidai ji tayi tai karo da mutum har ledar hannunta tafad'i duk'awa tayi tad'auka sannan tad'ago takalli wanda tabuge sanye yake da labcut da ka ganshi kaga ma'aikacin asibiti bakinta yana rawa tace dan Allah kayi hak'uri ban lura ba.
Yace ohk ba komai, kallon na kusa da shi yayi yace Haisam muje.
Sai a lokacin takai kallonta ga nakusa da shi gabanta saida yafad'i muryarta tana rawa tace kaine? Me kazoyi a nan ba dai biyoni kayiba?

Ta6e baki yayi yace akan wane dalili zan biyoki.
Shuru tayi tare da ratsawa ta gefensu tawuce cikin sauri dan takai drip d'in asa ma ummah.

Doctor d'inne yakalli Haisam yace ya dai abokina ka santane?
Yamitsa fuska yayi yace eh toh na d'an santa.
Murmushi yayi yace dakyau abokina muje.
Shuru Haisam yayi kamar me tunani ganin haka yasa Doctor d'in yace lafiya Haisam?
Kallonshi yayi yace Doctor Saif inaso indubo patient d'in da wannan takawo.
Murmushi Doctor yayi yace ohk ba damuwa ai a chan ward d'inne daman nima dana rakaka nadawo chan zanje Doctor Mu'az ya kirani a waya ya ce inzo zan duba wata patient da aka kawo.
Toh shikenan muje ai ba damuwa.
Wucewa sukayi suka nufi ward d'in suna shiga Nurse d'in da take tare da Doctor tace yauwa Doctor ga patient d'innan, juyawa Meerah tayi sai ganin su Haisam tayi tare da likitan cikin ranta tace wannan kuma me yazoyi a nan?

Tare da Doctor d'in suka iso wajen
Ummah tana maida numfashi sama-sama Nurse ce tasa mata drip Haisam saida yatausaya mata ganin yanayin da take ciki, Doctor Saif ne yace ma Doctor Mu'az ga abokina nan kugaisa, hannu Haisam yamik'a ma Doctor d'in suka gaisa sannan suka fara ba ummah taimakon gaugawa dan ganin numfashinta ya saitu batare da ansa mata oxgyen ba, ganin yadda ummanta take shan wahala yasa tafara sheshekar kuka nan sukace ma Meerah tad'an basu waje sudubata, wucewa tayi cikin sanyin jiki tafita bayanta Haisam yabi dan yabasu dama suyi aikinsu, Meerah jikin bango tajingine kanta tana me cigaba da zubar da hawaye wajenta ya iso yana kallon yadda take kuka duk sai yaji tausayinta yace baidace kidinga kuka ba addu'a yafi dacewa kiyi mata dan ita tafi buk'ata awajenki.
Bud'e idanunta tayi da suke rufe takalleshi chan kuma tajanye idanunta gefe tace bazaka ganeba bakasan yadda nakejiba game da ummana ina tsoron inrasata dan itace gatanmu.
Koma me kikeji ai addu'a itace gaba da komai
Shuru tayi dan tasan tabbas maganarshi gaskiya ce saidai batasan yadda zatayiba.
Shi kanshi shurun yayi yana kallon wasu patient da aka wuce da su I.C.U tausayinsu yakamashi musamman da yaga danginsu suna kuka,
 Meerah zagaye wajen tashiga yi tana jira Doctor yace tashigo chan sai ga nurse ta fito da prescription sheet tamik'a ma Meerah tace aje asiyo wannan injection d'in yanzu za'ayi mata.
Kar6a Meerah tayi jikinta yana rawa tawuce pharmacy. 
Tana zuwa tamik'a nan sukace 10 thousand zata biya, tsaye tayi takasa cewa komai dan duk atunaninta bazaifi 1k ba, d'aukarshi batajiba nan yak'ara maimaita mata ajiyar zuciya tasafke tace yanzu babu yadda za'ayi arage wallahi kud'in da nafito da su basukai hakaba.
Girgiza kai wanda yake cikin pharmacy d'in yayi yace gaskiya baza'a iya ragewaba a duk inda kikaje hakane kud'inta.
Innalillahi wa inna'ilaihiraji'un toh kutaimaka kuban bashi dan Allah zuwa anjima sai inkawo maki.

Yace gaskiya kiyi hak'uri bamu bada bashi kidaice kikawo kud'in.
Tsaye Meerah tayi tarasa yadda zatayi tana neman mafita sai kuma chan tatafo cikin sanyin jiki tazo tatsaya bakin k'ofar ward d'in, Haisam da yake tsaye yana dannar wayarshi d'ago kai yayi yakalleta ganin babu allurar a hannunta yace ya dai baki samoba?
Girgiza mashi kai tayi kawai batare da tayi maganaba.
Ganin haka yasa yata6e baki yacigaba da abinda yakeyi, Nurse d'ince talek'o tace kin samo?

A'a dan Allah za'a iya bari har inje gida indawo kud'in hannuna bazasu isaba dan ya ce allurar 10k take inkuma akwai me sauk'i dan Allah ataimaka arage mana.
Ahankali tayi ma nurse d'in magana ammah Haisam saida yajiyota, ita kanta nurse d'in shuru tayi sai chan tace gaskiya babu k'aramarta kidaure kawai kije kisiyo dan tana jin jiki sosai allurar ce zata taimaka mata idan anyita.

Share hawayen fuskarta tayi tace toh Sister bari inyi sauri inje gida ind'auko dan Allah kukular min da ummana.
Shikenan kiyi sauri kidawo.
Juyawa tayi zata tafi sai jin muryar Haisam tayi ya ce sister wace irin injection ce za'a asiyo?
Fad'a mashi sister d'in tayi yace ohk bari inkawo, ko kallon inda Meerah take tsaye baiyiba yawuce tagabanta yanufi pharmacy tsaye Meerah tayi tana binshi da kallo har cikin ranta ba haka tasoba bai dad'eba sai gashi ya dawo rik'e da leda yamik'a ma Sister d'in tashiga da ita.

Meerah har lokacin kallonshi take shiko baima san tanayiba dan bai kalli inda takeba matsowa tayi kusa da shi tatsaya batare da tayi maganaba, d'ago kai yayi yakalleta yace ya dai?
6ata fuska tayi tace meyasa kaje kasiyo da kabarshi inje gida ind'auko kud'in insiya.
Just na siya ne dan kar ayi wasting time sosai kuma sunce yanzu suke buk'atarta.
Tsare shi tayi da ido har yakai aya sannan tace meyasa zaka taimaka mana ai da ka barni dan ayanzu bana buk'atar taimakon kowa zanyi amfani da abinda ubangiji yahore mana wanda kuma bamudashi zamu hak'ura mufauwala ma ubangiji lamurranmu, bansan dalilinka na biyomuba ko kaima so kake ka6ata min rayuwa kamar yadda d'an uwanka yayi, wai meyasa ko kad'an bakuson ganina cikin farin ciki? Me natsare maku?

Cike da mamaki Haisam yake kallonta dan sosai yake mamakin kalamanta matsowa yayi gaf da ita cikin idanuwanta yakalla sannan yace kar kisaki kiyi tunanin na taimakekine saboda wani abu ni nayine saboda Allah ba dan komai ba domin bakida abinda zan nema atare da ke sannan koda kika ganni a nan ba ke nabiyoba kawai dai mun had'une duk abinda nayi ba dan ke nayiba saboda mahaifiyarki nayi da kuma gudun kar kije kik'ara jefa kanki ga halaka saboda fitarnan da zakiyi nasan zaki iya zuwa wajen wani ko Ammar dan abaki kud'in 

Tun kan yarufe baki tatari numfashinshi cikin d'aga murya tace wannan wane irin zance ne kakeyi? Au kallon 'yar iska kake min? Inko hakane toh kayi kuskure domin ko wanchan lokacin ma da na amince ma Ammar k'addarace tasa na amince mashi ba wai da son raina bane danni ba irin 'yan iskar matan nan bane jikina me tsadane ga kowa ba wai a son raina na amince mashiba, ko ance maka zanje insiyar da mutuncina hakanan? kaje katambayi d'an uwan naka kaji wannan ba halina bane bana tunanin sake maimaita kuskuren da nayi a baya tana fad'in haka tawuce chan gefe tasamu waje tazauna saman wani d'an dakali tahad'e kai da gwiwa tana me cigaba da kuka.

Fitowar Doctor Saif ne yasa tayi saurin mik'ewa tanufo wajenshi tana cewa Doctor ya jikin ummana da sauk'i?

Eh dasauk'i dan yanzu haka ma ta samu bacci ga dai Doctor chan akanta yana cigaba da aiki, 
ajiyar zuciya tasafke dak'arfi wanda har su saida sukaji k'ara, Doctor Saif kallon Ammar yayi da shima yake kallonshi yace ya dai abokina?
'Dan yatsine fuska yayi yace daman kai nake jira ni zan wuce gida.
Ohk ba damuwa muje toh intaka maka.
Saida yakalli Meerah da itama take kallonsu sannan yawuce suka jera da Doctor suka fara tafiya.
Tanaso tayi mashi godiya ammah takasa ahaka suka wuce, har wajen mota doctor saif yarakashi suna hira sannan sukayi sallama yashiga motarshi yatafi.

Doctor Saif dawowa yayi yatarar da Meerah tsaye sai zirga zirga take a wajen murmushi yayi yace kikwantar da hankalinki ummanki insha Allahu zata samu sauk'i.
Allah yasa Doctor fatana dai ba irin wanchan ciwon da tayi bane dan banason takuma yin irinshi.
Kar kidamu idan Doctor yafito zai miki bayanin komai kinji?
'Daga kai tayi alamun toh nan yawuce yashiga.

Kusan minti goma da shigarsu sai ga nurse ta fito ta ce tashigo, bi tayi cikin sauri tashiga tatsaya daga saitin gadon ummah tana kallonta cike da tausayi inda ummah take baccinta, Doctor Mu'az ne tsaye yana rubutu a file d'inta inda Doctor Saif yake round sauran gadon patients. 
Doctor nagama rubutun yad'ago yakalleta yace nasan dai kinsan ciwon mahaifiyarki?
Eh doctor na sani shine yatashi?
Shuru yayi sai chan yace meyasa kukeson 6ata mata rai bayan kunsan ta dalilin haka har aiki akayi mata kuma na tabbata anfad'a Doctor ya fad'a maku kudaina bari ranta yana 6aci?
Kallon ummah tayi da take bacci tarasa amsar da zataba doctor. 
Ganin tayi shuru yasa yace gaskiya ya kamata kukiyaye duk wani abu da zai 6ata mata rai inko ba hakaba kuna gab da rasata.
Gaban Meerah wani irin fad'uwa yayi cikin sauri tad'ago takalli Doctor, cigaba yayi da cewa dan haka indai har kuna sonta dole kukiyaye dan Allah ya taimaka abun ya zo da sauk'i da sai ank'ara yi mata aiki ammah yanzu zamu d'aurata akan magani da allura muga yanayin jikin nata.
Toh doctor mungode sosai Allah yasaka da alkhairi, takardar hannunshi yamik'a ma nurse d'in da take wajen ya ajiye folder d'in ummah sannan yawuce yafita.
Wajen Doctor saif tawuce da prescription sheet d'in, Meerah zama tayi saman plastic chair d'in da aka ajiye ma masu jinya tazuba ma ummah ido tana kallon yadda take baccin wahala hannunta kuma drip ne akasa mata tana zaune sai ga Nurse d'in ta dawo da leda d'auke da drugs da injections nan ta ajiyesu a saman drawer d'in da take kusa da gadon sannan tace ma Meerah idan drip d'in ya k'are kiyi min magana inzo insa mata d'ayan.
Toh Meerah tace har nurse d'in ta juya zata tafi Meerah tace sister ina takardar maganin da aka rubuta inje insiyo?
Nuna mata ledar tayi tace gasunan tana fad'in haka tawuce tafita.
'Dauko ledar Meerah tayi cike da mamaki nan taduba taga duk drugs ne da injections cikin ranta tace toh ya akayi haka? Takardar maganin tad'auko taga duk anyi tick a ko'ina alamun duk ansiya daga k'asa akayi total 22 thousands. 
Zaro ido tayi tare da had'iye yawu dak'yar cikin ranta tace na shiga ukku ina zan samo wad'annan kud'in, tashi tayi tafita tanufi chan wajen da nurses suke zaune taga sister d'in da takawo mata mik'a mata takardar tayi tace sister yanzu wad'annan kud'in zamu kawo?
Dubawa tayi tace no ai anbiya ba sai kun kawo ba 
Zaro ido tayi tace wanene yabiya mana?
Murmushi sister d'in tayi tace nidai kawai ance inkawo nima kaina ban saniba kije kawai kikula da ita kar drip d'in yak'are.
cikin sanyin jiki tajuya tatafi cikin ranta tana mamakin ko wanene yayi masu haka.

{On Ward Together}



*PAGE 32*

Har wajen k'arfe ukku tana zaune ita kad'ai ummah bata farkaba meerah sosai tashiga damuwa addu'arta d'aya Allah yatashi kafad'an mahaifiyarta chan kuma tafara tunanin su Sadeek da sukaje school cikin ranta tace ko ya zasu k'are idan suka koma gida tunowa da tayi mariya tana nan sai tad'anji sauk'i dan tasan zata d'an kula da su.

Tana nan zaune sai wajen k'arfe biyar Doctor yazo yak'ara yi ma ummah injection yace ma Meerah indai ta farka taje tafad'a masu Meerah tace toh.

Kiran wayar Mariya tayi nan mariya tatambayeta jikin ummansu tace dasauk'i anma kwantar da mu
Kwanciya fa kikace Meerah ashe jikin nata ya tsananta
Eh ammah dasauk'i anmayi mata allura yanzu haka bacci takeyi.
Allah sarki Allah yabata lafiya yasa kaffarane.
Ameen ya rabbi, su sadeek sun dawo dai ko?
Mariya tace eh sunci abinci har suntafi islamiyya, ajiyar zuciya tasafke tace toh shikenan sadeek baidai yi rigima ba ko?
Eh toh ya d'an ta6a rigima ammah kad'an dan lokacin da suka dawone yayi rigimar ammah daga baya yayi shuru.
Toh shikenan ai nasan zai daina  tunda ga Safiya nan kusa da shi.
Hakane, Allah dai yatashi kafad'unta.
Ameen ya rabbi mungode sosai.
Haba bakomai Allah dai yabata lafiya.

Suna gama wayar tagumi tazuba tana kallon ummantasu daga k'arshe da taji yunwa tana cinta tamik'e takalli me jinyar wata patient takusa da gadon ummah tace dan Allah bari inje indawo kid'an kular min da ita ko da ta farka.

Matar tace bakomai kije, wucewa Meerah tayi tafita taje chan wani shago da yake cikin asibitin tayo takeaway da nata da na ummah tasiyo ruwa leda biyu tadawo tasamu ummah bata farkaba godiya tayi ma matar tazauna nan tabud'e takeaway d'inta tafara ci duk yunwar da takeji ammah bataci na kirkiba ta ajiye dan ji tayi abincin ya fita ranta.
 wasa-wasa har sukayi sallar isha'i ummah bata farkaba.


Haisam koda yabar asibitin wajen aikinsu yaje yana zuwa direct office d'in Ammar yawuce ko izini bai nemaba yatura yashiga lokacin Ammar yana zaune yana dannar system ganin Haisam ne yashigo yasa yature system d'in gefe yamik'e tsaye yace Bro.
Batare da yayi magana yaje yaja kujerar da take opposite d'in wadda Ammar yake yazauna ganin haka yasa Ammar shima yazauna yace tun d'azu nake nemanka ina kashige da alama ma yau baka shigo aikiba.


Agogon da take manne a tsintsiyar hannunshi yakalla sannan yace eh ban samu nashigoba sai yanzu.

Ohk, hope dai lafiya ce ta6oye ka dan ni nan so nayi da nagama abinda nake zanje gida indubaka inga ko lafiya tunda yanzu ka raba mana gida.

Yamitsa fuska yayi yace lafiyata lau na d'anje wani wajene, yauwa intambayeka mana.

Ido Ammar yazuba mashi yace ina jinka Bro.

Shuru Haisam yayi sai chan yalleshi yace wai ina wannan yarinyar da take da ciki kana dai kula da ita ko?

lokaci guda Ammar yahad'e fuska yace wai menene haka me kake nufi dani na fa fad'a maka ba cikina bane kawai sharri take son ja min.

Jinjina kai yayi yace a kullum haka kake cewa Ammar ammah ai zata tahaihu.

Koma ba haka bane babu yadda zatayi dan cikin ma da yake jikinta da mukaje asibiti cewa akayi cikin ya zube.

Wani irin kallo Haisam yayi mashi yace ban gane nufinkaba kana nufin zuwa kukayi akace cikin ya zube?

'Daga kafad'a yayi cikin rashin damuwa yace haka nake nufi man ba dai daman aja min sharri ace d'an shege nawane.

Murmushi Haisam yayi yace gaskiyarka ko saidai ni dai bazan ta6a yarda da kaiba kuma wannan cikin ina zargin da sa hannunka wajen zubewarsa.

Mik'ewa Ammar yayi tsaye yace haba Haisam wai wace irin maganace kakeyi haka wai meyasa baka yarda da abinda zance maka ni fa bani nazubar mata da cikiba bansan komai ba akan zubewarsa kuma ma menene abun damuwa dan cikin da bana sunna ya zubeba? Kaga dan Allah kadaina wannan maganar kar ma su mum suji.

Girgiza kai Haisam yayi yace bazan ta6a damuwaba dan sunji dan banga abun 6oyewaba acikin wannan halin naka koda ni ban bari sunjiba akwai ranar k'in dillanci dakansu zasu gani da idanunsu,,,,yana fad'in haka yamik'e yawuce yafita daga office d'in yabar Ammar tsaye yana binshi da kallo cike da mamakin maganganun da yayada mashi.

Haisam koda yakoma office d'inshi zama yayi yana tunanin maganar Ammar da yace cikin Meerah ya zube saidai ko kad'an bai yarda da hakanba cikin ranshi yace anya ba dad'in baki yayimataba akaje aka zubar da cikin, kokuma yabata kud'i sukaje suka zubar,,, chan kuma yaja tsaki tare da janyo system d'inshi yakunna.

Ammar wajen k'arfe biyu yabar office d'inshi saida yabiya restaurant yaci abinci sannan yawuce gida.

Haisam ko har bayan la'asar yana nan dan lunch ma saidai yabada kud'i aka siyo aka kawo mashi a nan yaci sai wajen biyar sannan yatattara komai shima  yatafi gidanshi.



Meerah har wajen k'arfe goma tana nan zaune saman plastic chair hankalinta ya ida tashi ganin har lokacin ummah bata farkaba wata nurse ce da tashigo yi ma wata patient allura bayan ta yi Meerah takirata idanuwanta cike taf da hawaye tace dan Allah sister kizo kiduba min ummana har yanzu bata farkaba tun safe. 

Kallon ummah sister d'in tayi sannan tace kar kidamu tana baccine wannan baccin da takeyi hutu ne kar kitasheta kibari sai ta farka da kanta.
Meerah tace toh sister nagode sosai.

Wajen k'arfe sha biyu a nan saman Kujerar bacci yad'auketa saidai tad'aura kanta a kan gadon ummah saboda batazo da ko tabarmaba babu abinda tad'auko masu ita kanta baccin wahala tayi dan ba ita tafarkaba sai gab da asuba nan tatashi taje tayo alwallah ta aro darduma wajen mak'otan gadonsu tashimfid'a tafara gabatar da raka'atanil fijr tana zaune tana lazimi aka kira sallar asuba tatashi tagabatar da sallah

Wajen k'arfe takwas Doctor yazo round yana zuwa wajen gadonsu yace ma Meerah har yanzu bata farkaba?
Meerah tace Eh doctor. 
Yace ohk ba matsala ai anason baccin saidai matsalar rashin cin abincin da batayi ammah bari muga zuwa anjima idan bata farkaba sai muga yadda za'ayi.
Meerah tace toh doctor mungode nan yawuce gado na gabansu.

Wajen k'arfe tara takira Mariya a waya suka gaisa tatambayeta su Safiya sun tafi makaranta tace eh sun tafi ammah fa dakyar suje da sunce bazasujeba wajenku zasuzo hatta safiya saida tayi kuka.

Jinjina kai Meerah tayi tace babba da ita tayi kuka, ita da zata lallashi Sadeek.

Kedai bari kalacima k'inyi sukayi da nahad'a masu saidai naga sun siyo wainar gero sun ci kafin sutafi.

Meerah tace toh shikenan mungode sosai da d'awainiya.
Haba bakomai, ai da anjima zamu shigo ni da suwaiba sai kifad'i abinda kukeso akawo maku
Nan Meerah tafad'a mata duk abinda zata d'auko mata a d'akinsu sannan sukayi sallama tace sai sunzo.

Tana nan zaune har wajen sha biyu sai ga Mariya da Suwaiba sunzo nan suka gaisa tare da tambayar me jiki tace dasauk'i, kayan da tace sukawo matane suka bata tare da kular abinci nan tayi masu godiya sosai sannan tatashi taje tawanke d'aya daga cikin toilet d'in da suke ward d'in tas da klin tayi wanka tad'auro alwallah a chan ciki tasaka kayanta tafito saboda mutane 'yan dubiya da suke zuwa cikin ward d'in har da maza.

Bayan ta shafa mai ne mariya tace taci abinci d'an kad'an tazuba taci dan ko breakfast batayiba nan suka d'anyi hira kad'an.

Sai wajen azuhur sannan suka tafi gida saboda 'yan makaranta da zasu dawo, godiya Meerah tayi masu tarakasu har wajen ward d'in sannan tadawo.

Wajen k'arfe biyu tana zaune tana karatun alk'ur'ani cikin zuciyarta chan taga ummah ta fara motsi, rufe alk'ur'anin tayi ta ajiye tare da mik'ewa cikin sauri tana kallon ummah, cigaba tayi da motsa hannunta ahankali chan kuma sai tabud'e ido.
Meerah na ganin haka wani irin farin ciki yakamata lokaci guda tafurta Alhamdulillah sannan tawuce dagudu tanufi wajen Doctors tana zuwa tashaida masu farkawar patient d'inta, likita d'aya ne da nurse suka tashi suka biyo bayanta suna zuwa suka sameta idanunta bud'e tana kallon ceiling, Meerah gefenta tatsaya tana ta jera mata sannu, d'aga mata kai kawai ummah tayi tana kallonta.
Nurse d'inma sannu tayi ma ummah
 Doctor injection yafara yi mata sannan yace ma Meerah abata d'an tea tasha.
Da toh ta amsa mashi nan tad'auki cup tafita taje tasiyo mata ruwan zafi tasiya madara peak d'aya da sugar tadawo had'a mata tayi nan tabarshi yad'an huce sannan tataimaka mata tatashi zaune tajingineta jikin pillow sannan takai mata cup d'in a baki.

Janye kai ummah tayi alamun bazata sha ba.
Meerah kamar zatayi kuka tace dan Allah ummah kiyi hak'uri kisha kinga duk jiya baki ci komai ba bacci kikeyi.

Kallonta ummah take dan ita kanta Meerah ta fita hayyacinta kamar itace me jiyyar ganin kamar zatayi kuka yasa ummah tayarda tasha tea d'in saida tasha fiye da rabi sannan Meerah tabata maganin da Doctor yace tabata idan tasha tea.

Tana shan maganin takoma takwanta nan bacci yad'auketa. 






Hajiya Binta ce zaune a gidan Yayansu da takaima ziyara bayan sun gaisa da matarshi nan suka d'an ta6a hira kad'an dan ita kanta Hajiya Binta ba sosai matarshi take sakar mata fuskaba saidai bata wulak'antata kamar yadda takeyi ma Maryam, yaranshi duk saida suka fito suka gaisheta dan ita basuyi mata kamar yadda suke ma maryam saboda ita Hajiya binta fad'a takeyi masu indai basuzo suka gaishetaba dan tana da fad'a sosai kuma suna shakkarta sa6anin maryam da batada hayaniya, abinci da abinsha ne aka kawo mata nan tad'anci sannan tatambayeta yayan nata 
Hajiya ramatu saida tad'an 6ata fuska sannan tace yana cikin d'akinshi dan ita a rayuwarta ta k'i jinin wanda zai ra6i mijinta hatta danginshi saboda kar yabasu wani abu dad'inta d'aya da ba ma badawa yakeyi ba sosai.

Tashi Hajiya Binta tayi tanufi hanyar d'akinshi dan dama tasan halin matar tashi, tana shiga da sallamarta tasameshi zaune a parlour yana kallo, ganinta yasa yarage volume tare da cewa Binta yau kece a gidan namu?
Murmushi tayi tace eh Yaya nice saida tazauna sannan tagaisheshi ya amsa
Bayan sun gaisa ne tatambayeshi yara yace Alhamdulillah nan suka d'an ta6a hira dan suna shiri sosai a tsakaninsu ta sha tayo mashi pep chicken ta aiko mashi ko wani abu na dad'i saisa yafi sonta akan maryam kuma ita ko tazo bata cika tambayarshi wani abu ba saisa bai damuwa idan tazo.

Suna cikin hirar ne tace wai ni Alhaji ina maryam tana ko zuwa gidan nan?

Shuru yayi sai chan yace Binta ai ke zan tambaya maryam dan ni ta fi k'arfina tun lokacin da aurenta yamutu nace tamaida yaranta gidan ubansu rabon da inganta saidai 'ya'yan take turo min shima idan tanaso inbata wani abu ammah kwata-kwata bata zuwa tagaisheni koda dama chan idan tazo wani abu takeso inbata saisa ma nafita harkarta.

Toh nima haka take min wallahi yaya narasa me yake damun Maryam ni tun lokacin da d'iyarta tazo tace min tana asibiti yanzu bansan halin da suke cikiba, haba kodai ba komai ya kamata taturo d'iyarta tafad'a min ko ta samu sauk'i dan ni ba sanin gidanta nayiba naga kamar ma bataso asan gidan nata 6oyenshi take.

Alhaji Muhammadu yace toh me gareta a gidan da zatayi 6oyo tasan dai babu abinda zamu nema a wajenta indai wannan rayuwar taza6a ma kanta toh taje tayi ita tasani.

Wallahi kau yaya ita tasani daman nunawa takeyi yaranta sunfimu sau dubu a wajenta bata had'asu da kowaba ai gata gasu sai suci abinda zasuci mudai nan babu me neman wani abu gareta Alhamdulillah muna da rufin asirinmu.

Gaskiyarki ai ni tuni nagano haka saisa ma kikaga na fita harkarta.

Ai hakan yakamata ko tajima ko tadad'e dole dai tadawo mana dan mune nata, Allah dai yakyauta.

Ammen ya rabbi.
Toh yaya ni bari intashi intafi daman cewa nayi bari inzo mugaisa kwana biyu bamu had'uba.

aikuwa dai kin kyauta kuma naji dad'i bari inzo,,, nan yamik'e yashiga cikin bedroom chan sai gashi da dubu ukku yamik'a mata yace ga wannan kinyi kud'in abin hawa.

kar6a tayi tai godiya sannan tafito cikin ranta tana cewa duk kud'in yaya muhammad ammah bai iya yima mutum kyautar kud'i sama da dubu biyar, Allah dai yakyauta saboda jin haushi ko wajen matarshi bata biyaba sukayi bankwana tawuce gida.
