{On Ward Together}



*PAGE 33*

Bayan sallar la'ar Meerah tana zaune wani Doctor yazo yana round tunda yashigo kallon Meerah yake duk d'ago kan da zatayi sai sun had'a ido dan haka daga baya tadaina d'aga kanta ahaka gar yazo wajen gadonsu yana zuwa yace sannu ya me jiki?

Ranta yabata still dai kallonta yakeyi dan haka tak'i d'ago kai saidai tace yauwa, d'aukar folder d'in ummah yayi yaduba nan yad'auko allura yayi mata sannan yace ya kamata asa mata drip yanzu ma dan bata shan ruwa dayawa tana buk'atar ruwa a jikinta.
Sai a lokacin tad'ago takalleshi suna had'a ido dasauri tajanye idanunta dan wani irin kallo da yake jifarta da shi, drip yarubuta a prescription sheet yamik'a ma Meerah yace akawo yanzu asa mata.

Kar6a Meerah tayi tace toh nan tawuce still dai kanta a k'asa tana fita cikin sauri taje tasiyo takawo lokacin yana tsaye kusa da gadon da yake gabansu yana duba patient koda tashigo saida yakalleta cikin ranta ko wani irin haushi takeji ganin yadda yake kallonta saida yagama duba wacchan sannan yadawo wajen gadon yace 'yanmata ansamo?

Jin sunan tayi wani iri ammah tashare tace eh, kar6a yayi yahad'a yasa ma ummah yace yauwa Allah yak'ara sauk'i kidinga lura idan yak'are sai ki kashe.

Batare da ta kalleshiba tace ni ban iya kashewaba, Murmushi yayi me sauti yace haba ba zan yardaba kina wayaiya haka kice baki iya kashe drip ba, shuru tayi takyaleshi.

Ganin haka yace ohk ba damuwa sai ki min magana zanzo inkashe inkuma kinaso sai inkoya maki dan ba wani wuya ke akwai ba.

Girgiza kai tayi tace no need inkoya tunda ba cadre na bane just in yak'are zan fad'a maka.

Girgiza kai yayi tare da yin 'yar dariya yace dakyau yan mata hakan shine daidai bari intafi sai kinzo.

Shiru tayi takyaleshi yawuce yatafi har yagama yafita bata sake d'ago kantaba sai bayan ya fita nan tad'ago tana kallon ummah ahankali ruwan yake shiga jikinta.

Komawa tayi tajingine kanta jikin plastic chair d'in da take zaune dan itama kanta ita kad'ai tasan abinda takeji saboda ba lafiya ne da itaba daurewa kawai takeyi.

Tana nan tana lura da drip d'in chan da taga saura kad'an yak'are tamik'e tafita chan inda likitocin suke zaune taje nan tasamesu zaune wasu suna duba patients d'in da aka shigo da su ganinshi tayi zaune tare da wani Doctor suna hira tun kan ta isa wajen yamik'e yanufota yana zuwa yace ya dai 'yan mata?

Juyawa tayi sannan tace ruwan ya kusan k'arewa.
Ohk ba damuwa muje inga nan tawuce gaba yana biye da ita a baya suna cikin tafiya sai ji tayi ya ce ammah fa kin burgeni sosai dan yanayin kalamanki sun nuna ke d'in kina da hankali da nutsuwa.

Ta6e baki kawai tayi batare da tayi maganaba tayi saurin shigewa ward d'in, yana shiga wata tsohuwa tace yauwa likita zo kacire ma wannan abun ruwan ya k'are, ganin ya k'are yasa yatsaya yayi saurin cirewa sannan yawuce wajen Meerah yasameta tsaye kallon drip d'in yayi yaga da d'an saura kad'an yace ai wannan bai k'areba da saura.

Itama kallo tayi taga har lokacin da d'an saura tace toh ai kar yak'are kafin inkiraka wani abu yasameta.

Murmushi yayi yace babu abinda zai sameta sai alkhairi shikenan tunda kin kirani bari injira yak'are sai incire.

A'a zaka iya tafiya inyaso inyak'are sai inkiraka.

No bari dai injira yak'are incire mata hakan zaifi ammah dan Allah intambayeki man.

Batare da ta kalleshiba tace ina jinka 
Gyara tsayuwarshi yayi sannan yace wai dan Allah ya sunanki?
Shuru tayi batace komai ba.
K'ara maimaita mata tambayar yayi sai a lokacin tad'ago takalleshi tace meyasa kakeso kasan sunana?

'Daga kafad'a yayi yace just haka kawai ko laifine dan nasan sunanki?
Laifi ma babba.
Niko a wajena ba laifi bane sanin sunanki ni sunana Doctor Suraj.
Kallon hannun ummah tayi tace ya kamata tacire mata hakanan ruwan fa ya k'are.
Wucewa yayi yacire mata sannan yace toh kifad'a min sunanki.

Fuskarta a d'aure tace banaji zan iya fad'a maka sunana.
Murmushi yayi yace shikenan nasan dai ahankali wata rana za'a fad'a min bari inkoma Allah yak'ara sauk'i zan dawo zuwa anjima ko akwai abinda kikeso inkawo maki?
 kallon inda yake batayiba tana ji yawuce yatafi.
Tsaki taja tace mutum sai surutun tsiya ina ma amfanin irin haka.


Ammar da Haisam a ranar sukaje chan gidan iyayensu sukasa aka gyara ko'ina tas koda kusan kullum cikin gyarawa akeyi hatta rooms d'insu saida suka bud'e aka gyara Ammar ne me k'ok'arin kula Haisam koda shi yana d'an shareshi saidai hakan bai damu Ammar ba dan aganinshi nad'an lokacine zai safko daga fushin nashi.

Chan bayan sallar isha'i Haisam yana zaune a d'akinshi da yake cikin gidansu lokaci guda yamik'e yazari key d'in motarshi yafito yana fitowa sai ga Ammar zai shigo d'akin tsayawa yayi yana kallonshi yace Bro ina zakaje yanzu?
Ko inda yake Haisam bai kallaba yaratsa ta gefenshi zai wuce yace anguwa zan je.
Ammah ai wajenka nazo dan Allah kadawo muyi magana sannan katafi.
Yana cigaba da tafiya yace koma menene kabari idan nadawo sai kafad'a min.
Da kallo Ammar yabishi har yaficce ba dan ya soba yakoma d'akinshi cikin ranshi yana cewa Haisam bansan lokacin da zaka canza ba.

Haisam yana fita motarshi yashiga yatada yai ma megadi horn aka hangame mashi gate dagudu yafita da motar yana tafiya yajanyo wayarshi yai dialing d'in number d'in Doctor saif yace Hello Doctor kana gida inzo insameka?
Daga chan 6angaren Dr Saif yace no ina hospital ina call yau.
Ohk ba matsala bari inzo insameka, baijira jin abinda zaiceba yakashe wayar yacigaba da tafiya cikin lokaci kad'an ya isa hospital d'in.




Safiya biye ma Sadeek tayi suka dinga kuka tare nan tamatsa ma Mariya tafad'a mata asibitin da su ummanta sukaje ammah mariya bata fad'a mataba dan tasan idan tafad'a mata zata iya zuwa saidai tace mata ai sunyi waya sunce yau zasu dawo, murna tayi sosai nan takoma lallashin Sadeek a ranar k'in zuwa islamiyya sukayi ita a dole sai taga dawowar su ummanta nan taje tagyara masu d'aki tas tasamo turaren k'amshi takunna, taliya da mai da yaji tadafa tazuba a kula tana jiran dawowarsu har wankinsu tatattaro tayi masu, ganin har anyi magrib basu dawoba nan tafara shiga damuwa wasa-wasa akayi isha'i fushi tafarayi tana cema mariya kikace yau zasu dawo ammah gashinan basu dawoba.
Mariya tace toh ai nidai haka sukace min saidai idan wani abu yatsaidasu k'ila kuma sai zuwa gobe.
Saboda haushi Safiya batace mata komai ba tashige d'akinsu.





Yana isa asibitin a daidai emergency yasamu waje yayi parking d'in motarshi yafito
Yana shiga ganin baiga doctor saif ba yasa yatambayi wata sister tace yana cikin I.C.U dan haka yawuce cikin ward yana zuwa daga bakin k'ofa yatsaya juyowar da Meerah zatayi sai suka had'a ido kallon ummah tayi da har lokacin take bacci sannan tataso tafito daga chan gefen ward d'in tatsaya inda naciki ba zai hangotaba saida tahad'e rai sannan tace malam lafiya? Me yakawoka nan?

Ta6e baki yayi cikin rashin damuwa yace lafiyarce takawoni  inaso kifad'a min gaskiya akan zubewar cikinki shin ke kika zubar da shi dakanki kokuma da sa hannun Ammar kuka zubar da shi.

Kallonshi Meerah tayi cike da mamaki tace ban gane nufinkaba Ammar d'in yafad'a maka mun zubar da ciki?

Inma zubarwa kukayi wannan ba matsalata bane kawai dai inaso intunatar da ke wannan babban zunubine kuka aikata domin kisan kai kukayi, baidaceba ace kina bari zuciyarki tana kwad'aitar da ke kud'i kidinga sa6a ma ubangiji burina d'aya Ammar yadaina aikata abinda yakeyi saisa nakeso inrabashi da duk wad'anda yake tare da su.

'Daga mashi hannu Meerah tayi cikin d'aga murya tace dakata malam ya isa haka, wai meyasa kake son zargina a kowane lokaci wayace maka ni nazubar da cikin idan shi Ammar d'in yafad'a maka toh ya yi k'arya dan ciki shi yaxube da kanshi asibiti kawai yakaini nima ba da son raina nabishiba.

Cikin muryar kuka tacigaba da cewa anfad'a maka nima son Ammar d'in nake? Ko anfad'a maka dad'i nakeji akan shige min da yake? Me zanyi da shi ni da ace zaka taimaka kasashi yarabu da ni da nafi kowa farin ciki domin duk matsalar da nake shiga shine sila, duk farin cikina saida yaruguje min shi gabad'aya babu wani sauran farin ciki da yarage a rayuwata kaduba kaga mahaifiyatace a kwance ta sanadiyar halin da yajefani shin taya kake tunanin ina jin dad'in kasancewa da shi alhali mahaifiyata da takasance komai a rayuwata tana fushi da ni?

Haisam ido yazuba mata batare da ya ce komai ba
Meerah hawayene suka fara gangaro mata a kumatunta tace Ammar ya ci nasara a kaina ya nuna yana min so nagaskiya ammah ba hakaba jikina kawai yakeso kuma saida yasan yadda yayi yasamu nayi bak'in cikin faruwar hakan saida naji gwanda mutuwata akan abinda na aikata dan nasan makomata a wajen ubangiji saisa tun daga lokacin duk wani farin ciki yagushe daga rayuwata koda daman bansan wani farin ciki ko jin dad'iba sai guda d'aya shine inga farin cikin mahaifiyata tahakane nima nake kasancewa cikin farin ciki,  d'aura hannunta tayi saman cikinta tace nafi kowa bak'in ciki da zubewar cikin nan domin naci alwashin inhaifeshi innuna ma duniya halin d'an uwanka iyayenku susan bunsuru ne suka haifa ba mutumin kirkiba naso ace Ammar ya gane cewa akwai ranar k'in dillanci iyayensa su kansu suyi nadamar haihuwarsa.

Katseta Haisam yayi yace wannan wace irin maganace kikeyi miye ruwan iyayenmu a ciki.

Tabbas babu ruwan iyayenku saidai ni nawa iyayen keda ruwa, kowa kanshi yasani babu meso nashi yasamu matsala kaduba kaga halin da uwata take ciki rai a hannun Allah duk a saboda haka, saisa a yanzu na gama yarda da kowa a rayuwata daman chan rayuwata batada sauran amfani dan Ammar ya riga da ya yi mata illah.

Haisam jikinshi sanyi yayi sosai duk sai yaji tausayinta ya kamashi ajiyar zuciya yasafke yace bahaka bane Meerah tabbas Ammar ya yi maki ba daidaiba kuma insha Allahu zaizo yabaki hak'uri


Cikin sauri tagirgiza kai tace bana buk'atar zuwanshi wajena dan kullum ba alkhairi yake kawoshiba yarik'e hak'urinshi banaso kaje kawai, hannunta tasa tadafe kanta da taji ya sara mata tare da runtse idanunta fara takawa tayi zata koma cikin ward taku d'aya biyu nan tayi baya zata fad'i cikin zafin nama Haisam yarik'ota wani irin hucin zazza6i yaji a jikinta yace subhanallah kibi ahankali kar kifad'i kema bakida lafiya jikinki akwai zafi.

Tureshi tayi tafara tafiya cikin ward d'in ahankali tana tafiya kamar zata fad'i tana shiga tasamu ummah ta farka dakyar cikin dauriya ta ida isa wajenta tace sannu ummah kin farka me kikeso akawo maki kici?

Bud'ar bakin ummah sai cewa tayi Meerah kitaimaka min kikula da kanki kar kik'ara siyar da mutuncinki akaina indai har bamuda kud'i dan Allah mukoma gida dan mutuwata ta fiye min akan abinda zaki aikata hankalina ba k'aramin tashi yakeba meerah idan naga yadda kikeso kimaida rayuwarki kinaso kikoya ma kanki halin da ba nakiba.

Fashewa Meerah tayi da kuka tace kiyi hak'uri ummah wallahi na daina ba zan sake yin abinda zai 6ata maki rai ba, nayi maki alk'awali ba zan sake aikata mummunan aiki irin wanchan ba kiyarda da ni k'addara ce tasa na aikata a chan baya ba wai halina bane sannan cikin wallahi shi yazube da kanshi dan Allah kiyafe min ummah kidaina sa damuwa a ranki banason wannan ciwon naki yana d'aga min hankali sosai.

Murmushin k'arfin hali ummah tayi cike da dauriya tad'aga kai batare da tayi maganaba dan bakinta ji tayi yai mata nauyi bata iya cigaba da magana.

Meerah murmushin jin dad'i tayi nan tagoge hawayen fuskarta.

Haisam da yake tsaye daga bayanta yana jin duk abinda ummah take cemata da kuma amsar da tabata sosai yaji tausayin matar matsawa yayi daga gefenta yace sannu ya jikin naki?

Ummah d'aga kai tayi takalleshi duk a tunaninta Ammar ne ganin bashi bane yasa ahankali tace dasauk'i.

Kallonta tamaida ga Meerah da tayi mutuwar tsaye tana tsoron ummah tazargi wani abu akan ganin Haisam da tayi, muryarta tana rawa tace ummah akwai abinda kikeso inkawo maki.

Ahankali tace tea nakeso ammah banda sugar.
Toh Meerah tace nan tajuya tafita dan nunawa tayi makar batasan Haisam ba.

Dubu goma yazaro ya ajiye a gaban gadon ummah yace Allah yak'ara sauk'i.

Tun kan ummah tayi magana ya wuce gadon gabanta yagaishesu ya ajiye masu 2 thousand,  gado gado yabi ya ajiye masu duk dubu bibiyu nan akaita yi mashi godiya, ummah da taso tatuhumi meerah  akanshi dan duk a tunaninta wajenta yazo ammah ganin ko'ina yabi yana gaishe da maras lafiya yana bada kud'in sai hankalinta yakwanta tasan kawai zuwa yayi ziyarar marassa lafiya.

Haisam yana gamawa fita yayi yaje yasamu Dr saif da yake ta kiran wayarshi yana tambayarshi ko bai shigo asibitinba shikuma lokacin yana cikin ward d'in saisa yagaji yadaina picking. 

Meerah zuwa tayi aka had'a tea d'in tazo takawo ma ummah nan tataimaka mata tatashi tasha sannan takoma takwanta, kud'in da Haisam ya ajiye ummah tanuna mata tace gasunan wannan da yazo ziyarar maras lafiya yabani.

Gaban Meerah saida yafad'i cikin ranta tace na shiga ukku meyasa yayi haka Allah yasa dai kar ummah tazargi wani abu.

Muryar ummah taji ta ce tunanin me kikeyi?
Girgiza kai tayi tace bakomai ummah gani nayi bamu sanshiba kuma yazo yabamu kud'i.
Dakyar ummah take bud'e baki tayi magana tace taimakone yayi kuma bamu kad'ai yababa kowane gado saida yabi yabasu.

Ta6e baki Meerah tayi cikin ranta tace lallai Haisam d'an rainin wayo ne, d'auko kud'in tayi tak'irga taga 10k cike da mamaki take kallon ummah tace ummah wad'annan kud'i haka dayawa, dubu goma fa ne.
Itama ummah mamakine yabayyana a fuskarta tace ikon Allah wannan kud'i ai sunyi yawa Allah dai yabiyashi.
Jinjina kai kawai tayi batare da tayi maganaba dan taso ace ummah bataga kud'inba da sai ta maida mashi kud'in saidai yanzu tasan intamaida sai ummah ta zargi wani abu, d'aukar sauran tea d'in ummah tayi tashanye nan tashimfid'a tabarma a k'asa takwanta saboda zazza6in da takeji itama.

Shuru tayi batare da tayi maganaba.
Gyara tsayuwarshi yayi yace 'yanmata ya kamata kitaimaka kirage d'aure min fuska kina shareni dan idan naga kina had'e fuska irin haka har tsoro kike ban sunanki ma kanshi kink'i fad'amin i don't know why nifa sonki nake dagaske.

sai a lokacin tad'ago takalleshi nan idanuwanta suka cika da k'wallah tace kadaina furta kalmar so agareni dan ba zaka ta6a samun masauki acikin zuciyataba babu namijin da zan sake amince mawa yaimin wasa da rayuwa na rufe babin soyayya a rayuwata dan haka indai har kanason incigaba da ganin mutuncinka kar kasake min maganar soyayya.

Cike da mamaki yake kallonta yace meyasa kikace haka ai baidace ace......d'aga mashi hannu tayi tace kaga malam ya isa haka ummana bacci takeyi kar katasheta idan ka gama zaka iya tafiya.

Ledar da yashigo da ita ya ajiye a k'asa gefenta yace Allah yak'ara sauk'i.
batare da ta kalli inda yake tsayeba tace kad'auki ledarka kar kamanceta a nan dan bani buk'ata.
Murmushi yayi yace wannan na ummah ne ita nakawo mawa ba wai ke ba,,, yana fad'in haka yajuya yayi tafiyarshi cikin ranshi yana mamakin maganganun da tafad'a wai ta rufe babin soyayya a rayuwarta ta gama amincema kowa, tambayar kanshi yashiga yi me hakan yake nufi? Kodai anta6a yaudararta a so shiyasa tace haka? Rasa amsoshin tambayoyin yayi dan haka yabarsu acikin ranshi.


Tana ganin ya fita nan takife kanta jikin gadon ummah tana me kuka k'asa-k'asa cike da tausayin kanta dan a yanzu babu abinda tatsana sama da a furta mata kalmar so dan hakan da yayi ya sa tatuna da abinda yafaru tsakaninta da Ammar ya dawo mata sabo.

_BAYAN KWANA BIYU_

Hankalin family d'in a kwance kowane lokaci suna cikin farin ciki da jin dad'i a kowane lokaci a main parlour suke taruwa susha hira cikin nishad'i da jin dad'i hatta abinci tare sukeci babu me zuwa ko'ina dan ko wajen aiki Ammar da Haisam sunyi kusan kwana biyu basujeba saida Dad yaga abun nasu ba me k'arewa bane sannan yasasu suka koma wajen aiki inda shima yacigaba da hidimunshi.



Meerah hankalinta ya fara kwanciya ganin cikin kwana biyu jikin ummah ya fara sauk'i sosai dan har tana mik'ewa tataka dakanta, koda itama nata jikin ta d'an fara jin dad'inshi a yanzu dan zazza6in ya d'an safka saidai har lokacin batajin k'arfin jikinta ga jiri da take yawan ji yana d'ibanta saidai har lokacin bata iya cin abinci sosai saidai idan tana a gaban ummah shine take d'an ci da d'an yawa dan kar ummah tace wani abu.


Dr Suraj shi yake yawan zuwa round da marece a cikin ward d'in da aka maida ummah dan haka idan yazo wajen gadon ummah suna gaisawa sosai cikin sakin fuska yayi mata tambaya ko akwai inda yake mata ciwo saidai tace a'a ahaka har 'yar hira suke ta6awa shi da ita, meerah ko wani irin haushi takeji idan taga ma suna hirar saidai tatashi tafita tabar d'akin dan tasan duk yanayi ma ummah hakane dan neman shiga itako ummah batasan abinda ake cikiba gani take haka yake me kirki dan daman shi akwai fara'a da barkwanci.

Ummah kanta tafiso ace shine zaiyi round dan 'yan kwanakin biyu har ta fara sabawa da shi saboda barkwancinshi.

Meerah ko daga lokacin da zata hangeshi shikenan ta gama fara'a har sai ya bar wajen duk yadda zaiso suyi magana bata bashi dama dan haka shima yadaina bin ta kanta saidai yazo wajen ummaj susha hira.



Ranar talata bayan ta gama sallar azuhur tana zaune tana game da wayarta nan kiran Amnar yashigo a cikin wayar gabanta saida yayi wani irin mummunan fad'uwa da taga sunanshi ya yi appearing jikin screen d'in wayar d'agowa tayi takalli ummah da hankalinta yake ga wani wajen sauri tayi tai rejecting kiran cike da jin haushi cikin ranta neman tsari tashiga yi daga sharrin Ammar dan tasan akowane lokaci babu alkhairi acikin kiran da zaiyi mata.
Daga k'arshe gabad'aya takashe wayar dan ma kar yak'ara kira.


A chan 6angaren Ammar daman zaune yake a office bayan ya gama aiki meerah ce tafad'o mashi a rai kuma yana yawan kiran number d'inta baya samu dan haka ne a yanzu yagwada kira yayi sa'a tashiga
Wani irin farin ciki yaji da kiran yashiga saidai tana fara ringing akayi rejecting,  nan fara'ar fuskarshi tarage sake trying d'in number d'in yayi yaji an kashe gabad'aya dan haka ya ajiye wayar cike da jin haushi tare da jan tsaki yace yarinyar nan bansan me take tak'ama dashiba ta cika taurin kai dayawa duk yadda za'a lalla6ata bata gani,, cikin d'aga murya yace toh wai ma meyasa nadamu da ita haka, meyasa bazan iya rabuwa da itaba kawai, duk matan da suke bibiyata gasunan burjit ammah na nace ma wannan yarinyar inaaa ba zai yuwuba tadinga wulak'antani me tad'auki kantane? Buga table d'in da yake gabanshi yayi dak'arfi yace lallai Meerah har yanzu bakisan wanene Ammar ba.



ummah ido tazuba ma Meerah da take kallon waje guda yanayinta kawai zaka kalla kagane cewa tunani takeyi ahankali ummah takira sunanta 
Juyowa tayi takalli ummah tare da amsawa.
Ummah tace Meerah tunanin me kikeyi haka bakisan yawan tunani yana jaza ma mutum ciwoba?
Murmushi tayi tace ummah ba tunani nakeyiba.
Toh me kikeyi? Ummah tatambaya
Duk'ar da kanta tayi tace ina kallon chan wajen ne.
Toh shikenan tunda haka kikace yanzu dai tashi kije kisamo min kankana da ayaba a bakin titi.

Toh tace nan tamik'e tana mik'ewa jiri yad'ebeta tayi baya zata fad'i cikin sauri tarik'o gadon ummah tare da zama gefenta.

Kallonta ummah take cike da mamaki tace Meerah lafiya? Me yake samunki kodai baki lafiya.

Girgiza kai tayi tace lafiya ta lau ummah kawai hakanan naji jiri ammah ba abinda yake damuna kar kidamu.

Anya kuwa Meerah,  kodai zakiga likita?
Rik'o hannunta tayi tace dagaske ummah babu abinda yake damuna bari inyi sauri insiyo maki lemun indawo.
A'a kibarshi ba zaki iya zuwaba kinga kar jirin yad'ebeki a hanya kifad'i.
Insha Allahu bazai d'ebeniba sai na dawo.
Mik'ewa tayi tafita da kallo ummah tabita cike da tausayin d'iyartata da tasan ko bata fad'a mataba ita kanta tana cikin damuwa tana buk'atar taimako saidai zurfin cikin meerah bazaisa tabari agane matsalartaba dan hatta ita da take mahaifiyarta tasan halin d'iyarta tun tana k'arama bakomai bane take bari asani game da ita.

Meerah tana fita daga ward five tafiya take cikin dauriya ahankali har ta'iso wajen dental department tana d'an wutoshi kad'an wani irin jiri yafara d'ibarta dafe kanta tayi tai tsaye wajen takasa cigaba da tafiya kanta cigaba yayi da sara mata, Su Dr saif da fitowarsu kenan daga cikin wani d'an restaurant rik'e da lemu hannunsu zasu koma chan emergency dept hango wata sukayi tsaye ta dafe kai yace abokina kalli wacchan ko lafiyarta ji yadda ta dafe kanta anya kuwa lafiya.
Ko inda take bai kallaba yace toh menene naka aciki?.
Tsayuwar ce taji ta kasa komai juya mata yake tana ganin komai bibiyu gabad'aya tarasa ya zatayi dakyar tak'ara taku d'aya lokaci guda jirin yakwasheta sai gata k'asa.

su duka biyun da suka kallota lokaci guda dasauri duk sukayo wajen kafin su iso wasu mata biyu har sun iso wajenta, idanuwanta a rufe suke ko motsi batayi gabad'ayansu saida sukayi mamaki da sukaga Meerah ce waya Doctor Saif yad'auko yakira wata nurse yace a aiko masu da wheelchair yanzun nan yafad'i inda suke, 
Matan da suka fara isowane suka fara yi mata fifita suna cewa bata numfashi doctor kodai mutuwa tayi.
Tsaye Doctor Saif yayi yana kallonta dan shi kanshi bazai iya tantance da rai ko babu rai ba atare da ita a wannan lokacin.

Cikin mintuna kad'an sai ga attendoji biyu sun turo wheelchair nan yace sud'agata sud'aura sama, zaunar da ita sukayi a sama sannan suka wuce da ita aka tsallaka titi zuwa emergency d'in suna shiga nan yawuce da ita wani room da ba kowa ake kaiwaba sai staffs ko danginsu ana kaita a saman gadon attandojin suka d'aurata nan yaje yad'auko kayan aiki lokacin da yadawo har ansiyo mata folder,  dubata yashiga yi  tana numfashi kad'an kad'an ya dad'e akanta sannan yarubuta drip da injections yana fitowa Haisam yatareshi yace me kake buk'ata?
Mik'a mashi takardar yayi cikin sauri yakar6a yanufi pharmacy yana zuwa yamik'a masu takardar nan suka mik'o mashi suka fad'i kud'i yafiddo yabasu ko canji bai tsaya amsaba yaje yakaimashi.

Kar6a Doctor yayi yashiga yayi mata allura sannan yasa mata drip yana sawa yafito waje nan yatareshi yana cewa ya dai me yake samunta?

Ajiyar zuciya yasafke yace gaskiya abokina daman tunda naga yarinyar nan nasan batada isashiyar lafiya kwata-kwata jikinta ma batada wani ishashen ruwa da jini ta dalilin hakan ne yasa jiri yad'ebeta yanzu haka da leda d'aya ya k'are na ruwa toh  jini za'a sanya mata yanzu na d'ibi jininta zanyi mata test inga group d'in jinin sai aje asiyo asanya mata.
Kasa magana yayi nan suka wuce chan lab d'insu da yake cikin wajen ya auna jinin yaga  A+ ne yace toh yanzu ya za'ayi muje asamo jinin ko kuma wani d'an uwanta za'ajira intafarka takira sai yazo yabada.

Taya hakan zata yuwu kace har sai ta farka bayan ka ce tana buk'atar jinin cikin sauri why not kawai kad'ibi nawa kasa mata tunda ni O+ ne kuma kasan haka
 
A'a abokina bazan d'ibar maka jiniba leda biyu fa ak'allah akeso muje kawai asamo.
Rik'oshi Haisam yayi yace ni nace kad'iba dan haka kad'iba kawai ko da ace ba ita bace zan iya bada jinina ga duk me buk'ata indai har ina da wadatacce.

Shuru Dr Saif yayi dan tabbas yasan wannan halin abokinshi ne ya sha yaje asibiti ad'ibi jininshi yace aba mabuk'aci sadaka.

Ganin ya yi shuru yasa yace ko ba zaka iya d'iba ba inkira ad'iba?

Girgiza kai yayi yace zan iya ammah ai sai anfara maka test. 
Yace ohk ba matsala kad'iba sai kayi.
Syringe yad'auko yad'ibi jininshi yashiga lab ya auna bai dad'eba yafito yace normal saura muje induba inga idan zamu iya d'ibar har leda biyu batare da ansamu matsalaba.
Tsaki yaja yace kaima kasan fiye da leda biyu ma za'a iya d'iba a jikina intashi garas wani ko ko leda d'aya tsoron badawa yake.
Dariya yayi yace nasan haka abokina ai kai jarumine niko buk'atar k'ari nake kaga ko ai nawa bazai d'ibu ba.

Kaidai kasani ai ni ban ta6a ganin raggo irin kaba.
Eh d'in naji.

Suna shiga gudan gadon haisam yakwanta nan Dr Saif yaduba yaga yana da isashen jini sosai a jikinshi dan haka yafara d'iba

Leda biyu yad'iba nan Haisam zai mik'e doctor yayi saurin maidashi yace kar kakatashi yakamata kad'an huta sannan, bai musaba yakoma yakwanta, nan Dr Saif yaje yacire mata drip d'in da yak'are a daidai lokacin yasanya mata k'arin jinin sannan yace ma Haisam bari yaje yadawo kar yatashi.

'Daga kai kawai Haisam yayi batare da ya yi maganaba yana fita maida idanunshi yayi yarufe yana jin wani iri a ranshi dan yasan wannan halin da tashiga duk d'an uwanshine sila kuma shi kanshi saboda d'an uwan nashine yataimaketa dan yana jin tausayin bayin Allah nan sosai zaiso ace Ammar ya...maganar Dr Saif ce takatse mashi tunani yace ba dai bacci kakeba abokina.

Bud'e idanunshi yayi yakalleshi har lokacin bai kalli inda meerah takeba maltina da madara yasiyo nan yahad'e a cikin cup yamik'a mashi yace kasha ko kasamu k'warin jikinka kar kaje kafad'i a nan daman abu ga mutum marar afki.

Murmushi kawai Haisam yayi yakar6a yasha saida yashanye sannan ya ajiye cup d'in yamik'e yace bari injiraka waje idan ka gama sai ka sameni.

Ohk nima ai fitowa zanyi kafin tafarka.

Yana fita shima Dr Saif yafita aka bar meerah kwance batasan inda kanta yakeba.

Haisam kallon Dr saif yayi yace ni zan wuce gida koma me kenan sai munyi waya.
Haba abokina ya za'ayi katafi bayan ga wannan marar lafiyar kasan dai tana buk'atar taimako cikin gaugawa nikuma gashi aiki nake kar intafi kuma tafarka.
Toh in ta farka menene ni miye nawa a ciki ina ce taimako ne kuma nayi.
Murmushi Dr Saif yayi yace nasan da haka ammah ai sai ka ida ladarka.
Wani irin kallo Haisam yawurga mashi yace indai kai zaka ban ladar toh sai karik'e.
Dariya yayi yace ba ruwanka abokina gaskiya kana burgeni yanzu dai ina tunanin ya isa tafarka tunda ba allurar bacci akayi mataba muje mudubata.

Kaje man kai kad'ai ni babu inda zanje.
Haba please muje. 


Ahankali tabud'e idanunta a kan fanka da take wanawa tasafke idanun nan tafara tunanin a ina take lokaci guda tunaninta yadawo cikin sauri tatashi zaune tana bin d'akin da kallo cikin ranta tace wanene yakawoni nan wajen kallon hannunta tayi da akasa mata k'arin jini nan takai hannu zata fizge daidai lokacin taji ance please kar kicire.

Juyowa tayi takalleshi ganin Dr Saif ne yasa tace Doctor wa yakawoni nan? ni lafiyata lau kacire min wannan abun inje insamo ma ummana kankana da lemu kaga batada lafiya na barota ita kad'ai 

Isowa yayi wajen yace kema fa bakida lafiya Meerah yakamata kikwanta.

A'a ni lafiyata lau kabarni intafi kaga kar ummah tayi ta nemana kar tad'auka wani waje naje.

Toh shikenan bari inyi maki allura sai incire maki wannan d'in kitafi ko?.

'Daga kai kawai tayi nan yad'auko allura yahad'a yayi mata yana gamawa nan tafara gani bibiyu hamma tafara tare da lumshe ido tana cewa Doctor kacire min toh intafi wajen ummah kar hankalinta yatashi,,, tana fad'in haka takoma takwanta tare da rufe idanunta nan bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.

Doctor kiran wata nurse yayi tagyara mata kwanciyarta sannan yafito yasamu Haisam da yake tsaye bakin k'ofar yana saurarensu yana fitowa Haisam yace meyasa kayi mata allurar bacci kar fa mahaifiyarta tayi ta nemanta

Toh ai babu yadda za'ayi tatafi kaga fa condition d'in da take ciki bama zata iya tafiyarba dan fad'uwa zatayi.

Shuru Haisam yayi sai chan yace shikenan saidai ya kamata mahaifiyarta tasan halin da d'iyarta take ciki.
Eh hakan yakamata dan kar hankalinta yatashi itama.

Ammah Haisam da kai kataimaka kaje kafad'a mata halin da d'iyartata take ciki.
  
Shuru Haisam yayi baice komai ba.
Dr Saif yace kayi hak'uri nima dan ina aikine saisa ba daman infita da ba hakaba da ni zan je dakaina.
Na ji shikenan zanje ammah daga chan zan wuce gida.
Haba abokina bama zaka dawo muyi bankwanaba.
Bazan dawoba daga chan zan wuce malam.
Toh shikenan zan shigo gidan da anjima.
Ohk sai ka zo.

Ummah jin shuru yayi yawa Meerah bata dawoba nan hankalinta yatashi tafara tunanin meerah anya lafiya ko?
Wata yarinya da sukazo dubiya kusa da gadonta ta aika tace taje waje tad'an duba mata ko zataga meerah ammah tadawo tace bata gantaba nan hankalin ummah ya ida tashi.



Yana fita wajen da ya ajiye motarshi yaje yashiga yatada saida yafita daga gate d'in asibitin yaje bakin titi yasiyo lemu da ayaba da kankana sannan yadawo cikin asibitin a chan kusa da Ward 5 yayi parking d'in motarshi yafito da nik'i-nik'in ledojinshi yashiga ciki yana shiga yahango ummah ta yi zugum ta zuba ma k'ofa ido tana kallo, takawa yayi ya ida isa nan yaimata sallama.

Sai a lokacin tajuyo takalleshi dan ko da yashigo bata lura da shiba saboda hankalinta ya yi chan wani wajen na daban amsa mashi sallamartayi tana mashi kallon sani, ajiye ledar da take hannunshi yayi sannan yad'an rissina yagaisheta.

Fuska asake ta amsa tana me cigaba da kallonshi tana so tatuno inda tasanshi.

Shima Haisam kallonta yakeyi yace ya jikin?

Alhamdulillah dasauk'i saidai ban ganekaba.

Murmushi yayi yace eh daman baki sanniba Allah yak'ara sauk'i.

Ameen nagode.

Shuru yayi yarasa ta inda zai fara yi mata bayani sai chan yakalleta yaga har lokacin kallonshi takeyi yace hajiya daman zuwa nayi infad'a maki d'iyarkice batada lafiya yanzu haka tana chan a kwance ammah dasauk'i.

Wuf tayi tatashi zaune tace wace d'iyar tawa ba dai meerah ba?

'Daga mata kai yayi yana me kallon yanayin da tashiga cike da jin tausayinta yace kiyi hak'uri ba wani abu bane yake samunta kawai dai jirine yad'ebeta yanzu haka Dr Saif yana chan a wajenta tasamu sauk'i bacci ma take.

Hawaye ummah tafara tace nashiga ukku daman tun tuni nake ganin d'iyarnan kamar batada lafiya ammah tadinga min gardama tana cewa lafiyarta lau koda zata fita tasiyo min lemu saida jiri yad'ebeta nace tabarshi kar taje tafad'i ahanya ashe saida tafad'in yanzu muje wajenta.

A'a Hajiya kizauna ai jikin nata dasauk'i kema fa bakida lafiya.

Mik'ewa tayi tace naji sauk'i muje kawai ni ciwonta ya fi d'aga min hankali idan banjeba hankalina bazai ta6a kwanciyaba.

Ganin yadda duk tatashi hankalinta yasa Haisam yace shikenan muje kiganta, hijab d'inta tajanyo tasaka tawuce gaba yabi bayanta tana taku dakyar har suka fito daga cikin ward d'in.

Haisam ne yace ga mota ta nan ko zaki shigo sai muje in ajiyeki a wajen emergency d'in.

Kallon motar tayi sannan takalleshi tace a'a bazan shigaba zan taka a k'asa ai babu nisa.

Ganin ta nuna bata yarda da shiba yasa yace ummah ba cutarki zanyiba kinga bakida lafiya baidace ace kinyi wannan tafiyarba a k'afa kiyarda da ni bazan cucekiba.

Tsaye tayi nan yabud'e mata k'ofa yace bismillah kishigo.

Ganin bataga alamun ya yi kalar macuciba yasa tashiga gidan baya da yabud'e mata bakinta d'auke da addu'a.

Maida murfin yayi yarufe sannan shima yabud'e yashiga yaja motar, yana kallon ummah ta madubi duk ta takure tana raba ido ahaka har yazo daidai wajen A&E yasamu waje yayi parking d'in motar yabud'e yafito sannan itama yabud'e mata tafito suka nufi cikin ward d'in suna shiga yagamu da Dr saif zaune yace yauwa ga Doctor nan.

Ummah muryarta tana rawa tace Doctor ina d'iyata me yasameta?

Dr Saif yace Hajiya kikwantar da hankalinki d'iyarki tana lafiya da baki tasoba keda bakida lafiya kuma kin baro gadonki.

A'a Doctor ni na warke dan Allah kakaini inga d'iyata.

Dr Saif kallon Haisam yayi nan Haisam yai mashi nuni alamun zai tafi.

'Daga mashi kai kawai yayi nan yawuce yatafi.

Dr Saif ganin yadda hankalin ummah yatashi sosai yasa yace muje kiganta, suna juyawa zasu tafi sai ga Dr Suraj da yahango ummah yayo wajen yana cewa Hajiya lafiya me yakawoki nan keda kike kwance bakida lafiya, Dr saif lafiya me yake faruwa?

Ummah rasa bakin magana tayi saidai Dr Saif yace lafiya lau d'iyartace batada lafiya ammah da sauk'i shine zata ganta.

Ba dai Meerah ba? cewar Dr Suraj

Ummah cikin muryar kuka tace ita man dan Allah muje inganta.
'Dunguma sukayi gabad'aya zuwa chan d'akin da Meerah take kwance suna shiga Ummah tayi saurin isa inda take tana cewa Meerah kece kwance a nan? 

Dr Saif ne yace kar atasheta daga baccin tana buk'atar hutu.

jin haka yasa ummah tad'an ja baya tana goge hawayen fuskarta tace Doctor me nake gani haka kamar k'arin jini akeyi mata?
Dr Saif yace eh jinine dan gaskiya tana buk'atar jini a jikinta babu jini saisa aka sa mata.

Ummah bin hannun Meerah tayi da kallo.

Dr Suraj da tunda yashigo yake tsaye baiyi maganaba yana kallon Meerah sai a lokacin yace Haba Doctor ya za'ayi asa mata wani jini ina aka samo shi aka sa mata, jinin ma wanene?

Kallonshi Dr Saif yayi yace jinin wani bawan Allah ne yabada sadaka asa mata har ansa leda d'aya yanzu leda na biyu ne yake shiga.

Cikin d'aga murya yace wane irin sadaka aka bata bakasan jinin ko waneneba zaka saka mata k'ila ma mugun mutum ne ko yana d'auke da wata cutar, irin wannan ai da kun fara tuntu6ar mahaifiyarta kafin asa mata dan haka atsaida sa mata jinin ga nawa ad'iba asa mata.

Ummah jin haka yasa tayi kasafai tana saurarensu duk sai jikinta yayi sanyi da taji bayanin Doctor itama duk sai taji bataji dad'iba da akasa ma Meerah jinin wani mutum na daban.


Cike da mamaki Dr Saif yake kallonshi yace ammah dai Doctor kasan aikina ne kuma nasan abinda nakeyi bazan sa mata jinin wani muguba, kallonshi yamaida ga ummah da take kallonshi itama yace kiyi hak'uri Hajiya a lokacin da d'iyarki tafad'i aka kawota naduba naga tana buk'atar jini gudun kar a6ata lokaci wajen neman jinin da zaiyi daidai da nata wanda hakan zaisa a iya rasata yasa wanda muka d'aukota tare yace a auna jininshi indai ya yi daidai da nata ad'iba asa mata ya bata saboda Allah, saida na auna natabbatar bai d'auke da wata cuta sannan nad'iba nasa mata a jikinta hatta magani da injections duk shi yasiyo kuma yayi hakan ne saboda Allah kiyarda da ni babu abinda zai samu d'iyarki insha Allahu.

Ajiyar zuciya ummah tasafke tace bakomai Doctor mungode sosai ai taimakonmu akayi Allah yasaka da alkhairi ammah wanene wanda yataimaka mana haka nima inyi mashi godiya.

Murmushi Doctor yayi yace ya tafi shi ya ce saboda Allah yayi kuyi mashi addu'a kawai ta isa.

Ummah tace Allah yasaka mashi da alkhairi Allah yabiyashi yajik'an iyaye.

Ameen ya rabbi,,, cewar Dr Saif. 

Dr Suraj ba dan ya soba haka yahak'ura yana kallo jinin wani yashiga jikin Meerah,  d'aukar folder d'inta yayi yaduba sannan yace Allah yabata lafiya ammah ummah yakamata kikoma tunda kinga yanayin jikinta.

Girgiza kai tayi tace kabarni kawai Dr Suraj nafiso inzauna kusa da ita ni na warke kawai kusallameni.

A'a akwai sauran allurorin da suka rage ayi maki kar kidamu kikoma kawai insha Allahu mu zamu kula da ita kuma jikin nata ma dasauk'i,,, cewar Dr Suraj.

Dr Saif yace sosai ma ai da ta gama shan jinin nan zata mik'e daman matsalar rashin jinine yasa tashiga wannan condition d'in ammah insha Allahu zuwa anjima da allurar bacci ta saketa zata tashi normal. 

Shuru ummah tayi dan bataji zata iya tafiya tabarta, ganin haka yasa Dr Suraj yace toh ummah kije bisa wanchan d'ayan gadon kikwanta tunda kinfison zama kusa da ita.

Bata musaba taje tahau gadon takwanta tana kallon Meerah da take baccinta hankali kwance.

Dr Saif ne yace bari inje zan zagayo zuwa anjima kafin jinin yak'are,  Dr suraj yace nima bari inje daman kirana akeyi a chan ward. 

Nan duk suka fita sukabar ummah kawai a d'akin tana kallon Meerah cike da jin tausayinta.



Haisam yana barin asibitin gida yawuce yana zuwa gida a parlour yasamu mom zaune ita kad'ai tana kallo a faskekiyar plasma d'in da take manne a bangon d'akin da sallama yashiga saida tarage k'arar Tv d'in sannan ta amsa mashi tana kallonshi.

 murmushi yayi yace Mom sannu da hutawa ya gidan?

Itama murmushin tayi tace yauwa sannu son gida lafiya lau, ya aikin?.
Alhmdulillah mom, bari ind'an shiga inwatsa ruwa, har ya juya zai tafi sai cewa tayi son. 

Tsayawa yayi cak tare da juyowa yace na'am mom nan yadawo kusa da ita yazauna yana wasa da yatsun hannunshi tace yauwa wai ina kake zuwa 2 day's ina lura da kai ko lunch baka dawowa gida kayi. 

'Dagowa yayi yakalleta yace Mom aikine yaimin yawa a office saisa bana samun dawowa.
Ido tazuba mashi tana kallonshi tace kuma shine kake k'in dawowa kaci abinci ai da ka aiko an kar6ar maka zama da yunwa ai ba dad'i kakai har dare bakaci abinciba.

Mom ina ci a chan.
Gida ke akwai a chan d'in da zakaci abinci? Toh wai ma yau ina kaje Ammar yadawo yana nemanka yana cewa tun d'azu kabar asibiti.

Sosai k'eya yayi yace Mom naje wajen wani abokina ne.

Kaidai kafad'a min inda kaje.
Muryar Ammar ne sukaji ya ce Mom kika sani ko wajen surukarki yaje.

Juyowa Haisam yayi yakalleshi tare da wurga mashi harara yace kai miye haka nayi da kai?

Ba Ammar ba hatta ummah saida tayi dariya
Kallon mom yayi da taketa dariya yace wallahi Mom kar kiyarda da maganar wannan d'an rainin wayaun ni banda wata budurwa shi daine me 'yan mata.

Ammar guntse dariyarshi yayi yai shuru gudun kar Allura tatono garma.

Mom tace oho dai ni ai ban yarda da ku ba dukkanku kowa zaiyi bayyani.

Tashi Haisam yayi yana cewa wallahi ni mom banda kowa banma tunanin fara yin soyayya a yanzu dan ko kusa ba yanzu zanyi aureba.

Rik'e baki mom tayi tace ikon Allah Haisam anya kuwa lafiyarka? 

Lafiyata lau mom bari inshiga ciki ind'an watsa ruwa,,, bai jira jin abinda zata ceba yawuce cikin sauri yabar wajen.

Murmyshi tayi tare da juyawa takalli Ammar da yai tsaye kamar ruwa ya ci shi, cikin sauri shima yajuya yana cewa bari inje indawo mom.

Girgiza kai tayi tace Allah yashirya yarannan watau basu ma son ayi masu maganar aure, zamu had'e ai.


Meerah ba ita tafarka ba sai gab da magrib tana bud'e idanunta ummah tasafko daga saman gadon da take kwance tafara jera mata sannu, 'Daga kai kawai tace ummah shine kika taso keda bakida lafiya shine kika taso daga gadonki nifa lafiyata lau.

A'a Meerah ni nasamu sauk'i 
Kallon hannunta tayi da jinin da ake sa mata yakusa k'arewa tace ummah jinine suke sa min banda jini ne a jikina?

Itama ummah kallon hannun tayi tace eh haka Doctor yace.
Toh ba dai naki bane aka d'iba keda bakida lafiya ko?
  Girgiza kai tayi tace a'a ba nawa bane dan lokacin da nazo har ma ansa maki jinin nima kaina bansan jinin waneneba aka sa miki dan likita ya ce wani bawan Allah ne yabada yace saboda Allah yabaki, ban ga mutumin ba ammah Allah yasaka mashi da alkhairi addu'a kawai zamuyi mashi dan ya taimaka mana sosai inda bai bada ba da bamuda kud'in da zamu siya har leda biyu.

Shuru Meerah tayi tana kallon ummah cikin ranta tana tunanin wanene wannan wanda yabada jininshi aka sa mata.

Ummah kamar tasan tunanin da take tace kikwantar da hankalinki koma wanene wannan bamuda abinda zamu saka mashi da shi sai addu'a dan ya yi mana abinda ko d'an uwa na jini ba kowa zai iyaba.

Meerah tace hakane ummah Allah yasaka mashi da iyayenshi da gidan aljannah.

Ameen ya rabbi,  

Suna cikin hirar sai ga Dr Saif ya shigo yace patient har kin farka ashe, daman nasan zaki farka daidai wannan lokacin tunda allurar ba me k'arfi bace sosai akayi maki, ya jikin naki?

Jiki Alhmdllh Doctor ya yi sauk'i saura kasallameni.

Murmushi yayi yace na lura da ke tsoron kwanciya asibiti kikeyi kikwantar da hankalinki za'a sallameki.

Toh daman ai asibiti ba abin so bace sai dai dan dole ammah ai yau zaka sallameni ko?
Haba ba dai yau ba ina ma dai laifi kice sai kin k'ara samun sauk'i.

Yunk'urin tashi zaune tayi yace a'a kar kitashi kibari jinin ya ida k'arewa.

Komawa tayi takwanta tana turo baki shidai murmushi kawai yayi baice komai ba.

Chan sai ga Dr Suraj ya shigo rik'e da k'atuwar leda ko kallon inda yake batayiba tana ji suna gaisawa da ummah yana mata ya jiki da me jiki ita dai shareshi tayi tanuna kamar bata san ya shigoba.

Suna gama gaisawa da ummah ledar hannunshi ya ajiye yace ga abinci nan ummah nasan yau baku ci abinciba tunda itama me jiyyar ta koma itace ake jiyya, yafad'a yana kallon meerah da tanuna ko inkula.

Murmushi ummah tayi tace abun har da hidima ai da ka barshi kawai wallahi.

A'a ummah bakomai ai yi ma kaine albarkarki kawai nake buk'ata.
Har cikin ranta ta ji dad'in hakan tace toh shikenan mungode Allah yasaka da alkhairi Allah yayi albarka.

Shi kanshi ya ji dad'in addu'ar da tayi mashi yace Ameen ummah.

Matsawa yayi kusa da Dr Saif da yake k'ik'irin saka ma meerah drip bayan ya cire ledar jinin yace sannu da k'ok'ari Doctor. 

Murmushi Doctor Saif yayi yace yauwa Doctor Suraj kaima sannunka yau dai da alama zarcewa aiki zakayi har dare.

Gaskiya kam tunda ganinan har yanzu ban bar asibitin ba tun safe ammah da nayi magrib zan tafi.
Toh Allah dai yataimaka, yana gama sa ma meerah drip d'in yace bari inje indubo wata patient ko ta farka nasan har na gama abinda nake drip d'in bai k'areba inma ya k'are ga Dr Suraj nan.
Shuru Meerah tayi batace komai ba sai dai ummah da tace toh doctor sannu ai kana ma k'ok'ari Allah yabiya.

Bayan ya fita Dr Suraj matsawa yayi kusa da ita yace sannu Meerah ya k'arfin jikin?
Ciki-ciki ta amsa da dasauk'i.
Yace ko akwai inda yake maki ciwo?
Girgiza kai kawai tayi batare da tace komai ba.
Toh Allah yak'ara sauk'i yakamata da drip d'in nan ya k'are kici abinci ko kin samu k'arfin jikinki.
Ahankali ta amsa da toh dan duk ta gaji da surutunshi.
Juyawa yayi ga ummah yace ummah inmik'o maki abincin kici ko?
Girgiza kai tayi tace bazan iya ciba yanzu,, tunowa tayi da su lemun da taga mutumin nan ya shigo mata da su lokacin da yazo ward cikin ranta tace Allah sarki har da d'awainiya,,, muryar Dr Suraj taji ya ce ko akwai abinda kikeson ci?
A'a babu, lemun 6awone ammah sai zuwa anjima idan na koma chan d'akinmu sai insha suna chan d'azu aka kawo min.
Toh ummah ko inje insamo maki a bakin titi 
A'a Doctor kabarshi d'awainiyar ai tayi yawa wanchan ma da aka kawo min suna da yawa sosai.

Toh shikenan bari inje ind'auko maki sai inhad'o maki da drugs d'inki kisha dan nasan baki shaba.
Ummah tace toh shikenan na gode.
Saida yakalli Meerah suka had'a ido tad'auke kanta gefe sannan yajuya yafita.
Yana fita kamar jira takeyi tace wai ummah wane lemune naji kina magana d'azu fa ban siyo makiba wa yakawo maki.

Ummah kallonta tayi tace wallah wani bawan Allah ne yazo yadubani yakawo min shine ma yafad'a min kina nan bakida lafiya.
Kuma ummah baki sanshiba?
Eh gaskiya ban sanshiba ammah gani nake kamar na ta6a ganinshi k'ila ma d'an unguwarmu ne kinsan mutanen kirki yawa garesu baki ganiba dayawa yakawo cikin leda shi yama kawoni nan sannan yatafi k'ila shima marar lafiya gareshi.
Meerah tace wayyo Allah dai yabiyashi.
Ameen.

Suna cikin hirar Dr Suraj yadawo yakawo ma ummah ledar cike da su lemu da ayaba da kankana takar6a tasashi saida yad'iba shima nan tafara sha saida tasha taci ayaba da kankana sosai sannan tad'an ci abincin da Dr Suraj yakawo masu bayan ta gama tasha magani.

Meerah ko saida drip d'in yak'aro Dr Suraj yacire mata lokacin ana kiraye-kirayen magrib yamik'o mata takeaway d'in abincin da yasiyo masu da ruwa swan kamar ba zata amsaba saidai gudun kar ummah tace wani abu yasa ta amsa fuska a d'aure tace na gode.
Sallama yayi ma ummah yatafi masallaci.
Nan Meerah tabud'e tad'anci kad'an dan bata jin taste d'in bakinta kankana da ayaba ummah tamik'o mata taci bayan sun dawo sallah Dr Saif yazo yabata magani tasha yayi mata allura yana fita tabishi tana rok'onshi yasallameta.
Shi abun mamaki yabashi yace wai Meerah meyasa kika damu da asallameki kina gani fa bakida lafiya.
K'wallah ce tatarun mata a cikin idanu tace Dr kana gani ummana batada lafiya kasan kuma matsalarta ni yakamata ace ina jiyyarta ba wai ita take jiyya taba ganina a kwance ba k'aramin d'aga mata hankali yakeba kaga idan kasallameni ko yayane hankalinta zai kwanta.

Toh ke allurar da akeyi maki fa shikenan sai adaina?

Indai allura ce zan dinga zuwa kayi min kuma zan dinga shan maganin nidai kasallameni dan Allah.

Shuru yayi sai chan yace shikenan Meerah zanyi yadda kikace indai har dagaske zaki dinga zuwa ana maki allurar.

Eh wallahi zan dinga zuwa.
Toh kikoma da anjima idan nadawo zan sallameki.

Cikin jin dad'i tayi mashi godiya sannan takoma nan tasake tacigaba da hira da ummah kamar ba ita bace batada lafiya hidimar gabanta kawai take.
Koda Dr Saif yadawo bayan sallar isha'i sallama yarubuta mata yace ai yaga jikin ya yi sauk'i saidai tadaure tacigaba da shan magani.

Ummah batace komai ba dan taga alama itama jikin meerah ya yi sauk'in dan haka suka tattara suka koma a chan ward ummah takwanta gadonta, Meerah ko kusa da gadon ummah da akayi discharge d'in patient nan tagyara saida tagama ma ummah komai tasa mata net tashiga sannan itama taje takwanta.

Meerah bayan kwana biyu sosai take samun sauk'in jikinta dan a yanzu bata jin jirin da yake d'ibarta Dr Saif har tsiya yakeyi mata yana cewa Meerah kinsha jini kin tashi.
Saidai tayi murmushi kawai.
Ummah ma cikin ikon Allah tana samun sauk'in jikinta sosai dan a yanzu babu abinda bata iya yi ma kanta daga k'arshe ma sai tafara matsa ma Doctor akan yasallameta hakanan tunda ta samu sauk'i.
Saidai suce tajira suk'ara ganin yanayin jikin nata.

Dr Suraj ma kusan kullum sai ya zo sunsha hira tasha tace shi yasallameta saidai yace tayi hak'uri nan da akwana biyu ad'an k'ara ganin yanayin jikinta.

Jin ya ce nan da kwana biyu yasa tahak'ura tazauna, saidai duk da haka hankalinta yana ga yaranta da tabaro Meerah koda tafahimci haka yasa taje tad'aukosu wata ranar lahadi da ba'a makaranta nan sukazo sukayi wuni sosai ummah taji dad'in ganinsu suna cikin kwanciyar hankali, sai da marece Meerah tasasu a napep suka koma suna fushi sadeek ko har da kukanshi wajen ummanshi zai zauna.

Ranar litinin ko da wani babban likita yazo round yana zuwa wajen gadon ummah yadubata yace toh ai ke jikin naki Alhamdulillah sai sallama.

Wani irin farin ciki yadabaibaye ummah tace hakane Doctor dan ni yanzu garas nake jina jira kawai nake asallameni.

Doctor yace Masha Allah haka akeso, insha Allahu zuwa gobe da safe za'a sallameki tunda naga saura allurar da za'ayi miki dw anjima k'arfe sha biyun dare tarage daga ita babu saura.

Ummah kasa rufe baki tayi saboda farin ciki tace hakane Doctor Allah yakaimu.

Itama kanta Meerah tayi murna sosai dan dama zaman asibitin ya gundureta dan de babu yadda zatayi saisa.

Da dare koda Dr Suraj yazo yaduba folder d'in ummah murmushi yayi yace ummah ashe gobe sai gida.
Itama murmushin tayi tace kaidai bari Doctor tunda ku kunkama ni kun rik'e bakuso intafi Allah yataimakeni Doctor ya ce gobe zai sallameni.

Dariya Dr Suraj yayi sosai yace kai ummah bafa mu muka rik'ekiba munfiso daman sai angama allurar sannan musallameki tunda kinga har dare akeyinta kar musallameki kikoma gida Meerah tace kizauna kar kidawo a ida allurar tunda kin samu sauk'i,,,,yafad'a tare da kallon Meerah nan tahad'e fuska.

Murmushi Ummah tayi tace wane ita ai bama zata hanaba ita kanta hankalinta ba zai kwantaba har sai taga nazo asibitin.

Dariya yayi yace ai naga alama ummah dan wannan d'iyartaki tana ji dake sosai.

Cike da k'osawa da kallon da yakeyi mata Meerah tamik'e tace ummah bari inje chan bakin gate insamo maki kankana.

Su duka kallonta sukayi ummah tace da kinbar kankanar nan yau ta jiya ma ba zak'i kinga sai barinta mukayi.

Murmushi tayi tace yau ai zan samo mai zak'i, muryar Dr Suraj taji ya ce k'anwata tunda dai baki gane me zak'i kibarta kawai ni zanje insamo ma ummah me zak'i wadda zata manta inda take idan tasha.

Murmushi ummah tayi tace kaidai ko, kabarshi kawai Doctor ni na ma k'oshi haba hidimar ai tayi yawa kullum cikin hidima kake da mu.

Kai ummah kome nayi ai mahaifiyata nayi ma kinga ba abun damuwa bane wannan albarkar da kike sa min ina jin dad'inta sosai saisa kowane lokaci nakeso kiyi ta sa min albarka.

Har cikin ranta take jin dad'in kalamanshi indai zaiyi magana tabbas tasan Dr Suraj a matsayin uwa yad'auketa koda ita kanta tasan ba wani girme mashi zatayiba chan baifi tabashi shekaru goma sha ba ammah yana girmamata sosai.

Rik'o hannunta yayi yace ummah tunanin me kike kodai duk tunanin gidan ne?

Murmushi tayi batare da tayi maganaba, shima murmushin yayi yace ai insha Allahu gobe k'arfe sha d'aya kina d'akinki kina hutawa.

Girgiza kai tayi tace Dr Suraj kaidai baka gajiya.
Mik'ewa yayi yana dariya yace ummah bari inje insamo maki kankanar a bakin gate kokuma mawuyacine indawo ta nan saboda zan tsaya a chan A&E ko dai Meerah tazo muje ta amso maki.

Meerah kallon ummah tayi cikin sauri dan jin abinda zatace itama ummah shuru tayi tarasa me zatace dan bata ji zata iya amince ma wani yak'ara fita da d'iyarta.

Ganin tayi shuru yasa yace shikenan ma bari kawai sai inturo attenda takawo maki hakan ma zaifi, sai na dawo ummah.

Murmushi tayi tace toh Doctor nagode sosai Allah yasaka da alkhairi yajik'an mahaifa.

Ameen ya amsa tare da kallon Meerah sannan yawuce yatafi, tsaki Meerah taja ahankali bata san ya fitoba saida ummah takalleta ammah bata ce mata komai ba.



Bai dad-e da tafiyaba ya aiko atenda da manyan ledoji cike da kankana da lemu sai ayaba meerah ce takar6a ta ajiye dan lokacin ummah bacci takeyi, ido tazuba ma ledojin tana kallo cike da mamaki cikin ranta tana cewa shikuma wannan baida aiki sai wahalar da kanshi wajen yi mana hidima ai daga dai gobe shikenan mun huta da mun koma gida.


Koda ummah tafarka taga wannan tulin kayan mamakine yakamata tace Doctor ya cika hidima Meerah kalli irin kayan da yasiyo wannan duk ina zamu kaisu.

Ta6e baki Meerah tace tace nima maganar da nayi kenan ummah ai daga gobe dai shikenan sai yasan wanda zaiyi mawa mudai ya gama ganinmu.

Kefa meerah tsiyata da ke bakida kirki wani lokacin duk k'ok'arin da mutumin nan yakeyi mana baki gani ina lura da ke duk lokacin da zaizo wajen nan sai kin canza fuska shifa ba wani abu yake nema wajenkiba kuma wajena yake zuwa.

Marairaicewa tayi tace ummah ba haka bane kawai dai,, sai kuma tayi shuru.

Kawai dai me? Ni yanka min lemun insga kitashi kid'iba kikai ma kiwane gado.
Mik'ewa tayi tace toh ummah nan tad"auko wuk'a tayanka mata sannan tad'iba taraba ma sauran marassa lafiya nan sukayita godiya.

Murmushi tayi tare da cewa himm sannan tacigaba da cin abincinta su duka shuru sukayi suna me cigaba da cin abinci.

Dad ne yafara mik'ewa tsaye yace idan kun gama kusameni a parlour inason magana da ku.

Tun kan ya ida wucewa Haisam yace Mom me mukayi?

Toh Allah yasa muji alkhairi ni nama k'oshi da abincin,,, cewar Ammar.

Eh Mom na k'oshi.

A'a mom wallahi muna da gaskiya Allah dai yasa muji alkhairi.

Toh Ameen kuwuce muje mun barshi yana jira, nan suka d'unguma gabad'aya zuwa main parlour,

Zaune suka samu Dad yana jiransu nan mom tawuce tazauna kusa ds shi Haisam ne yafara zama saman lallausan carpet d'in da yake shimfid'e a tsakiyar parlourn sannan shima Ammar yazauna, shuru Dad yayi yana ksllonsu na d'an lokaci sannan yai gyaran murya yace nasan zakuyi mamakin dalilin da yasa nataraku a nan kokuma wannan ba abin mamaki bane tunda ba wannan bane karo na farko da nataraku ba saidai wannan tarin ya sha banban da wad'anchan,
Su dukansu shuru sukayi suna sauraren abinda zai ce, 
Kiran sunan Ammar yayi sannan yakira Haisam nan duk suka amsa mashi saida gyara zamanshi sannan yace  a yau idan har kuna da matan da kukeso ina so kufad'a min dan a gaskiya na gaji da zamanku haka ba aure.

Su dukansu kallon juna suka shiga yi tsakanin Haisam da Ammar kowa mamaki d'auke a fuskarshi shuru sukayi suka kasa magana.

Dad yace indai kuna da wad'anda kukeso sai kuyi magana nikuma insha Allahu zanje in nemo maku aurensu ko 'ya'yan wanene a garin nan.

Haisam duk'ar da kanshi yayi yana sosa k'eya inda Ammar yashiga wasa da wayarshi yana murmushi.

Ganin haka yasa Mom takalli Dad sukayi murmushi sannan tace kunyi shuru kufad'a mana man ko kuna da wasu iyayen da yakamata kufad'a mawa bayanmu?

Haisam ne yad'ago kai cike da jin kunya yace nidai gaskiya banda wadda nake so dan ban fara soyayya ba.

Ban gane baka fara soyayya ba soyayyarce bakayi kokuma budurwarce bakada ita.

'Daga kai yayi yace eh mom banda budurwa.

Jinjina kai Dad yayi yace dakyau naji naka toh shi d'an uwan nakafa?

Su dukansu zuba mashi ido sukayi suna kallonshi Ammar d'ago kai yayi yace Dad nima haka.

Kaima haka me? Kayi mana bayani,,, cewar mom.

Ina nufin nima banda budurwa.
Haisam cike da mamaki yake kallonshi yace bakada budurwa fa kace?
Shan mur yayi yace eh haka nace.
Dad ne yace kudakata nan dai mune baidace ku6oye manaba ba abin kunya bane dan kun sanar da mu 'yanmatanku tunda mu dai zamuje munema maku auren ko ba hakaba hajiya? 

Mom tace tabbas hakane toh shi Ammar idan 6oye mana yake sai d'an uwanshi shi yafad'a mana dan bazan ta6a yadda da Ammar baida budurwaba koda Haisam ya ce baida ita.

Dad yace nima haka wallahi bazan yarda da maganarshi ba kai Haisam idan yana da ita kafad'a mana.

Kallonshi Ammar yayi gabanshi yana fad'uwa kar Haisam yatona mashi asiri.

K'in kallon inda yake Haisam yayi yace eh Dad yana da ita akwai wata yarinya da yakeso kuma itama tana sonshi sosai kuma ina tunanin da alk'awalin aure a tsakaninsu.

Sakin baki Ammar yayi yana sauraren maganar da baisan da itaba.

Jinjina kai Dad yashiga yi cike da jin dad'i yana cewa dakyau toh wacece  ita? Miye sunanta?

Bud'ar bakinsa sai cewa MEERAH.

A firgice Ammar yad'ago yakalleshi tare da watsa mashi uwar harara yace wace meeeah d'in?

Cikin rashin damuwa yace Meerah dai wadda kasani.

Fuskar Smmar a d'aure yace kaga Haisam bana son irin wannan game d'in kafad'a ma su Dad dawasa kake dan kar suyarda da abinda kace.

Dad da Mom fara'a ce tabayya a fuskarsu suka kasa 6oye farincikinsu Mom tace miye kuma abun 6oye wannan ai abun farin cikine son.

A fusace Ammar yace mom ba abin farin cikj bane dan ni ba sonta nakeba taya zanyi soyayya da mazinaciya 'yar iska.

Dad da Mom sakin baki sukayi suna kallonshi, Haisam kam hacking yayi yakasa cewa komai duk mamaki ya cikashi saidai kallon Haisam da yakeyi.

Dad ne yayi k'arfin halin cewa wacece kuma haka?

Dad wadda Haisam yake cemaku ce wallahi ba mutuniyar kirki bace bin maza takeyi kuma ba d'iyarkowa bace a garin nan.

Katseshi Haisam yayi yace eh ba d'iyar kowa bace sannan kai yakamata atambaya halinta dan kafi kowa sanin halinta.

Koma me zakace Haisam bazan auri Meeeah ba dan bani sonta ko ana dole ne? Kai zaka iya auren 'yar iska ne?

Ku dakata ba zancen fad'a band indai har baya sonta toh shikenan kuma me ake da 'yar iska 'ya'yan mutunci yakamata kusamu ku aura.
Tabbas hakane Alhaji nima dai abinda nagani kenan dan shi aure ba abun wasa bane wata sabuwar rayuwace za'ayi gwara asamu agidana ta hanyar da tadace.

Su dukansu shuru sukayi ba wanda yatanka saima Dad da yace hakane zai dai fi masu kyau suje susamo matan kirki su aura ba wai sauri akeba ni banda matsala dan farin cikinku shine nawa saidai kawai inaso kuyi aurene inga 'yan jikokina, ko ba hakaba Hajiya?

Sosai ma Alhaji ai wannan maganar da kayi itace daidai munfiso suzauna sunutsu a cikin garin nan da kewayen garin duk macen da tayi masu sukaga suna so suyi magana insha Allahu zamu taimaka masu wajen ganin sun aureta dan nima ban goyi bayan auren 'yan iskan mataba wad'anda suka gama tambad'arsu a titi miye riba acikin auren irin wad'annan matan.

K'wantar da murya Ammar yayi yace Mom shi fa Haisam da wasa yakeyi maki kinsan dai ni sai mace me class wadda tayi daidai da rayuwata zan iya aure.

Toh marar kunya kai saikace class d'in gareka,, cewar mom

Dad ne yamik'e yace shikenan daman maganar da zanyi maku bari intafi ana jirana akwai wasu meeting da zamuyi nan da 30 minutes. 

Toh shikenan Dad Allah yakiyaye adawo lafiya

Ummah ce tamik'e dan tarakashi, suna barin wajen Ammar yajuyo yakalli Haisam fuska a d'aure yace kaga Haisam kar kasake yi min irin wannan game d'in inbanda abunka taya zan auri wannan yarinyar.

Wani irin kallo Haisam yayi mashi yace dole kace haka bayan ka gama wulak'anta mata rayuwa.

Look Haisam bana son jan sharri duk wanda yace maka ni nalalata mata rayuwa toh yayi k'arya dan tun kan muhad'u da Meerah take bin maza, dan haka kadaina had'ani da ita tunda nasan kaima kanka bazaka iya auren mace me irin halintaba.

Why not indai nasan ni na6ata mata rayuwa.

Dakyau Guy toh ni bani na6ata mataba so babu wanda ya isa yasa in aureta dan ba class d'ina bace ita, kai idan zaka iya sai kaje ka aureta kaga shikenan ka wakilceni,,, yana fad'in haka yamik'e yabar wajen yabar Haisam zaune yana binshi da kallo har ya6ace yabar wajen sannan yajanye idanunshi daga kallon k'ofa shima yamik'e yabar parlourn.

Da dare Meerah da ummah basu samu suka rintsaba sosai saboda farin cikin za'a sallamesu sukoma gida dan ummah har saida sha biyun dare tayi akayi mata allura sannan tasamu tayi bacci.
Meerah ko sai wajen k'arfe d'aya takwanta.

Tun da asuba da tagama sallah tafara had'a masu kayansu dan cewa tayi ana sallamarsu zasu wuce gida, 
ummah lokacin da tafarka Meerah ta gama had'a masu komai jira kawai take Doctor yazo yarubuta masu sallama sutafi, wajen k'arfe takwas tad'auki jug tace ummah bari inje insamo maki breakfast kiyi kafin kisha magani.
Toh ummah tace nan meerah tawuce tatafi tea taje aka had'a mata aka zuba acikin jug d'in sannan tasiya masu bredi tadawo nan sukayi breakfast. 

Wajen k'arfe tara Doctor yazo round koda yazo wajen gadon ummah yadubata ammah bai rubuta mata sallamaba ganin zai wuce yasa ummah tace Doctor ba yau za"a sallameniba?

Murmushi yayi yace yau za'a sallameki ammah sai zuwa anjima yanzu ga test nan na rubuta maki zakije Lab ayo akawo min sakamakon idan naga komai normal sai a sallameki.

Toh ummah tace yana barin wajen tace ma Meerah kinji abinda yace.

Murmushi Meerah tayi tace ai bakomai Ummah yanzu sai muje ayi ai ba sallamarba samun sauk'inki muka fi buk'ata muje lab d'in ayi.
Tashi ummah tayi suka fita suna zuwa lab akace suje subiya dubu ukku kud'in awon da za'ayi, chan wajen da ake biya meerah taje zata biya daidai lokacin Dr Saif yazo wucewa ta wajen tsayawa yayi nan suka gaisa cikin sakin fuska yatambayeta me zatayi a nan.
Murmushi tayi tace kud'i ne zan biya.
Kar6an takardan awon yayi yace mugani, tamik'a mashi yana dubawa yamik'a ma masu bada receipt yace ayi min discount patient d'itace kar6a sukayi sukace ba damuwa Doctor, awon dubu ukkune ammah sukace abada one thousand nan yad'auko yamik'a masu suka cike receipt suka mik'o mashi kar6a yayi yaba Meerah yace kuje kawai ayi.

Amsa tayi tace toh Doctor mungode sosai Allah yasaka da alkhairi.

Bakomai Meerah ai yi ma kaine kar kidamu Allah yabada lafiya bari inwuce sai mun had'u kafin ayi discharge d'inku.

Toh shikenan Doctor mungode nan tawuce tasamu ummah tana jiranta saida taba masu awon receipt d'in sannan talabarta ma ummah komai.

Murmushi ummah tayi tace Allah sarki mutanen nan akwai kirki Allah dai yabiyasu.

Ameen ummah.

'Dibar jininta akayi akace sutafi sai nan da awa d'aya sudawo sukar6i result sukace toh.

Komawa sukayi ward d'in suka zauna suna jira lokaci yayi aje akar6a Meerah sai duba lokaci take har awa d'aya tacika sannan tace ma ummah bari taje ta amso.

Ummah tace toh shikenan sai kin dawo.
Wucewa tayi tana fita zataje lab sai ganin Dr Suraj tayi ya fito daga d'akin yara (paediatric) nunawa tayi kamar bata ganshiba tawuce zata tafi, biyota yayi yana cewa haba Meerah bama zaki tsaya mugaisaba.

'Dan tsayawa tayi batare da ta kalleshiba tace ina kwana?

Eh dasauk'i.
Masha Allah, yanzu ina zakije?
Ta6e baki tayi cike da k'osawa da maganarshi tace zanje Lab inkar6o result d'in ummah.
Ohk, toh muje inrakaki.
Girgiza kai tayi tace no kabarshi kawai ba sai ka je ba nagode.
Matsowa yayi kusa da ita yace haba Meerah wai meyasa kike min irin haka a duk lokacin da kika ganni ina lura da ke in fara'a kike zaki d'aura fuska i don't know why, please in wani abu nakeyi wanda bakiso kifad'a min ni fa me k'aunarkine kuma aurenki nakeso inyi kibani dama innuna miki irin k'aunar da nakeyi maki.

K'wallah ce tacika mata idanu cikin sauri tagirgiza kai tace haka ba zai yuwuba Doctor bazan ta6a amince makaba dan bana sonka kai bama kaiba bana tunanin akwai wanda zan k'ara so a rayuwata dan gabad'aya rayuwata babu soyayya a cikinta dan haka kaje kanuna soyayyarta ga wata ba dai meerah ba.

Meerah kar kimin haka wallahi ni dagaske nake sonki kar kiyi tunanin zan cutar da rayuwarki, no ko d'aya ba cuta atare dani kid'auka cewa had'uwata da ke HA'DIN ALLAH NE na tabbata zakiji dad'i atare da ni.

Hawayene suka zubo mata lokaci guda tayi saurin gogewa tace ya isa haka Doctor bazaka ta6a samun wannan damar ba daga gareni kaje kawai nagode da tayin soyayyarka,, tana fad'in haka tajuya cikin sauri tawuce tabar wajen.

Doctor Suraj tsaye yayi yana binta da kallo cikin ranshi yana cewa me yarinyarnan take nufi, tana nufin bata sona duk son da nakeyi mata ammah bata gani, girgiza kai yayi yace bana tunanin zan rasaki meerah ina ji a raina zaki soni insha Allahu nine zan kasance miji agareki,,, juyawa yayi cikin sanyin jiki yawuce yatafi. 

Meerah tana zuwa takar6i result d'in tafito takoma ward tana zuwa tasamu Dr Suraj wajen ummah suna gaisawa ko inda yake bata kallaba tazauna tana kallonshi ya tsareta da idanu, ? Mik'a ma ummah result d'in tayi tace ummah gashi na kar6o kuma banga Doctor d'in da yarubuta awon ba.

Kar6a Dr Suraj yayi daga hannun ummah yaduba yace komai normal wannan ai consultant ne yarubuta maku dole dai shi zai sallameku kuma sai zuwa anjima zai shigo nasan idan yazo zai sallameki.

Ummah tace toh ba matsala Allah yakawoshi lafiya nidai fatana yasallameni inkoma gida yau.

Dariya yayi yace kai ummah kedai kin gaji da zaman asibitin nan.

Sosai ma wayakeson zaman asibiti ai sai inyazama dole. 

Hakane, insha Allahu yau ma zaiyi discharge d'inki, zan mashi magana idan yafito zuwa anjima dan yanzu yana chan theatre room, bari inje nasan bazaku kai la'asar ba za'a sallameku.

Toh Doctor nagode sosai Allah yasaka da alkhairi.

Bakomai ummah ai ba'ayi bankwana ba zan dawo.
Murmushi ummah tayi tace ba damuwa sai ka dawo.
Kallon Meerah yayi da kanta yake sunkuye sannan yawuce yatafi.

Tana ganin ya tafi tad'ago takalli ummah tace niko ummah banso mukai la'asar a nan Allah yasa yanzu Doctor d'in yazo.

Ameen nima zanso yazo kafin lokacin ammah ai doctor ya ce yanzu yana theater room dole sai ya fito zai zo, d'auko min lemu kiyanka min insha.

Toh ummah,, nan tad'auko tayanka tamik'a mata.


wajen k'arfe biyu bayan su Meerah sunyi sallah su Doctor Suraj suka shigo tare da Doctor yakar6i result d'in yaduba sannan yad'auko folder d'inta yayi rubutu yarubuta mata drugs a prescription sheet yamik'a mata yace shikenan zaku iya tafiya ansallameku Allah yak'ara sauk'i.

Cike da jin dad'i ummah da Meerah suka kalli juna sukayi murmushi sannan sukayi ma Doctor godiya.

Doctor Suraj ne yabi bayan doctor d'in suka fita.

Meerah cikin farin ciki ta ida had'a masu kayansu nan sukayi sallama da 'yan cikin ward d'in da suka saba suka d'auko kayansu suka fito a nan gaban ward d'in sukaga Dr Suraj jingine jikin motarshi ummah ce tace ga ma Doctor muyi mashi sallama sai mutafi.

6ata fuska meerah tayi tace ummah mutafi kawai tunda d'azu ya zo kinga ai munyi bankwana kenan.

Ke meerah banason irin haka mutumin nan anyi zaman mutunci da shi ya taimaka mana sosai kinga ai ya dace ace anyi mashi godiya.

Ganinsu tsaye yasa Doctor Suraj yatako ya iso wajensu yana murmushi yace ummah har kun fito.

Itama murmushin tayi tace eh Doctor zamu wuce mungode sosai da karamci Allah yasaka da alkhairi, Allah yajik'an iyaye.

Haba Ummah babu komai wallahi kidaina min godiya, ai ni zan ma maidaku gidan.

A'a Doctor kabarshi kawai kazauna kayi aikinka hakan ma mungode kayi mana abinda bazamu ta6a mancewa da kaiba.

Kallon Meerah yayi da itama take kallonshi sannan yamaida kallonshi ga ummah yace ai ummah anzama d'aya kunfi k'arfin hakan a wajena barinku asibiti ba shi zai hana adaina gaisawaba insha Allahu zan dinga zuwa har gida ingaisheshi, dan Allah ku amince inmaidaku gida kinga kuna da kaya.

Ummah shuru tayi nad'an lokaci ido meerah tazuba mata taji abinda zatace dan ita kanta bataso ummah ta amince, sai kuma chan ummah tabud'e baki zatayi magana, tarar numfashinta yayi tare da marairaicewa yace please ummah ki amince wallahi ba cutar da ku zanyiba.

Haba Doctor miyakawo kuma maganar cuta kokad'an ban ta6a tunanin zaka cutar da mu ba kawai gani nayi kar katafi kabar aikinka ko anemeka.

Ummah ba matsala ai na fad'a masu zan je indawo bazasu nemeniba.

Toh shikenan muje ba damuwa.

Cike da farin ciki yace yauwa ummah nagode sosai, juyawa yayi yakalli meerah da ta6ata rai yace k'anwata muje ko.
Harara tawurga mashi tare da kallon ummah tace ummah da ya barshi kinga kayanmu ba yawa a nan ma zamu samu me adaidaita.
Toh nikuma tawa adaidaitar batayi makiba?
Shuru tayi tak'yaleshi tai kamar bata ji shiba.
 Ummah ce tace kar kidamu Meerah muje yamaidamu kawai ba matsala.
Babu yadda ta iya dole tahak'ura, yace bismillah ummah muje.
Nan suka bi bayanshi har wajen motarshi yabud'e boot aka sa kaya su dukansu gidan baya suka shiga baice komai ba yabud'e shima yashiga yaja motar suna fita daga asibiti yace ummah wace unguwa zamuyi?
Fad'a mashi unguwar tayi.
Jan ummah yaitayi da labari suna hira ta cikin mirror yana kallon Meerah da tahad'e rai tana kallon window saidai yayi murmushi.

Duk d'an juyowar da Meerah zatayi sai sun had'a ido dan haka tadaina juyowa cikin ranta tace mutum sau kallon tsiya.

Tambayar ummah yayi lokacin da suka iso unguwar nan tacigaba da nuna mashi hanya har k'ofar gidansu
A daidai wajen gidan yai parking d'in motarshi nan suka bud'e suka fita ummah ce tak'ara yi mashi godiya yace bakomai ummah, nan yabud'e boot yafito masu da kayansu har ummah zata d'auka yace a'a ummah kibari asamu yara sushigarmaku da su keda kikasha jiyya.

Murmushi tayi tace ai yanzu Alhamdulillah sauk'i ya zo zan iya d'auka kabar yaran nan kawai kafin asamesu yanzu sai anwahala, banda abinda zance maka Doctor kamar yadda banda abinda zan biyaka da shi saidai addu'a Allah yasaka da alkhairi, Allah yabiyaka mungode mungode sosai.
Haba ummah ai kunfi k'arfin haka a guna kidaina min godiya.
Godiya dolece Doctor dan kayi abinda za'a gode maka saboda banda abinda zan biyaka da shi,, shuru yayi yana murmushi nan tad'auki wasu kayan tace meerah kid'auko sauran.

Meerah da take tsaye chan gefe tace toh ummah nan ummah tashige gida.

Kallon Dr Suraj tayi da yatsareta da idanu, janye idanunta tayi gefe guda sannan tace mungode Doctor da taimakon da kayi mana.
Murmushi yayi yace bakomai meerah ai yi ma kaine, dan Allah inaso kimin alfarma guda.
Juyowa tayi takalleshi batare da tayi maganaba.
Ganin haka yasa yacigaba da cewa inaso kitaimaka kisama min matsugunni ko kad'anne a cikin zuciyarki meerah wallahi sonki nake da gaske.
Gefe guda takalla tace kayi hak'uri bazaka iya samun wannan ba agareni dan kwatakwata zuciyata babu waje ko kad'an da wani zai shiga nagode sosai da hakan,, nan taduk'a tad'auki sauran kayan tajuya zata tafi.

Ahankali yakira sunanta nan taja tatsaya batare da ta juyoba, muryarsa chan k'asa yace please Meerah kar kimin haka kitaimaka min kiban dama ni zan sama ma kaina waje acikin zuciyarki amincewarki kawai nakeso.

Girgiza kai tayi batare da tayi maganaba
Why Meerah? Kokina tunanin zan cutar da ke ne?
Juyowa tayi takalleshi sai kuma tajuya cikin sauri tashige gida.

Tsaye Doctor Suraj yayi yana binta da kallo yana jin wani iri acikin ranshi tambayar kansa yashiga yi meyasa meerah ba zata amince min ba? Ko tanada wanda takeso? A fili yace toh intana da wanda takeso ai da ta fad'a min, saidai bana ji zan iya hak'ura da ke meerah dan ina sonki ya dad'e tsaye a wajen sannan yabud'e motarshi yashiga yatada yakoma asibiti.

Toh kuma me nayi?
Haba ya sai muna cikin farin ciki sai kakawo mana maganar wata maryam indai sonta kake kaje kamaidota man ina ruwana.
Rik'o hannunta yayi yace haba Hannatu me yayi zafi haka, nifa ba ce miki nayi ina son maryam ba kuma maryam d'in da ba sauran igiyar aurenta a wajena.
Kwantar da murya tayi tace toh ai kaine Alhaji kadinga min zancenta kuma kasan banaso.
"Afuwan na daina"
Yauwa kokaifa yanzu taso muje ciki inbaka wani labari.
Mik'ewa yayi yana washe baki yana cewa kai Hannatu kina rikitani dayawa.
Gaba tawuce tana dariya yana binta a baya shima yana dariya.




*****

...Haisam narasa meyasa har yanzu ka k'i kadawo min a yadda kake a da, wai me nayi maka irin haka?

Kallon Ammar yayi na d'an lokaci sannan yajanye idanunshi gefe d'aya yace wani abu kaga ya faru.

Girgiza kai yayi yace no just  naga ka canza min dayawa har yanzu na kasa gane me na aikata wanda zaisa kacanza min dan tun tasowarmu bamu ta6a fin kwana d'aya muna fushi da juna ba, ammah yanzu kaduba tsowon lokacin da aka d'auka, na rasa gane kanka ada ko fita zakayi indai bamu fita tare ba toh zaka sanar da ni inda zakaje ammah yanzu duk ka canza min i don't know why,  ko zaka taimaka kafad'amin dalili.

Murmushi Haisam yayi yace ni bana fushi da kai kuma bani na canza makaba halinkane yacanza saisa nima nad'anyi baya da kai ko ka samu kad'an sarara.

Look Haisam kadaina maganar abinda yawuce dan ni tuni nabar wannan rayuwar kaine dai kakasa gane haka.
Kuma ba zan ta6a ganewaba Ammar.

Dafe kansa yayi da hannu biyu yace Haisam kar kabari su Dad sugano halin da muke ciki dan suma bazasuji dad'iba.
Ta6e baki yayi yace wannan matsalarkace.
Kuma kaima takace matsalar Haisam domin abinda yashafeni ya shafeka.
Juyowa yayi yakalleshi yace banda matsala a nan.
Toh ammah....ringing d'in da wayar Ammar tafarane takatsesu daga maganar da suke nan yakai idanunshi akan wayarsa da take ajiye gefen Haisam ganin sunan mufida da yabayyana yasa yai saurin d'auko wayar yai rejecting,  d'ago kai yayi yakalli Haisam da shima yake kallonshi murmushi yayi cikin alamum rashin gaskiya yace wani abokinane yake kirana tun d'azu wasu kud'ine zan bashi.
Ta6e baki Haisam yayi tare da mik'ewa yaje yabud'e freezer yad'auko Swan water yad'aga kwalbar yasha yana gama sha yarufe yamaida cikin freezer yakoma saman 3 seater yakwanta tare da lumshe idanunshi.

Ganin haka yasa Ammar yamik'e yace bro naga kamar bacci zakayi bari intafi inbarka kahuta.

Ohk, idan ka fita karufe min k'ofar.
Toh ba matsala atashi lafiya.

Yana safka daga saman step nan wani kiran yasake shigowa wayarshi tsaki yaja tare da yin picking yace ya dai Mufidah? 
Daga chan 6angaren Mufidah tace Dear ka 6oye ganinka yana neman yaimin wahala.
'Yar dariya yayi yace haba taya ganina zaimiki wahala ai sai idan bakiso ganin nawa ba.
Hmm haka dai kace ammah kaima kasan ka 6oye, kuma gaskiya urgently nake son ganinka.
Ah kice da matsala.
Dariya tayi tace no ba wata matsala just  dai kasa min time muhad'u kasan nayi missing d'inka sosai.
Juyawa yayi yakalli k'ofar d'akin Haisam da yake a rufe saida yarage murya sannan yace muhad'u a gidana bayan magrib hakan ya miki?.
Cikin jin dad'i tace as u wish dear. 
Dariya yayi tare da kashe wayarshi ahankali yace Mufidah matsala ina ma ace Meerah ce zamu had'u a yau, tsaki yaja yawuce yafita yabar gidan.


_____
Ummah tunda suka dawo gida sosai tamaida hankali da shan maganin da aka rubuto mata nan da nan tafara maida jikinta, tana k'ok'ari wajen ganin ta rage yawan shiga damuwa saidai duk da haka hankalinta yana tashi sosai idan tatuna halin da d'iyarta take ciki.

Ita kanta Meerah kawai tana k'ok'arin 6oye damuwartane ammah ko kad'an babu k'wanciyar hankali atare da ita idan tatuna da abinda ta aikata saidai ta6oye kanta tayita kuka.


Yau kasancewar alhamis su dukansu suna cikin d'akinsu Safiya da Sadeek sun tisa ummah a gaba suna ta mata surutu Meerah ko tana kwance saman katifa tana kallonsu ammah hankalinta ba wajensu yakeba.

Sallama yaro yayi yace wai ance Doctor Suraj yana sallama da Ummansu Meerah.
Murmushi ummah tayi tace Allah sarki doctor kace mashi ina zuwa.
Toh yaron yace nan yawuce yafita.
Kallon Meerah tayi tace Meerah tashi kije kishimfid'a mashi tabarma a zaure kar abarshi yayi ta tsayuwa.

Ahankali tace toh ummah nan tamik'e tasa hijab taje tad'auko tabarma tafita a zaure ta shimfid'a sannan talek'a waje taganshi tsaye wajen motarshi yana ganinta yamatso yana murmushi.

Sunkuyar da kanta tayi k'asa tace ina wuni.
Amsawa yayi batare da ya janye idanunshi daga kallontaba yace Meerah watau tunda kuka baro asibiti sai kukamanta da mu.
A'a bamu mance dakuba bismillah kashigo ummah tana zuwa.
Bai musaba yashigo nan zaure yazauna saman tabarmar da tashimfid'a mashi tace bari inkira ummah.
Toh yace nan tawuce yana cigaba da kallonta har tashige gida 

Ummah kud'i taba Safiya tace taje tasiyo mashi ruwa masu sanyi.

Meerah zama tayi tace ummah gashi chan zauren kije kugaisa.
Mik'ewa tayi tace toh shikenan bari inje kun dai gaisa ko?
Eh ummah mun gaisa.

Hijab ummah tajanyo tasaka tafita a zaune tasameshi tare da Sadeek yana ta mashi surutu, ummah tace ikon Allah watau Sadeek yana nan yana ta yimaka surutu ko?
Murmushi yayi yace gafa shinan ummah yana ta bani labari, duk'ar da kai yayi cike da girmamawa yagaisheta.
Ta amsa mashi fuska a sake tace kamar kan yau nagama tunaninku sai gashi ka zo.

Wallahi ummah nima kuna raina saisa nace bari dai inbiyo indubaku kullum nashiga ward sai na kalli gadonki inga kamar zan ganki saidai inga wata ta daban masha Allah naga jiki yayi kyau sosai ummah kamar ba keba.
murmushi tayi tace ciwo ai ba dad'i.
Ruwan da Safiya takawone ummah tace tamik'a mashi yasha nan tabashi yafasa yasha, saida yagama sha sannan yace ummah wannan ma d'iyarkice?
Murmushi tayi tace eh duk 'ya'yanane.
Allah sarki, Allah yaraya su.
Ameen Doctor na gode sosai ai kunsha hidima sosai ina d'ayan doctor d'in?
Oh Dr Saif yana nan d'azu ma yake cemin ansallameku bai saniba baizo kukayi bankwana ba.
  "Allah sarki ai kunyi d'awainiya da mu bazamu ta6a mancewa da alkhairinku agaremuba".
Haba ummah bakomai ai anzama d'aya.
Toh madallah.
Mik'ewa yayi yace toh ummah ni zan wuce daman cewa nayi bari inbiyo mugaisa sai kuma wani zuwan.
Gaskiya naji dad'i Allah yabada ladar zumunci mungode sosai agaishe da mutanen gida.
Zasuji sosai, kallon Sadeek yayi yace je kakira min Meerah muyi bankwana.
Toh yace nan yashige cikin gida dagudu yana k'walah ma Meerah kira.

Ummah cikin gida takoma tasamu Sadeek yana ce ma meerah tataso ana kiranta, ganin ummah ta shigo yasa tace ummah ke kike kirana wai?

A'a Doctor ne zakije kuyi bankwana tafiya zaiyi wai.
Toh ummah yatafi man me zan mashi 
Wani irin kallo ummah tayi mata tace ai daman ba abinda zakiyi mashi shima ba wani abu yake nemaba a wajenki kawai dai bankwana zakuyi.
Kiyi hak'uri ummah bari inje, mik'ewa tayi tafita tasameshi tsaye cikin zauren gefe d'aya taja tatsaya fuskarnan ba alamun fara'a tace mungode agaishe da gida.
Murmushi yayi yace watau meerah korana ma kikeyi irin kin gaji da ganina, niko kinga bank'i mutsaya ahakaba inta kallon wannan kyakkyawar fuskar taki, akowane lokaci bazan daina rok'onkiba akan kitaimaka kikar6i d'an k'ok'on barana asanmin masauki acikin zuciyarki.
Yamitsa fuska tayi tace kaga banason irin wannan maganar na fad'a maka ni bana soyayya ko ma maganar kayimin 6ata min rai kakeyi dan haka kadaina, sai anjima agaishe da gida,,, hannun Sadeek tarik'o nan Dr Suraj yai saurin rik'oshi yace k'anena zo kashigar ma ummah da tabarmarta.
Toh yace meerah cikin sauri tashige cikin gida tabarsu.
Sadeek ne yashigo dagudu yana zuwa wajen ummah yamik'a mata kud'i yace ummah gashi me allura yabani yace insha sweet.
Zaro ido ummah tayi tace doctor d'in dakanshi yabakasu.
Eh ummah shine yabani.
Kar6ar kud'in tayi tace ikon Allah har dubu biyar shi wai doctor bai gajiya da hidima wannan kud'i ai sunyi yawa ina ma laifi yabaka naira goma duba fa kigani meerah kud'in da yadamk'a mashi.
Ta6e baki tayi tace inma dan yaburge yayi toh be burgeba,,, ta d'auka cikin zuciya tayi maganar sai jin ummah tayi ta ce gidanku meerah wa kike nufi zai burge?
Lah ummah ni badashi nakeba Sadeek ya gode sosai Allah yasaka da alkhairi.
Safiya ce tayi dariya tace kai Aunty Meerah kodai..?
Harara Meerah tawurga mata tace kodai me?
Rik'e bakinta tayi tace bakomai.
Tsaki Meerah taja tare da kwanciya saman katifa.

Safiya juyawa tayi takalli ummah da take rik'e da kud'i tace ummah tunda shi yabashi ba rok'onshi akayiba kinga sai muyi amfani da su daman kinga bamuda kayan abinci sai musiyo kuma a makaranta ance mukai d'ari biyu ga kud'in islamiyya bamu biyaba.

Ajiyar zuciya Ummah tasafke tace Allah dai yak'ara rufa mana asiri.
Ameen ummah.

Meerah dai tanaji batace komai ba dan indai dan itane da tace amaida mashi kud'inshi dan har ga Allah yanzu tatsani abinda zai had'ata da wani tasan dan ummah batasan sonta yakeba da ace ta sani da ita dakanta zata maida mashi kud'in saidai bataji zata iya fad'a ma ummah gudun kar hankalinta yatashi.



Ummah ce takatseta daga tunanin da take tace Meerah sai magana nakeyi maki ammah kinyi shuru me kike tunani ne?

Kallon ummah tayi tare da girgiza kai tace bakomai ummah.

Ummah badan ta yarda da maganartaba tace toh tashi kije kisamo maku abinda zakuci kafin dare yayi kinga ko darana bawani abincin kirki kukaciba d'an sauran garin da yarage ne kuka kwad'a kukaci.

Amsawa tayi da toh nan tajanyo hijab d'inta tasaka sannan ta amshi kud'in, Sadeek yace Aunty Meerah ina zuwa.
Toh taso muje, ummah me zan siyo mana?
Duk abinda kukeson ci kusiyo kuci.
Toh ummah intafo maki da hura ko?tunda d'azu ma bakici komai ba.
Eh in an samu sai kisiyo min dan zanfi son insha furar.
Da toh ta amsa nan suka fita.

Suna tafiya ummah tace Safiya taso kilinke kayan nan da kuka wanke kafin sudawo.
Toh tace nan tatashi tad'auko kayan tafara linkewa.
Ummah tagumi tazuba tana tunanin rayuwarsu da ba me dafa masu abu d'aya yafi d'aga mata hankali k'addarar da tafad'a ma d'iyarta gashi 'yan uwanta da tafi k'auna sune suka watsar da ita basu san a halin da take cikiba a yanzu bare halin da yaranta suke ciki, basu damu da cinta ko shantaba tabbas tasan ubangiji ne yake taimakonsu acikin al'amurransu.


Batasan dawowarsu Meerah ba saidai ji tayi sadeek yana ta6ata yana kiran sunanta d'agowa tayi takalleshi tace ya dai Sadeek kun dawo?
Sweet d'in da take a hannunshi yanuna mata yace kinga Aunty Meerah tasiya min.
Murmushi tayi tace toh ka gode, kallonta tamaida ga Meerah da take tsaye tana kallonta cikin ranta tana jin ba dad'i dan duk lokacin da zataga ummah tayi shuru ta zuba ma waje guda ido ranta abu d'aya yake bata tunanin rayuwar da tajefa kanta ummah takeyi,, k'wallah ce nan tacika mata idanu tayi saurin maidasu, ajiye ledar da take hannunta tayi gaban ummah tace ummah gashi ansiyo dankalin hausa ne muka samo soyayye.
Cikin sanyin jiki tace toh meerah kutashi kuyo alwallah gashichan ana kiran sallah inyaso in anyi sallah sai aci.
Toh sukace nan suka tashi gabad'aya suka fita dan suyi alwallah.

Sunayin sallar magrib ummah tasasu suka d'auko alk'ur'ani kowa yakaranta bayan sun gama ne suka ci abinci.
